Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the calendar is shared with the firm's people in Google by the person who connected it (asking Google for the sharing too would let ONE change the sharing of every calendar of the account); while the project's consent screen is in Testing a grant ends after seven days and the firm connects again (the same calendar is taken again); in TJAM and TJPE a person says the zone of each hearing; an event Google kept after somebody deleted it in the calendar and then purged cannot be written again under the hearing's id (`event_unavailable`, said on the hearing), and the firm makes a new calendar for it; the date of a hearing is typed by a person, unless the firm lets ONE read it from the publication (the eighth part of S10).
- **M3, sprint S10, second part (the notices to clients on WhatsApp, and the client's lawsuit on their link) is built.** A firm's clients are told by themselves, from the firm's own WhatsApp number, of four things (migration `0038_client_notices`, 38 pgTAP assertions with two firms): a hearing coming (`hearing_reminder`: a month, a week, the day before and the day itself, the steps a setting, each in its window of the hearing's own days), a hearing somebody of the lawsuit was told of (they, or the phone they share) that moved, was cancelled or was put back (`hearing_change`: once per change, and it stands for the reminder of the new moment), the course of a filed lawsuit every fifteen days from its filing (`lawsuit_update`: the last movement the court registered and the next hearing, which then counts as told, so a change of it is told; never to who was told anything of that lawsuit in the last seven days) and, the day a person triages a publication of the lawsuit as a payment, the warning against the scam of the fake lawyer (`payment_alert`: the firm never asks for money to release a value; one per lawsuit and month). Each kind is a template of the firm's own account at the vendor: the platform proposes its text (`notice_kinds`, plain and with nothing the bar's rules would weigh), who manages the firm submits it or its own words keeping the kind's values, each once and in order and never at an end (`begin_notice_template`, with the second factor, never a support visit, refused for a firm whose numbers are of more than one account), the vendor is asked through the new `createTemplate` of `packages/whatsapp` (a utility template with positional values and an example of each, read in Meta's reference on 2026-10-11; the simulator approves at once; no answer, or a server error, is `unconfirmed`, and after a refusal or an `unconfirmed` the account's templates are read again, so one the vendor already has under that name and with that text is recorded instead of submitted twice), ONE keeps what it answered (`record_notice_template`), and a kind is switched on only once the vendor approved it as a utility message (`set_notice_kind`); a template the vendor later calls marketing (`template_category_update`, read on 2026-10-11, `wa_set_template_category`) carries no notice any longer; a new text of a kind that is on waits for the vendor while the one before goes on, and takes its place by itself once approved (`promote_notice_template`, on the template's own status). What a notice says is filled by the database from the case's own record, never by a model. A notice goes only to who agreed to be told at the number the firm has for them (`notice_optins`, never rewritten: one agreement in force per person, ended when taken back or replaced by one for another number, each kept with its words, its number, its moment and how it was given): on their own link, only in the very words the page showed them, with what the server saw of the request (a few agreements a day; stopping always), or as a person of the firm recorded it, saying how; never to who asked the firm to stop (`wa_optouts`, a mobile found with and without its ninth digit); a minor's lawsuit is told to who answers for them, never to the child; and a phone a family shares is told a thing once. What is due is recomputed on every sweep (`notice_candidates`, less what was told already, `notice_already_told`) and each notice is reserved in the statement that writes its message to the outbox of 0019 (`reserve_client_notices`: one per person, kind and occurrence, under a lock of the firm, within the firm's ceiling of 24 hours, which starts at the 250 a new account has at the vendor, a part of it kept for what is about a hearing, so the words on the lawsuits never take the room a change needs, in the hours of the place's day the setting gives, from 8 to 20, and from 6 for what is about a hearing of the morning of that very day, which the usual hours would not reach); the outbox sends it once; a message refused for a passing reason is reserved again as the same notice, as the case is then, after a wait that doubles each time (half an hour, then one, two and four hours), one whose fate is unknown never, and counts as told; what a person was told of a hearing is only what went or may have gone; while the vendor refuses the firm's token (its connection failed) nothing goes and no notice spends a try. A notice of a hearing or of a payment waits the setting's fifteen minutes after a person last wrote what it tells (`hearings.changed_at`, its own moment: the calendar's writes of the row are no change of the hearing), so a mistake corrected at once never reaches the client; how to be at a hearing is said within what a value of the vendor takes, and a link too long to fit is never cut. The job `one-client-notices` sweeps every ten minutes, and `one-client-notices-now` on `one/notices.sweep` (a kind switched on, somebody agreed), the requests that come together making one run. The client's link gains their lawsuits (`client_link_course`: the number, the court, the next hearing with how to be there, the latest movements in the court's words, and under them the warning against the scam) and the agreement (`client_link_notices`, `client_link_set_notices`; asked only while the firm has a notice on); a link made for a case with a lawsuit the court has lasts the setting's 180 days (`client_link_days_filed`). The app: the card "Avisos ao cliente" on a case (who may be told and why not, recording an agreement or that the person asked to stop, with how, what is due and what went, with what became of each message), the card of the notices in `/settings/connections` (each kind with when it goes by the setting's days, how a client reads it with an example of each value, its state at the vendor and what became of a new text, its text to submit and its switch, and the notices of the last 24 hours against the ceiling), "Cliente sem aviso" on a hearing in `/agenda` and on its case (`firm_hearings_unwarned`), and the count "Audiências com cliente sem aviso" in `/inicio` (`firm_today.hearings_unwarned`, to anybody of the firm: anybody records an agreement). `lib/notices/notices.ts` holds the pure rules and words, `facts.ts` what the screens read, `actions.ts` the writes, `work.ts` the backend's half and `jobs.ts` the jobs. The worker has its own test against the embedded database, with the simulator where the vendor stands (`apps/web/src/lib/notices/notices.db.test.ts`, part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`): two sweeps at once on two connections send each client one message, and a hearing that moves is told once. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a template the vendor did not answer for and does not list yet is not recorded, and the firm submits it again (under a new name, if the account took the old one meanwhile); a reminder whose window passed while the firm was at its ceiling, or outside the hours, is not sent late (a hearing before seven in the morning has no reminder of its day); the agreement a person of the firm records is the firm's word of it (the client's own is on their link); a family that shares a phone is told in the name of one of them; a change of only the link or the place of a hearing is told by its next reminder, never by a notice of its own; Meta sends a template's new category only to an app subscribed to `template_category_update`, which the platform's app at Meta must be; the warning against the scam goes when a person triages the payment, not when the money is released, which ONE never sees; the partner's weekly digest is not built yet.
- **M3, sprint S10, third part (an answer of the AI sent back with what to change) is built.** It is the first of the owner's points of 2026-10-13 to be built; the others wait for the owner's decisions (see "Waiting on the owner"). The firm's queue has a third decision (migration `0016_ai_runs`, edited in place: `approvals.status` `returned`, `ai_tasks.revisable`): who may decide an answer sends it back with what to change, with the second factor, only for a task whose code reads the note (a chronology and the text of a piece; never a transcription, which is the client's own words, nor the check of a document), and what the task answers then is another run with its own row in the queue; the operator's measure counts the answers sent back as answers that were not good as they came. The versions of an answer (`record_versions`) keep who decided it, how and when, and never the answer as approved nor the words of a decision (`audit_approval_version`): what a lawyer asked to change, like the text of a piece, is not the operator's to read. A chronology sent back (`0026_statements`) is asked again at once: the statement keeps the firm's words and the proposal they are about (`returned_note`, `returned_run_id`) until a proposal that read them is decided, the model reads both (a fact that is not in the client's account enters only when the firm says it in so many words, and its line then ends with "(informado pelo escritório)"), and the proposals of one statement are sent back as many times as the setting allows (`max_returns`). The text of a piece (`0029_drafts`) is sent back where it is read, on its case, by a partner or a lawyer (`return_case_draft`: the version they read, the second factor; the queue refuses it, `return_on_case`; only a text whose citations were read, with no mark of a person left in it or written in the request, and no longer than the AI gives back whole, `max_revision_chars`): the AI's own text that waited is decided as sent back, and the draft owes its rewriting (work `revise`), during which the text is nobody's to edit, to send back again or to approve; who may ask gives it up (`withdraw_draft_revision`), a rewriting that could not be made leaves the text as it was with the request for a person to put back to work or to give up, and each rewriting asked for has runs of its own, told by the key the database gives them (`draft_awaits_run`), so the answer of one given up, arriving late, is never taken for the next. **What identifies a person never reaches the AI, and comes back**: the text and the request are handed to the worker with every name of the people of the case, CPF, CEP, telephone and e-mail under a numbered mark ("[nome 1]", the same words under the same mark, `case_text_keyed`; a name is found however it is written: whole with no accent or in capitals, each of the names a hyphen or an apostrophe joins alone, a letter and its accent typed apart, never as a part of a longer word, `case_names`), the list is kept on the draft while the work lasts (`revision_keys`, a column no member is granted), the worker is told which marks hold the same name when one is written in more than one way (`case_marks_same_name`, by the marks alone: in general one person, and the instructions of the task say no more; a name alone counts only where it is written with its capital), and what the AI writes becomes the next version with the words put back from that very list (`case_text_restored`), read from the run by the database and never from the worker, beside what was asked and by whom (`case_draft_versions.note`, `asked_by`, `based_on`) and what the AI said it changed, with the same words back (`notes`: the screens read it there, never from the run, whose answer keeps the marks; before it, the words of each mark that appear more or fewer times in the new text than in the one before, counted where they are written in the two texts, since a name dropped or repeated may be what was asked and refuses nothing); a mark the AI changed or made up stays in the text, however it wrote it (another separator or space, another language, a bracket lost or changed: the gate reads the text as its document writes it, and the preview names the marks it found), keeps the piece from being approved and from being sent back again as it is (`has_person_mark`, `placeholder_left`, which also stops a plain "[nome]" a first text copied: a mark left would be read as one of the next rewriting's list, and get somebody else's words); `case_text_for_ai` is now the same reading with plain marks, so the two never differ. A rewriting turned down leaves the draft with the text there was before it, put back as the next version (`restored`: no version is ever changed or removed; the answer of the AI that text came from is the draft's again, with what the AI said of it, `draft_run_at`), unless a lawyer already wrote on from it, and then the text is the lawyer's and stays; the answer of a rewriting whose work was given up, turned down in the queue, ends the request; a rewriting is put back to work by who may ask for one; a text is turned down as the version that was read (`reject_case_draft` takes it), and one a lawyer sent back or had rewritten is given up by who signs only; approved as the AI wrote it, it counts as unedited (what is approved is the very version its run wrote). The instructions of the task cover both requests, and the first line of what the model reads says which. The app: "Devolver com anotações" on the card of a chronology in `/aprovacoes`, with the request and the proposal it answers shown beside the next one; in the editor of a piece, "Pedir ajuste à IA" from the text or from the preview, the text read only while the AI rewrites it and while the request is being written (a request is about the text as it is saved and read, and the words typed outlive a refusal, a box closed and a newer version to load), and, when the new version comes, what changed from the one before, paragraph by paragraph and word by word (`lib/cases/text-changes.ts`, pure: it shows where to look and decides nothing, and two texts too far apart are shown whole, never as a comparison cut short); "Recusar o ajuste", "Tentar o ajuste de novo" and "Desistir do ajuste" on the case. pgTAP `0042_returned_answers` (52 assertions with two firms, each with a piece of its own); the round trip of a piece sends a text back with the model told what to write and reads the names back in the next version (`apps/web/src/lib/cases/drafts.db.test.ts`). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a name of somebody who is not of the case, an address and a document other than a CPF are under no mark and go as the lawyer wrote them (the screen says so where the request is typed); the lawyer reads what changed and still approves the whole text in the preview; a rewriting spends a run of the model on the whole piece, however small the change; the measure of the task counts first texts and rewritings together; a name two people of the case share is under a mark the AI is told nothing about; a word of the text that is also a name of somebody of the case ("nascimento", "dias") is under a mark too, and comes back as it was; what is still handed over as written: a name spelled otherwise than on record (Sousa for Souza), "Júnior" as a first name, a letter outside the alphabet of Portuguese, and a telephone written with spaces inside its number; if the operator gives the task an autonomy in which its answers do not wait in the queue, a rewriting is undone by hand, since turning one down is the decision of the answer that waits.
- **M3, sprint S10, fourth part (the photo of a document measured where it arrives) is built.** The first half of the owner's point of 2026-10-13 on the quality of what a client sends, the half that depends on no decision. The rule is `@flyee/documents/quality` (`qualidade-da-imagem-v1`): `measureImage` reads a photo upright on a grey copy of a fixed number of points (those of a page of 768 by 1,024, whatever the photo's shape) and measures its sides, how sharp its edges are, and how dark the dark side of its edges and how light the light one are. **Everything but the size is read at the edges that stand out of the picture** (its own steepest, above its own grain), so a photo in focus measures the same whether much or little is written on the page, in dark ink or faded, and whether the paper fills the frame or lies small on a table, on wood or on a woven cloth: a till receipt with three faded lines measures as a full page does. Sharpness is how much each edge bends for how steep it is, each edge read whole, along the softer of the two ways, across and down (200 and more for print in focus, under 30 for a photo out of focus); the grain of a sensor is taken off it and the weave of a cloth under part of the picture is not; and where grain, a weave, a pattern or a blur is all over the picture and nothing stands out of it by its slope, what bends far more than the rest is read (a paper in focus on a cloth with a smooth pattern), or else the whole of it, so that a weave in focus says nothing and a page far out of focus is smooth, and blurry. The light is that of the two sides of the edges, the ink and the paper, read on a finer copy where a thin stroke is still ink. `imageProblems` reads the measure against limits that are data and says what keeps the photo from being read, each once and in the order a person fixes them: `too_small` (too few pixels along its longer side: a strip is not small for being narrow); `blurry` (its edges are soft); then `too_dark`, or, only of a photo that is crisp or has no edge at all, `too_bright` or `flat` (a photo a little soft has greyed its thin strokes, and is not called burnt or flat for that). `judgeImage` answers what is kept of a measure (the rule's version, the measure, the problems, the limits it was read against), or nothing for a setting that does not hold every limit. What cannot be measured (a format this build has no decoder for, a damaged file) is never said good or bad. The rule errs towards saying nothing, and its cases are a table: the pictures it is tried on are drawn (`quality.fixtures.ts`: pages and screens with much or little on them, in dark, grey or faded ink, with a barcode, rules or blocks of colour, a small receipt on a table, on wood, on a coarse or a woven cloth and on a cloth of smooth waves, out of focus, shaken, grainy, dark, burnt by a flash, fogged, small, a strip), and `npm run quality -w @flyee/documents` prints what it measures of each; on 2026-10-13 it was also tried by hand on receipts and sheets in real type (three inks, three tables, woven cloths and cloths with smooth patterns, a fifth to four fifths of the frame's width), and none in focus was spoken against. Migration `0024_document_checks`, edited in place (pgTAP `0043_document_quality`, 19 assertions with two firms): the setting `document_quality` (the kinds whose photo is measured: papers to be read, never a photo of what happened, which nobody takes again; the six limits), `document_quality_rule(kind)`, the one place that says which kinds are measured (the limits whole, or nothing), `case_documents.quality` (the rule's version, the measure, what keeps the photo from being read, the limits it was read against, when, and whether the client sent it all the same), written once by `record_document_quality`, which keeps only what the rule says, each thing of the kind it is, `record_document_insisted` (said only of a photo still the client's to send whose measure on record has something against it), `client_link_upload_to_finish` (what the backend is handed of an upload of a link, by the link's own token), `client_link_discard_upload` (a client takes back a photo they did not send: an upload of that very link, never one the firm received; the document that waited goes, and the record of its file with it) and `case_document_to_process`, which hands the work on a document what was measured and the rule of its kind. **The client is told at once, on their link**: the second half of an upload (`finishLinkUpload` in `lib/cases/client-uploads.ts`) measures the photo before keeping it (`judgeArrivedPhoto` in `lib/cases/document-quality-work.ts`), puts the measure on record and answers `photo_poor` with what is wrong, while the person still has the paper in hand. A photo is measured once and every later request answers from the record, one that came at the same moment included, so "sent all the same" is taken only from a client the record says was told, whatever the request asks; a measure that could not be kept is told to nobody; and what did not work for a moment (a database or a storage that did not answer) is answered as that, never as an upload that is gone. The page says it under the line the photo was sent from (one notice per document, which stays while the client sends another's, with what went wrong said on that line), in a sentence that says how to take the next one, with "Tirar outra foto" and "Enviar esta assim mesmo" (a paper may be faded, a rule may be wrong: the rule measures and a person decides); the photo the client did not send is taken back once the new one arrived (`takeBackClientUpload`), so nothing of it waits in storage for its reservation to run out. A photo that came some other way (a conversation, a person of the firm) is measured by the sweep of documents (`lib/cases/documents-work.ts`), which keeps the measure too. The firm reads it on the card of documents of the case and beside the AI's suggestion in `/aprovacoes` ("Foto difícil de ler pela medida do ONE", with what and whether the client was told), and the AI's check reads it in its briefing (`checkBriefing`; the instructions of the task say the measure is of size, sharpness and light, never of what is written). `lib/cases/document-quality.ts` holds what the screens read and the words. The path has its own test against the embedded database, with drawn photos and the bucket in memory, running what the link's own actions run (`apps/web/src/lib/cases/document-quality.db.test.ts`, part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`): kept, told, told again from the record, a request another got ahead of, sent all the same, taken back, refused, and measured by the sweep. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the limits are a first calibration, made on pictures the tests draw, which are crisper than a camera's, and are tuned on what the pilot firm's clients really send, which is what the measure kept beside each document is for (also when nothing is against it); a shaken photo is seen only where the shake softened its edges: a hand that moved at an even pace leaves thin strokes fainter and wider with ends as clean as before, and the rule says nothing of it (the check of the document, which reads the page, is what sees it); the same blur is told the same of large print and of small, which stops being read sooner; the edges read are the picture's steepest, so a page burnt by a flash on a dark table is answered for by the edge of the table, a page out of focus on a cloth in focus by the weave, and a small faint paper out of focus by the edge of the paper; a small paper in faded hairline print that is a little soft may be called flat or burnt (the edge of the paper is crisp, and its print is greyed); grain hides a blur that is all over the picture (heavy grain on any page; an ordinary grain on a dense page of a few megapixels, where nothing stands out of the blur and the grain makes it read rough); a photo burnt by a flash or fogged that is also a little soft is not called so; a page small in the middle of a picture is as sharp as any, though nobody reads it at that size; every photo taken again counts against what a link may send (60, a setting); a client who leaves the page with a photo waiting loses the choice, and the photo goes with its reservation in one to two hours; the rule refuses nothing: ONE refuses through its check, which reads the measure, and the client is asked for the paper again (the ninth part of S10); a person of the firm who adds a photo is not told at once (the measure shows on the card once the sweep read it); a HEIC photo is measured only where the deployed `sharp` reads it.
- **M3, sprint S10, fifth part (a family with a minor goes to the civil court together, and the client's word on it) is built.** The owner's decision of 2026-10-14, from the rulings read in full on 2026-10-13. The rule is `tipo-de-processo-v2` (`@flyee/rules/proceedings`): a minor can only claim in the civil court; when a minor is among the claimants, the whole family goes there in one lawsuit (`together`, the default), and the split (the adults in the small-claims court, the minors in the civil court, linked lawsuits as the first version planned) is a lawyer's recorded choice (`split`); an adult whose own claim exceeds the ceiling still goes to the civil court alone, the rest of an adult family staying in the small-claims court (the law lets that adult stay too by giving up the excess, art. 3, § 3, which is the firm's to weigh); the plan says how the family stands (`single`, `together`, `split`). Migration `0025_case_rules`, edited in place (pgTAP `0044_civil_court`, 21 assertions with two firms): `cases.family_choice` with its reason, who and when (`set_family_choice`: a partner or a lawyer, a reason for a split, never a support visit; before any lawsuit is planned the choice plans them, and once some are it records what they are: a split beside a small-claims lawsuit planned already, as when the adults were planned before a minor joined, the family together only with none planned beside its minor); `save_proceeding` (0022) refuses a planned small-claims lawsuit beside a minor of a case nobody split (`family_split_unchosen`); `court_notes`, what the platform read of how each court decides a split (its stance, a summary, each decision with its address, the day read; the operator's, `admin_save_court_note`, kept in `/admin/calendario`), seeded with the TJMT (against: the 5ª Câmara de Direito Privado extinguished the minor's lawsuit in March and April of 2026) and the TJDFT (divided: the 2ª Turma Cível fined the firm and wrote to the bar in March of 2026, the 7ª Turma Cível accepted the split in June of 2025). Each claimant of a lawsuit planned in the civil court says they accept it, through who gives their word today (`civil_court_answerer`: the claimant, or who answers for a minor now; an answer of somebody else, a guardian replaced or a minor who came of age, no longer counts), in the words of the private setting `civil_court_consent` (one for a minor, one for an adult who goes together with a family that has a minor, one for any other adult, a split family's included: the costs due at the start unless a judge grants free justice, the costs and the other side's fees if the claim loses and, under free justice, that they are charged only if the person can pay within five years and then are extinguished, the public prosecutor beside a minor, a longer course), on their link (`client_link_civil_court`; the answer by the backend, `client_link_answer_civil_court`, in the very words the page showed, compared as words, with what the server saw of the request; a few acceptances a day per claimant, and a no whenever asked) or through the firm, saying how, in the words its screen showed and over the answer the person read (`record_civil_court_answer`, anybody of the firm; other words, or another answer given since, are not overwritten unseen, `wording_changed`, `answer_changed`); `civil_court_answers` keeps every answer as given, never rewritten, the latest in force. What blocks a filing is one function, `filing_blocker` (a small-claims lawsuit beside a minor of a family nobody split, or a claimant of a lawsuit in the civil court whose word in force is not a yes), asked when a package is requested (`request_filing_package`, 0036) and again when its receipt is attached (`attach_filing_receipt`, 0027); a package keeps the kind of lawsuit it was built for, and one whose lawsuit changed kind is built again (`filing_package_outdated`); a package something now blocks says so on its case and in `/protocolo` (`filing_blockers`) and is not counted among the ones to file; a "no" gives up the package in course, told in the timeline, and is counted for the firm (`firm_today.civil_court_declined`, the filter `?ver=vara-civel` in `/casos`); the word given by a claimant of a lawsuit filed stays on the case as the firm's record. The app: the card "Como este caso vai a juízo" says how the family goes, lets a partner or a lawyer split it with the reason after reading what the court of the domicile decided (or put it back together), says when the lawsuits planned and the choice disagree, tells what to declare in each initial when it is split, and lists each claimant's word with the firm's way to record one and what to do after a no (`casos/[id]/components/family-court.tsx`, `lib/cases/family.ts`); creating the lawsuits refuses a family whose choice changed since the screen was read (`family_changed`); the case's list of what it lacks names a missing word or a no, and whose; the client's link asks for the word, own and of the children, and asks twice before a yes becomes a no (`c/[token]/client-civil-court.tsx`, `answerCivilCourt`). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the words of the acceptance are the platform's (a setting), not each firm's; anybody of the firm records a client's acceptance of costs, as with the agreement to the notices, with no second factor; only two courts have their decisions on record, and a court with none says so and nothing more; the TJMT's decisions are reached through searches of its portal, whose results may change; a claimant removed from the case takes their answers with them (the timeline keeps that they answered); the initial does not yet write the declaration of the linked lawsuit by itself (the lawyer is told to); a lawsuit recorded as filed by hand, outside a package, is recorded as it is.
- **M3, sprint S10, sixth part (what each firm lets ONE decide alone, automation first) is built.** The owner's decision of 2026-10-14: ONE operates by itself by default, the lawyer's own acts included, and each firm changes that per decision. Migration `0016_ai_runs`, edited in place (pgTAP `0045_autonomy`, 14 assertions with two firms): `autonomy_decisions` is the catalog of what ONE may decide alone, as data (each with the ways a firm may choose among: `auto`, ONE decides; `sampled`, ONE decides and a share waits for a person's check; `approval`, a person decides; and the platform's default, `auto`), seeded where each feature is built: `document_check` (0024, the AI's check of a document), `chronology` (0026, the order of the facts sent to the client), `piece_text` (0029, the first text of a piece), `deadline_confirm` (0034) and `file_or_hold` (0025, the Tema 1.417 decision). `firm_autonomy` keeps what each firm chose (`set_firm_autonomy`: a partner, with the second factor, never a support visit, on the record; `firm_autonomy` is what anybody of the firm reads), and the operator may hold a decision for every firm with the reason (`admin_set_autonomy_hold`: a person decides it everywhere until it is let go; the firm's choice stays; a decision an AI task takes is also held through the task's own autonomy set to approval, `autonomy_held`; a firm reads whether a decision is held, never the operator's reason, `admin_autonomy_catalog` being the operator's read). `finish_ai_run` reads it: an answer of a task that takes a decision waits only as the firm chose (the operator's autonomy of the task is now a ceiling, open by default for the three tasks, and holding it holds every firm; both samples, the larger counts). What ONE decides alone: a document check takes effect (the unattended path of 0024); a chronology goes to the client (0026); the first text the AI wrote of a piece is approved only right after its citations were read, untouched by anybody, when the same gate a lawyer's approval passes lets it (`case_drafts.auto_approval_owed`, set by the reading and tried once by `approve_case_draft_unattended`, called by the worker in its own step and in the queue's lock order, and by the job's sweep for a worker that stopped, `approve_case_drafts_unattended`, each draft on its own; a text the gate refuses then, or one a lawyer edited, sent back or had rewritten, stays a lawyer's to approve, and is never approved by ONE later; the answer that waited in the queue is decided as ONE's, `approvals.decided_auto`, left out of the operator's measure and shown in `/aprovacoes` as ONE's; the PDF follows as after a lawyer's approval, and the card "Protocolo" says a petition ONE approved was read by no lawyer; a rewriting still waits in the queue, so it may be turned down); a deadline is confirmed on the day counted when its count is recorded, unless something asks a person to look at it (`confirm_deadline_unattended`; a person's confirmation is the person's again, `deadlines.confirmed_auto`); and each new triage of the Tema 1.417 decides to hold on a high risk and to file on a medium, low or out of scope one, and decides nothing when the rule cannot tell, until a person decides (a decision a lawyer saves, even the same one or pending, is theirs: `cases.suspension_decided_auto`). Every such decision is told in the case's timeline as ONE's (actor `system`, the platform named ONE on the screens) and kept in the audit trail with `auto`. The app: `/settings/autonomia` ("O que o ONE decide sozinho": each decision with what ONE does alone in it, how the firm has it now and who chose it, the way to change it for a partner with the second factor confirmed (the way to set it up or confirm it said otherwise; only read in a support visit), the share checked when sampled, the operator's hold when there is one, and, beside the piece and the decision to file, item 3.1 of the OAB's Recomendação n. 001/2024 as it is written: no activity privative of the bar delegated to generative AI; `lib/firm/autonomy.ts`, `lib/firm/autonomy-actions.ts`), the card of the holds in `/admin/ai` (`autonomy-hold-card.tsx`), and the words that say a deadline was confirmed, a piece approved or a filing decided by ONE. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: item 3.1 of the OAB's Recomendação n. 001/2024 says no activity privative of the bar is delegated to generative AI systems, and the default lets ONE approve the text of a piece the AI wrote (the screen says so to the partner who chooses, and the card of the filing says a petition ONE approved was read by no lawyer); what is in force applies to what ONE decides from then on (a deadline proposed while a person confirmed waits for the next count); a firm that lets ONE send the chronology loses the queue's way to send one back (it asks for another on the case); creating the lawsuits and asking for the piece and the filing package are the seventh part; the deadline and the hearing read from a publication are the eighth, and the filing itself comes later (the custody of a lawyer's credentials for the courts' own channel); the Tema 1.417 triage runs only when somebody reads the weather or writes what the airline said, so a case nobody touched has no decision yet.
- **M3, sprint S10, seventh part (ONE carries a case from one step to the next by itself) is built.** The owner's decision of 2026-10-14 again: ONE operates the case until it cannot, or the firm said otherwise. Three more decisions of the catalog (migration `0039_case_advance`, pgTAP `0046_case_advance`, 20 assertions with two firms), each `auto` by default: `create_lawsuits` (once a case that never had lawsuits has what they need: nothing the case screen lists as missing, what each claimant asks, no document waiting for the firm; the lawsuits the rules plan, created through `create_planned_proceedings` with every check of a lawsuit, each signed by the firm's signers at the bar in its court's state, and a case still being gathered moved to drafting; a plan read before the case changed is refused and made again at the next look, and one that leaves out a claimant is refused), `start_piece` (a lawsuit nobody asked a piece for has the firm's signers named when it never had any (`proceeding_signers_seen`: signers a person took off are never named again, and the piece waits for a lawyer to name them), and its first text asked of the AI from the firm's default model of its kind through `request_case_draft`) and `request_package` (a lawsuit whose piece became its accepted petition has its package asked for through `request_filing_package`, with the accepted papers of its people (identity, proof of address, power of attorney: only of somebody of the lawsuit or who answers for them) and of the trip (also of nobody in particular), never the firm's contract; only while the case has every paper it needs accepted and none waits for the firm, never for a case the firm holds on the Tema 1.417, and, where the firm left that decision to a person, only once a person decided to file; never past a filing blocker, nor over a petition the firm added by hand (told apart from ONE's by the purpose of its file)). What a person did is never undone: lawsuits a person removed are never made again (`case_advances.lawsuits_at`), a piece a person turned down or gave up is never asked again, signers a person took off are not named again, a package a person cancelled is never asked again (`filing_packages.cancelled_by_person`) and one that failed waits for a person, and a petition the firm added by hand keeps ONE from asking for a piece of that case. When a lawsuit names no system and the platform confirmed its court's, ONE names it (a fact of the court; confirming one wakes the cases that waited for it). Who signs what ONE prepares is the firm's (`firm_signers`, named by a partner with the second factor in `/settings/autonomia`; who stops being a partner or a lawyer stops signing), and so is the model it writes from (`draft_templates.is_default`, one per kind, in `/biblioteca`; with no default, the only model of the kind). ONE writes through the very functions a person writes through, under the case's own lock: `case_for_writer` lets the backend's own functions in for the case they name (`case_system_writer`: the service role, with a setting of the transaction no API role can reach), and the timeline says it was ONE (`case_actor`); a piece ONE asked for says so (`case_drafts.requested_auto`). The backend's record of each case (`case_advances`, one per case, made by a trigger) holds when to look at it again, a numbered claim, its failed tries and what ONE waits for, which the case screen says with the way to put it right to who may take it (what each claimant asks, the ceiling, the domicile, the court, no signer at the bar in the state or signers a person took off, no default model, papers waiting for the firm, the court's system not confirmed, the case held or its decision left to a person, the family's choice, the client's acceptance of the civil court, a step that failed, and, after the setting's tries, that ONE needs a person); the database wakes a case when something somebody else writes changes what it reads (what ONE writes while it looks wakes nothing, so ONE holds no record of a case while it writes to it: its documents, parties, legs, lawsuits and their signers, pieces, packages, the answers on the civil court, a person's data, the decision on the Tema 1.417 and the family's choice, the firm's autonomy, a model, a signer, a registration at the bar, a minimum wage, a court's system), a case with nothing for ONE is set aside for the setting's hours, and one that ended or is with the court is never read; each lawsuit's step is its own, so one that fails leaves the others as they went. The job `one-case-advance` (every ten minutes and on `one/case.advance`, sent by the person's writes that may let ONE go on, by a piece just issued and by a paper ONE accepted) claims and carries one case at a time, each case locked on its own while it is looked at; whether a case has what its lawsuits need, and how they are planned, are the case screen's own rules (`caseMissing`, `lawsuitPlan`, now shared by the person's "Criar as ações" and the worker; `lib/cases/advance.ts` holds the pure rules and words, `advance-work.ts` the backend's half). The app: the line "O ONE leva este caso adiante sozinho e agora espera" on the case, the three decisions and the card "Quem assina o que o ONE prepara" in `/settings/autonomia`, the default model in `/biblioteca` (read again when its dialog opens, and saying which model stops being the default), and "pedida pelo ONE" on a piece; what the case lacks is shown at any stage while something is missing. The worker has its own test against the embedded database (`apps/web/src/lib/cases/advance.db.test.ts`, part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`): a case carried from its lawsuits to its package, its piece written, approved and issued by ONE on the way, its package waiting for the court's system until the platform confirms it; a case that lacks a paper or a claim, said as such and carried on once it is there; and a firm where a person creates the lawsuits, left alone. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: every court's system is unconfirmed in the table until the operator checks the court's acts, so a package waits for that, or for a lawyer to name the system on the lawsuit (the case says so); the plan is the rule's (the domicile), so another forum is a lawyer's choice, made before ONE creates the lawsuits or by changing them after; ONE asks for the piece of every planned lawsuit, which spends a run of the model on a case the firm may still decline; with the decision on the Tema 1.417 left to ONE, a case whose triage never ran or could not tell the risk is packaged (only a hold stops it); a petition approved before its lawsuit changed is packaged as it is, as when a person asks; a partner names a lawyer to sign with no word from that lawyer; the operator's release of a hold is seen at the cases' next look, within the setting's six hours; the deadline and the hearing read from a publication are the eighth part, and the filing itself comes later.
- **M3, sprint S10, eighth part (the deadline and the hearing read from a publication) is built.** The owner's decision of 2026-10-14 again: what a person triages as a deadline or a hearing, ONE reads and records by itself where the firm lets it. Two more decisions of the catalog (migration `0040_publication_reading`, pgTAP `0047_publication_reading`, 46 assertions with two firms), each `auto` by default and taken by its task, so the operator holds it in every firm through the task's autonomy too (at approval or at a sample: a reading has no answer for a person to check, and one that would wait enters the queue already withdrawn): `publication_deadline` (the AI reads the term, task `publicacao.prazo`: how many days, counted how, from what, for which act of the list; ONE proposes the deadline through `propose_deadline`, counted by the rule over the court's calendar and confirmed as the firm has `deadline_confirm`) and `publication_hearing` (the AI reads the hearing, task `publicacao.audiencia`: the day, the hour, the kind, how it is held, the place and the link; ONE writes it through `save_hearing`, and the firm's calendar and the client's notices follow). A publication triaged as either, of a lawsuit of a firm that lets ONE read it, owes a reading (`publication_readings`, made by a trigger on a change of the triage, the lawsuit or the cancelling, never on the same word said again; one triaged as something else since, cancelled or no longer of a lawsuit gives up the reading still due). The database takes the AI's answer from the run, never from the worker (`record_publication_reading`), only where the publication's own words say it and say nothing that could make it wrong. A term: the text is whole (one the gazette keeps cut short may say anything in the part not kept, `text_truncated`), the excerpt the AI copied is in the text (as the app reads a page for the AI: its marks, scripts, styles and comments aside, the marks that only dress words joining them, its entities read once, and the address the app writes beside a link's words left out, `reading_unmarked`, `reading_in_text`), its number of days is written right before "dia(s)" in the excerpt and the text sets no other, a list ("15 (quinze) e 30 (trinta) dias") included and the words of a number put together as Portuguese writes them ("cento e vinte"; `reading_number_values`, `reading_term_values`), nor a term in hours, weeks, months or years, however it is written ("48h", "72 hs", "em até 48 horas"), or named by its length ("decêndio", "quinquídio", "tríduo"; `reading_other_units`: "às 14 horas" is an hour), the term counts from the publication by the AI's word, the excerpt's and the words around each place the excerpt is in the text (`reading_other_start`: "a contar desta data", "da juntada", "do trânsito em julgado", "antes da audiência", and, right before it or at the start of the excerpt, "Nesta data", "em audiência" or what has to happen first ("Após o trânsito em julgado", "Uma vez juntado o laudo"), are another start; "contados a partir da intimação" and "após o que" are not), the text is no record of a hearing or a session and does not say the parties were told in the hearing itself (`reading_hearing_intimation`: "termo de audiência", "aberta a sessão", "presentes as partes", "saem as partes intimadas", a sentence given in a hearing; the term may have started there; "ficam as partes intimadas para...", outside such a record, is how a publication tells them), no word of the text is struck through, a term in calendar days by the AI's word or the text's is counted so ("decorrido" is no "corrido"), and the act the AI names is kept only when the text names it (`reading_act_in_text`). A hearing: the text is whole, the AI says the text sets a new one, and the text speaks of no hearing moved, adjourned, put back, made void, cancelled, suspended, brought forward or converted to another way of being held, nor of an erratum or a new hearing in the place of another (`reading_changes_hearing`: a cancelled flight or ticket is no cancelled hearing), nor of a session of judgment, the excerpt is in the text and names a hearing, it writes one day and one hour, the AI's, and the text has them (`reading_dates`, `reading_times`: "10/11" is a day and never "10:11", a case number is no day, "14 h 30" is no whole hour, a signature's time with its seconds is no hour), the text sets no other day to come, no day without its year (`reading_loose_days`: "24/11" may be a second hearing; the address of a link and a reference to a part of the case, "mov. 12.1", are none) and no other hour (a conciliation and then an instruction in one text are a person's, and an hour whose minutes are written in words is no hour an answer could be), an hour said in Brasília's time, or by a zone ("(Brasília)", "UTC-3"), of a court of another zone is a person's (`zone_named`), the hour is one a hearing is held at and the moment has not passed, the way it is held is the text's ("audiência de conciliação virtual" is by video, and a "processo virtual" says nothing of it), and a text that speaks of being there and of a distance without saying the hearing is hybrid, or a hearing in person whose excerpt writes a link, is a person's (`reading_mode`), the place is the text's, and the link is the text's, whole, and the only one the excerpt writes (`reading_links`; a hearing in person takes none). A hearing the agenda has at that very moment, held the same way (its mode and kind, and its place and link as the AI read them or, read none, as the text has them), is taken as the publication's (`already_in_agenda`); held another way, or beside another hearing of the lawsuit that day (even one cancelled or held) or still to come, it is a person's to weigh (`conflicting_hearing`). Anything else is left to a person, with why (`unread`), and what a person did first stands, settled before any model is asked. What is due that ONE will not read is settled before every claim and by the sweep even where no model can be asked (`settle_publication_readings`): a person did it first, the firm or the operator holds it, a hearing of a lawsuit not in court or of a court of more than one zone (`not_filed`, `zone_required`), work older than the setting's ten days, and what was tried, or given back by the provider, too many times, or waited longer than the setting's two hours for a model, which goes to a person as failed (a term may be running). The claim takes only the kinds a model can read here (`claim_publication_readings`), the rows in the order a person's own writes take them (the lawsuit, the publication, the reading), and a lawsuit that changed under the lock gives the claim back. ONE writes through the person's own functions for the case the backend names (`case_system_writer`), as ONE (`deadlines.proposed_auto`, `hearings.created_auto`, the timeline's actor `system`, the audit's `auto`), and only a new hearing, never a change of one. Nothing that owes a hearing is lost: a publication triaged as a hearing that no hearing came from, of a lawsuit in ONE or not, is listed in `/agenda` for the days a triaged publication is watched and counted on `/inicio` until its hearing is written or who writes hearings says it needs nothing more (it is in the agenda already, or the publication moved or cancelled one that is: `settle_publication_hearing`, audited; 0037, 0038); the watches of terms and hearings leave out what ONE is reading, for the setting's two hours at most, so a sweep that stopped hides nothing (`publication_owes_deadline`, `publication_owes_hearing`). The client is told nothing of a hearing a publication the court cancelled answers for (the one it came from, or the one ONE found it in the agenda for: `hearing_publication_cancelled`; its reminders, the word on the lawsuit, the next hearing on their link), and its card says so, until somebody says they saw the cancelling (0037, 0038). The job `one-publication-reading` (every five minutes and on `one/publication.read`, sent right after a person's triage) settles, then claims and reads one at a time, and what ONE recorded is followed at once (the deadline counted, the hearing written to the firm's calendar); a provider that fails twice in a row stops the run with every try given back. The app: each publication card says what ONE read or is reading, or why it left the publication to a person and what to do, and the list asks what ONE recorded of the ones it reads, more slowly as it waits and for about ten minutes, the newest answer winning, so a card never says ONE is reading after it read; a triage ONE will read no longer opens the form by itself (it opens as before where no model can read that kind here); a card whose triage changed says the deadline or the hearing that came from it stays; the form of a deadline says the publication has one already; "Proposto pelo ONE, lido da publicação" on a deadline, "Registrada pelo ONE, lida da publicação" on a hearing, and a hearing whose publication the court cancelled says so; the card of the publications that owe a hearing in `/agenda`, each with its form or "Já está resolvida", and ONE's reason in the card of `/prazos`; the tile on `/inicio`; the two decisions in `/settings/autonomia` and in the operator's holds. `lib/publications/reading.ts` holds what the model is handed, `reading-words.ts` the words the screens read (light: no reader of a court's text reaches the browser), `reading-work.ts` the backend's half. The worker has its own test against the embedded database, with the model told what to answer (`apps/web/src/lib/publications/reading.db.test.ts`, part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`): a deadline read, proposed, counted and confirmed by ONE; a hearing read and written, with its link as the page has it; a hearing moved, left to a person and watched until written; one cancelled, read by the firm's own people and settled; a model that answered badly; and a firm where a person proposes the deadlines, left alone. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a person still triages (the AI's triage of a deadline or a hearing stays in the shadow, 0035), so ONE reads only what a person said is a deadline or a hearing; the checks are of words: a term read from the right sentence for the wrong party is taken when its number is the text's only one, a condition before a term written in words the list does not have is not seen, and a hearing whose text hides a change in words no list foresaw is taken as new when the lawsuit has no other hearing to come; a lawsuit with a hearing still to come has every new one left to a person; a term in hours, months or years, a decêndio or a quinquídio, a day written without its year and an hour written with a dot are left to a person, and so are a text the DJEN keeps cut short, a text that sets two hearings or names two hours, and a term of a text that is the record of a hearing or says the parties were told in one; what a page hides is still read by the checks (which only makes them refuse more); the place of a hearing is taken only as the text writes it, word for word; a hearing ONE wrote stays when the court cancels its publication (the hearing and the publication say so, and a person cancels it); a firm that switches the reading on finds no reading of what was triaged before, and a hold of the operator or the firm gives up what was due then; the readings first ran against the real model in the rehearsal of 2026-10-27, once the model was handed each task's schema in the task's order (see the test court, below).
- **M3, sprint S10, ninth part (a document refused is asked for again) is built.** The second half of the owner's point of 2026-10-13 on what a client sends, now that ONE decides by default (the sixth part): ONE's check refuses a document the photo or the paper does not answer for, as before, and the client is asked for it again by themselves. A fifth kind of notice (migration `0038_client_notices`, edited in place; pgTAP `0041_client_notices`, 42 assertions with two firms), `document_refused`: a document of a client's kind the firm refused (ONE's check or a person; never a file the backend refused on its bytes, which the client's page says at once) is told on WhatsApp with the reason as it was written (in the plain words a message keeps: no dash, no exclamation), which paper (the day it was sent, and for a client of another case going at the firm, the trip) and the way back (the client's link, or a reply with the file), to who it is of, or who answers for a minor (`document_recipient`; one sent through a link, to its person while they are still of the case, and to nobody in place of one taken out of it; a paper of the whole trip the firm added, to who holds the case, an adult claimant or else who answers for a minor, one who agreed first), once per refusal (`client_notices.document_id`), the setting's minutes after it (a refusal a person undoes at once is never told), within the setting's seven days, in the hours of the day of the person's own state, and only while the case still takes papers on the link and the paper is still owed: no newer one of its kind and person sent after the refusal (a file refused on its bytes answers nothing; a newer one refused in its turn is asked for itself), and nothing else answering for its need as the client's page reads it (`case_document_owed`: another ticket or boarding pass of the case, another identity of the same claimant, another proof of address of the same person, which the firm did not refuse, one on its way included). The words a client agrees to be told in (`notice_optin_wording`) name this purpose with the others; under every rule of the other notices (the firm's own template, approved by the vendor and switched on, the client's agreement, the ceiling and the room it keeps for hearings, a retry as itself). The client's page reads the folder again every ten seconds, for five minutes, while a document sent in that time waits, so the check's answer and its reason show while the client is still there, and a new paper sent then answers the refusal before any message goes. The app: the kind in `/settings/connections` and on the case's card of notices (`notice-kind-document-refused`). The round trip of the notices sends one through the simulator (`apps/web/src/lib/notices/notices.db.test.ts`). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the message points to the client's link and does not carry it (a new link ends the one the client has: the link sent before is in the same conversation, or the client replies with the file, which a person attaches to the case); several papers refused at once are several messages; a paper the firm does not ask for (a receipt) is asked again unless a newer one of its kind came after the refusal, which may be another receipt; ONE refuses only through its check (with no model at hand, a document waits for a person).
- **M3, sprint S10, tenth part (a lawyer's signature in the cloud, kept by ONE, and the files of a Projudi package signed with it) is built.** The first piece of the filing by ONE that needs no court's authorization (the owner's decision of 2026-10-15), under the custody the owner decided on 2026-10-14. `packages/psc` is a lawyer's ICP-Brasil certificate in the cloud behind an interface: one client for every PSC (a provider the ITI accredited) as DOC-ICP-17.01 (version 3.0, IN ITI 20/2020, item 6.4; read on 2026-10-14) specifies its API (`authorize` with PKCE, `token`, `certificate-discovery`, `signature` over a SHA-256 hash answered RAW), configured by the PSC's base address and by how its own manual departs from the norm (BirdID's hashes in hexadecimal and token request in JSON); a refusal ends a lawyer's authorization only where the token endpoint says the holder said no; `certificateFacts` reads the holder's CPF only where ICP-Brasil writes it of a person (OID 2.16.76.1.3.1, at its positions, with its check digits), a company's CNPJ apart (2.16.76.1.3.3), strictly, and of a bundle the holder's certificate, never an AC of the chain, whatever the order; the scope the PSC says it granted is read, and an authorization that is not a session of signatures is kept by nobody; only words about the token spend a token; a fake with a test AC of its own that issues the certificate of the CPF the application hints, signs for real, and, for a preview, carries its codes and tokens sealed with a secret the instances share, the serial derived from the CPF; the contract (29 tests, against the fake and against the norm's client talking to a stand-in in the norm's shapes, once as the norm says and once as BirdID says). `@flyee/documents/cms` writes the attached `.p7s` the Projudi of TJGO takes around a signature made elsewhere (contentType, messageDigest, signingCertificateV2 over the very certificate, signingTime only when asked, in whole seconds and as GeneralizedTime from 2050; the document in one OCTET STRING; a signature handed without its leading zeros padded back, and one with a zero too many cut) and reads one back (the signer by issuer and serial, its ESS hash checked); OpenSSL's CAdES check accepts what it writes against the fake's AC; `detectMime` tells a `.p7s` by its bytes. Migration `0041_cloud_signatures` (pgTAP `0048_cloud_signatures`, 84 assertions with two firms; `0017_connections` edited in place: `begin_connection` refuses a PSC's provider, each seal of the vault has its own id): `psc_providers` (the PSCs as data, seeded from the ITI's list and the PSCs' manuals, none confirmed; `admin_save_psc_provider` asks the operator's second factor, and a PSC whose address moved or that is no longer offered ends every authorization given at it), `lawyer_cpfs` (`declare_lawyer_cpf`: the lawyer's own CPF, with the second factor; one account's alone once the PSC signed with a certificate of it the lawyer authorized and ONE checked the signature (`proven_at`, `prove_lawyer_cpf`), and until then held against nobody, so nobody blocks a lawyer by declaring their CPF first: the first proof takes it from every declaration nothing proved, on the record; a refusal is on the record too, and ten declarations or refusals a day are a person's most; changed only while nothing of theirs is in force or just started, and a start over an authorization whose time ran out ends it; read by them alone), `connections.person_id` (a PSC's connection is a lawyer's own and never passes to another person, firm or PSC; `begin_lawyer_signature`, the lawyer's own act with the second factor once their CPF is declared; in force only with its end at most 7 days away, while they are a partner or a lawyer of the firm, and for a certificate whose CPF is the declared one, held by its middle against the declared CPF whatever writes it), `lawyer_signature_uses` (every opening declared first, bound to what it says: the test of an authorization that just arrived signs one text once; the files of a filing package are its very files, of a lawsuit the lawyer signs, under the claim the backend holds of it and by the lawyer it chose; with the seal and the claim it opened; read by the lawyer, the partners and who manages the firm), `lawyer_signature_open` and `lawyer_signature_finish` (the backend's: the certificate authorized by its serial and the declared CPF handed with the sealed token; what the PSC says follows only the seal the use opened; what a use signed while the authorization ended is never handed over, `ended_meanwhile`; what only the lawyer puts right, no certificate, another certificate than the one authorized, one out of its validity or a PSC that moved, ends the authorization as a PSC that no longer signs does), `end_lawyer_signature` (the lawyer, a partner or who manages the firm, no second factor; the PSC's return gives up only its own start), and the vault's general read and its key rotation never reach a lawyer's authorization. Migration `0042_package_signatures` (pgTAP `0049_package_signatures`, 38 assertions with two firms): where the court's system takes each file already signed (`court_systems.signed_files`, seeded `p7s` for the Projudi of TJGO, unconfirmed like its row), a package ONE or a partner or a lawyer asked for owes its signature from the moment it is built (`package_signs_itself`: one somebody else of the team asked for is signed when a lawyer of the lawsuit asks), as the firm lets ONE (`sign_package`, `auto` by default; at `approval`, a lawyer of the lawsuit asks for it with their own authorization, `request_package_signature`, with the second factor, never a support visit); the sweep recomputes what is owed, a package waits while the lawyer it waits for has no authorization in force and is signed once they have, a claim that never came back spends its try (and fails nothing of a package no longer built), a record refused gives its claim back, and a package that no longer says what its lawsuit is, or whose lawsuit something keeps from being filed, is signed by nobody nor asked for; `claim_package_signatures`, `package_signature_to_sign` (the signer: who asked, with their own authorization and nobody else's, or else the lawyer of the lawsuit whose authorization lasts the longest; the request of somebody who no longer signs the lawsuit stands no more) and `record_package_signature` (only a use of that signer, of this package and claim, signed over its files in order, while the signer still signs the lawsuit, and one stored `.p7s` per file under the `filing` purpose, named by nothing else; told in the case's timeline as ONE's); a package given up forgets its `.p7s` with its parts. A qualified signature carries its holder's data and stands for their word: the `.p7s` leave ONE only in the hands of the lawyer whose certificate signed them (`filing_signed_file`, never a partner, the attendance or a support visit; every other way to a file refuses a `.p7s`, and `discard_file` keeps one a package names), and another lawyer of the lawsuit who files asks ONE to sign again with their own authorization, the first lawyer's files going. The app: the card "Assinatura em nuvem" in `/settings/team` (the lawyer's CPF, the authorization at their PSC for 1 hour to 7 days with its holder, the CPF's middle, its end and the certificate's validity, ending it after a confirmation, the team's authorizations for a partner or who manages the firm, and what was signed, each with its case; the second factor and a support visit said before any click; the PSC's answer said once, at the card; a read that fails is the card's alone), the PSC's return at `/api/connections/psc/callback` (`lib/psc/provider.ts` chooses each PSC with the platform's client, `PSC_<SLUG>_CLIENT_ID` and `PSC_<SLUG>_CLIENT_SECRET`, or the fake, `PSC_FAKE=1`, never in production; `lib/psc/sign.ts` is the one path that signs, with the certificate authorized by its serial and its fingerprint, never an AC's; `lib/psc/actions.ts` the writes), the job `one-package-signatures` (every five minutes and on `one/case.filing.signature`, sent when a package is built, a lawyer asks and an authorization arrives; each package claimed and signed in one step; `lib/cases/package-signature-work.ts`), and on the card "Protocolo" who signed and when, the `.p7s` of each file beside its PDF for the lawyer who signed them, the steps of a filing whose files go signed, what a signature waits for, why a try failed and what to do, the way to authorize for a lawyer of the lawsuit who has no authorization and "Assinar com a minha autorização" (or "de novo") for one who has, a petition ONE approved said as signed by whom, and the card read again by itself while ONE signs and after each request; where no PSC can be used here, or the package is to be built again or something keeps it from being filed, nothing of the cloud signature is offered and the steps say to sign each PDF with the court's signer before attaching the `.p7s`; asking over a colleague's signature or request is confirmed first; the way to authorize carries the case, and the team's card offers the way back to it; the state of each package's signature in `/protocolo`; the decision in `/settings/autonomia`, beside the bar's note on a lawyer's own acts. The workers have their own tests against the embedded database (`apps/web/src/lib/psc/signatures.db.test.ts`: a CPF declared, an authorization kept and tried, a package's files signed only as the package has them, a certificate of another CPF and a signature that does not hold never used, a certificate that changed and a PSC that no longer signs ending the authorization, a return that is not the lawyer's own, a lawyer who stops being one; `apps/web/src/lib/cases/package-signatures.db.test.ts`: a package built by the real worker, waiting, signed once the lawyer authorizes, each `.p7s` holding its very PDF and taken only by the lawyer who signed, a firm that keeps it to a person, and a package given up). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: nothing ran against a PSC yet (the platform registers as an application at each PSC with an ICP-Brasil SSL certificate of its domain, the owner's checklist), and whether a PSC's RAW carries the DigestInfo, and whether BirdID takes hexadecimal, is seen at its test environment (ONE checks every signature before using it, so another answer is found on the authorization's own test); whether the Projudi of TJGO takes a `.p7s` with SHA-256 and signingCertificateV2 (its own signer writes SHA-512 with signingTime) is seen at the test of the filing station; the CMS carries no chain and no ICP-Brasil policy; a CPF is a person's word until an authorization of it is kept, so a partner who declares a colleague's CPF and holds the colleague's factors at the PSC defeats the check (the declaration is audited, and a proven CPF serves one account); the operator changes a PSC only by its function (no console yet), and the court systems' `signed_files` only by a migration; the files are signed by one lawyer of the lawsuit, the one who asked or whose authorization lasts the longest, and only that lawyer takes them out of ONE (another lawyer of the lawsuit who files asks for the signature again with their own); who names the lawyers of a lawsuit is anybody of the firm who works on it (0022), so a lawyer's authorization signs the packages ONE or a partner or a lawyer asked for of every lawsuit the firm names them on, which they read in their uses and file themselves; each hash a use sends goes under an id of that use alone, and whether a PSC takes such ids is seen at its test environment; the filing itself, by the MNI or the court's screen, comes later, and binds what ONE signs tighter, since nobody downloads the files there.
- **The ONE Supabase development project has the baseline (2026-10-21).** The 43 migrations went in through `npm run db:migrate:remote`, and the whole pgTAP suite (1,397 assertions) passes against it (`ONE_ENV_FILE` unset, `node scripts/remote-db-gate.mjs --test-only`), so the rules hold with the real `auth` and `storage` too. What the hosted project has that the embedded database does not: Supabase's own event trigger `ensure_rls` (its function `rls_auto_enable`, which turns RLS on in every new table of `public`, as ONE's rules do anyway; an event trigger's function is called by no role, so the catalog test of 0012 leaves it out) and the default grant of every new table of `public` to `anon` and `authenticated` (the runner's own history, `schema_migrations`, is revoked from them by the gate on every apply). The project's direct host answers on IPv6 only, so `DATABASE_URL` is the session pooler's (`aws-0-us-east-1`: the project is in us-east-1, where the checklist planned São Paulo for staging and production). **From now on every change to the database is a new migration file** (0043 on): an applied migration is never edited again.
- **The first walk through the app against the development project (2026-10-21)**, the built app on this machine with Inngest's own dev server, the WhatsApp simulator, the stand-ins of the PSC and the calendar, and the Anthropic key: a firm founded by an owner with the second factor, every screen of the menu and the operator's consoles, a case opened and its client's link on a phone (her data, the CEP filled by its lookup, an identity, a proof of address and a boarding pass, the account of what happened, "terminei"), each paper checked by the real model (Sonnet 5.5, three to seven seconds) and accepted by ONE, the leg read from the boarding pass into the case, a conversation through the simulator matched to its case by the phone and the link sent in it, the executor check (Opus 5.5) decided in the queue with the second factor, an invitation accepted, a member made administrator while working, and ONE carrying the case: the lawsuit created by itself, its signer named once a partner chose who signs, and the first text of the piece written by Opus 5.5 (24 seconds, 3,323 tokens in and 2,312 out) from the client's own account, citing nothing (the library was empty, and the model said so) and leaving two `[COMPLETAR: ...]`, so it waits for a lawyer. What it found was fixed at once: a screen left blank for good after signing in and after founding a firm (the layout's redirect on a request of the client router is kept by the router and thrown again on every page under it: a session change now loads the page anew, `enterAfterSessionChange`, and the layout leaves its sends inside the logged area to the shell on such a request, `shellHold`, told by the browser's `Sec-Fetch-Mode`, since Next.js keeps its own headers from the pages and from the middleware); the second factor skipped by any link (the shell holds who owes it on every page); the menu's items out of reach of the keyboard (links now); "Sair" during a support visit signing the visited person out of every device (it ends the visit; every sign-out is of this browser only); controls with no name; a decided card still clickable in the queue. Found and left for the owner: the model accepted an identity and a bill marked "documento de teste, sem validade" (it noted the mark, and the task's instructions do not ask it to refuse such a paper: a new version of them is published in `/admin/ai`); the price table of the models is empty, so no run has a cost yet (the operator fills it in `/admin/ai`); the one leg of a case read from a ticket is not marked as the affected one, which the rules read (the AI's text of the piece pointed it out). The accounts and firms of the walk (`@one-teste.invalid`) stayed in the development project until 2026-11-03, when the owner had every account, firm and agency there removed with what hung on them (and the bucket of backups), the platform's own tables kept, so the next tests start from a clean base with the same e-mails; no operator was left, and one is made again on the account the owner creates.
- **The adjustments before the tests (the plan of 2026-10-22, validated by the owner the same day): phase 1 is built.** The plan is the Claude Doc "ONE · Plano dos ajustes antes dos testes" (https://claude.ai/artifact/NNaEnK1A6HVP6AA8Koqucn): the conversation with the passenger starts with ONE when a flight it follows has a problem, the passenger only decides, and the firm is called only when ONE cannot go on; its phases are 1 (prices, a firm that starts ready, what waits for somebody), 2 (the platform's default models and sources, the link sent by ONE), 3 (the test chat with four ready trips, ONE in the conversation, the case opened at the passenger's yes) and 4 (the real flight source and the release to real passengers, after the legal clearance on soliciting clients). **What each model costs** (migration `0043_ai_prices`, pgTAP `0050_ai_prices`, 24 assertions with two firms): the price book gains the long-prompt tier (Claude Haiku 5.5 over 100,000 input tokens pays 0.50 and 2.50 for the whole request), rates that take effect on a day (Gemini 3.6 Flash: 0.75 and 3.75 until 31 December 2026, 1.50 and 7.50 from 1 January 2027, from Google's page as of 2026-10-19) and an audio rate; the audio a model hears is counted at last (`audio_tokens_in` on the run and on the ledger: the run store dropped it), a rate that is not a number prices nothing and never stops a run from closing, pricing again reaches the run as well as the ledger, and the operator's price screen edits the tier, the dated changes, the audio rate and the source while keeping whatever else the book holds; seeded with the four models the tasks pin (Anthropic's prices checked against its page on 2026-10-22), and every run on record priced again. **A firm that starts ready** (migration `0044_firm_setup`, pgTAP `0051_firm_setup`, 21 assertions with two firms): who manages a firm answers three questions once, on the home itself while they were never answered, and again at `/configurar`: their registration at the bar with "Eu assino o que o ONE prepara" already on (`set_firm_signer`, for a partner, with the second factor just set up), whether the firm works alone (`organizations.works_alone`, which takes the team out of the list: nothing in ONE needs a second person) and the channel to the clients; "Fazer depois" sets them aside for good (`setup_answered_at`, `answer_firm_setup`); the list of the home reads the registration with a signer, the team only where there is one and the channel (it also listed the first case until 2026-11-03, when the owner had it out: the first case is ONE's to receive, never the firm's step), and the CNPJ is asked when the firm subscribes. The questions are shown in place, never by a redirect: the shell wraps every page of the logged area in a Suspense boundary, so a page's `redirect()` is the client router's, and in the production build that path broke the screen (React error #310); `/agenda`, `/prazos` and `/publicacoes` still send a page past the end of their list back that way. **What waits for somebody, on every screen**: the center "Pendências" in the header (`components/layout/pending/pending-center.tsx`; `lib/firm/pending.ts`, pure and tested; `lib/firm/pending-actions.ts`), a number in red when something passed its time, and a drawer in four groups: Atrasado (each deadline past its day with its case, hearings with no word on them, papers nobody decided for longer than the setting's hours, cases ONE waits on for longer than the setting's days, messages that did not go out, complaints past their day, a calendar or a reading that stopped), Hoje e próximos dias, O ONE precisa de você (each case ONE waits on, with what it waits for and since when, and the decisions only a person takes) and Para conferir, left out of the number; each line one click from where it is done, read again at every change of screen and every minute while the tab is in front of the person, and what is new and urgent told at once in a notice. It reads `firm_today` and `firm_pending` (0044: `case_advances.waiting_since`, dated by a trigger; the setting `pending`), and each person sees what the home shows them (`todayViewerOf`). Walked in the production build against the development project on 2026-10-22: a new account founded, its second factor, the three questions, the home with its list, the center empty and then full (the firm of the first walk), at 1440 and at 390, with nothing unnamed and no sideways scroll.
- **The adjustments before the tests, phase 2 (what ONE gives every firm, and the link sent by ONE) is built.** **The platform's models and sources** (migration `0045_platform_library`, pgTAP `0052_platform_library`, 20 assertions with two firms): a source or a model of pieces with no firm is the platform's, read by every firm and written by the operator alone in `/admin/conteudo` (`lib/admin/platform-content.ts`, `admin/conteudo/actions.ts`; each change on the record, never from a support visit). Seeded with 22 sources, each quoted as its official page writes it (the consumer code, the Civil Code, the Constitution, the small-claims law, the CPC, the Montreal Convention, ANAC's Resolution 400 and the STF's themes 210 and 1.240; read on 2026-10-22), which answer the gate of any firm's piece as its own sources do (`legal_source_covers`; where a firm has its own source of a key, the AI is handed the firm's), and two models, the initial of the small-claims court and the one of the civil court, citing only those sources and marked provisional until the curator reviews them: ONE writes from the platform's model of a kind only for a firm that has no model of that kind (`draft_template_for`: a firm with models of its own keeps its rule, and adding a default of the platform wakes the cases that waited for one). The library shows each with its marks ("Modelo do ONE", "Versão provisória", "Em uso pelo ONE", "conferida pelo ONE"; `lib/library/facts.ts`), and a firm copies a model of the platform to make its own (`biblioteca/library-view.tsx`). **The client's link, sent by ONE** (migration `0046_client_link_by_one`, pgTAP `0053_client_link_by_one`, 23 assertions with two firms; the decision `send_client_link`, ONE's by default, first in `/settings/autonomia`): while a case is gathered (`intake`, `documents`, `signature`), each adult of it who never had a link of that case is sent one by ONE in the conversation they last wrote in to the firm's WhatsApp, inside the 24 hours, never to who asked to stop, never through a connection that failed and never twice (a link a person ended stays ended).
