The worker of 0039 does it before anything else of the case (`advance_client_links`, `advance_send_client_link`, `lib/cases/advance-work.ts`: the app mints the token, and the link and its message, in the words a person's link goes with, are written under the case's own lock; the message is nobody's of the firm, and what the client wrote last still waits for the firm's answer). `client_link_reach` is the one reading of who is owed a link and why ONE cannot send it (`by_person`, `no_phone`, `no_number`, `no_conversation`, `opted_out`, `window_closed`): the case's card "Link do cliente" says it for each adult with what to do (`case_links_owed`, with `sending` and `late`), and the center of what waits lists it in "O ONE precisa de você" (`firm_pending.links_owed`; a link ONE could send and did not after the setting's 30 minutes is late). A client who writes wakes the case in the database (`wa_conversations_wake_links`) and the inbox asks for it at once (`wa_contact_link_cases`, `lib/whatsapp/inbox.ts`), so the link goes in seconds, also with no job runner. The round trip `apps/web/src/lib/cases/client-link.db.test.ts` (part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`) opens a case for a client who never wrote, waits saying why, has the client write through the simulator and sends the link once, whose token opens the client's page. Both migrations are applied to the development project, where `0052` and `0053` pass with the rest of the files after `0045`; the whole suite there needs an empty database (14 older files collide with the firms and registrations the walks and the owner's account left), so the remote gate now tests some files (`--only=0052,0053`) or every file to the end (`--keep-going`). Walked in the production build against the development project on 2026-10-22: the operator's console at 1440 and 390, in light and dark; a firm's library with the platform's models and sources and a model copied; a case opened for a client who never wrote, its card and the center saying why ONE waits, the client writing through the simulator, the link sent in seconds and opening the client's page on a phone, the center then empty of it; nothing unnamed and no sideways scroll (the first field of a dialog had its label cut and a row's text was squeezed beside its buttons on a phone, both fixed). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: outside the 24 hours ONE sends nothing (a model the vendor approves, which waits for the platform's app at Meta, would carry it), so the firm copies that link or waits for the client to write; the platform's models and sources are the operator's to keep (a firm's copy is the firm's, and does not follow a later change of the platform's); a client with no WhatsApp has the firm send the link.

a third kind of number, `test`, honoured in production and sending nothing out of ONE, `lib/whatsapp/test-provider.ts`; a firm's first real number ends it), whose page `/t/[token]` (no account, no index, no referrer; `app/t/[token]`, `lib/test-chat/*`) stands where the passenger's phone stands: four trips ready (Mariana alone, a cancellation; Rafael with his daughter, six hours late; Lúcia, a connection lost; Pedro, an international flight; since 2026-11-03 it brings none, 0087, below), the tools to make each problem happen and to start a trip over (which gives its conversation back to ONE, 0049), the quick replies SIM, NÃO and SAIR, and what the passenger types, sent through the same inbox a real delivery goes through. **ONE's first word on a flight** (`@flyee/rules/flight-problem`, `flight-problem-v1`): a trip ONE follows (`tracked_trips`, registered with the passenger's agreement in its own words: no list of passengers exists) whose leg is cancelled or changed, or whose last leg lands four hours late or more (the setting), is told once per fact (`flight_alerts`), as the firm's template of the notice kind `flight_problem` (what happened, what the airline owes by ANAC's Resolution 400, and the choice), never to who asked to stop (`lib/flight-alerts/*`; the job `one-flight-alerts`, every two minutes and on `one/flight.alerts`). **ONE in the conversation** (migration `0048_one_in_conversation`, pgTAP `0055_one_in_conversation`, 23 assertions with two firms; the decisions `reply_client` and `open_case_from_chat`, ONE's by default, first in `/settings/autonomia`): the passenger's SIM opens the case with the flight, the problem and who travelled along (a child with who answers for them), and the client's link goes in the same conversation with the next steps numbered; a NÃO ends it kindly; a SAIR stops every message; anything else is read by a run of the task `atendimento.conversa` (Sonnet 5.5; the conversation with every identifier taken out, `lib/conversations/agent.ts`; the second version of its instructions, 0049, answers the course of a case itself), whose answer goes as ONE's, cleaned to the firm's rules of copy, or, where the firm keeps it to a person, waits in the conversation and in `/aprovacoes` for "Enviar" (`components/conversations/waiting-reply.tsx`, `aprovacoes/components/conversation-reply-card.tsx`: one waits per conversation, it leaves when the conversation moves on, and one approved goes once, said as approved by who sent it). When ONE cannot go on (a lawyer, an opinion or a person asked for, a complaint, an emergency, a fact of the case the data do not hold, too many messages in an hour) it says a person continues and calls one: the conversation waits in the center of what waits with why (late after the setting's 30 minutes). Each message says who wrote it (Cliente, ONE, ONE com IA, ONE com IA aprovada por a person, or the person; `wa_messages.by_one`, decided when it is written), what ONE did is told between the messages (the case opened, the link sent, a person called, taken over, given back), and a person takes a conversation over with a click or by writing in it, here or on the firm's phone, and gives it back with "Devolver ao ONE" (`lib/conversations/agent-work.ts`; the jobs `one-conversation-turn`, gathered per conversation for five seconds, and `one-conversation-turns` every two minutes; without a job runner the turn is taken after the response). The round trips `apps/web/src/lib/test-chat/test-chat.db.test.ts` and `apps/web/src/lib/conversations/agent.db.test.ts` (part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`) run the journey with the model told what to answer. Walked in the production build against the development project on 2026-10-22, with the real model: the test chat on a phone, a cancellation told, the SIM opening the case with its link and steps in seconds, a question answered by ONE (4 to 6 seconds, about US$ 0.006 a turn), a passenger who asked for a lawyer told that a person continues and found in the center, the conversation given back, and a firm that keeps the answers to a person sending ONE's answer, corrected, from the conversation; nothing unnamed and no sideways scroll at 1440 and 390. What the walk found was fixed at once: the first instructions called a person for a question of the course of the case, and a test trip started over left its conversation with the person called. **The files the passenger sends** (migration `0050_conversation_files`, pgTAP `0056_conversation_files`, 14 assertions with two firms): in a conversation ONE answers, a photo, a PDF or an audio joins the passenger's case being gathered in ONE's turn, which waits, the setting's three minutes at most, for a file still on its way: a photo or a PDF is read by the task `atendimento.arquivo.tipo` (Haiku 5.5: what paper it is and the name written on it, deciding nothing), joins as that paper of the person the papers a case needs say (an identity, of the claimant whose name it carries; a proof of address, of the passenger; a paper of the trip, of nobody in particular) and is checked at once by the check of 0024, as a paper sent through the link is; an audio joins as the passenger's account, to be transcribed and put in order; ONE says in one message what became of each (accepted, refused with the check's reason, waiting for the firm, no case being gathered, not received), and the conversation and the case's timeline tell it (`lib/conversations/agent-work.ts`). The test chat sends a photo, a PDF or an audio as the passenger (`startTestMedia`, `sendTestMedia`: the file waits where the vendor would keep it, `lib/test-chat/staging.ts`, and the media worker brings it in and forgets it there), and a delivery with a file has it fetched at once, with ONE's turn after it, also with no job runner (`queueMediaFetch`). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the trips are only the test chat's until the real source is connected (phase 4, FlightAware); outside the 24 hours ONE writes nothing in a conversation (only the alert, a template, opens it); a file sent before the passenger's yes, or while a person holds the conversation, stays in the conversation for a person; an audio is always taken as the passenger's account of what happened, even when it asks a question; a passenger who answers an alert while a person holds the conversation is answered by that person; the model's answers are measured only by the firm's "Enviar" where it keeps them to a person.
- **The adjustments before the tests, phase 4 (the flights ONE follows read at FlightAware, and the release to real passengers) is built** (migration `0051_flight_watch`, pgTAP `0057_flight_watch`, 15 assertions with two firms). A trip ONE follows (`tracked_trips.watch` `flightaware`; the test chat's four ready trips are `simulated`, made to happen by its tools) is read at FlightAware with the platform's key from the setting's six hours before its first departure to twelve after its last scheduled arrival, every twenty minutes, within the platform's daily reads (400 legs, each one billed), claimed under a number (`claim_trip_watch`) by the job `one-flight-watch` (every ten minutes; nothing is read where the key is not). What AeroAPI says of each leg is kept on it (`record_trip_watch`: cancelled, changed, landed, or late with when it is now expected, from the status's new `estimatedArrival`, AeroAPI's `estimated_in`, brought to the destination's local time by its difference to the scheduled arrival, `lib/flight-alerts/source.ts`), and the rule of 0047 decides whether to tell (`lib/flight-alerts/watch.ts`); a refused key stops the run and FlightAware out of reach gives the trip back, spending nothing of it. ONE's first word to a real passenger (a trip that is not a test) waits for the platform's release: the operator gives it in `/admin/ai` (`flight-release-card.tsx`, with the reads of the day against the ceiling) with the second factor and the note of the legal opinion on soliciting clients, and may take it back; until then such an alert is kept and skipped as `not_released`. The test chat has a fifth tab where the tester names a real flight of yesterday to the day after tomorrow (`followRealFlight`, `test_chat_follow_flight`), read at FlightAware as a real passenger's would be, ONE writing in the test chat when it is cancelled, changed or four hours late; no tool makes it happen nor moves its day. Since 2026-11-03 no screen offers it (0087, below). The round trip `apps/web/src/lib/flight-alerts/watch.db.test.ts` (part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`) follows a real flight in the test chat with FlightAware's fake and has ONE tell its delay there, keeps a real passenger's cancellation untold, and says a flight its source does not know. Applied to the development project on 2026-10-22, where `0057` and `0054` pass, and walked there in the production build: the operator's card, released with a note and blocked again, and the test chat's fifth tab at 390 and 1440, which says where the key is not and, drawn with a placeholder key, refuses a flight that is not one before FlightAware is asked, keeping what was typed; nothing unnamed and no sideways scroll. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: nothing ran against AeroAPI yet (its key waits for FlightAware's answer on the plan ONE's use needs); there is no way yet for a real passenger to register a trip, nor for a firm to register one for a client (both wait for the legal opinion and the words of the agreement, which are the owner's: the database takes such a trip with the agreement as it was given); the window reads local times as Brasília's, wide enough for every zone of the country; the daily ceiling counts legs read, whatever AeroAPI answers.
- **What follows the client's "Terminei", and what waits for the firm, is built (2026-10-26).** The test of that day found that after the client said they finished nothing happened on either side. Migrations `0052_case_carry_on`, `0053_firm_attention`, `0054_signature_papers` and `0055_client_progress` (pgTAP `0058` to `0061`, each with two firms; applied to the development project on 2026-10-26, where they pass). **The claim by default**: a firm says the claim it asks for each kind of problem (`firm_claim_defaults`, `set_firm_claim_default`, a partner with the second factor, in `/settings/autonomia#valor-da-causa-pedido`, named the Valor da Causa Pedido since 0064), and ONE writes it for a claimant with none when it creates the lawsuits (`advance_claim_defaults`, told in the timeline as ONE's); a claim somebody wrote is never changed. **A signer of another state**: a lawyer registered at the bar in another state signs ONE's lawsuits there within the five causes a year of art. 10, § 2º, of the Estatuto da Advocacia (`occasional_causes_per_year`, `lawyer_state_causes`, `firm_signers_in`), each told in the timeline (`signer_occasional`); past five the case waits for a signer (`no_signer`). **The papers the client signs** (`send_signature_papers`, ONE's by default): a model of the library says what it writes (`draft_templates.document_kind`: the piece of a lawsuit, a power of attorney or a fee contract; the platform proposes a power of attorney, provisional, and never a contract, whose fees are each firm's); once an adult of a case says on their link that they finished, ONE fills the firm's model (else the platform's power of attorney) with that person's facts (`case_paper_filled`: who grants it and each minor they answer for, the firm's signers with their registrations, the company, what happened, the place and the day), makes it a PDF of the firm's own, keeps it as an accepted document of the case and sends it to be signed through the firm's ZapSign, else by gov.br on the link (`advance_paper_to_issue`, `advance_record_paper`, `case_papers`, never twice); a paper with a fact not on record waits and the case says which (`paper_incomplete`); a signed paper is its signer's document, so it goes with their filing package; and a power of attorney ONE sent that nobody confirmed signed keeps the lawsuits of who grants it from being filed (`filing_blocker`, `power_of_attorney_unsigned`). `/biblioteca` and `/admin/conteudo` write and show each kind of model. **What the passenger hears**: on their link, what comes next, and the papers to sign as soon as ONE sends them (the page reads again for a while); in the conversation ONE holds, inside their 24 hours and where the firm lets ONE answer its clients, ONE's word that it all arrived with the next steps, the power of attorney first (`advance_received_people`, `advance_say_received`, recorded as the notice `case_received`); where ONE could not say it, the firm's template of that kind after the setting's minutes; and, for a lawsuit recorded filed within the setting's days of the day it was filed (`proceedings.filed_marked_at`; never an old lawsuit a spreadsheet brings), the template `lawsuit_filed` with its number and where it runs. The test chat turns every kind of notice on, and the words a client agrees to name the two new ones. **What waits for the firm, on the home**: the card "O escritório precisa agir" (`components/product/attention-list.tsx`, over `firm_pending`) lists in red what passed its time and in the attention tone what the firm must do for a case to go on, each with its tag and one click from where it is done (the claim, the signer, the model, a paper with a fact missing, a signature by gov.br to confirm, `firm_pending.signatures_to_confirm`, a package's signature that failed), and the badge of the center of what waits turns orange while ONE waits on the firm; what waits on the client (a power of attorney not signed) reaches the firm only after the setting's days. The round trip of the client's link (`apps/web/src/lib/cases/client-link.db.test.ts`) has the client finish, the power of attorney issued as a real PDF and ONE's word said once. Walked in the production build against the development project on 2026-10-26: the home's card, the claims by default, the library's power of attorney, and on the test chat the yes, the link's "Avisar que terminei", the power of attorney on the link 12 seconds later and ONE's word in the conversation, at 1440 and 390, in light and dark. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the companies are seeded with no legal name or CNPJ, so a power of attorney names the company by the name it flies under until the operator records them (a piece's `[[RE]]` needs them too); the contract goes only from the firm's own model; a power of attorney signed by gov.br is confirmed by a person; a paper whose facts were missing when the client finished goes later with no word of ONE in the conversation (the link shows it); the word that the lawsuit was filed goes only as the firm's template, to a client who agreed to the notices.
- **The firm's setup does every step in place (2026-10-26).** The owner asked that the setup cover what a firm needs for ONE to work and that no step send the person to another screen they would not come back from (a partner with no second factor could not leave its first step). `/configurar`, and the home while it was never answered, has five steps: who answers at the bar and whether they sign what ONE prepares; the claim the firm asks by default for each kind of problem; whether it works alone, with its invitations; what a client signs (the power of attorney ONE sends, the firm's own model or its fee contract written in the library's panel over the step, `biblioteca/template-panel.tsx`, and the firm's ZapSign); and the channel (the test chat turned on, the number connected, the notices of a real number). The second factor the database asks for (who signs, the claims, an invitation, a connection) is set up or confirmed inside the step (`components/product/second-factor-prompt.tsx`, sharing `lib/security/second-factor.ts` with the security card, which now takes off a setup left half way before starting another), the cards of the connections render in the step (`embedded`) over the screen's own read (`lib/whatsapp/connections-read.ts`), and a step that cannot be done now says why and goes on. Migration `0056_setup_claims` (pgTAP `0062`, 7 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project on 2026-10-26) adds `claim_defaults` to `firm_setup_facts`, and the list on the home leads each missing item to its own step (`/configurar?passo=oab|valores|equipe|papeis|canal`, `lib/firm/setup-steps.ts`). Walked in the production build against the development project: a partner with no factor through the five steps, the factor set up in the first, the contract written in the dialog, the test chat turned on, and each step opened by its address, at 1440 and at 390 in the dark. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the firm's calendar in Google and a lawyer's signature in the cloud stay out of the setup (each leaves for the provider's own consent and comes back to the connections and the team screens); a firm with no partner cannot say who signs or the claims, and its list keeps them (since 0078, who manages such a firm says them); an account that has a second factor is still asked for it at every sign-in, whatever the setting `security` says. Since 2026-11-03 the second factor is the setup's first step (`seguranca`): it was asked inside six of them, and a fresh account met it locking the signer's switch of the first; the setup opens on it whatever `?passo=` asked while the session has none, does not go past it until it is confirmed, and then goes on to the step it was going to (`setupOpening`). The same day the module's steps (the Valor da Causa Pedido, the partner agency) left for the module's own setup (below): the core has five. The owner's rule: whatever a later step cannot be done without is settled in a step before it (`apps/web/CLAUDE.md`).
- **The rehearsal of the end-to-end script (2026-10-26)**, from a firm's sign-up to its lawsuit filed, in the production build against the development project, found three things, fixed at once (migrations `0057` to `0060`, pgTAP `0063` to `0065`, each with two firms; applied to the development project, where they pass). A client whose case moved on before they said they finished (ONE had all it needed and created the lawsuits, which closes the link and takes its "Terminei" away) was never sent the power of attorney nor told anything: such a case now owes its papers and ONE's word too (`0057_papers_as_case_moves_on`), which the worker sends in the round that creates the lawsuits (`lib/cases/advance-work.ts`), telling that client that their case has all it needs (`caseReceived` with `finished`). The airlines had no legal entity, so a piece's `[[RE]]` stayed a `[COMPLETAR]` and no piece could be approved: the four Brazilian operators have theirs (`0058_airline_legal_entities`, read in the Receita Federal's open data through BrasilAPI: TAM Linhas Aéreas S.A. for LATAM, GOL, Azul and Passaredo), under LATAM's "LA" only (`0059_airline_one_code`: a party that names two airlines names none in an import). And a package carried the power of attorney as ONE issued it, unsigned, leaving out the copy the client signed: a paper that came back signed now goes as its signed document (`0060_signed_paper_in_package`). The script, written for the owner to present the test, is the Claude Doc "ONE · Roteiro do teste de ponta a ponta" (https://claude.ai/artifact/DiabVSj3SzPNNqoZWM92Hw).
- **The test court, where a test case goes on after its filing, is built (2026-10-26, completed 2026-10-27).** The owner asked to run the test to the real end of a lawsuit with no wait on an outside platform: what follows a filing comes from the court and from the DJEN, and no court has a case the test chat opened. Migration `0061_test_court` (pgTAP `0066`, 14 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project): a case a trip of the test chat opened is a test case for good (`cases.is_test`, marked by a trigger on `tracked_trips` and backfilled from the conversations of test numbers, since a trip started over lets go of its case), and its lawsuits are the test court's (`proceeding_tracking.source` `teste`): never read from a real source, whatever number they were given. By the test chat's own link, `/t/[token]/tribunal` (`app/t/[token]/tribunal`, `lib/test-court/*`; reached from the test chat's "Abrir o tribunal de teste") stands where the court and the DJEN stand: it receives a filing package of a test case and hands the number its lawsuit gets (`test_court_number`: of 2099, of the lawsuit's court, with its check digits) and a receipt that says on its face no court received it, for the lawyer to attach in ONE; then it moves each test lawsuit as a lawsuit of the small-claims court goes (`test_court_act`: distributed and the airline summoned; a conciliation hearing set about ten minutes ahead, in the hours of a hearing, and held only from its moment (0062: `hearing_later`, and the page opens that step at its time); the defence to answer in 15 days; the judgment; made final; the money released; archived), each movement kept as a reading of the court keeps it and each publication as a capture of the DJEN keeps it, addressed to the lawsuit's lawyers. From there everything is ONE's own: the course shows on the case and on the client's link, the firm triages each publication, ONE reads the hearing and the deadline, and the client reads the hearing on their link and is warned against the scam. The lawsuit's course card says "Tribunal de teste" and offers no reading of a court. A case follows its lawsuits into court (migration `0062_case_to_court`, pgTAP `0067`, 7 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project): once a planned lawsuit leaves the plan (a receipt attached, or a person saying it was filed) and none of the case's lawsuits is still planned while one was filed, a case still being prepared is at the stage "Ajuizado", told in its timeline as ONE's; a case written with its lawsuits in one transaction (an import) is left to what wrote it, and the cases already in court went there. **The rehearsal of 2026-10-27 found why ONE never read a hearing or a deadline with the real model** (commit `52905aa`): a task's output schema is kept as jsonb, which orders its keys shortest first, and Anthropic's structured output writes the properties in the schema's order, so the model wrote a hearing's day before it said whether there was a hearing; and the deadline's schema carried a number's limits, which the API refuses. `@flyee/ai/anthropic-schema` hands the model each object's properties in the order its `required` names them and leaves out the limits the API refuses (said in the description instead), and `@flyee/ai/schema-order` gives OpenAI's strict schema the same order; with it ONE read and recorded a hearing and a deadline with the real model on the development project. The rehearsal also found the client's page telling a client whose lawsuit was in court that the firm was still preparing the case; it now says the lawsuit went to court (`client-locked-court`). Known limits: the test court acts only on a filed lawsuit of a test case; a hearing set after 19:45 or before 6:45 goes to nine in the morning of the next weekday, and its outcome is said from its start; a hearing minutes away has no reminder (the reminder of the day goes up to an hour before a hearing); the client's notices go only in the hours the setting gives (8 to 20), and the warning against the scam goes 15 to 25 minutes after the payment's triage, only while the case is open.
- **The Valor da Causa Pedido is the firm's to give or not (2026-10-27).** The owner found the claim confusing as the setup and the cases named it ("o valor do pedido", "quanto o escritório pede", "valor pedido"), and decided that every screen calls it "Valor da Causa Pedido" and that the firm chooses whether to give it: where it gives none, the lawsuit goes with no value defined and the judge defines it. Migration `0064_claim_value_optional` (pgTAP `0069`, 7 assertions with two firms; `0032` and `0066` changed with it; applied to the development project): a claimant with no value asks what the court arbitrates, which the piece says beside their name (`[[PEDIDO_POR_PARTE]]`: "o valor que este Juízo arbitrar"), and a lawsuit none of whose claimants asks a value gives the cause the value the court arbitrates (`[[VALOR_DA_CAUSA]]`: "o valor que este Juízo arbitrar" where the text says "valor de" it, so the models' "Dá-se à causa o valor de [[VALOR_DA_CAUSA]]." reads "Dá-se à causa o valor que este Juízo arbitrar.", and "a ser arbitrado por este Juízo" anywhere else), so nothing of it is left to fill in and the piece can be approved; a lawsuit with values is given their sum, as before; ONE no longer waits for a value (`lawsuitsWaiting` never answers `claims_missing`, whose words stay for a record written before, and the cases set aside for one were woken); and the test court arbitrates the indemnity of a petition that asked none. The firm's value for a kind of problem (0052) is unchanged, and is now written for a claimant with none also when a person creates the lawsuits on the case (`createPlannedProceedings`), so both ways ask the same. The setup's step and the card of `/settings/autonomia#valor-da-causa-pedido` are one form (`settings/autonomia/claim-value-choice.tsx`, the pure rules in `lib/firm/autonomy.ts`): "Definir um valor por tipo de problema", a kind left blank going with no value, or "Não definir valor", which takes every value off, saved at once; the case's card says what a blank value asks (the firm's value for the case's kind until the lawsuits exist, else none, for the judge) and no longer says a value is missing; the home's list no longer asks for it (a firm that gives none lacks nothing). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the CPC asks every petition for a value of the cause (arts. 291 and 319, V) and leaves the judge to correct it (art. 292, § 3º), so a petition with none may be ordered amended (art. 321), while the small-claims court allows a generic claim when the extent of the obligation cannot be determined at once (Lei 9.099/1995, art. 14, § 2º); the court systems ask the value of the cause in their own filing form, which the lawyer fills there; the words ONE writes in the piece are the platform's, for the curator to review; and in a firm that gives a value for a kind of problem, a claimant left blank takes it when the lawsuits are created (to go with none, the value is taken off the case once the lawsuits exist).
- **A piece is approved from its case's card without opening it (2026-10-27).** The owner asked that the card "Peças" offer to approve a piece with no need to review it. "Aprovar sem revisar", beside "Revisar o texto" (a partner or a lawyer, once what the latest version cites was read and while the AI rewrites nothing), says in a confirmation what the approval does, and `approveCaseDraftAsIs` (`lib/cases/draft-actions.ts`) has the database write the text as its document will say it (`preview_case_draft`) and approves that very text by its version and mark (`approve_case_draft`, with the second factor): the gate is the same as an approval from the preview, so a citation outside the library, something left to fill in or a character the PDF cannot write is said on the card as what is missing, and a version that came meanwhile is never approved unseen (`approvalOf` in `lib/cases/drafts.ts`, `version_changed`). No migration.
- **What a client's documents say of them enters their record, and the client's link starts with the documents (2026-10-27).** The owner asked that the data a client fills on their link be taken from the documents they send, so the documents come first. The AI's check of a document (0024) reads, in the same answer, what an identity says of its person (the full name, the CPF, the birth date, the nationality) and the address a proof of address is for, never the issuing company's (`dados_lidos`; the second and third versions of the instructions of `caso.documento.conferencia`, migrations `0063_data_from_documents` and `0065_document_data_case`, pgTAP `0068`, 18 assertions with two firms; both applied to the development project). Once the document is accepted (by ONE, or by a person who approves its check), the database writes what it says where the person's record has nothing (`apply_document_data`, through `apply_person_fields`): only of a document that is the person's by its name, while the case is still gathered; a field somebody wrote is never changed; an identity completes the name on record once, when every word of it is in the document's, in order (`completed_name`: "Mariana Teste" becomes "Mariana Teste de Oliveira", the spelling with accents kept of each word both have), and never once a paper was issued to that person to sign; the address is taken whole, and only when what the record has of an address agrees with it; a CPF counts against what the link that sent it may write, and one the firm has for somebody else is never written; a minor's record takes no nationality; what a document gives in capitals is written as a name (`name_case`). Each field written is marked as read from its document (`people.read_fields`: the field, the document and its kind, never a value) until a person or the client writes it (`people_forget_read_fields`), and the timeline tells it (`person_read`, by the names of the fields). The client's link (`/c/[token]`) puts the documents first, after the texts to accept and the word on the civil court, and the agreement to the notices after the account of what happened; "O que ainda falta" lists the documents first, on the case too (`missingItems`); the person's card says what was read in the documents and, while one of theirs is being read, that the data are on their way (`client_link_open` gives each person's marks and whether each document is still being read), and each field read says so under it ("Lido do documento", `lib/cases/read-fields.ts`); ONE's message with the link names the documents first (`caseStepsMessage`), and so does the third version of ONE's instructions in the conversation (`atendimento.conversa`). The firm reads on the case which data ONE read from the documents, in the AI's suggestion what a document says of its person, and on a document a CPF, a birth date or a CEP that differs from the record (`readDataDiffers`), which ONE never changes. A CEP the person form opened with is no longer looked up again (`CepField`): the lookup rewrote, at the opening of the form, a street on record with the one its base has for that CEP. The round trip `apps/web/src/lib/cases/document-data.db.test.ts` (part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`) has a client send an identity and a bill on their link, and finds their data written, marked and, once the client corrects one, unmarked. Walked on the development project with the real model on 2026-10-27, with an identity card and a bill drawn with invented data: each read in about 20 seconds (the birth date and never the day of issue nor the validity; the installation's address and never the company's), the name completed, the address written as names, on the link at 390 and on the case at 1440. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: what is read is the model's reading, which the client confirms on the link by reading it (nothing asks them to confirm field by field), and ONE may go on with it before they do (it creates the lawsuits once nothing is missing); marital status and profession are on no document, and stay the client's to write; a nationality is taken only where the document writes it; a bill in somebody else's name is refused by the check, as before, and gives no address; a name the document writes hyphenated or abbreviated is not taken as a completion; a document a person of the firm accepts on the case without the second factor withdraws its check (0024), and writes nothing of what the AI read.
- **The court system of each lawsuit is ONE's to name and the firm's to approve or not, and what ONE generates says it may err (2026-10-27).** The owner found that choosing the court's system on the case was no task for a firm, and asked that ONE name it, the firm only approving it or not, the approval said as the firm's decision; and that whatever ONE generates, the lawsuits it creates included, say that ONE may err, so the firm always knows. Migration `0066_court_system_by_one` (pgTAP `0070`, 16 assertions with two firms; the tests `0030`, `0032`, `0039`, `0044`, `0046`, `0049`, `0060` and `0065` approve the system where they build a package; applied to the development project on 2026-10-28, see below): a planned lawsuit has the system the platform's table gives for its court and kind, confirmed or not (`proceedings_court_system`, `proceedings.system_by_one`), whoever wrote it and whatever system was sent, and none where the table has no row (ONE waits for the platform, `system_unknown`); a lawsuit the court has keeps its own. A partner, a lawyer or the controladoria approves it (`decide_court_system`: `system_approved_by`, `system_approved_at`, told in the timeline and the audit as that person's decision; never ONE, never a support visit), or does not, saying why in 8 to 500 characters (`system_refused_*`; the reason stays out of the timeline), which the operator reads by court and kind with how many lawsuits wait, never by firm (`admin_court_system_refusals`), and answers in `/admin/calendario` by confirming the row or changing its system, saying where it was checked (`court-systems-card.tsx`, `court-system-actions.ts`, `lib/admin/court-systems.ts`, through `admin_set_court_system`). A filing waits for the approval (`filing_blocker` redefined: `system_refused` and `system_unapproved` first), so ONE asks for a package only after a person approved, and the case says what it waits for (`system_unapproved`, `system_refused`, also in the center of what waits); a package in course is given up when its system is refused or changes (`give_up_lawsuit_packages`, `proceedings_system_changed`, `court_system_changed` in the timeline); a row the platform adds, changes or confirms is had again by the planned lawsuits of that court and kind (`court_systems_repropose`: an approval stays only for the same system, a refusal counts as answered so that firm is asked again, and their cases are woken). A lawsuit ONE wrote is marked so (`proceedings.created_by_one`, through `case_system_writer`; the ones before found by the first word of their timeline), and a system a person had chosen for a lawsuit still planned was kept as approved. The app: under each planned lawsuit of the card "Processos" (`casos/[id]/components/court-system.tsx`), the system with who defined it (ONE from what the court publishes, checked by the platform or not), that ONE may err and what to check, the firm's word with who and when, and "Aprovar o sistema" and "Não aprovar" with its reason (`decideCourtSystem` in `lib/cases/actions.ts`), also on the card "Protocolo" where the package is asked for; the lawsuit's dialog offers no system; a lawsuit ONE created says so, with what to check before filing. What else ONE decides or registers by itself says beside it that ONE may err and what to check: a deadline it proposed from a publication or confirmed on the counted day, a hearing it registered or found in the agenda, its reading of a publication, a publication it classified (until a person checked it), its decision on the Tema 1.417, and a piece or a petition it approved. The round trips of a package (`filing`, `package-signatures`) and of ONE carrying a case (`advance`) approve the system as a partner would, ONE asking for the package only then. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: every row of `court_systems` is unconfirmed until the operator checks the court's acts, so every lawsuit waits for a person's word on its system, ONE's package included (the automation stops there by the owner's decision); a firm's "no" stops its lawsuit until the platform answers, and the firm never names another system; a document ONE accepted or refused by itself is not yet marked as ONE's on the case. 0066 reached the development project only on 2026-10-28, after its code was deployed the day before: in between, every case screen of production answered its error (the screen reads the new columns), which is why a migration goes to the development project before the push of the code that reads it. There `0070` passes but for its count of the operator, across every firm, which the firms the walks left there also hold (5 planned lawsuits of the TJGO's small-claims court, 4 of them waiting for the approval of their system): the remote database is not empty, as said above of the older files.
- **A model of the library is made from a document the firm already uses (2026-10-27).** The owner asked that a person who adds a model to the library may send a document ready and ONE analyze it and prepare the model. Migration `0067_model_from_document` (pgTAP `0071`, 27 assertions with two firms; `0003` holds a row of the new table, and `discard_file` is redefined so that a file a request names is never forgotten under it; applied to the development project on 2026-10-28, where `0071` passes): a partner or a lawyer sends a Word file (`.docx`) or a PDF (purpose `model_source`, told by its bytes: `@flyee/documents/detect` names a Word package by its parts, never by its name) and asks for the model (`request_template_import`: their own upload, a PDF whose pages could be counted and within the setting's 30, the setting's 20 requests of a firm in a day, counted on the record; never a support visit). The backend owes a request two works, one at a time, each under a numbered claim (`claim_template_imports`, `template_import_to_work`, `record_template_import`): its text, paragraph by paragraph (a Word file read by the app itself, word for word, `lib/library/docx.ts`: its runs, the number or letter a list gives a paragraph as Word counts it, a table a line per row, the footnotes and endnotes called by their number and written at the end, and nothing the page hides; a PDF transcribed by the task `biblioteca.modelo.transcricao`, Haiku 5.5, whose answer the database reads from the run), after which the file is forgotten, record first, and its bytes removed; then the marking (task `biblioteca.modelo.marcacao`, Sonnet 5.5), which reads the paragraphs by their numbers, with what is written as a CPF, a CEP, a telephone or an e-mail taken out, and answers what the document is (a petition, a power of attorney, a fee contract, or none ONE uses, which is said so), a name, and which paragraphs change: the court, the parties, the company, the values and who signs under the marks the platform fills, the narrative of the original case under one `[COMPLETAR: ...]` that says what to narrate, and never a `[COMPLETAR]` in a paper a client signs. The database keeps the answer only when every change names paragraphs that exist, in order and apart (`template_import_proposal`), and asks another run otherwise. Every paragraph no change names is the firm's word for word: the model is written from the paragraphs and the changes (`composeModel` in `lib/library/imports.ts`). What ONE prepared waits for a person (`template_imports`, read by who writes the firm's models): the dialog of a new model (in the library and in the setup's papers step) offers "Enviar documento", says what ONE is doing while it works and may be closed meanwhile, and fills itself with the model, saying that ONE may err, what it changed, each change against what was there, and what still looks like the original case (a name the marking listed, a CPF or a CNPJ that checks, a CEP, a telephone, an e-mail, a registration at the bar, something left to complete in a paper, a character the PDF of a piece cannot write: `modelChecks`); a partner or a lawyer saves it as a model of the firm (`save_imported_template`, through `save_draft_template`, which closes the request in the same step) or gives it up (`dismiss_template_import`). The library offers "Criar a partir de um documento" beside "Novo modelo", lists in the card of the models what ONE prepares with its state and its next step, and looks again by itself while ONE works (`watchModelImports`). The job `one-template-imports` (every five minutes and on `one/library.import.requested`) does each work in a step of its own, and forgets what waited longer than the setting's seven days (`expire_template_imports`); without a job runner each work is done after the response, and the screen that waits asks for the next one (`lib/library/import-work.ts`, `import-actions.ts`, `import-read.ts`, `jobs.ts`; `biblioteca/import-panel.tsx`). What was read of a document is kept only while ONE prepares the model and it waits for a person. The round trip `apps/web/src/lib/library/imports.db.test.ts` (part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`) sends a Word file and a PDF through the real worker with the model told what to answer, and saves the model. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: neither task ran against a real model yet; the text of a PDF is the model's transcription and may differ from the original in a word (the dialog says so; a Word file is read as it is); a text box, a header or a footer of a Word file, and a list numbered through its paragraph style, are not read; a name of the original case the marking did not list stays in the text unseen, and a first name alone is pointed out only when the marking listed it; an old `.doc`, RTF or OpenDocument file is refused with how to save it. `apps/web/.env.local` was lost on 2026-10-27 (another session's `git worktree remove` followed the junctions of `node_modules` and deleted it with the tracked files, which were restored), so the code was deployed on 2026-10-27 before 0067 reached the database, and the library answered its error until 2026-10-28; the file was then made again from the production environment of the Vercel project (`vercel -Q C:\Users\tribo\.vercel-one env pull apps/web/.env.local --environment=production`, run by the owner), whose variables point at the development project, with the build platform's own (`VERCEL*`, `TURBO_*`, `NX_DAEMON`) taken out, and 0066 and 0067 were applied with it.
- **Direito do Passageiro is a module, and the travel agency partner of a firm has an area of its own (the plan of 2026-10-29, approved by the owner the same day; built 2026-10-29).** The plan is the Claude Doc "ONE · Módulo Direito do Passageiro e a área da agência parceira" (https://claude.ai/artifact/76z5aoDxkUvUanBBfpyg97). Its decisions: an agency works with one firm per module at a time; the firm invites the agency; the passenger confirms on a link of ONE; after the registration the agency sees only where the following stands; without a case, what the agency keeps goes 30 days after a trip with no problem and 180 after a problem that did not become a case (a setting); the AI's reading and FlightAware's reads count for the partner firm; the API after the rest. Six migrations, 0068 to 0073 (pgTAP 0072 to 0077, each with two firms; applied to the development project on 2026-10-29, where those six files pass). **The module boundary** (0068): `product_modules` and `firm_modules` (the passenger module on for every firm; the operator turns one off, `admin_set_firm_module`) and `cases.module`. The core never imports a module: it asks the registry (`lib/modules/registry.ts`: the kinds of problem, the partner's area) and the list of jobs (`lib/modules/jobs.ts`); a module's code lives in `src/modules/<key>/` and its routes in a route group named after it (`app/**/(direito-do-passageiro)/`); the lint refuses any other import (`eslint.config.ts`; the files from before are in `LEGACY_MODULE_IMPORTS`, which a test keeps exact). **Partners** (0069): `partners`, `partner_members` (a person is in one firm or one partner, never both), `partner_links` (one active per partner and module), `partner_invitations` (the firm invites an agency by e-mail, `/parceria/[token]`; an agency invites its own people); `/settings/parceiros` for the firm, `/parceiro` and `/parceiro/equipe` for the agency, the gate keeping an agency's people in their area. **The agency's area** (0070): `pax_travelers`, `pax_trips` with their legs, `pax_documents` (files kept in the firm's storage under the partner, `files.partner_id`, which no firm reads, nor its audit trail), `pax_consent_terms` (the clause of the agency's contract), `pax_confirm_links` and `pax_consents`; the AI reads each document (task `parceiro.documento.leitura`, a shadow task: the firm never reads its runs; job `one-pax-document-readings`) and the database writes what it read where the record has nothing; the passenger's page `/acordo/[token]` (the words, agreeing with a WhatsApp, documents); a trip ONE follows of an agency is hidden from the firm until its case opens. The screens: `/parceiro/passageiros` (the counts as filters, the search in a server action, the clause), the passenger (the shared person form, the link, the documents with what ONE read, the trips), `/parceiro/passageiros/importar` (an agency's export read in the browser). **The handoff** (0071): when the passenger answers SIM to the firm's word about a problem, `one_open_case_from_alert` finds each person of the trip among the firm's clients by CPF and a name that is theirs or makes them new, hands the agency's data, read marks and documents over (`pax_hand_over`: the files become the firm's and no longer the agency's; `case_documents.source` `partner`, checked as a client's), and marks the case as the partnership's (`cases.partnership_id`, origin `agency`); ONE waits for the client to confirm on their link (`client_confirmation`, a wait of the client's) before it creates the lawsuits; the client's link says the data came from the agency; the test chat follows an agency's passenger whose WhatsApp is its number for it (55 00 98888-0006, label `agencia`). **Retention and rights** (0072): the daily `one-pax-retention` forgets who is kept no longer (`pax_retention_sweep`, the setting `partners`); forgetting deletes the record, the trips ONE followed (but a case's) and the agency's files, whose bytes the backend removes after their records (`pax_file_removals`); the agency and the passenger take what is kept and delete it on request; a partnership that ends stops its trips; flight reads have a share per firm of the day (`watch_firm_daily_reads`); the operator reads each firm's reads and each partnership in counts (a tab of `/admin/escritorios`), and an agency's people leave the list of accounts with no firm. **The API** (0073): keys of a partnership (`partner_api_keys`: the hash alone, five at most, sixty calls a minute), `POST /api/parceiros/v1/passageiros`, `/links` and `/situacao`, and `/parceiro/passageiros/integracao`. **What an independent review of the six found, put right the same day** (`0074_agency_safeguards`, pgTAP `0078`, 25 assertions with two firms and two agencies): an agreement covers who gave it and the children it was given for, at the WhatsApp it was given with (`pax_agreed_by`), so a trip ONE follows names along only those children, an adult who goes along is followed on a trip in their own name and is never handed to the firm by another's yes, another holder or another WhatsApp waits for an agreement again (the link shows the end of the number and takes another, and lets the passenger agree again to a trip the agency added later), taking the agreement back stops a trip already told of a problem, and a yes after it, or after the partnership ended, takes nothing of the agency's record and is a person's to take up (`agency_closed`); one firm's share of the flight reads no longer holds the others back (a firm that spent it is left out before the trips are chosen), and each leg is read only around its own flight, so a round trip is not read through the days in between; the delay is judged at the end of each journey of a trip (`flight-problem-v2`: a round trip is two, and the legs are taken in the order they fly whatever their positions say); forgetting a passenger also clears what the AI read of their documents (the run keeps its cost) and touches only their trips; a deletion asked on the link keeps nothing of the request; the passenger's rights stay on the link after their data went to the firm and after the partnership ended, while the agency still keeps them; an upload whose answer was lost is finished by the next request or by the sweep; a date a document says that is no date is left out alone; an agency's abandoned upload is in no firm's audit trail; the AI reads at most the setting's 300 documents a day for a partnership; a trip takes at most the setting's 20 passengers and nine children of its holder; and the legs of a trip are kept in the order they fly. The round trip `apps/web/src/modules/direito-do-passageiro/agency/agency.db.test.ts` (part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`) runs it with the app's own code: the identity read by ONE for the agency alone, the agreement, the trip in the test chat, the cancellation, SIM and the case opened with the agency's record, the passenger's data taken and deleted, and the API with a key. Walked in the production build against the development project on 2026-10-29: the agency's home, the clause, a passenger with a trip, an identity read by the real model, the link, the passenger agreeing on a phone, the list and its counts, a spreadsheet, a key, the data downloaded and the firm's partnerships, at 1440 and 390, nothing unnamed and no sideways scroll; what the walk found was fixed at once (a kind of document capitalized inside a sentence, two cards of one title, the code blocks of the API, an agency's name after the word "agência", and the second-factor page, which did not verify when the sixth digit was typed). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the legal opinion on soliciting clients still decides whether a firm may receive an agency's passengers (ONE's first word to a real passenger waits for the operator's release anyway); the words before the agency's clause are the platform's setting, for the curator; a child is covered only once the agency names the adult who answers for them, and an adult who travels with somebody else is followed and handed over only on a trip in their own name, under their own agreement; a round trip with two delays is told of the first only (one alert per trip and fact); testing the whole way takes a second account, for the agency.
- **What a firm contracted, the modules to come, and the test that starts from the agency (2026-10-30).** The owner decided that a module of ONE is a package a firm contracts with its subscription (Direito do Passageiro the first; the checkout of the subscription and its packages is ONE's billing, M3), that the firm's people see which modules it contracted, and that Direito Previdenciário and Direito Trabalhista are shown as coming soon; that the firm follows its agencies by its own numbers, never a count of the agency's passengers who did not say yes (the legal opinion on soliciting clients decides that); and that the test of a flight's problem starts from the partner agency, which registers the passenger with the test chat's number, the trip, the data and the documents, the passenger agreeing on the agency's link to be told of a problem, before the steps of the script. Migrations `0075_module_offer` (pgTAP `0079`, 20 assertions with two firms and three agencies), `0076_agency_test_trip` (pgTAP `0080`, 5 assertions with two firms) and `0077_partnership_awaiting` (all three applied to the development project on 2026-10-30): `product_modules` gains its state (`status`: available, or `coming_soon`, which no firm can have: the founding trigger gives none, the operator's switch answers `module_coming_soon`, and a trigger on `firm_modules` refuses it however it is written), how ONE sells it (`sold_as`: a package added to the subscription, or included in it), a line for the screens and its place, and the two modules to come are seeded; `firm_cases` (0033) filters the cases that came from an agency (`agencia`, still going) and names the agency on each row; `firm_partnership_facts` is what a firm reads of each partnership: its own cases (going, in all, and still gathered with no word on any link that the client finished, which ONE waits for before it creates the lawsuits, 0071), and, to who manages the firm, what each costs today (the documents the AI read for it against the day's ceiling, the reads of its flights) with the firm's reads of the day against its share; a trip an agency registered keeps its day in the test chat (its tools moved every simulated trip to the day they were used, which a ready trip needs, so ONE told of a flight of another day than the agency wrote). The app: `/settings/modulos` (each module contracted, since when, a package of the subscription, the problems it covers and its partner; then the modules to come; `lib/modules/offer.ts` and `offer-read.ts`), the line of the modules under the title of `/inicio` and of the setup (`components/product/modules-strip.tsx`), and at the end of `/inicio` the quick ways into each module contracted (its partner agencies, the cases that came from them, the test chat while it is on, the modules); the setup's last step (`?passo=agencia`) invites the agency where the firm's module has one, optional and never missing, saying what the partnership needs to work; `/settings/parceiros` shows each partnership's numbers (`MetricBars`, `UsageMeter`); the bell tells who manages the firm that the agency accepted its invitation or ended the partnership, and who manages the agency that the firm ended it (`lib/partners/notices.ts`); the test chat opens on the agency's passenger, its first tab, with the steps of the test from the agency; the person form takes the test chat's numbers, (00) 98888-000N, as phones (`isTestChatPhone`), which an agency writes for the passenger it tests with; ONE's message after an agency passenger's yes says the data and documents came from the agency and asks to check them and say they finished, never to send them again (`caseStepsMessage` with `agency`); a document the agency sent says so on the case (`partner`, which the case screen did not know); and the page that asks somebody of the agency for the second factor speaks to the agency and leads back to its area. The script of the test, from the agency to the end of the lawsuit, is the Claude Doc "ONE · Roteiro do teste de ponta a ponta, pela agência" (https://claude.ai/artifact/5pCn7Zf68qHwtXK5jNx7cj). Walked in the production build against the development project on 2026-10-30: a firm founded for it, its modules on the home and their page, the test chat turned on, the agency invited from the setup's last step, joining and told of on the bell, its clause, the passenger with the test chat's number, the trip and three documents read by the real model, the agreement on a phone, the test chat on the agency's passenger, the flight cancelled, SIM, the case opened with the agency's record and found in `/casos?ver=agencia`, the partnership's numbers, and the client confirming on the link; at 1440 and 390, light and dark. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: nothing is bought in ONE yet, and every firm founded has the passenger module (the platform gives it, as before); the modules to come are shown in the app and not on the public site, whose rule names a module at most in a line; the remote copy of pgTAP `0057` counts every firm's followed trips, which the walks' agency trips change there (on the embedded database it passes).
- **Each module has an area of its own, and the pages of the work are read by module (2026-10-30, the owner's review of that day's build).** The owner found the modules mixed into the firm's pages and asked that each module the firm contracted open an area of its own with its lawsuits and nothing of another module's, that the home list the modules as its quick access (the template's quick-action card, each with the icon that says what the module is, the ones to come said "Em breve"), that the main menu have "Módulos" with a submenu of every module (the ones to come said so, an available one the firm did not contract with a lock), that every page of the work be reviewed for the mixing, that the setup carry nothing that leads away from it (a line of modules with "Ver módulos" on its first step took the owner out of a setup half done; the rule is in `apps/web/CLAUDE.md`, `.claude/rules/app-ux.md` and the `product-screen` skill), and that an invitation offer its link and its e-mail on its first screen. Migration `0078_module_areas` (pgTAP `0081`, 22 assertions with two firms and two modules): `product_modules.icon` (the plane, the umbrella and the hard hat, names of the app's set: `NiUmbrella` and `NiHardHat` joined it); `firm_cases`, `firm_deadlines` and `firm_hearings` take a module, counts included (each case row names its module, and the list keeps a case of a module with no kinds of problem); `firm_module_conversations` and `firm_module_approvals` give a module's conversations (a contact who is somebody of a case of it, with or without the ninth digit) and its answers of the AI (what each is about, a conversation as above); and what a partner decides for the firm (who signs, the Valor da Causa Pedido, what ONE decides alone, the AI's triage) is decided by `decides_for_firm`: a partner, or, in a firm where nobody is a partner, who manages the firm account. The owner's own firm of the walk was founded by a lawyer ("Advocacia" at the sign-up), so nobody could answer the setup's first two steps. The app: the menu's group "Módulos" (`moduleMenuItems` of `menu-items.ts`, the viewer's `moduleMenu` read by the layout from the member context's `catalog`), `/modulos` (it was `/settings/modulos`, still led there by `next.config.mjs`), `/modulos/[modulo]` (the module's lawsuits, its share of each page of the work and what only it has: `firmArea` of its definition, and the test chat while it is on), the home's "Acesso rápido" (`ModulesQuickAccess`; the module's shortcuts left the home for its area), the choice of every module or one above each page of the work (`ModuleScope`, `?modulo=`), the agency's filter and the portfolio import offered under the module only, and the invitations (the team's, the agency's, an agency's colleague, the setup's team step) with "Copiar link" and "Enviar por e-mail" on their first screen, the e-mail offered only where ONE can send one (`emailReady`): the invitation of the walk could not leave because the production environment has no `RESEND_API_KEY` (the Resend account and ONE's domain are on the owner's checklist). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the pages of every module stay in the main menu for who works across modules (the controladoria, a partner), each with the choice above it; a conversation or a publication of nobody's case yet, and a deadline of a lawsuit the firm has no case of, are read with every module only; a search of publications by case number looks at every module; with a single module the choice above a page shows that module beside "Todos os módulos", which differ only in what is of no case yet.
- **A model of the library is taken in Word (2026-11-03).** "Baixar em Word" (`components/product/word-download-button.tsx`) is on each model of the library (the firm's and the platform's, for anybody who reads it), on the paper ONE sends in the setup's step, on the text in the model's dialog, on the operator's models in `/admin/conteudo` and on the clause in force of an agency's contract. The .docx is written in the browser (`downloadWord` in `lib/library/word-download.ts`, `textDocx` and `writeDocx` in `lib/library/docx.ts`): a heading where the PDF of the filled document prints one, A4 with the margins of a petition, Arial 12, a line and a half, only the parts Word needs, the marks as they are, read back word for word by the library's own reader and opened by python-docx. It was built on a first reading of the owner's request of that day, which asked for the document's submission (the next item); it stays as a convenience, not as a rule of the screens.
- **ONE makes the firm's own model from the document the firm already uses (2026-11-03).** The owner asked that the firm submit the document it already uses (its power of attorney in the setup's step, as it could the contract), that ONE validate it and make of it the firm's own model adapted for ONE to read, refusing a document that cannot be one or asking for what it needs to build the model, and that this follow ONE's premise ("Product decisions the code assumes": what needs no person, ONE's own AI does). Migration `0079_model_from_firm_document` (pgTAP `0082`, 30 assertions with two firms; `0045` and `0071` follow it: the catalog has one more decision, and `0071` walks a person's way with the firm keeping the models to a person; applied to the development project on 2026-11-03, where `0045`, `0071` and `0082` pass). A request says what the document is sent as (`template_imports.expected_kind`: the setup's step sends the power of attorney or the contract, the library says nothing) and whether its model takes the place of the firm's default (`make_default`). The marking's second version (`biblioteca.modelo.marcacao` v2) first says what the document is (`o_que_e`), what it lacks of what its kind must hold (`faltas`, codes of `template_import_essentials`: a power of attorney's grantor, grantees and powers; a contract's parties, object and fees; a petition's requests and grounds) and what only the firm knows (`perguntas`: a blank of the firm's, a choice left open, a lawyer or firm that may not be this one; never what the platform fills nor a person's data), and the database decides from the run (`record_template_import`): a document that is no model ONE uses, not what it was sent as, or lacking what its kind must hold is refused with why (`refused`, `refusal`, what was read of it gone); questions wait for a partner or a lawyer (`asking`; `answer_template_import`: one answer per question, at least one said, the setting's 1,000 characters each; the marking is asked again with every round asked and answered, masked like the document, the setting's two rounds at most, after which what is still open is said among what to check); otherwise, where the firm lets ONE (`model_from_document`, a new decision of the catalog, ONE's by default, in `/settings/autonomia`), ONE saves the model itself (`saving` then `saved`): the backend checks the very text the database writes (`template_import_body`) with `saveGate` (`lib/library/imports.ts`: nothing of the original case, no character the PDF of a piece cannot write, nothing the marking asked a lawyer to check) and the database saves that text by its SHA-256 through `draft_template_write` (the function a person's `save_draft_template` now goes through too), as the firm's model marked `draft_templates.prepared_by_one`, the default of its paper or kind when it was sent for one or the firm has none (a name taken gets "(2)" to "(9)"); a text with something to check, a firm that keeps it to a person, a full library or a save that keeps failing waits for a person as before, with why (`held_reason`). The worker before 0079 asks the claim with two arguments and never claims a save (`claim_template_imports(..., target_saving)`). The app: in the setup's step "O que o cliente assina" each paper has "Enviar a procuração do escritório" or "Enviar o contrato do escritório" first (the chooser opens in place, the dialog follows over the step), and says what ONE does with the document sent (`readPaperImports`, `PaperRequestLine` in `configurar/setup-view.tsx`, looked at again while ONE works); the dialog's panel (`biblioteca/import-panel.tsx`) answers ONE's questions in place, says a refusal with why and the way to send another, and once ONE saved the model the dialog edits it, already saved, with what ONE changed; the library lists what ONE asks ("Responder"), refuses or saves, and marks the models ONE prepared ("Preparado pelo ONE", that ONE may err). The round trip `apps/web/src/lib/library/imports.db.test.ts` (part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`) walks a person's way, ONE saving a power of attorney sent as one, a contract sent as the power of attorney refused, a contract's blank asked and the answer in the model ONE saved, and a document that is no model refused. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: neither the marking's second version nor the transcription ran against a real model yet; a question is the model's words, cleaned to the copy rules; a document ONE refuses is forgotten, so the firm sends it again after fixing it; the questions wait in the library and in the setup's step, not yet in the center of what waits; a model ONE saved is used by ONE at once, the screens saying ONE may err until a person saves it.
- **The first case is ONE's, never the firm's step (2026-11-03).** The owner finished the firm's setup and found "Abrir o primeiro caso" pending on the home, which made the work look manual: the first case arrives through a module the firm contracted, and ONE opens it. The home's list ends at what the firm gives ONE (`lib/onboarding.ts`: who signs, the team, the channel); while no case ever reached the firm (`first_case` of `firm_setup_facts`, read for everybody, `waitsFirstCase`), the home says "O ONE espera o primeiro caso" with "Ver o que o ONE faz sozinho" (`/settings/autonomia`), and so does the empty `/casos`, which offers the portfolio's import where it may and opening a case by hand as the exception. The rule is in "Product decisions the code assumes".
- **The travel agency has a setup of its own (2026-11-03).** The owner, testing an agency's sign-up, got lost: everybody of a partner owes the second factor (0069), and the gate sent the agency's first person to the generic security screen, which said nothing of the agency. Migration `0080_agency_setup` (pgTAP `0083`, 18 assertions with two firms and three agencies): `partners.setup_answered_at` (an agency that already had a clause or a passenger was set up by then), `answer_partner_setup` (who manages the agency, never a support visit, on the platform's record that no firm reads), `my_partner` saying it, and the clause ONE proposes (`partners.clause_proposal`, the platform's words for the curator, filled with the agency's and the firm's names by `pax_clause_proposal`, short enough for the longest names: the agency reads it, changes what it wants and registers it through `pax_save_consent_term`; ONE never registers it). `/parceiro/configurar` (the passenger module's route group; `partnerSetup` of its definition, which the core reads through the registry's `partnerSetupOf` by the partner's kind): the second factor first, since every write of the agency asks for it; then, for who manages the agency, its name (what the passenger reads in the words they agree to), the clause of its contract (ONE's proposal in the field, said as ONE's with what to check, taken in Word), its team (colleagues invited in a dialog over the step) and how its passengers reach ONE (one by one, a spreadsheet, the agency's system), where the setup ends by opening that way with the page loaded anew; somebody who does not manage the agency walks the factor and the way in. The gate opens the setup for whoever of a partner owes the factor, never `/settings/security` with nothing said, and in place of the agency's home while who manages it never answered it (`decideGate`, `shellHold`, `partnerSetupPending`); the home and the menu offer it again ("Configuração"). Pure rules in `apps/web/src/modules/direito-do-passageiro/agency/setup.ts`; the answer in `lib/partners/setup-actions.ts`. Since 0081 the clause's step offers the words the agency's firm gave (`pax_clause_offer`), else ONE's, and says whose they are.
- **Each person's own setup, and each module's (2026-11-03).** The owner asked that the module's parametrization leave the firm's setup for one of its own, in the module's colour, for each module the firm contracted only, the others shown disabled, and asked whether the firm's other people had a setup (none had: a member was dropped on the home, a lawyer met the second factor half way and, with no registration at the bar, had their publications unread, since the DJEN is read by the registrations of the firm's people). **A person's own setup** (`lib/firm/person-setup.ts`, `configurar/person-setup-view.tsx`, no migration: `onboarding_state`, flow `person-setup`): the second factor first and, for a partner or a lawyer, the registration at the bar; on the home in place while something of it is owed and the person never answered it, at `/configurar` for anybody who does not manage the firm, answered for who manages it by the firm's setup's finish; a lawyer who set the registration aside is reminded on the home; and the gate sends whoever of a firm the rule asks the factor of to `/configurar`, never to `/settings/security` (the operator still goes there). **The core and the modules** (migration `0081_module_setup`, pgTAP `0084`, 22 assertions with two firms and two agencies; `0003` and `0012` follow the new table): the firm's setup keeps what every firm needs (the second factor, who signs, the team, the papers a client signs, the channel), and its finish opens the setup of each module the firm contracted and never answered (`firm_modules.setup_answered_at`, `answer_module_setup`, a firm that had answered its setup taken as having answered its modules'), one after the other, then the home; the home's list has a line per module setup. A module declares its setup in its definition (`firmSetup`, read through the registry's `firmSetupOf`; `lib/modules/module-setups.ts`), and the passenger module's is `/configurar/direito-do-passageiro` (its route group): the Valor da Causa Pedido, **the words of the clause its agencies register** (the firm's to give, as the legal party of the partnership: `pax_firm_clauses`, `pax_save_firm_clause` by who decides for the firm with the second factor, starting from ONE's proposal written for the firm, `pax_firm_clause_proposal`, which says the agency in general; the agency registers them in its own contract, 0080) and the partner agency, the test chat turned on in place when the partnership has no channel; the second factor first only for a session with none; ONE's modules the firm did not contract under the steps, disabled. **Each module's colour** (`product_modules.tone`, among the design system's tones: the passenger module the secondary, Previdenciário accent-1, Trabalhista accent-4; the primary stays the core's and the red is risk's) on the home's quick access, on its setup's rail (`SetupWizard` `tone`) and icon. Known limits: 0081 reached the development project on 2026-11-03, applied by the owner; the test chat stays in the core's channel step (a test number of the firm), its trips being the passenger module's; the core's papers (the power of attorney and the contract) are the same for every module.
- **A registration starts from documents (2026-11-03).** The owner, testing an agency's new passenger, found a form of typed fields while ONE already read the passenger's documents, and asked that the person send the documents and confirm what ONE read, typing being the contingency, in this registration and the others (the rule is in `.claude/rules/app-ux.md`, the `product-screen` skill and `apps/web/CLAUDE.md`). Migration `0083_documents_first` (pgTAP `0086`, 25 assertions with two firms and two agencies; applied to the development project on 2026-11-03): somebody of an agency starts a passenger as a draft (`pax_start_traveler`: `pax_travelers.draft`, under the setting's name of no person, in no list, count, search or picker, with no link to agree on, `pax_issue_confirm_link` answering `draft`; the writer's earlier draft goes with its documents), sends it the identity, the proof of address and the trip's document, and ONE's reading (`parceiro.documento.leitura`, its second version: a screenshot of the booking in the airline's app, its site or the e-mail, and a photo of a printed ticket, boarding pass or itinerary are a ticket, and its locator is read) names the draft whole, fills it, and says when the CPF it read is a registered passenger's (`duplicate_of`). A draft's CPF is kept apart (`draft_cpf`, by a trigger whatever writes it), so a passenger nobody confirmed never holds a CPF against a save, the import, the API or the unique index of the partnership's CPFs. The agency confirms the draft with what it wrote (`pax_confirm_traveler`: the name, the fields it changed and, for a child, who answers for them; the CPF it sent, or else the one read, refused naming who has it; a field left as read keeps its mark). A ticket or a boarding pass may wait for its trip (`pax_reserve_document` with no trip), its legs and locator read for the trip's form (`pax_document_legs`: the cleaned legs, or what was read when a leg fails its checks), and joins the trip once it is saved (`pax_attach_trip_documents`, only a trip of its own passenger). A draft nobody confirmed goes after the setting's 24 hours with its documents (`pax_forget_drafts`, in the daily `one-pax-retention`). The app: "Adicionar passageiro" opens on the documents (`passageiros/components/new-passenger-dialog.tsx`: the identity, the proof of address and the trip, each with what ONE read of it, then the fields to confirm with each one read marked and that ONE may err, a registered passenger of that CPF named with the way to their record, and the trip read from the ticket registered with the passenger, or opened for correction when a leg failed its checks), "Preencher à mão" being the contingency; a new trip opens on its document (`passageiros/[id]/components/trip-dialog.tsx`, which also takes a document already sent, `documentId`), its legs and locator read into the form. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: ONE's reading takes some seconds a document, and the dialog lets the agency go on after 45 seconds, what is still read then filling only what it did not touch; a child's ticket is not made a trip (a child travels on the trip of the adult who answers for them); the documents of a draft somebody leaves wait for the sweep, and those of a draft whose CPF is a registered passenger's do not pass to that passenger; the firm's own registrations followed the same day (below).
- **The firm's registrations start from documents too (2026-11-03).** The audit of that day listed where the firm's area typed what a document holds, and the owner approved the order recommended. Migration `0085_firm_documents_first` (pgTAP `0088`, 15 assertions with two firms): two readings that decide nothing and whose runs the firm reads, `caso.documento.leitura` (an identity, a child's birth certificate included, a proof of address, or a ticket, a boarding pass, a screenshot of the booking or a photo of a printed one) and `caso.protocolo.leitura` (the receipt a court's system gave: the lawsuit's number, the day, the court, the system, the unit and the class); `prefill_reading_allowed` (a member of the firm, never in a support visit, within the setting `document_prefill`'s 200 reads a day); and, on the client's link, `client_link_prefill_to_read` (the backend's: the document of a child sent before the child is in the case, an identity of the link with nobody yet, within the setting's 10 reads a link a day) and `client_link_assign_upload` (that upload becomes the child's once the client confirms them, and is then sent as any upload). A person of the firm sends the file as any upload (`startCaseUpload`), the backend measures it and reads it now (`readForPrefill` in `lib/cases/prefill-actions.ts`, `readStoredFile` in `document-prefill-work.ts`), and the answer fills the form (`lib/cases/document-prefill.ts`, pure: what does not pass the form's checks, a CPF whose digits do not check, a day still to come, an airport that is no code, a CNJ number whose check digits fail, is left out and said), each field read marked until somebody writes over it; once the record is saved, the document joins the case (`finishCaseUpload`), where its check of 0024 runs as on any other (an accepted ticket adds no leg the case has by its flight and day).
