The app: "Incluir pessoa" on a case opens on the identity and the proof of address (`casos/[id]/components/people-card.tsx`, which now also marks, when a person is edited, the fields ONE read from their documents); "Incluir trechos" opens on the trip's document (`ticket-dialog.tsx`: the legs read, which go in, and in which the problem happened, a choice no document holds); "Abrir um caso" opens on the client's identity and the trip's document, which give the name, the CPF, the airline and the day of the flight (`casos/components/new-case-dialog.tsx`, the documents joining the case once it is created); the receipt of a filing is sent first and ONE reads the number and the day into the fields, warning when the receipt is of another court (`filing-card.tsx`); on the client's link, the trip says the flights come from the document sent above, and that it is being read (`c/[token]/client-folder.tsx`), and a child is added from their document (read, confirmed, then the child's; a photo hard to read is said with the two ways on). `components/cases/document-slot.tsx` is the line of each document; "Preencher à mão" stays everywhere as the contingency. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: neither reading ran against a real model yet; each document is read once for the form and once more by its check when it joins the case (two runs); a document read for a form somebody closed is taken back, but one of a form left open in a tab waits as an upload; the manual hearing, deadline and lawsuit, the administrative complaint, the registration at the bar and the certificate inventory are still typed (the audit's next items).
- **What the passenger's link asks, the agency's flights as they happen, and the firm's forecast (2026-11-03).** The owner found the passenger's link asking for documents the agency had already sent, and an agreement confirmed by a line the passenger read past; asked that the agency follow each flight of a passenger who agreed as it happens, as the firm follows a case; and that the firm know from when its agencies' passengers agreed, to foresee the cases that may come, in numbers by date and nobody named (the owner's choice of that day; the decision of 2026-10-30 kept even the count from the firm until the legal opinion on soliciting clients). The rule for every card is in `.claude/rules/app-ux.md`, the `product-screen` skill and `apps/web/CLAUDE.md`: a card asks only for what is still owed (a request already fulfilled leaves for `QuickActions`, one click away), says where each thing stands in icon and colour (`CardTitle`, `StateLine`), and an act the person must understand is confirmed in an `OutcomeDialog`, never in an `Alert`; the passenger's link (`/acordo/[token]`: "Acordo registrado" with the trips ONE follows, the WhatsApp the alerts go to and how to leave) and the agency's documents card follow it (`modules/direito-do-passageiro/agency/agreement-documents.ts`, pure and tested). Migration `0084_flight_progress` (pgTAP `0087`, 28 assertions with two firms and two agencies; applied to the development project): `tracked_legs` keeps when each leg left and arrived (`departed_local`, `arrived_local`) as its source said, never from the clock; `record_trip_watch` (FlightAware's actual times, read by `legReadingOf`) and `test_chat_set_legs` (the agency's test trip gains "Simular partida do voo" and "Simular pouso do voo") write them; `pax_flight_progress` is what somebody of an agency reads (the trips ONE follows in the setting's seven days, `partners.flight_progress_days`, or every trip of one passenger but one whose agreement was taken back); `firm_partnership_facts` gains each partnership's `forecast` (the passengers whose answer in force is a yes and from when, eight weeks of agreements, the trips followed now and flying in the next days, the legs in the air, the passengers told of a problem who have not answered). The app: `agency/flight-progress.ts` (the steps of a flight: each leg's departure and arrival, nothing under way before the first departure, a cancelled leg stopping the way), `agency/trip-flight.tsx` under each trip of the passenger's page, `agency/partner-flights-card.tsx` on the agency's home (the module's `partnerHome`, which the core loads through the registry, `lib/modules/types.ts`), both read again every minute while a flight moves, and the "Previsão de casos" of each partnership in `/settings/parceiros` (`partnership-forecast.tsx`). `scripts/check-db-refs.mjs` now reads every column an `alter table` adds (it read the first only), and `TrendSparkline` keeps its drawing `inert` (MUI X 9.7's sparkline sent its `disableKeyboardNavigation` to the svg). Known limits: the steps are only what a source said (FlightAware from some hours before the first departure to some after the last arrival, once its key exists, or the test chat's tools), so a real flight before its window shows its scheduled times; the forecast counts each passenger whose answer in force is a yes, a child included, and dates each by the day of that answer.

- **The test chat is the agency's passenger's only (2026-11-03).** The owner, testing from the agency, found the test chat's own ready passengers beside the agency's (one of them bore the same name) and asked that they go, the test chat working only with the passengers an agency registers at the test WhatsApp. Migration `0087_test_chat_agency_only` (pgTAP `0090`, 6 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project on 2026-11-03, when the four ready trips there went): a test chat turned on brings no passenger of its own (`start_test_chat` no longer calls `seed_test_trips`, which stays for the database tests that use the ready trips as their scenes: pgTAP `0054` to `0057` and `0080` and the round trips of the test chat and of ONE in the conversation make them), and the followed trips of the test chats that no agency registered (the ready ones and the real flights) are deleted with their legs and alerts; a case one of them opened stays, as the firm's record of its test. The page `/t/[token]` shows the agency's passenger with its tools (cancel, six hours late, departure, landing, start over) and, until one agrees, the steps of the test from the agency; the tab of a real flight is gone (`followRealFlight` stays with no button, as the way the round trip of the watch follows a flight at FlightAware's fake).
- **The client's notices go with no agreement asked (2026-11-03).** The owner, testing the agency's passenger on her link, found the notices asked as an agreement ("Quero receber") and decided they go without one: they are the firm's only way to the passenger, and a client who never answered would miss a hearing and leave the lawsuit stalled (a claimant absent from a hearing of the small-claims court has the lawsuit extinguished, Lei 9.099/1995, art. 51, I). Migration `0088_notices_by_default` (pgTAP `0091`, 11 assertions with two firms; `0003`, `0041` and `0061` follow it): a notice goes to every person a lawsuit tells (its adult claimants, and who answers for a minor) at the WhatsApp the firm has for them, unless that person asked to stop: on their link (kept with what the server saw of the request), to a person of the firm (who records how), or with SAIR on WhatsApp, which the footer of every notice says (`wa_optouts`, 0019). `notice_stops` keeps every request to stop, one in force per person, holding whatever number the firm has for them later, until they ask to be told again (on their link, a few times a day, or to a person of the firm, saying how); `notice_person_blocker` answers `no_phone`, `opted_out` or `stopped`, never `no_optin`; `document_recipient` asks a paper of the whole trip of who may be told first; `case_notices`, `client_link_notices`, `client_link_set_notices` and `record_notice_optin` (which keeps its name) read and write the requests to stop. The agreements given before (`notice_optins`) stay on record and decide nothing, and a person whose last word was to take the agreement back keeps it as a request to stop. The app: the client's link says where the notices go and what they bring (`c/[token]/client-notices.tsx`), confirms a stop with what the client loses before it and says it whole after, as being told again; the case's card "Avisos ao cliente" says who is told, who asked to stop and how, and records either request (`notices-card.tsx`, `recordNoticeStop`); the timeline names `notices_stopped` and `notices_resumed`. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: WhatsApp's business policy asks a business for the person's opt-in before the messages it starts, gathered in any channel; the firm-client relationship, the notice on the link and the SAIR in every message are what ONE offers for it, and a line in the power of attorney or the fee contract, the curator's to write, would make it explicit; for the LGPD the notices rest on the contract with the client and the exercise of their rights in a lawsuit (art. 7, V and VI), not on consent.
- **A passenger with the firm stays in the agency's list, and the latest in progress on both homes (2026-11-03).** The owner found the agency's only passenger gone from its list once her case opened: the list kept her ("Com o escritório"), but the page read "no passenger yet" from the count of the agency's passengers, which counted only the ones still with the agency. Migration `0089_agency_keeps_handed_over` (pgTAP `0092`, 6 assertions with two firms and two agencies; applied to the development project on 2026-11-03, before the push of 4e1d4d8) makes that count the list's (every passenger the agency confirmed, handed over or not, never a draft), the counts of what the agency still does leaving out who went to the firm as before; the page now reads "nobody at all" from the list itself, so it holds before 0089 is applied. The owner then asked for the case's progress, compact, for the latest five in progress, on the firm's home and on the agency's: `ProgressList` (`components/product/progress-steps.tsx`, in the catalog) is one line per thing with the step in words and its `ProgressBar`, each a link to it; the firm's card "Andamento dos casos" (`inicio/cases-journey-card.tsx`) lists under its counts the latest five cases in progress (`firm_cases`, the latest moved first, read by the home) with "Ver todos os casos"; the agency's home opens with "Passageiros em andamento" (`modules/direito-do-passageiro/agency/partner-passengers-card.tsx`, in the module's `partnerHome`, `agency/partner-home-section.tsx`): the first five of its list (the next to fly first) whose way did not stop, each with the agreement, the flights ONE follows or the firm, and "Ver todos os passageiros". The client's link's card "Documentos" is compact (`c/[token]/client-folder.tsx`, the pure rules in `lib/cases/client-documents.ts`): one line per document the firm needs with its state on the right (`StateLine` gained `status`), a button only where a file is still owed (nothing sent, or only refused, with the reason under it), the documents sent beyond the needs listed too, the card saying first when nothing is left to send, and sending again, or another kind, kept in "Enviar outro documento"; the files a link still takes are said once five or fewer are left.
- **The firm's home says each thing once (2026-11-04).** The owner found `/inicio` redundant: "O escritório precisa agir" (what passed its time and what holds a case until the firm acts, the center's groups of 0044) and the tiles of "O que espera por você" were fed by the same counts, so approvals, publications, pieces, overdue deadlines and papers were said twice, and "Nada espera por você agora" showed under a list of cases waiting on the firm. The tiles now keep what that list does not hold (`homeTiles` of `lib/firm/pending.ts`, pure and tested: what falls today and in the next days, what is good to look at, the papers' tile only while none waits too long), under "Para acompanhar"; every need is a tile while the list could not be read; the empty state shows only when the list was read and holds nothing; the reminder of a person's own registration at the bar waits while the setup's list asks it. No migration.
- **The home opens on the greeting and the modules, and the DJEN asks the firm only what it can put right (2026-11-05).** The owner found "Inscrições com a leitura do DJEN a conferir" under "Atrasado" on the home, leading to `/publicacoes`, where nothing could be done: it counted every registration whose reading was late, and where the DJEN is not read (`DJEN_CAPTURE` off: the development project, a preview) every one is late for good. A late reading is ONE's own work, which the operator watches (`admin_djen_health`), so the firm's needs (`todayNeeds` of `lib/firm/today.ts`) count only what a person puts right, each under "O ONE precisa de você" and never as late: a reading that stopped for a lawyer still on record (`reading`, to `/publicacoes#leitura`, whose card now leads to the team) and a communication the reading could not keep (`skipped`, read at the DJEN by its certificate). Where the DJEN is not read, `/publicacoes` says only that ("Este ambiente não lê o DJEN", each registration "Não lida neste ambiente"), "Conferir as inscrições" shows only when the team fixes something (a registration the DJEN refuses, a reading that stopped), and a late reading says ONE tries again by itself. After the owner's model of the same day (the template's AI hero and its grid of projects), `/inicio` opens on `HomeHero` (`components/product/home-hero.tsx`: the greeting, the firm's line and the modules' tiles in one card, dashed arcs with small icons at its sides; the page's title is "Início"), and "O escritório precisa agir" is a grid of one card per thing (`AttentionList`: the icon of its kind, its tag, two lines of what it is, how many). No migration.
- **`/publicacoes` reads in the order of the work, and ONE's latest work on a case is a row of cards (2026-11-05).** The owner found the screen polluted and out of order (an alert, three tiles, a search card, the reading's card and a legend before the first publication, each with its actions in three rows), and the case's "O que o ONE fez há pouco" a list of grey ticks with "Ver" drifting at the edge. `/publicacoes` now opens on the reading of the DJEN as one `CollapsibleCard` (`#leitura`, its state on its head, open by itself only when the team has something to put right there, every warning of the reading inside it), then the filters, the list's head with the search of a lawsuit's publications beside it, "O que cada resposta quer dizer" one click away, and each publication as `PublicationCard`, moved to `components/publications/publication-card.tsx` so the case shows the same card: what it is with the icon of its triage and its chip, its facts in one line, the court's words on a tint, the six answers, and at its foot the case, to whom, "Calcular prazo" or "Registrar audiência" where the triage says so, and the court's system and the DJEN's certificate in `QuickActions`. On the case, `ActivityCards` (`components/product/activity-cards.tsx`): one outlined card per kind of thing ONE did in the last 24 hours, its icon by the card it leads to, when, how many times, the movements a reading of the court brought, ONE or its AI as the author, and "Ver" (a publication's to `/publicacoes`). No migration.
- **ONE waits for the client's account before the lawsuits, and asks the client what the piece needs (2026-11-04).** The owner found a piece ONE had asked for come back with eight `[COMPLETAR]`, each a fact only the client's account gives (the purchase, how they learned of the problem, what the airline offered, when they arrived, the assistance, the expenses, what they lost): ONE had created the lawsuits waiting only for the documents, each person's data and a leg, and the piece is asked in the same look. Whatever a later step cannot be done without is settled before it, so migration `0090_account_before_piece` (pgTAP `0093`, 13 assertions with two firms; `0012` and `0029` follow it): where a case's account stands is one reading of the database (`case_account_state`: missing, preparing, proposed, incomplete, contested, failed, ready; `case_account` for the case screen, and in `client_link_statements` for the link), and the worker creates no lawsuit before it is ready (`lawsuitsWaiting` with `account`, the waits `account_*` in `lib/cases/advance.ts`; telling it and answering what is missing are the client's waits, ordering it is ONE's own work, never a task of the firm's pending center). The second version of the instructions of `caso.relato.cronologia` answers, beside the order of the facts, which points of the setting `statements.account_topics` (compra, aviso, solucao, chegada, assistencia, gastos, consequencias) the account does not answer (`faltam`) and one plain question to the client for each (`perguntas`, kept on the statement as `missing_topics` and `questions`), reading what the case registers of the trip (its legs and what ANAC's record says of each flight, `case_statement_to_process`), so it never asks what ONE knows; a point the client says they do not remember is answered. The client answers on their link (`client_link_complete_statement`, `completeClientStatement`) and ONE orders the account again at once, reading the answers (`client_added`); a client who says the order is wrong has it ordered again by ONE at once too (a person asked for it before); both within the setting's `max_rounds` (3), past which what is missing goes as it is and a correction waits for a person. A change of an account wakes the case. The app: the link's "Conte o que aconteceu" lists the points to tell before the client starts, asks "Faltou contar" with the questions and one field for the answers, and says when the firm is ordering it again (the client reads the firm, never ONE); the link's and the case's "O que ainda falta" name the account; the case's card "O que aconteceu" says what the account lacks, what was asked and what the client answered. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the second version never ran against the real model yet; a case whose lawsuits already exist keeps the piece it has (send the account, then ask for the piece again). The second stage of the plan is the next bullet.
- **The piece reads what ONE already knows, and ONE asks what the account misses on WhatsApp too (2026-11-04).** The second stage of the plan the owner approved: what ONE can take from another source, it takes, so the client answers only what no document holds and the lawyer only decides. Migration `0091_piece_reads_documents` (pgTAP `0094`, 7 assertions with two firms; `0068` follows it): the fourth version of the instructions of `caso.documento.conferencia` reads, of a ticket, a boarding pass or the e-mail of a purchase, the purchase (`compra`: its day, where it was bought, what was paid, the locator) and, of a receipt, the expense it proves (`gasto`: what, how much, when); `case_document_readings` gives what the documents the firm accepted say of them, as their check was applied (by ONE, or approved by a person; one decided without the AI says nothing); the facts of the piece (`case_draft_to_work`) carry them (`compras`, `gastos`) and, for each leg, what ANAC's record says of its flight (`registro_oficial`); the second version of the instructions of `caso.peca.minuta` says what each is, and to write `[COMPLETAR]` only for what neither they nor the account answer; the order of the client's account reads them too (`case_statement_to_process`, `readings`), so ONE never asks what a document already says. Migration `0092_account_asked_in_chat` (pgTAP `0095`, 8 assertions with two firms): where the firm lets ONE answer its clients, ONE asks what an account is missing in the client's conversation too, once per round, inside the 24 hours, in a conversation it holds, never to who asked the firm to stop (`account_asks_owed`, `one_ask_account`; the statements' sweep asks, `askAccountQuestions`), and what the client writes next there is their answer: it joins the account as the link's answer does and ONE orders it again (`conversation_account_open`, `one_complete_statement`, in ONE's turn before the model, which a read that fails never stops); the timeline says it was asked (`account_asked`). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: neither new version ran against the real model yet; a reply in the conversation after ONE asked is taken whole as the answer, whatever else it says (a question of the client's in it goes to the account, and ONE answers it only at their next message); a receipt is read only by ONE's check, so one a person accepts without the AI gives the piece no amount (the account may still say it).
- **ONE works without waiting on the job runner, and the runner holds one clock (2026-11-04).** On the 4th of November the owner's agency could not get two documents read: the runner stopped calling every function of ONE that morning, most likely because Inngest's free plan (50 thousand executions a month, each step counting) was spent on the fourth day of the month by ONE's 33 crons (some 15 thousand executions a day), so nothing ONE does by itself ran (the readings, ONE's answers in a conversation, the checks, the case carried on, the notices). The owner chose to stay on the free plan. No migration. What a person's action leaves owed is done right after their answer, on the same server, never through the runner: `soon(label, task)` (`lib/jobs/soon.ts`, over Next's `after()`, nested calls included) in every worker's `queue*` and `settle*`, which send no events any longer (`sendEvent` and the event map left `@flyee/jobs`); what ONE asks for while carrying a case on goes the same way (`followSoon`), and so does a statement joined from a conversation, its questions in the conversation included. Every old function is a sweep (`Sweep` of `lib/jobs/schedule.ts`, pure and tested: a key, when the clock runs it, whether it also runs while ONE is used, its `run(service, until)`), declared beside its worker and listed in `lib/jobs/registry.ts` (34, tested to be each named once on a schedule the clock meets), taking a few works a round. The runner holds one function, `one-tick` (`lib/jobs/tick.ts`): a beat every five minutes that runs every sweep due at it in one step (`runSweeps` of `lib/jobs/sweeps.ts`: what has only this beat first, the frequent ones turned by the beat so none starves, each on its own, nothing started after 200 seconds), two executions a beat, about 18 thousand a month. The sweeps that serve what people wait on also run while ONE is used, once in five minutes on each server, after the answer (`sweepWhileUsed` of `lib/jobs/while-used.ts`), from the logged area's layout, the pending center's read, the test chat's read, the client's link and the passenger's agreement link; the DJEN's reading, the tribunal's, ANAC's months, the backup and the newsletter stay the clock's only. The sync of a deploy with Inngest retires the old functions. Checked live the same day, after the owner's push: the two documents that waited were read by a round while ONE was used, new ones are read six or seven seconds after they arrive, and the runner calls `one-tick` every five minutes (whether the stop before it was the quota was not confirmed: the runner's dashboard is the owner's). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: if the runner stops again, nothing runs by the clock, so what waits for an hour of the day happens only while somebody uses ONE (a hearing's reminder at night does not go), and the DJEN's reading, the tribunals', ANAC's months, the backup and the newsletter wait; a work right after an answer has the request's five minutes, and one that does not finish waits for the next round; a sweep takes a few works a round, so a backlog clears more slowly than before (the DJEN's reading above all: about five thousand readings a day now).
- **The firm's own power of attorney and contract keep what the firm wrote (2026-11-04).** The owner found that the model ONE made of the firm's own power of attorney (0079) dropped what the firm had written: the client's data, which the firm's form writes one field a line, became one mark of a single paragraph, and the firm's own society with its CNPJ and its two lawyers with their registrations became `[[ESCRITORIO]]` and `[[OUTORGADOS]]`, which ONE fills with less (the firm's name with no CNPJ on record, the one lawyer who signs in ONE), although the firm had answered that they are its own; the owner decided the same day. Migration `0093_paper_fields` (pgTAP `0096`, 13 assertions with two firms; `0082` follows it): a paper names the client's data field by field (`case_paper_filled`: `[[NOME]]`, `[[NACIONALIDADE]]`, `[[ESTADO_CIVIL]]`, `[[PROFISSAO]]`, `[[CPF]]`, `[[NASCIMENTO]]`, `[[ENDERECO]]`, `[[CEP]]`, `[[EMAIL]]` and `[[TELEFONE]]`, said "não informado" when the client has none, `[[REPRESENTADOS]]`, the minors the client represents, whose paragraph goes when there are none, and `[[DATA]]`, today written out for a model that keeps its own city; what is not on record keeps the paper from being issued, as before); a change of the marking may replace some words of a paragraph and leave the rest of it as the document writes it (`pieces`, each kept only when its words are in that paragraph: `template_import_proposal(jsonb, jsonb)`, `template_import_body`, `composeModel`), and no change writes a mark the reading put where somebody's data was; the firm's own data (the society, its CNPJ and registration, the lawyers with theirs, the address, the telephone and the e-mail) stay as the firm wrote them once it answered that they are its own, which the marking asks (`paragrafos_do_escritorio`), and the check of a model ONE saves by itself takes the identifiers of those paragraphs as the firm's (`template_import_firm_texts`, `firmTextsOf`, `modelChecks`; never a CPF, and none before the firm answered); the third version of the instructions of `biblioteca.modelo.marcacao` says all this. A lawyer who signs a planned lawsuit and whose registration the firm's own power of attorney does not write (a model with `[[OUTORGADOS]]` names every signer) is said under the lawsuit on the case (`case_power_signers_missing`, the card "Processos"): the owner chose to keep the firm's list over the signers ONE names. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the third version never ran against the real model yet, and a model saved before keeps its marks (the firm sends its document again for the new one); a model ONE saved keeps the firm's lawyers fixed, so a lawyer who joins the firm is in its powers of attorney only once the firm edits the model; the warning reads the registration the model writes (OAB, the state and the number), so a lawyer named only by name is said as not named.
- **A check can be given, a wait on the client has a way forward, and the agency is told before a flight with no agreement (2026-11-04).** The owner's agency test case had its piece never asked: ONE waited for the client's answers to what the account lacked (0090; asked on the link and in the test chat, never answered, and the client had said "Terminei"), while the firm saved the person twice to "validate" what ONE had read of her documents, which no save cleared. Migration `0094_confirm_and_go_on` (pgTAP `0097`, 16 assertions with two firms and two agencies): anybody of the firm confirms what ONE read of a person (`confirm_person_read_fields`: the marks of 0063 go, the timeline says which fields, `person_read_confirmed`), from "Conferi com o documento" beside the line on the case's card of people or by saving the person (`confirmPersonReadFields` after `saveCasePerson`), and with no CPF lookup in the environment the person shows no "CPF não consultado"; a partner or a lawyer decides the case goes on without what the client did not answer (`close_account_gaps`: what was missing is kept as `case_statements.gaps_closed_topics` with when and who, the questions go, so the link and the conversation ask nothing more and an answer is refused as nothing to answer, the account is ready and the case woken; `account_gaps_closed` in the timeline), from "Seguir sem essas respostas" on the card "O que aconteceu", which says where ONE asked (the link, and the WhatsApp when it asked there) and that ONE creates the lawsuits only after the answers; the client's "Terminei" says what they still owe of the account (`FINISH_OWED_KEY`), never "o escritório prepara a sua ação" while ONE waits on them. The agency's home opens with "Voam em breve sem acordo" (`partner-unagreed-card.tsx`, `pax_departing_unagreed`): its adults with a trip departing in the setting's `partners.unagreed_days` (7) that ONE does not follow, who never answered or whose agreement no longer covers the trip (0086), each with the day it registered and the way to their agreement (`#acordo`); and its bell tells who manages the agency and who registered the passenger, once per trip (`pax_unagreed_notices_owed`, the sweep `pax-unagreed-departures`, hourly, `unagreed-notices.ts`). Never who said no or took the agreement back (following them, or approaching them after a problem, is what the legal opinion on soliciting clients weighs), and nothing is read of a flight. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: going on without the answers leaves `[COMPLETAR]` in the piece for each point, for a lawyer; an order of the facts asked again after it (a client who contests it) may ask new questions; the bell is the platform's words, not the agency's.
- **The passenger is interviewed one question at a time (2026-11-04).** The owner's last test case had its piece come back with ten `[COMPLETAR]`: the five questions of what the account lacked went in one message (0092), the client never answered, and the firm went on without them. The owner approved an interview built from the firm's own attendance script (the purchase taken out: the piece speaks of it only where a document says it). The rule is `@flyee/rules/interview` (`entrevista-v1`): the questions of a flight's problem in order (whether the passenger was at the airport, when and how the airline told them, the reason it gave, what it offered, when they arrived, the assistance their wait was owed by art. 27 of Resolution 400, what they spent, what they lost, who travelled, their bag), each left out when ONE knows it (at the airport, a cause on record, an arrival on record, a short wait, who travelled on the trip) or it does not apply (no offer for a delay, none of them for a bag), and when the change was told against the 72 hours of art. 12 (`noticeStanding`). Migration `0095_case_interview` (pgTAP `0098`, 21 assertions with two firms; `0003`, `0012`, `0041` and `0061` follow it): `case_interviews` (one per case of a flight's problem being gathered, who answers, the question asked now, the reminder, the template sent outside the 24 hours, done or closed) and `case_interview_answers` (answered, not known, or left out with why; the passenger's words and what a rule reads of them, `sim_nao` and `quando`; from the conversation, the link, the firm or ONE); while it is open, ONE waits for it before the lawsuits (`case_account_state`: open is `incomplete`, done or closed is `ready`), and a free account's gaps are never asked (`case_statements_interview_gaps`). ONE asks in the conversation it holds, inside the 24 hours (`interview_ask`: `ask` while none waits, `again` once when the reply did not serve, `remind` once after the setting's 4 hours, `back` after ONE answered something the passenger asked meanwhile); the task `atendimento.entrevista.resposta` (Haiku 5.5) only says what the passenger wrote (the answer, its yes or no, its moment, and what else of the questions to come the same message answers); an audio sent while a question waits is read once transcribed (`answerFromStatement`), and a paper sent while the reason or the expenses are asked answers it; outside the 24 hours the firm's template of the new notice kind `case_questions` asks to go on, twice at most (`interview_resume`). It starts right after the passenger's yes, after the next steps (whose step 3 now announces the questions), or from the sweep `case-interviews` for any case owed one; the piece reads it (`case_interview_for_piece`, `entrevista` and `aviso_72h` in its facts) under the third version of `caso.peca.minuta`, which never writes `[COMPLETAR]` for the purchase nor for what the passenger said they do not remember. The app: `lib/cases/interview.ts` (the words and readings), `interview-work.ts` (the backend's half), the section "Perguntas sobre o que aconteceu" of the card "O que aconteceu" (`interview-section.tsx`: each question with its answer and where it came from, why one was left out, the 72 hours, "Responder pelo cliente" for anybody of the firm, "Seguir sem as respostas" for a partner or a lawyer), and one question per screen on the client's link (`c/[token]/client-interview.tsx`). The conversation round trip asks, takes an answer with another it carries, leaves a question out, comes back after a question of the passenger's and is closed by the firm (`apps/web/src/lib/conversations/agent.db.test.ts`). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the interpreter never ran against the real model yet; a child named in the answer about who travelled is not added to the case (the firm reads it); a bag's problem has no interview yet; the template needs the firm's approval at Meta like the other kinds (the test chat approves it at once).
- **The case screen says what happens now, whose turn it is, and follows ONE while it is open (2026-11-05).** The owner felt lost on an open case: what was happening, whether something held it, whose it was to solve, and when ONE was working. An audit of the screen (74 findings, each checked against the code; a simulation of a case from the SIM to the payment found 5 moments where a person is lost and 8 confusing) found the top said the holder by fixed lists and the stage (`journeyHolder`: "Com o ONE" while the package waited for the lawyer to file, "Com o cliente" while a power of attorney returned by gov.br waited for the firm, "A audiência ainda não foi marcada" after it happened), only one wait at a time, two spinners on every case whatever ONE did, and only the pieces and the package read again by themselves. Migration `0096_case_live` (pgTAP `0099`, 19 assertions with two firms; `0085` follows it): `case_pulse` (a mark over every row the case screen reads, and whether ONE is looking at the case now or owes it a look, `claimed_at` and `woken_at` said without granting them), `firm_pulse` (a mark of what the home shows the person who asks: `firm_today`, `firm_pending`, the journey and the latest cases moved), and a decision is the judgment only when it is (`publications.judgment`, `set_publication_judgment`, `firm_case_journey` replaced): the app reads the publication's type and words when a person triages it as a decision (`@flyee/rules/judgment`, `sentenca-v1`) and keeps what they say clearly; when they say nothing clear the person is asked, on the publication's card and at the top of its case (the owner's rule of that day: ask only when it is not clear); decisions triaged before are judgments when the DJEN typed them "Sentença" or "Acórdão". The app: the top of the case ("Andamento do caso", `journey-card.tsx` over `caseNow` of `lib/cases/now.ts`, pure and tested) says who has the case, what ONE is doing now (each with since when, a late one said as late), what holds the case with whose turn each thing is (the firm and the function that acts, with the way to it for who may; the client; ONE; the platform, "o escritório não precisa fazer nada") and what ONE did a moment ago, each line one click from its card; the steps read the case's work (the signature while a paper waits to be signed or confirmed, the filing once a package is built or ready) and lead to their cards; on a phone, who has the case comes before the steps. One table says the owner of every wait (`WAIT_PLACE` of `lib/cases/advance.ts`), read by the top and by the center of what waits (a client's wait is no longer under "O ONE precisa de você"). The case and `/inicio` follow the database by themselves (`useLiveRead` of `components/product/use-live-read.ts`, `readCasePulse` and `readHomePulse` of `lib/cases/pulse-actions.ts`): every 4 seconds while ONE works there or right after the person acted, every 20 otherwise, never on a hidden tab, reading the page whole only when the mark changed, and saying when they last knew they were current; the cards' own polling is gone. Each card says on its head, with the icon of what it is, whether something there asks the firm, waits for the client, failed or is being done by ONE ("O ONE está conferindo"), opens when something comes to ask the firm there and closes once it is done unless the person opened it (`CollapsibleCard` marks and looks, tested with the app's first component tests, `collapsible-card.test.tsx`; vitest now compiles JSX). The documents ONE checks are its work, never "para conferir", and deciding one before it finishes says what is lost; "O que ainda falta" became lines of the top; the cards go in the order of the way; "Mudar etapa" moved to the header's quick actions and says what each stage switches off, confirmed in an `OutcomeDialog`; taking a person out, ending a link, removing a planned lawsuit, cancelling a package and removing a complaint are confirmed with their consequences; the cards' words say what ONE does by itself (it creates the lawsuits, writes and approves the piece as the firm configured, sends the power of attorney, asks for the package) and call ONE by one name. **The 72-hour notice** (the airline told of the change 72 hours or more before, Resolução ANAC 400, art. 12): for now ONE only says it on the case and goes on, so the tests do not stop (the owner, 2026-11-05); before production it becomes a block, the case not opened under that condition or waiting for a lawyer to release it, which the owner decides later. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the pulse of a case hashes every row of about thirty tables on each read (cheap at the pilot's size, an index per table read by the case; a firm's home runs its four reads on each pulse); the conversation on WhatsApp is not on the case yet (the top says what ONE asks there, not each message); a document ONE accepted or refused alone is not yet marked as ONE's on its card; the screens were checked by types, unit and component tests and the database's, not walked signed in.
- **A piece written before the client's account is written again by ONE, and the purchase is asked nowhere (2026-11-05).** The owner's agency test case looked stalled: it was opened before the interview (0095), ONE created its lawsuit and asked for its piece the same night with no account, and the piece came back with eight `[COMPLETAR]`; the firm registered the account the next day, the order of the facts of 0090 asked the client four questions on the link (the purchase among them, which the owner had taken out of the interview), and the card "O que aconteceu" said ONE created the lawsuits only after the answers, which existed already, while the piece already written never read the account. Migration `0097_piece_redone_with_account` (pgTAP `0100`, 12 assertions with two firms; `0093` follows it): the purchase leaves the points of an account (`statements.account_topics`), the instructions of `caso.relato.cronologia` (a new version with no `compra`, saying never to ask it) and the accounts that still asked it, its question with it; and ONE writes a piece again in the look of the case it carries on (`advance_redo_pieces`, called by `advanceClaimedCase` before the steps): a piece the AI wrote that still leaves something to complete (`[COMPLETAR`), that nobody of the firm edited, sent back or had rewritten, of a lawsuit still planned, of a case that did not end, whose latest version is older than the account's last change (`case_account_changed_at`: its order of the facts approved or confirmed, its interview done or closed, or the firm going on without what was missing), as the firm lets ONE start pieces (`start_piece`); the piece before is given up (what waited of it leaves the queue) and a new first text is asked of the AI from the same model, which reads the account (`draft_redone` in the timeline, as ONE's); each change of the account writes it once, never the same change twice, and the rounds of the account (`max_rounds`) bound how many times. The sweep hands such a case to the worker (`0098_piece_redo_due`, pgTAP `0100` follows it): which pieces are to be written again is one reading (`case_pieces_to_redo`), which the sweep asks through `case_advance_steps_due` and the worker writes from, since the sweep sets aside, without the worker ever looking at it, a case nothing due is known for (the owner's case, woken once 0097 was in, was set aside fifteen seconds later); 0098 wakes the cases that have such a piece. The card "O que aconteceu" asks for the answers and offers "Seguir sem essas respostas" only before the lawsuits; once they exist it says the answers hold nothing and go to the piece when they come (`case-statement-gaps-piece`). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: each rewriting spends a run of the model on the whole piece; a piece a lawyer touched keeps its `[COMPLETAR]` for the lawyer, who asks the AI for an adjustment or a new piece; a piece the AI wrote and ONE approved (nothing left to complete) is never written again, whatever the account says later.
- **Each decision is taken where it belongs, and "Aprovações" left the main menu (2026-11-05).** The owner reached a piece's text in `/aprovacoes` from a line of the home and asked whether a list of its own made sense for what is part of a case: the piece could not even be approved there (only on its case, as the text a lawyer just read). Every answer of the AI that waits for a person is now one line of the home and of the center of what waits, leading to where it is decided (`readWaitingDecisions` of `lib/ai/decisions-read.ts`, `decisionItems` and `decisionHref` of `lib/ai/decisions.ts`, `withDecisions` of `lib/firm/pending.ts`): the text of a piece to its case's "Peças", the check of a document to its "Documentos", the order of the facts to "O que aconteceu", where it is now approved, corrected, sent back or refused in place (the same `ApprovalCard`, drawn `onCase`, with the second factor asked in the card; `lib/ai/pending-approvals.ts` reads what each answer is about for the case and the list), and ONE's answer to a passenger to its conversation. `/aprovacoes` is the list of every decision the firm left to a person ("Decisões que esperam uma pessoa"), for whoever reviews many in a row, reached from the center's "Ver todas as decisões", out of the main menu and of a module's area, and never holds the text of a piece. No migration.
- **The piece leaves to complete only what its claim cannot stand without, and ONE asks the client what it lacks (2026-11-05).** The piece ONE wrote again for the owner's case still had six `[COMPLETAR]`, each a fact only the client knew, and a lawyer was the only way out; the owner approved that a lawyer complete a piece as rarely as can be. Migration `0099_piece_asks_what_it_lacks` (pgTAP `0101`, 13 assertions with two firms). **What a piece cannot stand without** is data (`drafts.essentials` by kind of problem: the flight and, for a passenger late or stranded, when they arrived; for a bag, what happened to it), handed to the piece as `essenciais` (`piece_essentials`, `pieceEssentials` in `drafts-work.ts`); the fourth version of `caso.peca.minuta` writes a `[COMPLETAR]` only for an essential fact, once, writes any other fact at the detail the client gave it or leaves it out (never saying as a fact what the client did not say, such as no assistance or no offer), takes out a claim no fact supports (material damages with no expense of a known amount), says each in its notes, and says what each `[COMPLETAR]` asks (`pendencias`, in the interview's codes, kept in the run's answer). **ONE asks the client what the piece lacks**: a piece the AI wrote that still has a `[COMPLETAR]` and that no lawyer edited, sent back or had rewritten, of a lawsuit still planned, as the firm lets ONE start pieces, has the interview of 0095 opened with only those questions (`case_interviews.scope`, `piece_interview_scope`; a piece written before says nothing, and the account's gaps stand for it, else every question of the problem), or the interview done before opened again with them; a question answered or said not known is never asked again, and an interview the firm closed is never opened again. The interview is opened right after the piece's citations were read (`queueInterview` in `drafts-work.ts`) and by the sweep `case-interviews` (`interviews_to_start`, `interview_start` redefined), asked one question at a time in the conversation ONE holds, on the link, or by the firm's template outside the 24 hours in any stage before the court (`interview_resumes_owed`); its first question says the firm needs that many more answers to finish the action. While it is open no piece is written again (`case_pieces_to_redo`); an account changes when an answer comes, not when an interview ends (`case_account_changed_at`), so once the client answered ONE writes the piece again once (0097), and a firm that goes on without the client has nothing written again for nothing. The top of the case says the piece waits for the client (`case-now-draft-asking-chat`, `-link`) while ONE asks, and for a lawyer otherwise. **Only of a piece written after the client's latest answer** (`0100_piece_rounds_follow_the_piece`, pgTAP `0101` covers it, 15 assertions): the owner's case had a second round of seven questions open thirty seconds after the first round's answers, read from the piece written before them (which ONE was about to write again) and, saying nothing of what it lacked, falling back to every question of the problem (the airport the client had said they never went to, a bag in a cancellation). Which pieces ONE carries is one reading (`case_pieces_one_writes`), read by the rewriting (`case_pieces_to_redo`), the questions (`piece_interview_scope`: nothing is asked of a piece older than the latest answer, ONE writes it again first; a piece written before 0099 is asked only what the account said was missing, never every question) and the case screen (`case_drafts_one_redo`: `asking` or `rewriting`, so the card "Peças" says "O ONE pergunta ao cliente" or "O ONE escreve de novo" with no review, approval or turn-down offered meanwhile, and the top of the case says it as ONE's work); a round opened of such a piece was ended (`interview_withdrawn`). The interview's section keeps every answer of a round before in sight and counts only the round asked now (`interviewRows`, `interviewCounts`), says after the lawsuits that the answers go to the piece, and the timeline says a round about what a piece lacks in its own words (`eventKeyOf`). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a piece that left a non-essential fact out (the assistance, the expenses, the commitment lost) is not asked for later, so a firm that wants it in the piece answers for the client or a lawyer writes it; the fourth version never ran against the real model yet; a fact under `outro` or the flight is a lawyer's; a lawyer who edits the piece while the client is being asked keeps the piece, and the answers stay on the card for them.
- **ONE files the lawsuit itself, through the court's own machine channel (2026-11-05).** The owner named the filing one of the crucial steps to automate (forgetting, delays, a court out of the air, a person's difficulty): with the package built and signed, a lawyer still logged in to the court's system, filled the lawsuit, attached each file, sent it and brought the receipt back. A robot on the court's screen is not built: the TJGO writes that a lawyer's password and key are "de uso pessoal e intransferível" (Resolução TJGO 59/2016, art. 11), and by the owner's decision of 2026-10-15 nothing is automated over such a rule. The channel is the CNJ's MNI (`entregarManifestacaoProcessual`, 2.2.2), which the Projudi of TJGO exposes and which authenticates the platform by the court's grant, never a lawyer's login; until a court grants it, a test case is filed in the test court of 0061, which stands where the court stands. `packages/courts` has the channel's contract (`FilingChannel`, `LawsuitFiling`, `FilingOutcome` in `types.ts`), the real client (`@flyee/courts/providers/mni`: the SOAP envelope built from the MNI's WSDL and XSD as read on 2026-10-14, the answer read, and every transport outcome sorted so nothing ambiguous is ever taken as not sent), a fake and its contract test; `@flyee/documents/cms` gives the signer's certificate in PEM beside a `.p7s`. Migration `0101_filing_by_one` (pgTAP `0102`, 14 assertions with two firms; `0003`, `0009`, `0012` and `0045` follow it, and `discard_file` keeps a receipt a submission names): `court_filing_channels` (each court's channel as data, the operator's: its address, whether the court's homologation confirmed it, whether ONE files through it, and the court's codes: the class of each kind of lawsuit, the type of each document, the subject of each problem, and by comarca the locality, the competence and the judging body; seeded with the TJGO's Projudi, off and unconfirmed, with the national codes of the TPU and no comarca's), the decision `file_lawsuit` (ONE files alone by default; at approval a lawyer of the lawsuit asks for each filing), and `filing_submissions` (one per package, reserved, claimed and then sent: `claim_filing_submissions`, `filing_submission_to_send`, `filing_submission_sending`, `record_filing_submission`; a filing that went and whose answer nobody could read is `unknown`, never sent again until a lawyer says the court's system does not have it, `retry_filing_submission`; a court out of reach is tried again after a growing wait, the setting `filing_submit`; one that refuses says why; filed, the lawsuit takes its number and receipt as a receipt a lawyer attaches files it (0027), the receipt kept under the purpose `filing_receipt`, or a lawyer attaches the number when the court gave none). What keeps ONE from filing a package is one reading (`filing_submission_blocker`: the package not ready or outdated, the lawsuit blocked, no channel, no lawyer of the lawsuit with a registration at the bar, no company on the case, the court's codes of its comarca or of a document's kind missing, the files not signed where the court takes them signed, or the firm keeping the filing to a lawyer who has not asked), which the case screen reads (`case_filing_by_one`). The app: `lib/cases/filing-request.ts` (pure: the lawsuit as the court's channel takes it, a registration at the bar as the MNI writes it, nothing invented and no code guessed: the test court alone takes placeholders), `lib/courts/filing-channel.ts` (the test court's channel, and the MNI with the credential the court grants, which is an entity's: the pilot firm's in the server's environment, `MNI_<COURT>_MANIFESTANTE` and `MNI_<COURT>_SENHA`, each firm's in its vault once more than one files through ONE), `lib/cases/filing-submit-work.ts` (the worker, right after a package is ready or signed and in the sweep `filing-submissions`), `requestFilingByOne` and `retryFilingByOne` in `lib/cases/filing-actions.ts`, the block "O ONE protocola" of the card "Protocolo" (`casos/[id]/components/filing-by-one.tsx`: about to go, going, tried again at a time, filed with the protocol and that ONE may err, refused with the court's reason, failed, sent with no answer, and the lawyer's way to ask or send again; the lawyer's own steps step back while ONE files), the line of the filing at the top of the case as ONE's work, and the decision in `/settings/autonomia` among a lawyer's own acts. The round trip of a package (`apps/web/src/lib/cases/filing.db.test.ts`) leaves a real lawsuit to the lawyer while the court gives ONE no channel and has ONE file a test case in the test court, its number, receipt and timeline as a lawyer's receipt would leave them. What is to be obtained or confirmed before the simulator gives way to the court is listed in `packages/courts/README.md` ("Para trocar o simulador pelo tribunal real"). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: nothing ran against a court yet; the MNI's access, its authentication (the firm's or the platform's credential, or an ICP-Brasil certificate as a TLS client, which this build does not carry yet), the form the court takes the signature in, the encoding of the hash, how the answer carries the new lawsuit's number, and every local code of the TJGO are seen only in the court's homologation; whether the court takes an initial petition from a law firm by the MNI at all is the first thing to ask; a filing refused or failed is told on the case, in its timeline and, since 0102, in the center of what waits.
- **What ONE's filing needs of a person is asked ahead, and what came back of a filing waits where the firm looks first (2026-11-05).** The owner asked when the accesses ONE needs to file are set up or asked, and whether anything keeps ONE from stalling at the filing: a court that takes the files signed (the Projudi of TJGO) waited, once the package was ready, for a lawyer of the lawsuit to authorize ONE's signature in the cloud, an authorization that lasts seven days at most (the PSC's own limit) and so runs out between one lawsuit and the next; and a filing ONE sent that came back was said only on its case. Migration `0102_filing_readiness` (pgTAP `0103`, 9 assertions with two firms; not yet applied to the development project): `lawsuits_awaiting_signature` (internal: the planned lawsuits of a firm's open cases, never a test case, whose court takes the files signed, whose package is not signed yet, and that no lawyer of them covers with an authorization in force for the setting's hours ahead, `pending.signature_ahead_hours`, 24: asked from the moment ONE creates the lawsuit), `firm_filing_attention` (anybody of the firm: each partner or lawyer such lawsuits wait on, with no CPF declared, no authorization, or one that ends within those hours, with when, how many lawsuits and cases and the first; and each filing ONE sent that came back to a person, refused, failed, sent with no answer, or taken with no number, the oldest first) and `case_signature_ahead` (the same for one case, with whether the person reading signs each lawsuit and declared their CPF). The app, only where a provider of the cloud signature can be used here (`cloudSigningOffered`; none in production yet): the center of what waits and the home's list of what the firm must act on say each lawyer's need under "O ONE precisa de você" (a lawyer reads their own; a partner, or who manages the firm, the team's), and each filing that came back to who files, one click from where it is done (`lib/firm/filing-attention.ts`, pure and tested, which `pendingGroups` takes); the team's card "Assinatura em nuvem" opens by itself, says how many of the lawyer's lawsuits wait on their authorization and, when it ends within the hours ahead, makes "Renovar a autorização" its main button, the last provider already chosen; the card "Protocolo" says on each planned lawsuit, before its package exists, that its files go signed in the cloud and no lawyer of it covers them yet, with "Autorizar a assinatura em nuvem" for one who signs it (the way back to the case kept); and the setup of who signs, the firm's and a person's own, asks the lawyer's CPF beside the registration at the bar, optional (`configurar/setup-cpf.tsx`: the authorization itself is asked when the first lawsuit needs it). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: nothing of it shows until the platform has a provider of the cloud signature (`PSC_<SLUG>_CLIENT_ID`), so in production a package of the Projudi still waits for its files signed with the court's signer by hand; a lawsuit with no lawyer named waits for a signer, which the case already says; whether the court gives ONE its machine channel (the MNI) is still said only once the package is ready, and the platform's own readiness to file (the court's grant, its codes) has no operator's panel yet.
- **A test case's client is told at once, and the top of a case says a publication that waits may be the hearing (2026-11-05).** The owner marked a conciliation hearing in the test court and asked where the parties learn of it and how they attend: the test court marks it about ten minutes ahead, while a notice of a hearing waits fifteen minutes after it was written and the reminder of the day goes up to an hour before it, so the client of the test chat was told nothing; and the top of the case said the hearing was not marked while the publication that marked it waited for triage. Migration `0103_test_notices_at_once` (pgTAP `0104`, 7 assertions with two firms; not yet applied to the development project) redefines `notice_candidates` (0055): in a test case (`cases.is_test`) the reminders of a hearing and the word that it changed go until the hearing itself, and they, the word on the lawsuit, the warning against the scam and the word that the lawsuit was filed wait no minutes after what they tell; a real case is told as before, and a refused paper and the word that everything arrived still wait their minutes in a test case. The firm's notices are reserved and sent right after a hearing is written (by a person, or by ONE from its publication), a payment triaged or a lawsuit filed (`settleFirmNotices` in `lib/notices/work.ts`), not only at the next sweep. With no hearing set and none triaged before, the top of the case says a publication that waits for triage may be the hearing the court marked, and that ONE registers it and tells the client once a person triages it as Audiência (`case-now-publications-untriaged-hearing`), instead of saying the hearing was not marked. How the parties learn of a hearing and attend it is as before: the court intimates the lawyers through the DJEN, a person triages the publication, ONE reads and records the hearing (0040), the client is told on WhatsApp (a month, a week, the day before and the day itself, from 8 to 20) and reads it on their link with how to be there (the place, or the link of a video hearing), and the airline is summoned by the court. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the triage of a hearing is still a person's (the AI's triage of a hearing stays in the shadow, 0035); a hearing the test court marks after 19:45 goes to nine in the morning of the next weekday.
- **ONE triages the publications by itself, the lawyer confirms being aware, and a case's publications are worked on the case (2026-11-05).** The owner asked why ONE could not triage a publication that already says what it is and then ask the lawyer to confirm being aware until they do, that the step be clear on the case, where everything of a case is, and whether `/publicacoes` is still needed. A deadline, a hearing, a decision and a payment never left the AI's shadow (0035), so a hearing the court marked waited for a person to say it was one. Migration `0104_one_triages_publications` (pgTAP `0105`, 11 assertions with two firms; `0045` follows it; not yet applied to the development project): the decision `publication_triage` (the task `publicacao.triagem`, ONE's by default, in `/settings/autonomia`); the second version of the task's instructions says how sure the AI is (`certeza`); where the firm lets ONE, an answer it is sure of, of a whole text nobody triaged, is the triage (`record_publication_triage` redefined), anything else is left to a person, and an answer with no certainty goes 0035's way; a triage ONE made owes a person's awareness (`publications.ack_owed`, `acked_by`, `acked_at`), given by the lawyer it was addressed to or anybody who triages (`acknowledge_publication`, audited, in the timeline) or by triaging it themselves (`triage_publication` redefined); `firm_publication_acks` (with who it waits on, and late after the setting's 4 hours, `publications.ack_late_hours`), `publication_acks_owed` (the bell's) and `publication_triage_states` (ONE triaging one, or leaving it to a person) are what the screens read; `case_pulse` and `firm_pulse` follow the awareness. Tried with the real model (Haiku 5.5) on invented texts of the test court: a hearing, a term, a sentence, a payment and an archiving each in its category and sure, a moved hearing a hearing, and a hearing beside a term not sure, which a person triages. The app: ONE triages right after a capture of the DJEN and a step of the test court (`queuePublicationTriage`), then reads the deadline or the hearing, rings the bell of the awareness and sends the client's notices (`triage-work.ts`); the bell tells the lawyer once and again each day once late (`lib/publications/ack-notices.ts`, the sweep `publication-acks`); the center of what waits and the home list the awareness owed, red once late (`lib/firm/publication-acks.ts`); the case has the card "Publicações" (`casos/[id]/components/publications-card.tsx`, the very card of `/publicacoes`), its head saying whether ONE is triaging or the firm owes its awareness, and its top says what ONE is triaging, what it left to a person and whose awareness is owed; `/publicacoes` lists ONE's triage that waits under "Conferir". `/publicacoes` stays as the firm's list: the publications of lawsuits with no case in ONE (the DJEN reads every one addressed to the firm's lawyers), the DJEN reading's health, the search by number and working through the volume a mass-litigation firm receives; a case's own publications are worked on the case. Migration `0105_triage_again` (pgTAP `0106`, 4 assertions with two firms) puts back in front of ONE's triage what the AI had answered in the shadow before 0104 reached a project (an answer with no word on certainty is never asked again, so the owner's test court hearing waited for a person): nobody's triage yet, a whole text in force, captured within the days ONE reads, in a firm that lets ONE triage (`reopen_unsure_triage`, run once). The card of a publication says when ONE is triaging it or left it to a person, and the head of the card "Publicações" says "Falta triar" or "Falta a ciência" by what waits. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a term or a hearing ONE triaged wrong acts until the lawyer corrects it (what ONE recorded from it stays, said on its card); the instructions never saw the text of a real court.
- **A passenger whose trip became a case stays the agency's for their next trips (2026-11-05).** The owner asked what happens when a passenger who had a case travels again: the handover closed the agency's whole record (`handed_over_at`), so the agency could register a next trip but not ask the passenger's agreement to it, send its documents nor update the record, the passenger could not agree on the link, ONE never followed the flight, and the record went 30 days after the handover. "Com o escritório" is now of the trip that became a case, never of the person (migration `0106_returning_passenger`, pgTAP `0107`, 13 assertions with two firms and two agencies; not yet applied to the development project): the agency keeps the record up to date, sends documents, registers trips and sends the link for each next trip (`pax_save_traveler`, `pax_reserve_document`, `pax_issue_confirm_link`, `pax_api_issue_link`, `pax_link_answer`, `pax_link_reserve_upload` and `pax_apply_reading` no longer refuse a passenger handed over); a next trip nobody agreed to is an agreement owed again (`pax_reagree`, `trip_waiting`), listed and counted as one (`pax_travelers_page`, `pax_overview`) and said to the agency before it flies (`pax_unagreed_departures`), and the link asks for it (`pax_link_open` is `open` while a trip of theirs other than the case is ahead, waiting for the agreement or followed, and `handed_over` once none is, so a passenger who just agreed to a next trip reads the agreement, never that their data went to the firm); once the passenger agrees ONE follows it, and a next trip that becomes a case goes to the same client of the firm (`pax_client_of`, by the CPF) with the documents sent since (`pax_hand_over` hands each document once). The trip that became the case takes nothing more (a document of it is refused, `trip_with_firm`, from the agency's screen, its API and the passenger's link, `pax_attach_trip_documents` included), is never the list's next departure nor a trip flying soon, and keeps its state; each document says whether it went to the firm (`handed`), which the agency no longer opens; the record is kept while a later trip of theirs is ahead or recent (`pax_retention_sweep`); and what the agency changes never reaches what the firm received. The app: the passenger's page has every card open again, its banner saying a trip of theirs is the firm's and that a change here does not reach the firm; the trip card withdraws the actions of the case trip only ("Caso no escritório"); a document that went with the case says so (`pax-document-handed`); the list and the agency's home read one rule (`passengerStanding` of `agency/journey.ts`: an agreement owed again first, "Com o escritório" only with nothing ahead, "Caso no escritório" beside a next trip); and the passenger's link offers nothing of the case trip. The round trip of the agency (`agency.db.test.ts`) saves the record after the handover, finds the firm's client unchanged and the case trip refusing a document. `0104`'s hearing minutes ahead no longer crosses midnight in Brasília (it failed between 23:50 and 24:00). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a next case receives only the documents the agency sent after the first, so its client is asked again for an identity the firm already has from the first case (the reuse of the accepted documents waits for the owner); the firm's case does not say yet that its client has other cases; a WhatsApp the agency changes asks the agreement again even with no trip ahead, as for any passenger.
- **What follows a person's triage also follows ONE's (2026-11-07), the first step of the plan of the controladoria, the outcome of the cases and the firm's finance** (the Claude Doc "ONE · Plano: controladoria, resultado dos casos e financeiro do escritório", https://claude.ai/artifact/3RBM3ZBZ4dTv6ioYBKmW76, agreed by the owner the same day; the Casos session's case journey plan shares pieces with it, each with one owner). Migration `0107_one_triage_follow_ups` (pgTAP `0108`, 11 assertions with two firms):
  - A payment ONE triaged (`ai_applied`) warns the client against the scam, as one a person triaged does (`notice_candidates` redefined from 0103).
  - A decision ONE triaged keeps what the rule of the judgment (`sentenca-v1`) reads of its type and words. The backend writes it right after ONE's triage (`judgeTriagedDecision` in `lib/publications/triage-work.ts`), through `record_publication_judgment_rule`, which only the service role may call. It writes only for a decision ONE triaged that nobody said a word on, and never over a word somebody said.
  - The decisions ONE triaged before take what their DJEN type says, as 0096 did.
  - The capture's round trip (`capture.db.test.ts`) has ONE triage a judgment and an unclear decision through the real worker.
- **Phase 0 of the same plan, the screen pattern after ADVBox, is under way (2026-11-07).** The owner approved the drawings of the canvas "ONE · Padrão de tela" (https://claude.ai/artifact/998EppHGSznFY9B7YGXfmr) the same day, with one change: the menu as ADVBox's thin rail of icons. Built so far:
  - The rail (`MenuType.Minimal` in `src/config.ts`): 20px icons in 40px buttons (`components/layout/menu/primary-item.tsx`), each named in a tooltip. On a phone the menu that opens is the list with the names (`layout-context.tsx`).
  - The search at the top (`components/layout/search/global-search.tsx`, the pure rules in `lib/search/global-search.ts`): the firm's cases by the client's name, a CPF, a lawsuit's number or the case's own number, the ended ones included, through `listFirmCases`. It opens with "/" or Ctrl+K and is a button that opens it over the page on a phone. It is not shown on the setups, and phase 9 links it to people, conversations and leads.
  - The cards' and papers' corners are `rounded-xl` (12px), and the header's and the menu's `rounded-*-2xl`, by the owner's own edits of the same day (`style/surfaces/card.css`, `paper.css`). VS Code's CSS formatter writes a space before Tailwind's important modifier (`shadow-sm !`), which makes Tailwind refuse the stylesheet; `npm run check:mui-classes`, in the pre-commit and in `ci:verify`, refuses it.
  - The menu's states float (`CornerTag`, `components/product/corner-tag.tsx`): "Em breve" and the lock of a module over the item's top edge at its right end (2026-11-08), so the names keep one line, in a submenu of 264px and a phone's list of 300px. No width keeps a submenu open, for any kind of user, the operator's "Admin" included (the owner, 2026-11-08; it covered the left of every page of "Módulos" from `xl` on): it opens over the page on its icon and closes on the same icon, on a click away and on following any of its links, and it marks the page the person is on when opened again. A group's own page is the subtle link beside its submenu's title (`OverviewLink` in `components/layout/menu/secondary-item.tsx`, `MenuItem.overviewLabel`): "Todos" beside "Módulos" and "Visão geral" beside "Admin", never an item of the submenu.
  - Every registration opens in the side panel (`SidePanel`, `components/product/side-panel.tsx`: the page behind under a veil, 540px wide and the whole screen on a phone, the head with the icon and one line of what happens, the buttons kept at the foot, the focus on the first field once it is in place). The forms of a deadline and a hearing are panels (`components/deadlines/deadline-panel.tsx`, `components/hearings/hearing-panel.tsx`, the dialogs they were renamed), from every card that opened them.
  - "Adicionar" at the top (`components/layout/add/`): by group, what this person may add (today "Casos": Novo prazo and Nova audiência, to who signs or runs the deadlines), opening its panel wherever the person is, the case searched as the top searches (`useCaseSearch`, shared with the search) and then the lawsuit the court has, taken by itself when the case has one (`lib/add/actions.ts`: `readAddContext`, `readCaseLawsuits`); once saved, an `OutcomeDialog` says what now holds with the way to the case. Not shown on the setups (`isSetupPath`), to a partner's people, nor before the second factor. The finance's entries and "Nova pessoa" join it with their phases.
  - `/casos` is the first list in the pattern (migration `0108_case_list_bar`, pgTAP `0109`, 16 assertions with two firms; `0036` follows it). `firm_cases` takes the order (`target_sort`: the latest change, the oldest change, the newest case, the oldest case) and the list's own filters, the kind of problem and the airline (`target_incident`, `target_airline`), which narrow every count; three more views count the month in the days of Brasília (`novos-mes`, `ajuizados-mes`, `encerrados-mes`); and its answer carries `numbers`, the four cards' numbers each beside the same days of the month before (the cases going at the end of the same day; opened, filed and ended from the first to the same day), so a card never compares a few days with a whole month. The app: `NumberCard` (`components/product/number-card.tsx`, the arithmetic in `lib/numbers/compare.ts`, the cards in `lib/cases/numbers.ts`) and `ListBar` (`components/product/list-bar.tsx`: the ready views with their counts, the search, "Filtrar", "Ordenar", "Exportar" only where a list exports, "Mais ações", the filters in force as chips); `DataTable` takes `hideToolbar` under such a bar. Opening a case by hand and importing the portfolio left the page's head for "Mais ações". A state badge (`StatusChip`) is no tap target any longer, so it keeps its height on a phone (`style/inputs/button.css`). "Esperando o escritório", "Parados", the views of what moved and "Exportar" belong to phase 1, the controladoria.
  - `/protocolo` and `/publicacoes` have the same bar (no migration). The queue of filing packages is read whole (at most `QUEUE_SHOWN`), so its views, its search (a client, a case's number, a comarca or a court, words in any order and without accents), its filters by court and system and its order (the package asked for the longest first, or the latest) are decided in the browser (`lib/cases/filing-queue.ts`, pure and tested). Its four cards count the packages ready to file, being built, held by something of their case and not built, each the filter of the list; a package something holds is never said ready, nor counted so (`QueuedFiling.requestedAt` orders it). `?ver=prontos` opens the ready view, where the home's tile of packages to file now leads. Publicações's four tiles are number cards opening the same lists as before. The search by a lawsuit's number moved into the bar (`ListBar` `search.numeric`): it asks the server only from 7 digits on, says under the bar what it waits for, and the newest answer wins. "Ler o DJEN agora" moved from the page's head to "Mais ações". The bar's common words are the list-wide keys `list-views-title`, `list-bar-search`, `list-bar-filter`, `list-bar-filter-clear`, `list-bar-sort` and `list-bar-more`, and a field of the bar has no margin under it (the theme's, for helper text, put the search 8px above the buttons).
  - While ONE works on a case, the case screen waits (no migration). `CaseAtWork` (`casos/[id]/components/case-at-work.tsx`) reads what the top says ONE is doing now (`caseNow().working`) and, for ONE's own work only (`holdingWork` of `lib/cases/one-at-work.ts`: never a look ONE takes soon, a work said late, nor a filing ONE tries again later at the court, `NowItem.outside`), holds the screen with `AtWorkNotice` (`components/product/at-work-notice.tsx`), drawn as the canvas: a turning ring with ONE's mark, the steps (what ONE recorded since the notice opened, what it is doing with how long, and what always follows: a piece's citations after its text) and when it started; the case follows the database and is read again when the work ends, and the notice closes. From two minutes on, counted from the work's own start (or when the tab first saw it), it says ONE is taking longer than usual, with "Continuar sem esperar" (focused) and "Esperar mais"; the tab remembers in `sessionStorage` what the person went on without, so the notice opens again only for another work, and it waits while the person types in a field or has another window open. Nothing closes it otherwise, Escape included.
  - A page loads in its shape (no migration): `PageSkeleton` (`components/product/page-skeleton.tsx`: `list` with its cards of numbers, bar and rows; `record`; `home`; `split`; `setup`; `cards`) in a `loading.tsx` of each main route, the group's own (`(dashboard)/loading.tsx`) catching the rest, and the shell's fallback (`dashboard-shell.tsx`) is the same skeleton: the logged area opened every page on one spinner fixed over the whole screen, menu included (`loading-screen.tsx`, kept for `/auth`). A boundary under the logged area's layout leaves the layout's gate as it was; a page's `notFound()` or `redirect()` there was already inside the shell's own boundary.
  - Every registration is managed the same way (no migration), written in `.claude/rules/app-ux.md` and the `product-screen` skill: the list with the bar and its "Novo" at the right (`ListBar` `create`: one kind is one button, several a menu, the one that starts from a document first; none on a list of what ONE makes by itself); creating and editing in `SidePanel`, the document first where there is one, a row opening its record by its name and the rest in `QuickActions`; what another record uses archived with its reason and able to come back, and only what nothing uses deleted, both after a `ConfirmActionDialog` that says what it does and what stays; every change audited, the finance's registrations with the second factor; import and export by spreadsheet where it makes sense. `/biblioteca` is the first in it: four `NumberCard`s (faltam na biblioteca, modelos, fontes conferidas, enviados ao ONE), the views (`lib/library/list.ts`, pure and tested: the library is read whole, so views, counts, search, the filter by what a model writes and the order are decided in the browser), "Novo" (modelo a partir de um documento, modelo escrito aqui, fonte conferida), the model in `SidePanel` `wide` (`biblioteca/template-panel.tsx`, the dialog it was, also in the setup's step of the papers), a source in the panel and retired with its reason, back from "Fontes retiradas", and a model removed after the confirmation that says whether ONE stops writing from it. On the way: a menu's action (`QuickActions`, the bar's "Novo") runs once the menu finished closing, and `SidePanel` puts the focus on its first editable field also when it is created already open (MUI runs no entrance then, so the focus stayed on the panel's frame in every panel opened from a card).
  - The team (`/settings/team`) and the partners (`/settings/parceiros`) are in the pattern (no migration): cards of numbers for who manages the firm, the views (`settings/team/team-list.ts`, `lib/partners/partners-list.ts`, pure and tested), the search, "Convidar pessoa" and "Convidar agência" as the "Novo" of each, the invitations and a person's function in `SidePanel` (`settings/team/components/invite-panel.tsx`, `team-roster.tsx`; `components/partners/partner-invite-panel.tsx`, which the module's setup, the agency's setup and its team use too), the rest of a row in `QuickActions`, and an ended partnership kept with its reason in a view of its own. A field in a side panel takes no `autoFocus` (the drawer blurred it as it opened, and the invitation opened saying the e-mail was missing), and a choice starts on the option chosen.
  - `/atendimento` is in the pattern (migration `0109_inbox_list`, pgTAP `0110`, 19 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project on 2026-11-08, about an hour after its code was deployed: in between, `/atendimento` of production answered its error, which is why a migration goes to the development project before the push of the code that reads it). It read only the 60 latest conversations, so a view or a search could look only among them: `firm_inbox` reads a view over every conversation of the firm (or of one module, as 0078), searched by the name the firm or WhatsApp has, the name of somebody of the firm with that number (with or without its ninth digit) or the number's digits, in the order asked (the latest message or the oldest first), with the list's total and every view's count (todas, esperando and fechando by `firm_today`'s rule, chamou, resposta, nao-lidas, com-pessoa, falhou, pararam). The page reads it (`lib/whatsapp/inbox-read.ts`, which the search's server action `searchInbox` shares, `lib/whatsapp/inbox-actions.ts`; the pure rules in `lib/whatsapp/inbox-list.ts`); the view and the order are in the address (`?ver=`, `?ordem=antigas`), the search in the request's body. Four `NumberCard`s (janela fechando, aguardando resposta, o ONE chamou uma pessoa, respostas do ONE para enviar), the bar with the views, the search and "Ordenar", no "Novo"; the list says when it shows fewer than its view holds; the home's tiles and the center of what waits lead to their views.
