The conversation's head is a `CardTitle` with the window in its chip and the person's other cases, a case opened by hand and the request to stop in `QuickActions`. `CardTitle` wraps its chip to the next line when it would squeeze the title (on a phone, a name became a column of syllables). With it phase 0 is built: every screen of today is in the pattern.
  - Found and not fixed: the theme provider reads the stored mode (`react-use`'s `useLocalStorage`) in its first render in the browser, so a page loaded after another in the same tab may hydrate with ids that differ from the server's (seen in development as "A tree hydrated but some attributes... didn't match", on the ids of fields). It is at the root of every page and was there before phase 0.
- **Phase 1 of the same plan, the controladoria, first cut (2026-11-08): the stock, the court, the ready views and the Excel.** Migration `0110_controladoria` (pgTAP `0111`, 22 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project on 2026-11-08, ahead of its code). `firm_cases` is redefined with the court's views, each counted: moved today, in the last 7 and 30 days (`mov-hoje`, `mov-7`, `mov-30`: a movement of a lawsuit in court, by the day of Brasília), quiet for more than 30, 45 and 90 days (`parado-*`: a case in court whose lawsuits ONE read did nothing for that long, their last movement or else their filing), and never read by any source (`nunca-capturados`), with the cases archived in the month (`arquivados-mes`: a case that went to court and ended, beside the same days of the month before in `numbers`). Each row says when its lawsuits last moved (`moved_at`), their numbers (`cnjs`) and what they show in court (`court`, which `withCourtMarks` read apart, two more reads a page, and is gone). The list goes to a spreadsheet written in the browser by the project's own writer (`lib/export/xlsx-write.ts`, read back by `readXlsx` in its test, and opened by openpyxl: 100,000 lines in about 5 MB and some seconds, during which the tab is held): a partner, the controladoria or who manages the firm (`may_export_cases`, `overseesFirm`), never a support visit, the setting's 20 times a day each and 100,000 cases (`platform_settings` `controladoria`). An export is begun on the record (`begin_case_export`: `case.exported` in the audit trail with the view, the module, whether a search narrowed it and how many cases, never the words searched; no table of its own, the day's count is read from the trail), and only an export begun within the setting's hour reads the list's pages, of the setting's 2,000 (`firm_cases_export`; a transaction setting no API role reaches raises the page's 100 for its call alone). `lib/cases/export.ts` holds the pure rules and each case as a line, by the words the list shows; `lib/cases/export-actions.ts` the actions; `casos/components/use-case-export.ts` the run, which saves nothing of a list it could not read whole. The app: "Exportar" and the court's views in the bar of `/casos` (while the firm has a case in court), and `/controladoria` after Casos in the menu, for those who oversee the firm (`MenuAccess.oversees`; anybody else is told whose it is, with nothing read): the stock, the cases new, filed and archived in the month against the same days of the month before, the stock by step and by module (`firm_case_journey`), the court's seven numbers, each opening the list under them (`?ver=`, the list being `CasesView` `embedded` with its `heading` and `hideNumbers`), read when it opens and on "Atualizar", saying when; where the environment does not read the courts, it says a real lawsuit appears as never captured. Still to come of phase 1, in the order of the plan: the read-only board by step, the closing goal (the first registration of the pattern; what counts as a closing is the plan of the case journey's), the test cases counted and said so, "Parado há 31, 46 ou 91 dias" in the test court, the PDF, the whole portfolio with the second factor, and the weekly new try of a reading that stopped. The names Inicial and Distribuição are the owner's of 2026-11-07, said by every step since 2026-11-10, and the page reads the same words as the top of a case.
- **Phase 1, second part (2026-11-08): who has each case, how long it is at its step, and the stagnant ones.** Migration `0111_case_standing` (pgTAP `0112`, 14 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project on 2026-11-08, where `0036`, `0111` and `0112` pass). `case_standing` is the rule of the top of the case (`caseNow`) over every open case of a firm, a set at a time: each line the top says becomes a mark of whose it is, decided in the order the top decides (the firm, ONE at work, the client, the platform; else the court for a case in court, and ONE for any other), with the reason (`why`), the step of its way (as the top: at the signature while a paper waits, at the filing once a package is asked for) and since when (a stage by when the case entered it, the hearing by the lawsuit's filing, the judgment by the latest hearing held, the payment by the judgment). `wait_owner` is `WAIT_PLACE` in the database. A case is stagnant past the setting's days of its step (`controladoria` `stagnant_days`, a first proposal: 7 days at the first contact, 15 at each step before the court, 120 at the hearing and at the judgment, 90 at the payment); the rule is `estoque-v1` (`@flyee/rules/stock`). `firm_cases` (same arguments) gives each row of its page `holder`, `why`, `step`, `step_since`, `step_days` and `stagnant`, read for the page's cases alone (`case_standing(firm, module, cases)`), and six views (`com-escritorio`, `com-cliente`, `com-one`, `no-tribunal`, `com-plataforma`, `estagnados`), which read every case only when one of them is asked; `firm_case_stock` (the module, the kind of problem, the airline: the list's own filters) answers the panel and the counts of those views, read once a load and again when those filters change (`readCaseStock`, `readFirmCaseStock`, `standingCounts`). Measured on 20,000 invented cases on the embedded database: a page of the list in about 0.3 s, a view of who has the case 0.6 s, the stock 0.6 s, a page of an export (2,000 cases) 0.6 s (when every read computed every case, a page took 1.5 s; who is owed a link is read for the whole firm even for a page, since case by case it cost a millisecond a case). **The database and the top of the case are compared case by case** (`apps/web/src/lib/cases/standing.db.test.ts`, part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`): the case screen's own read (`loadCase`, moved out of the page into `casos/[id]/load-case.ts`) and the assembly of its facts (`caseFacts`, moved out of `case-view.tsx` into `casos/[id]/case-facts.ts`) run against the embedded database, and each case's holder and step must be the same as `firm_cases` says; the stand-in client now reads embedded resources as PostgREST does (`lib/testing/embedded-select.ts`: a correlated subquery per foreign key, `!inner`, a hint, filters on an embedded column) and the filters and orders the reads use. A test keeps the reasons the database gives with their words (`lib/cases/standing.test.ts`). The app: `/casos` has the columns "Na etapa há" (with "Estagnado") and "Com quem está" (with why) in place of "Para o escritório", and the views of who has the case; the spreadsheet carries them; `/controladoria` reads the stock by the same rule (each step with its median and oldest days and its stagnant cases, who has the cases, each line opening the list). The stock shows the last step of the way ("Pagamento publicado, a arquivar") while a case whose money was released is not archived yet, so its steps add up to it (the development project had one, which the bars left out); the stage view `open` of the list is "Em atendimento" (it was "Com o cliente", the words of the view of who has the case). On the way: the top of a case said "O ONE está conferindo Documento de identidade de ..." (the label of a kind, capitalized, in the middle of a sentence); it has words of its own now (`case-now-document-*`). Known limits: the top reads a case's publications up to the card's page, and the database all of them, so a case with more publications than the card shows may differ; the limits of each step are the platform's for every firm.
- **Phase 1, third part (2026-11-08): the board by step.** Migration `0112_case_board` (pgTAP `0113`, 9 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project on 2026-11-08, where `0012` and `0113` pass). `firm_case_board` reads who has each case and its step by the rule of the top of the case (`case_standing`) and answers a column per step with how many cases are at it, how many stagnated, its limit and its first cases, the longest at the step first (then the oldest case), each with its client (named as the list names it), its days at the step, whether it stagnated and who has it and why; or, for one step and from a position, that column's next cases (about 0.5 s on 20,000 invented cases). `/controladoria` shows the cases as "Lista" or "Quadro por etapa" (a switch beside the head of its list, `headingAction` of `CasesView`; a number of the page opens the list again): the board (`controladoria/case-board.tsx`, the pure rules in `lib/cases/board.ts`) has every step of the way in its order, an empty one saying so, and the last one ("Pagamento publicado, a arquivar") only while a case is at it; each card opens its case; "Mostrar mais" reads ten more of a column (`readFirmCaseBoardColumn`). Only to look at: nobody drags a card, ONE carries a case from one step to the next. The round trip of `standing.db.test.ts` puts each case of the board at the step, the days and the holder the list says.
- **Phase 1, fourth part (2026-11-08): the closing goals.** Migration `0113_closing_goals` (pgTAP `0114`, 14 assertions with two firms; `0003` holds a row of the table; applied to the development project on 2026-11-08, where `0012` and `0114` pass, and `0003` fails only its three counts of every firm of that project, known above). It is the first registration in the pattern of phase 0. `closing_goals`: a period in days of Brasília (a year at most), the part of the cases it is of (a module the firm has, a kind of problem, where the cases came from, one partnership of the firm, which makes the origin `agency`; each left as every one) and how many closings it asks. A partner or the controladoria (`may_manage_closing_goals`: `decides_for_firm`, or the controladoria) creates and changes one (`save_closing_goal`), archives it with a reason of 3 to 300 characters and brings it back (`archive_closing_goal`), never a support visit, each change in the audit trail (`closing_goal.created`, `changed`, `archived`, `restored`); anybody of the firm reads them (`firm_closing_goals`). **What counts as a closing is the plan of the case's journey's to say, which is not built:** until then `rule` and every goal's `closings` are null, and the screens say the count is not on ("O ONE ainda não conta os fechamentos"); the progress bar is written and tested, and shows once the function answers a count. The app: `/controladoria/metas` (`metas/goals-view.tsx`, `goal-panel.tsx`, `load-goals.ts`, the words of a goal in `goal-words.ts`, the pure rules in `lib/controladoria/goals.ts`): the bar with the views Em vigor, Próximas, Encerradas and Arquivadas and "Nova meta"; a row opening its goal by its period, the rest in `QuickActions`; the goal in `SidePanel` (this month, the next, this quarter or two days; the part of the cases; the closings); the list goes to the view a saved goal landed in; archiving in `ConfirmActionDialog` with its reason. The card "Metas de fechamento" on `/controladoria` (`goals-card.tsx`), between the court and the cases, shows the goals in force and leads to the list (`?nova=1` opens a new goal). Known limit, for the owner to weigh: nothing is counted until the plan of the case's journey starts, and a goal of every module is not listed under one module's scope.
- **Phase 1, fifth part (2026-11-08): the test cases in the numbers, and the test court's quiet lawsuits.** Migration `0114_test_cases` (pgTAP `0115`, 14 assertions with two firms). `firm_cases`, `firm_cases_export`, `firm_case_stock` and `firm_case_board` take `target_tests` (true by default, as every other page reads them; false for the firm's real cases alone), and each row and card says when a case is a test (`is_test`; "Teste" on the list, on the board and in the sheet's last column). `firm_test_cases` says how many real and test cases a firm has, which `testCaseScope` (`lib/cases/test-cases.ts`) reads: while a firm has no real case the numbers of `/controladoria` count its test cases and say so; once it has one they leave its numbers, its list, its board and its export, with "Incluir casos de teste" one click away (`?testes=1`, kept by the page's links). `test_court_quiet` (for the backend, by the test chat's link, only on a filed test lawsuit, on the record as `test_court.quiet`) leaves a lawsuit quiet for 31, 46 or 91 days: its movements go back in time, so its last one was that long ago, a later filing goes back too, and it counts as read, so each mark of "Não movimentados" can be seen; a row "Deixar o processo parado" on each lawsuit of `/t/[token]/tribunal`. A lawsuit moved today in the test court is in "Movimentados hoje" (asserted in `0115`). Known limit, for the owner to weigh: `/casos` and `/inicio` still count the test cases (each marked on `/casos`), since a firm tests through them; only the controladoria's numbers leave them out.
- **Phase 1, sixth part (2026-11-08): the list of cases as a PDF.** Migration `0115_case_export_pdf` (pgTAP `0116`, 12 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project on 2026-11-08, where `0111` and `0116` pass): `begin_case_export` takes the format `pdf` with a limit of its own, the setting's 2,000 lines (`controladoria` `export_pdf_rows`), the same people and the same exports a day as the workbook, the two counted together, on the record with its format and never with the words searched. `@flyee/documents/table-pdf` (`writeTablePdf`) is the project's own table, which the finance's entries will use too: A4 lying down, the title and what the list is on the first page, the titles of the columns repeated and each page numbered and named, nothing of a row cut (a row taller than a page goes on over the next). It needs nothing of a server, so it is written in the browser and its code loads only when somebody asks for a PDF; `@flyee/documents/pdf-text` holds what the standard fonts write, apart from `text-pdf`, which carries `sharp` and the verification mark, and `closestText` writes a letter outside Latin-1 by its base letter and what has no plain form as "?" (a list of people is read, not signed, so a name is never shown shorter as if it were whole). 2,000 lines of the list's shape take about 2.5 seconds and 1 MB. The app: "Exportar" in the bar of `/casos` and of `/controladoria` is a menu ("Planilha do Excel", "PDF para ler ou imprimir"; `ListBar` `exporting.formats`); `pdfColumns` and `pdfLine` of `lib/cases/export.ts` hold what the list shows, a line per thing in each cell (who has the case in bold and why under it, each lawsuit on its line, "Estagnado" and "Teste" in words); the first page says the firm, the module, how many cases, when in Brasília's time, the filters, that a search narrowed it (never its words) and that the test cases were left out; a list past the limit is refused with the limit and the way out (a view, a filter, or Excel). Known limit, for the owner to weigh: a name outside Latin-1 is written by its base letters in the PDF, as the standard fonts of a PDF write no other (the workbook keeps it whole).
- **One list of the cases, and one rule of the step (2026-11-09).** The owner found the controladoria repeating the list of `/casos` under its numbers, asked for the board right under them, and asked whether a module's area and `/inicio` repeated it too. Two things were found: the list of cases was embedded whole in the controladoria and in each module's area, and the app said where a case is along its way by two rules (the top of the case, the board and "Na etapa há" read `case_standing`, 0111; the home's card of the steps and the little bar of the list read the case's stage and what its lawsuits show, `firm_case_journey` and `stepOf`), so a step of the home opened a list of another size. Migration `0116_case_step_views` (pgTAP `0117`, 10 assertions with two firms): `firm_cases` lists the cases at each step by the rule of the top of the case (`etapa-intake` to `etapa-payment`, and `etapa-closed` for a case paid and not archived), read as the views of who has the case are, and the stock counts them. `/casos` is the one list: its views of a step (listed while a case is at the step, `STEP_VIEWS`, `stepCounts`), the bar under each row's stage by the top's step (`stepOfRow`), and `?testes=fora` with a removable chip "Sem os casos de teste", the cut the controladoria opens it with. `/controladoria` (the owner's order): the numbers of the month, the board by step (each column's head with its cases, their median and oldest days and the stagnant ones, "Ver na lista"; the card "Estoque por etapa" it repeated is gone), then who has the cases and each module beside the court's numbers, then the goals; every number, line and column opens `/casos` with the page's cut, and "Exportar" is the list's. `/inicio` and a module's area show one card (`components/cases/cases-progress-card.tsx`, "Andamento dos casos": the stock's steps, each opening `/casos?ver=etapa-*` under the scope, the latest five in progress, how many are going and ended, the ended opening their view); the area no longer embeds the list (the owner's decision of 2026-10-30 that a module's area has its lawsuits: in summary now, the list one click away), and says ONE waits for the module's first case when it has none. `journeyCounts` and `lib/cases/journey-read.ts` are gone; `firm_case_journey` stays only in the home's pulse. Checked and kept: the center of what waits and the home's "O escritório precisa agir" (the same list on purpose, the center on every screen), the modules on the home, `/modulos` and the menu (ways in), and the three numbers `/casos` and `/controladoria` share (each page opens on its numbers; on `/casos` they filter in place).
- **The round trips run whole again (2026-11-09).** The stand-in client of the embedded database (`lib/testing/embedded-select.ts`, rewritten on 2026-11-08 to read embedded resources) stopped reading `column::type`, so the worker of `advance.db.test.ts` took a read that failed as a failed look and did nothing: the product was never wrong (PostgREST reads such a select), and the suite was not run whole between the two days. It now reads a column as PostgREST does (`alias:column`, `column::type`, `column->field`, `column->>field`), `embedded-select.test.ts` tries every select the app writes with no database, and `standing.db.test.ts`, which the notes called part of the round trips, is in `npm run test:db:roundtrip` (23 files, 128 tests). Since that day `.github/workflows/ci.yml` also runs on every push to `main`, beside the deploy, which does not wait for it: a red run warns and stops nothing, a newer push cancels the run before it, and the suites are still run by hand before a slice is called done.
- **Phase 1, seventh part (2026-11-09): the whole portfolio.** "Levar a carteira inteira" answers who asks how to leave ONE: everything of the firm in one Excel workbook, for who decides for it (`decides_for_firm`: a partner, or who manages a firm with none), with the second factor, never a support visit. Migration `0117_firm_portfolio` (pgTAP `0118`, 25 assertions with three firms; applied to the development project on 2026-11-09, where `0012` and `0118` pass): `begin_portfolio_export` refuses or begins it (`not_allowed`, `support_visit`, `second_factor`, `empty`, `too_many`, `daily_limit`), on the record (`portfolio.exported`, with how many lines of each sheet and never anything of anybody); only a portfolio begun within the setting's hour reads its pages (`firm_portfolio_page`: the sheets `clientes` with CPF, telephone, e-mail and address, `casos`, `acoes`, `prazos` and `documentos`, the index and never the files, a page of 2,000 lines, each moment as Brasília's clock and each module by its name, also one the firm no longer has). The setting `controladoria` holds the 3 times a day per person and the 300,000 lines a sheet takes (`portfolio_daily`, `portfolio_rows`). `writeWorkbook` of `lib/export/xlsx-write.ts` writes several sheets (`writeXlsx` is one of them), in the person's browser; `lib/controladoria/portfolio.ts` holds the pure rules, each row as a line by the words the screens use, and the first sheet, "Sobre este arquivo" (the firm, when, how many, that test cases stayed out and that it holds personal data); `portfolio-actions.ts` the actions, which ring the bell of the firm's other partners and of who manages it (`portfolioTaken`). The app: the button in plain sight beside "Atualizar" in `/controladoria`, never in a menu, and `controladoria/portfolio-panel.tsx`: what goes in the file, that it holds everybody's data, that the taking is on record and who is told, the second factor in place, the progress per sheet, a file saved only of a portfolio read whole, and a refusal that names why, with "Tentar de novo" only where trying again can help. Test cases follow the Controladoria's cut. Measured on 20,000 invented cases on the embedded database (20,000 clients, 20,000 lawsuits, 40,000 deadlines, 80,000 documents): begun in 0.3 s, read in 31 s (95 pages, the slowest 0.8 s), written in 4 s, 8.9 MB, and opened by openpyxl with its six sheets whole. The round trip is `apps/web/src/lib/controladoria/portfolio.db.test.ts` (part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`). The panel was seen in the browser in each state with invented data and the server's answers stood in, at 1440 and 390, light and dark. The owner confirmed the same day the bell to the others, the 3 times a day (a try that fails half way counts), that the controladoria, who exports lists, does not take the portfolio, and the test cases following the Controladoria's cut, and asked for the hearings too: migration `0118_portfolio_hearings` (pgTAP `0119`, 8 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project on 2026-11-09, where `0118` and `0119` pass) adds the sheet `audiencias` (each hearing of every state, its day and hour on the clock of the zone it is said in with the zone beside it, how and where, who goes, what came of it or why it was cancelled, and whether ONE registered it), counted and on the record with the rest. Known limits: the publications are not in the file (public data of the gazette, read again at the DJEN); nothing of it ran signed in with a real second factor.
- **Phase 1, eighth part (2026-11-09): a court reading that stopped is tried again every week before a person is called.** Migration `0119_court_reading_retries` (pgTAP `0120`, 12 assertions with two firms; `0034` follows it with no weekly try left; applied to the development project on 2026-11-09, where `0120` passes and `0034` fails only two counts of every firm's `court_moved` events, which the walks left there): a reading given up after its attempts (0031: no answer from the source, a claim never finished, an answer that could not be kept) pauses (`proceeding_tracking.status` `paused`, `retry_at`), and the claim starts it over a week later (`retry_days`, 7) with its attempts back, up to the setting `tracking`'s three times (`weekly_retries`, counted in `retry_rounds`); only a reading that stops after the last is `failed`, which calls a person (the home's `firm_today.tracking_failed` and the list's view `andamento` read `failed` alone, so a paused reading calls nobody). A source that answers (read, secret, not found), another number or a lawsuit no longer read starts the count over (a trigger keeps it, `proceeding_tracking_retries`); a person asks for a reading of a paused one as of one given up (`request_proceeding_read`); a case that ended is not started over. The lawsuit's card "Andamento no tribunal" says the reading stopped, when ONE tries again by itself and that the firm is told if it does not manage, with "Ler agora no tribunal" for who needs it sooner (`course-paused`); one given up for good says ONE already tried in the last weeks. The round trip of the court's reading (`apps/web/src/lib/courts/tracking.db.test.ts`) has the worker pause a reading its source does not answer, the case say when, and the source answer once its week passed. Known limits: the weekly tries are the platform's for every firm; a reading that cannot be kept (an answer too large) waits the three weeks too before a person sees it; the page was checked by its tests, not walked signed in.
- **Phase 2 of the same plan, the work of ONE (2026-11-09).** Every month the firm sees what ONE did for it, by kind of task, and about how many hours a person would have taken by the firm's own estimates: the argument of the renewal, never a measure of a person. Migrations `0120_one_work` (the catalog `one_work_kinds`: 35 kinds in 10 families, each with ONE's conservative minutes and the decision of `autonomy_decisions` it belongs to; `firm_one_work_minutes` and `firm_one_work_cost`, read only by who decides for the firm; `set_one_work_estimates`, never a support visit, on the record and never with the cost), `0121_one_work_count` (`one_work_acts`, internal, and `firm_one_work`) and `0122_one_work_record` (the record of a kind sent back to ONE's minutes kept its null) (pgTAP `0121`, 14 assertions with two firms, an act of every kind and its undone twins; `0003` holds a row of each new table; all three applied to the development project on 2026-11-09, where `0121` and `0012` pass). The count reads the records ONE already keeps, so the months before show on the first day, and counts each act once and only what stands: the case opened at the passenger's yes (the timeline's `created` by the system from a conversation), ONE's answer that went out (a run of `atendimento.conversa`), the link ONE sent that nobody ended, a file of the conversation joined, an answer of the interview read in the conversation, a document decided by ONE's check, data read from a document that stand or were confirmed, a photo made a PDF, an audio transcribed, the order of the facts sent, the lawsuits ONE created, the court's system ONE named and a person approved, a leg checked at ANAC, the weather read, the Tema 1.417 decided by ONE, the first text of a piece not turned down, a piece ONE approved, a paper sent to sign whose signature was not ended, a dossier issued, a package built and signed, a lawsuit filed by ONE, a publication read and triaged by ONE (kept), a deadline read and counted and confirmed by ONE (not dismissed), a hearing read (not cancelled) and written to the firm's calendar, a movement of the court after the first reading's history, each notice that went out to the client (never a flight's alert) and a model made from the firm's document while it exists (the record of its saving, the only one it leaves). What a person approved after reading what ONE prepared counts apart (`assisted`), the test cases apart again, a month is Brasília's, and an act of no case (a conversation, a model, a publication of no case) counts only with every module. A pgTAP assertion fails when a decision of the catalog has no kind of work, so the count stays whole as decisions are added. The app: `/trabalho-do-one` (in the menu after Controladoria, for anybody of the firm; `trabalho-do-one/one-work-view.tsx`, `estimates-panel.tsx`, `load-one-work.ts`): the month chosen of the last twelve (`?mes=`), the module (`?modulo=`), the tasks and the hours (a whole month against the month before, in hours), the money only to who decides with a cost said, "Peças aprovadas pelo ONE" on a card of its own beside item 3.1 of the OAB's Recomendação 001/2024 (and out of its family's card), a card per family with each kind and what a person approved, "Como contamos", "Ajustar estimativas" (the minutes of each kind, blank for ONE's, and the cost of an hour in a money field) and "Baixar planilha" (a line per month and kind of the twelve months read); the line "Neste mês, o ONE fez N tarefas pelo escritório, cerca de X horas" on `/inicio` and on each module's area (`components/product/one-work-line.tsx`), the same total the page shows. `lib/one-work/work.ts` and `estimates.ts` hold the pure rules (tested), `read.ts` the read, `actions.ts` the save. The page was seen in the browser with invented data at 1440 and 390, light and dark. On the way, the CI's first runs on a push failed on its own: the type check ran out of Node's heap (the verify job gives it 6 GB) and the embedded database's Linux build keeps its extensions in `share/postgresql/extension` (`scripts/db-embedded/run.mjs` finds the folder that holds `plpgsql.control`). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the kinds and the minutes are a first proposal, to be checked with the pilot in the first month; the weather and the dossier are asked by a person and done by ONE, and count as ONE's; a hearing a person cancelled and a deadline dismissed do not count even when the court moved them; a piece ONE wrote again counts once per piece; only the latest reading of the weather and the latest sync of a hearing leave a date.
- **One action at a time on the case (2026-11-10).** The owner found that a click in one card of `/casos/[id]` left the other cards' buttons live, that nothing said an action was running nor that it ended, and that "Andamento do caso" kept the old state for seconds after the cards had changed; the canvas "ONE · Ações no caso" (https://claude.ai/artifact/9qypAYMV2DzqsgvC8Y5AUf) was approved the same day. No migration. A write of a card goes through `useCaseAction(section).run` (`casos/[id]/components/case-action.tsx`; the state is `useCaseActions` in `case-view.tsx`, over a router refresh inside a transition): the button turns and says the verb, every other control of every card waits (`CollapsibleGroup` `lock`: each body is a fieldset, disabled, saying why on pointing; opening a file is drawn as no button and stays free), and the lock lifts only once the case read again is on screen, the focus back on the button. What came of it is a green line with the time at the top of that card (`notes`), a card it changed is ringed (`flash`) and one "Ver" away in a toast, a failure stays in its card. The Andamento says the action under way with a bar and dims what may change, says "Atualizando o caso" while a change made by ONE or the client is read again (nothing locked), changes at the same moment as the cards and marks what changed with "Novo" (`useFresh`, `components/product/use-fresh.ts`). Converted: Pessoas, Documentos, Assinaturas, Como vai a juízo (the family's choice included), Processos, Peças and Protocolo; every other card's write that calls `onChanged` locks the same way until the case shows it, unless the person is typing in a field. Known limits: a card not converted locks the case only once its write answered (while its server call runs, only its own button waits), and so does a dialog's own save; the screens were checked by types, lint and component tests, not walked signed in.
- **A card's list that piles up is one line per thing, what asks first (2026-11-10).** The owner had to read every publication of a case, all open, to find the one that asked for the lawyer's awareness; the canvas "ONE · Publicações no caso" (https://claude.ai/artifact/KGngdewFaS9T14DJMPRGEB) was approved the same day, with the other cards of the case audited one by one. No migration. `ItemRows` (`components/product/item-rows.tsx`) draws a card's list as sections of lines: "Pedem a sua ação" first (each line says what is missing and holds its one button, or a button that opens it), then the product's work as lines that wait, then what is settled, the newest first, a few in sight and "Mostrar mais"; a line opens in place, one at a time, kept mounted once opened; arriving by the card's address (the top's "Resolver") opens the first line that asks. Publicações (`lib/publications/rows.ts`: the awareness of ONE's triage given on the line, a triage, the word on the judgment, what ONE left to a person, a cancelling to see; ONE triaging or reading; settled with the hearing registered, the deadline, who gave the awareness; the publication opening as `PublicationCard` `inline`, which leaves out its head, the way to the case and the awareness), Documentos (`lib/cases/document-rows.ts`: what to decide with ONE's suggestion, a bill of another city, ONE checking, decided), Avisos ao cliente (a message whose sending is unconfirmed or failed first, what goes now, what went with its words in the line) and Assinaturas (what waits for the firm, what waits for the client to sign, what ended); Prazos puts what waits for confirmation or a second look first, and Resultado lists the facts that ask (confirm ONE's reading, the awareness, the value received) above the lawsuits. `/publicacoes`, worked in volume, keeps its open cards. Known limits: the order within "Pedem a sua ação" is the oldest first, whatever the urgency; the screens were checked by types, lint and unit tests, not walked signed in.
- **Less text on the screens, and no section left blank (2026-11-09).** The owner found the screens tiring to read (136 blocks of text under titles in the logged area, about 2,800 words, 82 of them longer than a line) and asked that a section with nothing in it never be left as blank space; the before and after of the controladoria, a module's area and the library were validated on a canvas the same day. No migration. What a page, a card or a section is for went behind the "i" beside its title: the `description` of `PageHeader`, `CardTitle`, `CollapsibleCard`, `CasesProgressCard` and the new `SectionTitle` (`components/product/section-title.tsx`, the heading of a section of a page that is not a card) is now an `InfoHint`, of 200 characters at most (`lib/i18n/hint-budget.test.ts` reads every such text where the code writes it, messages and literals, and fails on a longer one). `detail` is the one line kept in sight, for state or data (the kind of a case, a passenger's agreement, a conversation's number, how many a list shows) and for what must be read before acting (leaving the firm, what each decision of `/settings/autonomia` does, what a lawyer authorizes with the cloud signature, the operator's release of a real passenger's alerts and the goal of the triage policy, and the instructions on the client's and the passenger's own pages). The texts longer than a tooltip were cut to fit. `EmptyState` takes an `illustration` (the template's 16 line drawings of `@/icons/illustrations`, unused until then, in the secondary hue), `layout="row"` and `framed={false}`: the controladoria's court with no lawsuit (documentation), its board with no case going (launch) and its goals with none (performance), and a module's area with no case (launch). The controladoria's lines of who has the cases show a chevron instead of a sentence, and the definitions that repeated a title went (the stock by module, the goals); a module's area lost the line of its contract (said in `/modulos`) and the lines under its tiles (a tile is its icon and its name, and `FirmAreaEntry.descriptionKey` went with them); the library's chip of a provisional model says "Versão provisória". The lists' empty states have their drawing too (the same day, the owner): the cases (the first case, launch; none going, storage; a search or a filter with nothing, documentation), the conversations (support; no number connected, phone; none chosen, email), the publications (nothing yet or in a view, email; none to triage, configure; no registration at the bar, accounts), the deadlines (configure), the agenda (platforms), the filing queue (documentation), the library (documentation; nothing missing, configure) and the approvals (configure); a state of permission or of a failed read keeps its icon. On 2026-11-10 (the owner) the operator's consoles' lists got their drawings too, and the case screen had the same pass, card by card, no migration: each card with nothing in it shows its drawing beside its words (the complaint and what happened, support; the documents, the pieces, the filing and the signatures, documentation; every lawsuit already filed, configure; the lawsuits and the trip, launch; the publications, email; the deadlines, configure; the hearings, platforms; the dossier, in the narrow column, above its words), a section inside a card says what it is behind the "i" of its sub-heading (`SectionTitle` takes `variant="subtitle2"` and `as="h3"`: the suspension of the Tema 1.417, the acceptance of the civil court, the audios of the case, the preview of a piece, whose "leia antes de aprovar" stays in sight), the way to a petition written outside ONE and why a family goes together sit behind an `InfoHint` beside their line, a piece written outside joined the card's own "i", and the empty texts, the reading that stopped and the intros of the person's and the trip's panels were cut. Known limits: the other tiles of the app (the home's modules, the agency's home, the setup's other modules) keep their line; the top of the case ("Andamento do caso") keeps its lines as they were, each a state.
- **Phase 3 of the same plan, the declarations a client signs, first cut (2026-11-09): the declaration of residence.** Many clients live with their family and have the electricity or water bill in somebody else's name. Migration `0123_residence_declaration` (pgTAP `0122`, 18 assertions with two firms; `0003`, `0012`, `0052` and `0068` follow it; applied to the development project on 2026-11-09, where those files pass and the fifth instructions are in force): a firm says whether it accepts a proof of address in somebody else's name (`organizations.third_party_bill`: `accept`, the default, or `refuse`; `set_third_party_bill`, who decides for the firm, never a support visit, on the record), and the fifth version of the instructions of `caso.documento.conferencia` no longer refuses such a bill for the name on it: it says the holder as the bill writes it and that it is not the person's (`confere_com_a_pessoa` "nao"). The check then decides in the database (`apply_document_check` redefined): where the firm refuses, or the holder could not be read, the bill is refused with the way to send one in the client's name; where it accepts, the bill is accepted, its address written as the client's (0063's rules), and a declaration is owed (`residence_declarations`: the person, the bill, the holder written as a name and as read, who the holder is to the client, who said it and with what the server saw, the bill's city; one open per person, ended when the person sends a bill in their own name, when the bill is refused afterwards, or when another bill takes its place). The client says on their link who the holder is (mother, father, spouse or partner, child, sibling, grandparent, another relative, the owner of the home, or somebody else in their words; `client_link_residence`, `client_link_answer_residence`), and anybody of the firm may record it as the client told it, correcting the holder's name (`record_residence_relation`); once the paper was issued the answer stays. ONE issues the declaration from the firm's model of the kind (`draft_templates.document_kind` `declaracao_residencia`, written in the library), else the platform's (seeded, provisional, with Lei 7.115/1983, art. 3º: the declarant answers for a false statement), filling the client's data, the holder (`[[TITULAR]]`) and who they are (`[[RELACAO]]`: "minha mãe"), and sends it to sign as the power of attorney goes (the firm's ZapSign, else gov.br on the link); the papers a client signs are a queue (`advance_paper_to_issue`: the power of attorney, then the declaration, then the contract, the first complete one going, so a paper with a fact missing never holds the next back). A signed declaration is a document of its own kind (`residence_declaration`), goes to the court beside the bill in the filing package (`case_advance.package_kinds`, `FILING_ORDER`, `filing_types_ok`), and the filing waits for it (`filing_blocker`: the client's word on the holder, then an address on record in another city than the bill's, which waits for a person because it decides the forum, then the signature). A partner or a lawyer may go on without it, with the second factor and a reason of 8 to 500 characters, after a confirmation that says what it may cost (the bill may not prove the address, and in the small-claims court an address not proved may extinguish the lawsuit, Lei 9.099/1995, art. 51, III; `waive_residence_declaration`, audited). Who decides for the firm reads how many filing packages of the month carry a bill in somebody else's name (`firm_third_party_bills`), since the CNJ's Recomendação 159/2024 lists documents in third parties' names among the signs of abusive litigation. Whose each wait is: the client's word and the signature are the client's, the address of another city the firm's (`wait_owner`). The app: under the bill on the case's card of documents, where its declaration stands (`casos/[id]/components/residence-line.tsx`, the pure rules in `lib/cases/residence.ts`, the writes in `residence-actions.ts`: the holder with who they are, that ONE read the name and may have erred, recording who the holder is in `SidePanel`, going on without it in a `ConfirmActionDialog`, the second factor in place); on the client's link, the bill in somebody else's name with the question and, once issued, that the declaration waits below to be signed (`c/[token]/client-residence.tsx`); the firm's choice and the month's count in `/settings/autonomia` (`components/firm/third-party-bill-card.tsx`); the kind in the library and in the operator's content; the top of the case, the center of what waits and the card "Protocolo" name each wait. A declaration that ends takes its paper with it (`0124_residence_paper_again`, pgTAP `0123`, 5 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project): the paper's row goes, so a declaration owed again (another bill in somebody else's name) is issued again, its PDFs (issued and signed) are refused so no filing takes them, and only a paper issued for the declaration in force counts as its signature. The round trip `apps/web/src/lib/cases/residence.db.test.ts` (part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`) has ONE's check accept Maria's bill in her mother's name, Maria answer on her link, the worker issue the power of attorney and the declaration as PDFs to sign by gov.br, the filing wait for the declaration and the package take it once signed. Tried with the real model (Sonnet 5.5, 3 to 7 seconds) on drawn bills: one in the client's mother's name is accepted with the holder written and "nao", one in her own name with "sim", each with the installation's address and never the company's. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a bill in a company's name is taken as one in somebody else's name too (the real model accepted one; the client then says who the company is to them, the owner of the home or somebody else); the words of the platform's declaration are for the curator; a firm's own declaration is written in the library (ONE does not make it from a document the firm sends yet); the hipossuficiência declaration of a family in the civil court, the reading of the powers of the firm's power of attorney (CPC, art. 105) and each paper's own signature level are the next parts of phase 3.
- **Phase 3, second part (2026-11-09): free justice for a family in the civil court, the powers of the firm's power of attorney and each signature's level.** In the civil court there are costs at the start (the small-claims court has none in the first instance, Lei 9.099/1995, art. 54, so nothing is asked there). Migration `0125_free_justice` (pgTAP `0124`, 17 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project): each claimant of a lawsuit planned in the civil court says whether they ask for free justice (`free_justice_answers`, through who gives their word today, `civil_court_answerer`: the claimant or who answers for a minor), on their link (`client_link_free_justice`, `client_link_answer_free_justice`, the backend's, with what the server saw, a few changes a day) or through the firm saying how (`record_free_justice_answer`, never over an answer given since). Who asks signs one declaration of economic hardship for themselves and each minor they answer for who asks (`declaracao_hipossuficiencia`, third in the queue of papers, from the firm's model of it, else the platform's, seeded provisional with CPC arts. 98 and 99 and Lei 7.115/1983, `[[GRATUIDADE_PARA]]`: "Rui Teste, em nome próprio; Pedro Teste, menor de idade, por quem respondo"), a document of its own kind (`free_justice_declaration`) that goes to the court with an optional proof of income (`income_proof`, sent on the link and checked by ONE); a changed answer ends the declaration it no longer fits (`end_free_justice_paper`: its PDFs refused, its row gone) and a new one is issued. The piece writes the request by name where its model puts `[[GRATUIDADE]]` and `[[PEDIDO_GRATUIDADE]]` (`case_draft_filled`; the platform's civil model has them now in place of its `[COMPLETAR]`, and a fifth version of `caso.peca.minuta` tells the AI they are the database's), a piece of the civil court is not approved while a claimant has not answered (`free_justice_unanswered`, and ONE's own approval waits instead of giving up), and one without the mark, while somebody asks, waits for a lawyer to put it (`free_justice_mark_missing`); the filing waits for every answer and the declarations of who asks, signed (`free_justice_blocker` in `filing_blocker`). The case screen follows the declarations of residence and these answers by itself (`case_pulse`). The app: the answers under "Como este caso vai a juízo", each with where the declaration stands and "Registrar a resposta" in `SidePanel` (`casos/[id]/components/free-justice.tsx`, `lib/cases/free-justice.ts`, `free-justice-actions.ts`), the question per claimant and the optional proof of income on the client's link (`c/[token]/client-free-justice.tsx`), the kind in the library and the operator's content. **The powers of the firm's power of attorney** (CPC, art. 105) are read from its text by a pure rule (`lib/library/powers.ts`: receiving and giving discharge, signing a declaration of hardship) and said under the power of attorney in the setup's step of the papers, that ONE read them and may err; **each signature says its level** on the case's card of signatures (Lei 14.063/2020: ZapSign simple, gov.br advanced, e-Notariado qualified with ICP-Brasil). The round trip `apps/web/src/lib/cases/free-justice.db.test.ts` (part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`) has Rui ask on his link for himself and his son and the worker issue one declaration for both as a PDF to sign by gov.br. On the way, the database tests failed on CI between 21h and midnight of Brasília (the tests used the server's `current_date`, UTC there, and the functions Brasília's day): both runners now open each test's session in Brasília's zone. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: an answer changed after the piece was approved does not reopen the piece (a lawyer reopens it); the proof of income is read by ONE's check like any paper, and nothing weighs it against the court's criteria; the powers are read by words, so a power written in words the rule does not know is said as missing.
- **Phase 4 of the same plan, the firm's books, first cut (2026-11-10): accounts, entries and the bank's statement.** The line of the money is the plan's decision 1: ONE records, reads, calculates, prepares and warns, and never holds, receives, passes on, splits, pays or charges money, not even on a firm's behalf ("Product decisions the code assumes" above, and `docs/PRODUCT.md`); the code of the books never reaches ONE's own billing (a rule of the lint, `apps/web/eslint.config.ts`). Migration `0126_finance_books` (pgTAP `0125`, 22 assertions with two firms; `0003` holds a row of each new table; applied to the development project on 2026-11-10, where `0125` and `0012` pass and `0003` fails only its three counts of every firm, known above). **Who reads the books** is said before any number (decision 2): a partner, or who manages a firm with none, sees and configures all (`finance_level`); anybody else reads the costs of the cases as they read the cases and nothing of the books, unless one who decides grants them `view`, `operate` or `configure` (`finance_access`, `set_finance_access`, with the second factor); a support visit reads nothing. Every table of the books is read only by who reads them (`finance_reads`) and written only through its functions, each with the second factor (`finance_write_refusal`) and in the audit trail. **Opening the books** (`open_finance_books`: their first day, `organizations.finance_opened_on`) copies the platform's provisional chart of accounts for the firm (`finance_categories`, 26 rows, each with its line of the income statement and of the cash flow, for an accountant to review) and a cost centre per module the firm has (`finance_cost_centers`). **Accounts** (`finance_accounts`: bank, cash, card with its closing and due days, a receiving account of the firm's payment machine, a custody account of clients' money), each with its opening balance; **entries** (`finance_entries`: an income, an expense, a transfer between two accounts, a client's money, never revenue, or a partner's money, in signed cents), written by hand (`save_finance_entry`, its problems said by `finance_entry_problem`) or by ONE from a statement, each saying who made it; nothing is deleted: an entry is cancelled with its reason (`cancel_finance_entry`), a registration archived with its reason and brought back (`archive_finance_record`), ONE's act undone (`undo_finance_line`) and a statement imported by mistake removed whole (`remove_finance_statement`). **The bank's statement** in OFX is read in the person's browser, never uploaded (`lib/finance/statement-file.ts`: UTF-8 first, whatever the header says, else Windows-1252; `readOfx` of `@flyee/rules/finance`), and its lines sent with the file's SHA-256 (`begin_finance_statement`, `add_finance_statement_lines`, the setting `finance`'s 500 a call): the same file twice adds nothing, a line the bank already gave is known by its own id, and one of another id with the same day, value and memo is kept as a possible repetition, said and never dropped. ONE then decides what a rule decides (`finance_decide_lines`), in this order: a credit that is a client's money (a court's release, a judicial deposit: the setting's words, the same in the code and the database, `CLIENT_MONEY_WORDS`), a line a manual entry already wrote within the setting's 5 days (joined to it, never counted twice), a transfer between two of the firm's accounts within 2 days, a line a firm's rule answers for (`finance_rules`); the rest waits for a person, who decides it (`decide_finance_line`: ignore it, or an entry, keeping the answer as a rule if they want). After each import the balance the books compute on the bank's day is compared with the bank's (`finish_finance_statement`, `finance_account_balance`). The app (`/financeiro`, in the menu for who reads the books, `MenuAccess.finance`): the books' own setup for who decides while they are closed (`financeiro/finance-gate.tsx`: the second factor first, their first day, their first account; never a step of the firm's setup), the home (`finance-home-view.tsx`: the balance in the accounts, the month's income and expense and the clients' money in the accounts, each against the same days of the month before, the accounts with the lines that wait and how the books stand against the bank's last balance, the latest entries, `firm_finance_home`), `/financeiro/lancamentos` (the bar with its views, "Novo lançamento" for each kind in `SidePanel`, `entry-panel.tsx`, a cancelled entry in a view of its own), `/financeiro/conciliacao` (the statement chosen for an account, the lines that wait with "Decidir" in the panel, what ONE decided and why with the way to undo it, a possible repetition said) and `/financeiro/configuracao` (the accounts, the chart and the cost centres, and who sees the books, `firm_finance_access`). What ONE entered says it may err. `lib/finance/books.ts` holds the pure rules and words, `read.ts` the reads, `actions.ts` the writes. The round trip `apps/web/src/lib/finance/books.db.test.ts` (part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`) reads an OFX as the browser does, has ONE set the court's release apart and join the bill to the entry written by hand, and gives the screen the home; who reads only the costs reads nothing. The screens were seen in the browser with invented data at 1440 and 390. **The second cut** (migration `0127_finance_more`, pgTAP `0126`, 11 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project on 2026-11-10, where `0125`, `0126` and `0012` pass): a statement may also be a CSV or an Excel workbook the bank exports, read in the browser by the portfolio's own reader (`lib/finance/statement-table.ts`: the columns found by the names banks give them, a debit and a credit made one value, a "D" or a "C" beside a value, the balance lines kept apart; `finance_statements.format`), each line with no id of the bank's taking the hash of its key and of how many times the key came before it in the file, so the same export twice adds nothing and two equal payments of one file are two lines; a PDF is named and refused with what to do. A client's money that came into an account of the firm's own waits until a person says it was set apart (`finance_entries.apart_at`, `set_finance_money_apart`; one in a custody account is apart by being there), in the view "Dinheiro de cliente a separar" of the entries. What waits in the books (the lines ONE left, an account whose statement ended more than the setting's 10 days ago, a client's money to set apart) is read by who reads the books and nobody else (`firm_finance_attention`, null to anybody else), and said in the center of what waits and the home's list under "O ONE precisa de você" (`lib/finance/attention.ts`) and on the books' home. The entries go to Excel or PDF from the list as it stands (`lib/finance/export.ts`, the project's own writers), on the record with the format and how many and never what was searched, within the setting's 20 a day (`begin_finance_export`). "Nova receita", "Nova despesa" and "Transferência" are in the top's "Adicionar" for who writes in the books (`components/layout/add/finance-add-panel.tsx`, `MenuViewer.financeWrites`). And the books keep who a movement was of for the setting's 6 years: then the daily sweep `finance-retention` takes out the counterparty, the description, the case and the bank's memo and keeps the value (`finance_retention_sweep`). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the chart of accounts is the platform's proposal until an accountant reviews it; a statement or a card's bill in PDF is not read (the plan leaves it out when time is short), nor a receipt into an entry (phase 5 reads the documents of what is owed); a CSV whose columns the names do not tell is refused with the way to the OFX, with no screen to say which column is which; the years kept count from the movement, never from the last movement of the client, and do not lengthen for a minor. **The third cut** (migration `0128_finance_lines_ai`, pgTAP `0127`, 10 assertions with two firms; `0045` and `0121` follow it): ONE enters a line first by rule and then by the AI. A statement whose lines no rule decided owes the AI's reading (`finance_statements.ai_state`); the backend claims it under a number (`claim_finance_categorization`), hands the firm's chart and the lines numbered, a few dozen a run (`finance_lines_to_categorize`), each memo with its identifiers taken out by the app (`lib/finance/categorize.ts`, `maskMemo`: a CPF, a CNPJ, an e-mail, a telephone, a run of six digits or more), to the task `financeiro.extrato.categorizacao` (Haiku 5.5, a shadow task: its runs are nobody's of the firm to read), and the database reads the answer from the run (`record_finance_categorization`): a sure answer of a category that fits the way of the money, where the firm lets ONE (`finance_lines`, a decision of the catalog with no task, ONE's by default, so no answer of the books ever waits in the firm's queue), is the line's entry, marked as ONE's (`decided_rule` `ai`); anything else is kept on the line as ONE's suggestion (`ai_category_id`, `ai_counterparty`, `ai_sure`), which opens the panel of the line already chosen, said as ONE's and that ONE may err. Each line is asked once. The worker runs right after a statement is finished and in the sweep `finance-categorize` (`lib/finance/categorize-work.ts`). What ONE entered in the books counts in the work of ONE (`linha_extrato`, family `financeiro`), said only to who reads the books (`firm_one_work`). Tried with the real model on twelve invented lines (a fee, a power bill, fees received, a rent, a pro-labore, a software, the DAS, court costs, a yield, a client's money passed on, and two PIX with nothing written): each in its category and sure in 2.4 seconds, the two PIX left with no category and unsure. Known limits of the third cut: a line the model left unanswered is asked no more and waits for a person; a card's bill paid from the account is a transfer only when both accounts' statements came.
- **Phase 5 of the same plan, the bills to pay and to receive, first cut (2026-11-10).** The typed line of a bill is read and checked by a pure rule (`@flyee/rules/boleto`): a bank's collection bill of 47 digits (each field's module-10 digit, the barcode's module-11 digit, the due factor read in the cycle nearest to the day, since it started again at 1000 on 2025-02-22, and the value) and an arrecadação of 48 (each block's digit of module 10 or 11 as its third digit says, and the value when it carries one; its due day stays as the document says it), the barcode of 44 taken too. Migration `0129_finance_bills` (pgTAP `0128`, 11 assertions with two firms; `0003` and `0009` follow it; applied to the development project on 2026-11-10, where it passes): `finance_bills` (to pay or to receive: who, what, the category of its way, the cost centre, the case, the value, the due day, the month it belongs to, the typed line and its barcode, a series of the months asked, `save_finance_bill`, `operate` with the second factor) and `finance_bill_payments` (each payment's entry, what went to the bill, the interest and fine above it, the discount below it). The person pays at the bank and ONE settles the bill from the statement (`finance_bill_for_line`, read by `finance_decide_lines` before an entry written by hand): a line of its way with the same value within the setting's 30 days before and 60 after its due day, or more after the due day up to the setting's 20 percent (interest and fine), or a little less up to the due day down to 10 percent (a discount); its entry is the bill's, marked as ONE's (`decided_rule` `bill`). A person registers a payment by hand (`settle_finance_bill`, through `save_finance_entry`, so ONE joins it to the line when it arrives), in part (the bill stays open by the rest) or with the rest said as a discount. Nothing is deleted: undoing ONE's act or cancelling the entry undoes the payment and opens the bill again (`finance_cancel_entries`); a bill is cancelled with its reason, renegotiated into installments of what is owed that add up whole (`renegotiate_finance_bill`), and a receivable a client will not pay is written off by who configures (`close_finance_bill`). ONE never charges anybody: the receivables past their day are "Para cobrar ou informar", the firm's people's to act on outside ONE. What waits (`firm_finance_attention` gains `bills`): a bill to pay late is under "Atrasado" of the center of what waits, one due in the setting's 7 days under "Hoje e próximos dias", a receivable to charge under "O ONE precisa de você" with how many near the five years of art. 25 of the Estatuto (`financeAttentionGroups`), each only to who reads the books. The app: `/financeiro/contas` (`contas/bills-view.tsx`, `bill-panels.tsx`; `lib/finance/bills.ts` pure and tested): the month's numbers (a receber no mês, a pagar no mês, em atraso, recebido no mês), the views (a pagar, a receber, vencidas a pagar, para cobrar ou informar, pagas e recebidas, canceladas e renegociadas), "Nova conta" with the typed line first (the value and the due day it says fill the form, said as checked and that ONE may err), each bill with where it stands, "Registrar pagamento" or "Registrar recebimento", and in `QuickActions` edit (while nothing is paid), renegotiate, cancel and write off; the books' home links it and says the bills late. The round trip (`books.db.test.ts`) writes a receivable with a typed line checked by the rule and has ONE settle it from the statement. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a bill is not yet read from its document by ONE's AI (the typed line is pasted; a boleto's PDF, a payslip and the payroll's guides, the series ONE would find after three months, the receipt issued to a client and the legacy open bills by spreadsheet are the next cuts); the interest and fine are one figure, not split; a bill paid in part by the statement is not recognized (only the whole, or with interest or a discount).
- **Phase 6 of the same plan, the outcome of each lawsuit, first cut (2026-11-10).** The firm no longer reads each decision to know how a lawsuit ended. The rule `resultado-v1` (`@flyee/rules/outcome`) reads the title of a court's movement (the judgment and its result, the judgment become final, an appeal and by whom, a judicial deposit, a release of money by alvará, transfer, RPV or precatório, a settlement approved, the definitive archiving; a provisional archiving, a suspension or an order to pay costs never), a judgment's operative part (the result, the value beside the condemnation and whether each claimant's, a settlement with its value) and a release's order (its value). Migration `0130_lawsuit_outcomes` (pgTAP `0129`, 10 assertions with two firms; `0003`, `0045` and `0121` follow it): `lawsuit_facts` (each fact of a lawsuit with its result, who appealed, how money was released, its value, its day, the court's words, ONE's or a person's; a judgment, a settlement, the final judgment and the archiving one in force per lawsuit, a second reading completing the first, the words of the judgment's publication taking the place of a movement's title); the movements and publications read before it count as history (`outcome_read_at`). The worker (`lib/courts/outcome-work.ts`, the sweep `lawsuit-outcomes` every ten minutes and right after the test court acts) reads each new movement and each publication the triage said is the judgment, or a payment, and the database records what the rule read (`record_movement_outcome`, `record_publication_outcome`), as ONE's where the firm lets it (`record_outcome`, ONE's by default) or waiting for a person's confirmation where it does not. What changes the course (the judgment become final, the archiving, a judgment against the client) asks the lawyers' awareness; a partner, a lawyer or the controladoria records a fact by hand, corrects one with its reason (never deleted), confirms one ONE read, and registers what a release paid when it arrived (`save_lawsuit_fact`, `act_on_lawsuit_fact`). When every lawsuit of a case was archived definitively and every release received, ONE closes the case (`close_cases_by_one`, `close_case`, ONE's by default; `cases.closed_by_one`), asking the lawyers' awareness (`acknowledge_case_closure`) with the way to reopen it. What the results ask (`firm_outcome_attention`) is in the center of what waits and the home's list (`lib/cases/outcomes.ts`). ONE never sends the client its own word on a judgment: a loss is the lawyer's conversation. The case's timeline says each fact's kind, never a value. The test court judges in part, denies, approves a settlement with its value, takes the defendant's appeal and releases a value (`test_court_act`). The app: the card "Resultado" on the case (`casos/[id]/components/outcome-card.tsx`: each lawsuit with where it stands and its facts, each said as ONE's and that ONE may err with the court's words beside it, "Estou ciente", "Confirmar", "Registrar o valor recebido", "Corrigir" in `QuickActions`, "Registrar fato" in `SidePanel`, and the case ONE closed with "Estou ciente" and "Reabrir"), the two decisions in `/settings/autonomia`, and the two kinds of ONE's work. The round trip `apps/web/src/lib/courts/outcome.db.test.ts` (part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`) has the worker read a judgment, its final judgment and a release from movements and publications, nothing of a provisional archiving, nothing twice, and close the case once the release is received. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the words of a judgment are read by rule, not by the AI yet (the plan frees the AI's reading only after its checks are attacked with realistic texts), so a judgment written another way is read from its movement only, with no value; the result per claim (each request of the petition) and the movements of the two years before are not read; the client's money (phase 10) does not hold the closing yet, only a release not received does.
- **Phase 7 of the same plan, the fees and the costs of a case, first cut (2026-11-10).** Migration `0131_case_fees` (pgTAP `0130`, 9 assertions with two firms; `0003` follows it): how the firm charges is its fee terms (`fee_terms`: an entry, installments, a share of success with its minimum and its share of a settlement, per kind of problem or every one), confirmed by who decides for the firm with the second factor (`save_fee_terms`, decision 4; the terms before archived, never deleted). When a client signs the fee contract (the envelope of a `contrato` paper signed), the terms in force are the case's (`case_fees`, `fee_terms_for`: the kind of problem's, else every one's) and, where the books are open, ONE writes the entry (due in a week) and each installment a month apart as bills to receive of the case (`case_fees_bills`, source `fees`); a partner, a lawyer or the controladoria sets a case's fees by hand or from the terms (`save_case_fees`, for a contract signed outside ONE or an imported case). When a release the firm registered as received arrives (0130), ONE writes the success fee (the share of what arrived, a settlement's share for a settlement, never under the minimum) as a bill to receive, once per release (`finance_bills.fact_id`). The costs of a case (`case_costs`) are written by anybody of the firm who works on it (`save_case_cost`: what, how much, when, who paid, who owes it back; one the firm paid and the client owes is a bill of the reimbursement), and absorbed by who operates the books with the reason (`absorb_case_cost`, its bill cancelled, never deleted). Who reads only the costs of the cases reads the costs and nothing of the fees. The books say how many cases in court have no fees (`firm_finance_attention.fees_missing`, a line of the center for who reads the books). ONE never charges the client: those bills are "Para cobrar ou informar". The app: the card "Honorários e custos" on the case (`casos/[id]/components/fees-card.tsx`: the fees for who reads the books, "Usar os termos do escritório", the costs with who paid and who owes them, "Registrar custo" in `SidePanel`, "Absorver o custo" in `QuickActions`) and the card "Honorários" in `/financeiro/configuracao` (`fee-terms-card.tsx`: the terms per kind of problem, confirmed with the second factor in the panel); `lib/cases/fees.ts` holds the pure rules and words, `fees-actions.ts` the writes. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the terms are typed, not read by ONE's AI from the firm's contract yet; a monthly fee, a fee per act, a fine and interest of the contract and the split with another lawyer or firm are not modeled yet (an agency is never a recipient, Estatuto, art. 34, III and IV); a cost is typed, not read from its photo yet; the success fee is computed on what the release paid, never on the condemnation.
- **Phase 8 of the same plan, the fatal deadline outside ONE and the agenda by month and week (2026-11-10).** Migration `0132_agenda` (pgTAP `0131`, 10 assertions with two firms; `0003` follows it, and `discard_file` is redefined so that a file a cost of a case names (0131) is never forgotten). A deadline is the market's "prazo fatal", and the firm works to an internal one some of the court's business days before it: how many is the firm's (`organizations.internal_deadline_days`, two by default, zero switches it off), said by a partner or the controladoria (`set_internal_deadline_days`, never a support visit, on the record). The day itself is the rule's (`internalDeadline` of `@flyee/rules/deadlines`: backward over the days the count saw, weekends, national holidays and the days the court was closed never counting, a suspension of the terms counting since the firm works on it, never before the first day of the term), computed by the app wherever it is shown (`lib/deadlines/facts.ts` `withInternal`, `lib/agenda/agenda.ts`). Who answers for each deadline and hearing is deduced, never assigned (`agenda_deadline_people`, `agenda_hearing_people`): the lawyers of the lawsuit, else those its publication was addressed to, else who is responsible for the case, and for a hearing who attends it too; only people still of the firm. `firm_agenda` (anybody of the firm, at most 62 days, under a module or every one) answers the items with who answers for each, the firm's internal days and the person's own preferences. Each person has an e-mail of the morning (`agenda_preferences.digest_off`, on by default; stopped on the agenda's panel or in one click from the e-mail, `stop_agenda_digest` by the person's own unsubscribe token, RFC 8058, at `/api/public/agenda/parar`) and a calendar link of their own (`start_agenda_calendar`: the app shows the address once and hands the database only the SHA-256 of its token, which no member reads, `calendar_hash` out of the column grant; a new link ends the one before, `end_agenda_calendar` ends it, and the preferences go with the membership). The partners and the controladoria read the whole firm in both (`agenda_whole_firm`). The backend sends each e-mail once (`agenda_digests`, one per person and day: `agenda_digests_owed`, `reserve_agenda_digest`, `finish_agenda_digest`; a person with nothing that day is recorded `empty` and gets none; a refusal by the provider's quota gives the turn back), on business mornings of Brasília from seven to noon, with the day's fatal and internal deadlines and hearings and those of the next 48 hours, marking what changed in the last day, the test cases out (the sweep `agenda-digests`, every 15 minutes, the clock's alone; `lib/agenda/digest-work.ts`; nothing is reserved without `RESEND_API_KEY`, so the e-mail goes the morning the key exists; the template `agenda-digest` of `packages/email`). The link (`/api/public/agenda/<token>.ics`, `agenda_calendar_feed`, `lib/agenda/ics.ts`) is an iCalendar file of thirty days back to 180 ahead: each fatal and internal deadline a whole day, each hearing at its moment in UTC (so each calendar shows it in its own zone, Manaus's included), a cancelled one kept as cancelled; an e-mail and an event name the act or "Prazo" and the case's number, never a typed title nor anybody of the case. The app: `/agenda` has Lista (the hearings' list as before), Dia, Semana and Mês (`agenda/agenda-calendar.tsx`: each day's fatal deadlines, hearings by their hour in their zone and internal deadlines, of "Minha agenda", the whole firm or one colleague; a month's day is a button the arrows move between; on a phone the week and the month are read as a list of days), the mode and the day in the address (`?modo=`, `?dia=`), and "Assinar no calendário" (`agenda/agenda-subscribe-panel.tsx`, in `SidePanel`: the e-mail's switch, the link made, copied, opened as `webcal:` and ended after a confirmation, that Outlook or Google may take hours to a day to show a change and ONE and the e-mail are what count, and the firm's internal days for a partner or the controladoria; `?assinar=1` opens it, where the e-mail's own link leads). Each open deadline card says "Prazo fatal em" and "Prazo interno em"; `/prazos` and the list of `/agenda` have the number cards and the list bar of phase 0. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the internal days are one number for the firm, not per kind of act; a calendar subscribed on the iPhone, Outlook and Google was not tried here (the file is checked by its tests, the times in UTC); the e-mail ran against no provider (its template renders in the e-mail package's tests); a court whose calendar is not on record counts the days it was closed as working ones in the internal day, which then comes later than it should; the two points the plan of the case journey corrects (the intimation by the court's portal before the DJEN, the comarca taken from the client's city) are still the count's assumptions, said by the screen as before.
- **Phase 9 of the same plan, the search at the top, the people and the marketing segments (2026-11-10).** Migrations `0133_people` (pgTAP `0132`, 9 assertions with two firms) and `0134_marketing_segments` (pgTAP `0133`, 7 assertions with two firms; `0003` follows both; both applied to the development project the same day). **The search at the top** finds, beside the cases it found before, the firm's people by a name in any order and without accents, a CPF or a telephone by its last eight digits (with or without the ninth), and the WhatsApp conversations by their name or number (`firm_people_search`, anybody of the firm, a few of each, who has a case going first; `lib/search/global-search.ts` `readPeopleSearch`, `globalHits`; `components/layout/search/use-global-search.ts`): people first, then cases, then conversations, each opening its page, what was typed in the request's body. **The page of a person** (`/pessoas/[id]`, `firm_person`): their data (changed on their case, where they are read and checked), their cases with their role, the lawsuits, the documents, the conversations at their telephone, the notices sent, their word on marketing and, for who decides for the firm, their data. **"Não quero receber marketing"** is a person's word with how and when (`people.marketing_off_at`, `marketing_off_how`): recorded by anybody of the firm (`set_person_marketing`) or said on their own link (`client_link_marketing`, the backend's; the card "Novidades do escritório", `c/[token]/client-marketing.tsx`); it takes them out of every segment and never stops the notices of their lawsuit. **A person's data** (`person_data_requests`): who decides for the firm, with the second factor and never in a support visit, exports everything the firm keeps of them (`person_data_export`: the data, cases, lawsuits, the documents' index, the accounts, the acceptances, the notices and the messages at their telephone, a JSON file for them) and answers a request to erase it (`erase_person`): refused with its reason and on the record while a case of theirs is going or a lawsuit of theirs is not closed (LGPD, art. 7, VI); else the telephone, the e-mail, the address and the rest of their data go, and what the AI read of their documents and accounts (the runs keep their cost), and the name and the CPF stay (art. 16, I). **The screen Pessoas** (`/pessoas`, in the menu after Casos): the number cards of the people registered, with a case going, without and who asked for no marketing, each a view; the numbers of who the firm serves by origin, age band, kind of problem, outcome of the lawsuit, city, airline and profession (`firm_people_numbers`, the test cases out; `people_facts`); the list with the bar (`firm_people`, searched by a name, a CPF or a telephone, each row opening the person). **The segments** (`marketing_segments`, in the registration pattern of phase 0): a set of filters with a name (origins, ages, kinds of problem, outcomes, a case going, airlines, cities, states, professions; `segment_people_ids`), made, changed, archived with its reason and brought back by who decides for the firm or the controladoria (`may_manage_segments`), its count shown live while it is made (`segment_count`); only once a partner accepted, with the second factor, the term by which the firm takes the use of the data in marketing (`firm_marketing_terms`, the words kept as accepted; the platform's text in `platform_settings` `marketing_term`, for the curator and the contract with ONE); never a minor (by birth date, or a claimant a guardian answers for), who asked for no marketing, anybody whose data were erased, nor a test case; exported to Excel in the browser with the columns chosen, with the second factor, on the record (who, when, which segment, how many, which columns; `export_marketing_segment`, at most 20,000 people). ONE sends no campaign. The app: `lib/people/people.ts` (pure, tested), `read.ts`, `actions.ts`, `search-actions.ts`; `pessoas/people-view.tsx`, `segments-card.tsx`, `pessoas/[id]/person-view.tsx`. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the search does not find an agency's lead (the plan of the case journey hands the agency's passenger to the firm on the agreement, not built); "Nova pessoa" in the top's "Adicionar" waits for the same plan (a person enters ONE with a case today); the reuse of a returning client's identity and proof of address in the next case is not built yet (a document's file is one case's, so the backend copies it in storage, a slice of its own); a person with no birth date and no guardian counts as an adult in a segment; the term's words are the platform's proposal, for the owner's lawyer; the export of a person's data is a JSON file, not a PDF.
- **Phase 10 of the same plan, the client's money, first cut (2026-11-10).** ONE never holds, receives or passes on money (decision 1): it splits, warns and records, and the firm pays at its bank. Migration `0135_client_money` (pgTAP `0134`, 8 assertions with two firms; `0003` follows it; applied to the development project the same day). Each court's way of paying is data (`court_payment_paths`: whether the court pays the client's part straight to them, as an account or a Pix, setting the fees apart, Estatuto, art. 22, § 4º; the TJGO seeded as the firm's path, unconfirmed until read in the act in force). A release the firm registered as received (0130) owes its split (`money_releases`, by the court's way); the backend splits it by claimant with the rule `repasse-v1` (`@flyee/rules/payout`, pure and tested: each claimant's part, their own value or an equal part; the success fee kept only with a fee contract the client signed, else held as the part in discussion (Código de Ética, art. 48, § 2º); the costs the firm advanced with their receipt; the fee never above what the client receives (art. 50), the excess held; a minor's part held whole with no fee) and the database records it only when every part adds up to what arrived (`record_money_split`; the sweep `client-money` and right after a receipt is registered, `lib/finance/money-work.ts`).
