Each part (`money_shares`) is passed on within the setting's 5 business days (`client_money.transfer_days`, ONE's proposal: no law sets one) and is red once late; a part held for a partner is decided with the second factor (`decide_money_hold`: the fee lowered to the limit, the part passed on whole with the fee as a bill to receive, or the authorization sent again); what the judge ordered of a minor's part is recorded by a partner or a lawyer (`record_minor_order`: deposited, released to the representative, with accounts to the court); the transfer paid at the bank is recorded by who operates the books (`record_money_transfer`). The client sees on their link "Valores do seu processo" (`client_link_money`, the backend's): their part and a child's they answer for, what the firm kept by the contract, when it comes; gives the account they receive in, always in their own CPF (a Pix key that is their CPF, or an account they say is theirs; `client_payment_accounts`, shown by its last digits); and confirms they received; the card says the firm never asks for money to release a value. Who reads the books reads the parts (`firm_money_shares`), each case's card "Dinheiro do caso" (`casos/[id]/components/money-card.tsx`) and the page "Repasses a clientes" (`/financeiro/repasses`, linked from the books' home: owed, late, held, passed on), over the shared list `components/finance/money-shares.tsx`; the center of what waits says the transfers late and the parts held (`firm_finance_attention.money`). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the statement's line of the release is not yet matched to it by ONE (a person registers the receipt, as in phase 6), nor the transfer's line to the part (a person records it); the court's direct path only says the parts, the fees set apart are not yet matched to the court's order; the accountability statement with a public code, its notice to the client and the question to the firm's person on WhatsApp when no statement answers are not built yet; the client is asked for the account only once a part exists (the plan asks it before the request of the release, with a new link when the old one expired); the check of the condemnation's update (Lei 14.905/2024) belongs to the plan of the case journey; the success-fee bill of phase 7 stays beside a fee the firm kept from the money, for a person to settle; the power of attorney's power to receive is not yet read before offering the firm's path.
- **Phase 11 of the same plan, the firm's numbers, first cut (2026-11-10).** Migration `0136_firm_numbers` (pgTAP `0135`, 5 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project the same day). One set behind every number (`firm_lawsuit_numbers`, for partners, lawyers and the controladoria, `reads_firm_numbers`): each lawsuit the firm filed with its case's airline and kind of problem, the court, the comarca the court records, the kind of lawsuit, the day it was filed and the day its case opened, its judgment (the latest not corrected) with whether a person checked it, a settlement, the judgment become final, the first money received, the value per claimant, whether its case ended and how many of its facts ONE read and nobody checked; the test cases out unless asked (`?testes=1`). The app groups that very set (`lib/numbers/lawsuits.ts`, pure and tested), so a number always equals its list: by airline, kind of problem, court, comarca or kind of lawsuit, each group with how many lawsuits, the success rate (won in whole or in part, of the judged), the value per claimant, the median days to the judgment and the firm's own time from the case opening to the filing, never an average, median or rate of fewer than five (it says the sample instead); "Só o que uma pessoa conferiu" leaves out what ONE read and nobody checked; the cohorts by the month of the filing (filed, judged, final, executing, paid, closed, success, lost); each group opens its lawsuits, and "Baixar em Excel" takes the set as it stands. Each case's money for who reads the books (`firm_case_margins`: the fees received against its direct costs). The app: `/numeros` (`numeros/numbers-view.tsx`), in the menu for who oversees the firm. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the projection of wins per month, the cut by the judge's unit and the import of the old system's past results are not built yet; the jurimetry of the plan of the case journey (public and paid data by court) is not a source yet; the comarca is the one the lawsuit records; the value per claimant is the judgment's, never what was received.
- **Phase 12 of the same plan, the books' reports, the month closing, the invoices and the accountant's package, first cut (2026-11-10).** Migration `0137_finance_reports` (pgTAP `0136`, 6 assertions with two firms; `0003` follows it; applied to the development project the same day). Managerial reports, said so on every screen and file (never the accounting nor the Livro Caixa; ONE computes no tax, only what was posted appears): the month's income statement by competence (`finance_report`, `finance_month_lines`: revenue, deductions, costs, expenses with the pro-labore, the financial result, the losses of receivables written off on a line of their own, the result and the distribution of profits below it; the clients' money, the transfers and the partners' capital out of it, Código Civil, art. 668), against the month before and the same month a year before, saying "Tributos deste mês ainda não lançados" while none was posted; the cash flow by the direct method with the clients' money in a block of its own; the next 13 weeks from the open bills and today's balance, the clients' money never counted (`finance_forecast`). A month closes once its checklist is green (`finance_month_check`: no line of its statements waiting, every account with a statement to its end, no client's money to set apart), by who configures the books with the second factor (`close_finance_month`) or by ONE on the 25th of the next month (`close_finance_months_by_one`, the daily sweep `finance-closing`), said as ONE's that may err, and is reopened with a reason and the second factor (`reopen_finance_month`; `finance_closings`). The data of each invoice of fees received, the firm's part only (`finance_invoice_data`), and the invoice the firm issued recorded against its receipt, which closes it (`record_finance_invoice`, `finance_invoices`); ONE issues no invoice and keeps no certificate. "Baixar o pacote do contador" writes the month's income statement, cash flow and invoices to a workbook in the browser, with each case by its number and no client's name. The app: `/financeiro/relatorios` (`relatorios/reports-view.tsx`; `lib/finance/reports.ts` pure, `reports-actions.ts`), linked from the books' home. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a closed month still takes entries (only the report says it is closed); the budget against the actual, the revenue projection, the expected fees in the forecast, the cost-centre and partners' reports, the reading of an issued invoice by ONE's AI and the registration of the accountant with the e-mail of the package (the e-mail package carries no attachment yet) are not built; the package holds the reports, not the Livro Caixa's movement nor the receipts; "Levar tudo do escritório" does not carry the books yet.
- **The test tools reach the books (2026-11-10).** The owner asked whether the simulation tools carried a test of the plan from the passenger to the books, end to end; four were missing. Migration `0138_test_tools` (pgTAP `0137`, 6 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project the same day, where `0137`, `0132`, `0133` and `0012` pass). **A bank's statement of the test cases**: the test court's "Baixar extrato de teste" (`/t/[token]/tribunal`) writes an OFX the books import (`test_court_statement`, the backend's, by the test chat's link: each release of a test lawsuit as the court's money in, with the words the books read as a client's money, each client's part owed or passed on as the firm's Pix out with the client's name and CPF, each bill open due within three days as paid or received on its day, and the month's bank fee; `lib/test-court/statement.ts`, pure and tested: every line keeps its id, so importing it again adds nothing). **The test cases in Pessoas**: while a firm has no real case, the numbers and the segments count its test cases and say so (`firm_has_real_case`; `people_facts` and `firm_people_numbers` redefined, `tests_counted`), as the controladoria does; the first real case takes them out. **The e-mail of the morning, seen**: "Ver o e-mail desta manhã" in the agenda's panel shows the e-mail ONE would send the person today (`previewMorningEmail` in `lib/agenda/actions.ts`, the same lines and words as the digest's worker), and says when none goes (no provider's key, no business day, nothing in the 48 hours). **A signature simulated**: on a test case only, "Simular assinatura" on a paper waiting to be signed (`test_signature_to_simulate`: who writes on the case, never a support visit, on the record as `signature.simulated`; `simulateTestSignature` in `lib/signatures/actions.ts` keeps a copy of the paper as its signed document through `record_signature_signed`, its evidence saying it was simulated), so a fee contract signed starts the fees (0131) and a power of attorney signed frees the filing without ZapSign or gov.br. Known limits: the statement is of the firm's books as they stand (a bill of a real case, open and due, goes in it too); the simulated signature's file is the paper as ONE issued it.
- **What the plan's first cuts left behind, put right (2026-11-10).** The owner asked whether the steps had their new names and what else of the plan was forgotten. The steps of a case's way say "Inicial" (where the piece is written) and "Distribuição" (the first filing) wherever a step is named (the top of a case, the list's views and its little bar, the board, the stock, the home's and the module's progress), as the owner decided on 2026-11-07; the cards keep "Peças" and "Protocolo", and the stages of a case (`case-status-*`) keep theirs. A person's data reach the client's money (migration `0139_person_data_money`, pgTAP `0138`, 5 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project the same day, where `0138` and `0132` pass): `person_data_export` carries their parts of a release, the account they receive in, their declarations of residence and their answers on free justice, and `erase_person` takes the account they receive in and what the server saw of their requests on the link, and is refused on the record (`money_owed`) while a part of theirs is owed, held or waits for the judge. The CI run of 50a57d1 failed on the menu's test, which did not expect Pessoas and Números (fixed in 42b7adc). What else the plan asks and its first cuts did not build is in each phase's known limits above.
- **A bill or an entry starts from its document (2026-11-10), the first of what the plan's first cuts left behind.** Migration `0140_finance_documents` (pgTAP `0139`, 7 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project the same day, where `0139`, `0022` and `0012` pass): a document of the books is a file of its own purpose (`finance`), reserved only by who writes in them (`reserve_finance_upload`; the generic reservation refuses it) and read, its row and so its download, only by who reads them (`files_select_member` redefined); ONE reads it for the form (task `financeiro.documento.leitura`, Sonnet 5.5, a shadow task, so what a document says reaches only who sent it; `finance_document_reading_allowed`, the setting `finance.document_reads`, 200 a day): what it is (a boleto, a utility bill, an invoice, a receipt, a court's guide, the DAS, the DARF, the ISS, a payslip, the FGTS guide, a proof of payment), who receives, the value, the due day, the month it is of, the day it was paid, the typed line and the category of the firm's chart (by its number, never an id). The rule `lib/finance/documents.ts` (pure, tested) keeps only what passes the form's checks and gives a typed line that checks the last word on the value and the due day; a line that does not check is left out and said. "Nova conta" opens on the document (`DocumentSlot`), the typed line and "Preencher à mão" being the other ways in; an entry takes its receipt as an option, and ONE fills the value, the day, who received and the category from it; each field read says "Lido do documento" until somebody writes over it, and the panel says ONE may err and what to check. The document is kept with the bill (the first month of a series) or the entry (`attach_finance_document`, `finance_bills.file_id`, `finance_entries.file_id`), opened from their quick actions ("Ver o documento"), never forgotten under them (`discard_file` redefined), and one sent for a form nobody saved is taken back after a day by the sweep `finance-documents` (`finance_unused_documents`). Tried with the real model on drawn documents: a rent boleto, a DAS and a Pix receipt each read in its kind, value, day, month and category in 3 to 4 seconds; the model miscopied the 47 digits of the boleto's line, which the rule left out. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a typed line read from a photo is seldom whole, so the person pastes it from the bank's app when the panel says it did not check; a payslip's names and salaries are read by whoever reads the books (the plan keeps them to who operates or configures); a statement or a card's bill in PDF is still not read.
- **The bills that repeat become ONE's series, and the default of what the firm is owed is seen (2026-11-10).** Migration `0151_finance_series` (pgTAP `0150`, 7 assertions with two firms; numbered after 0141 to 0150, which the Casos session holds; applied to the development project the same day). A movement of the books (an expense or an income, never a transfer nor a client's money, nor the payment of a bill) seen once in each of the setting's last three whole months (`finance.series_months`) with the same counterparty (or, with none, the bank's memo without its numbers, `finance_series_key`), the same way and values within the setting's 20 percent of each other (`series_spread_percent`), and with no bill of that counterparty and way still open, becomes a series of ONE's (`finance_series`, on the record as `finance.series_made`); ONE writes its next bill on the day of the month it is usually paid, once the bill before is no longer open and within the setting's 31 days ahead (`series_ahead_days`; a bill of `source` `series` marked `by_one`), and the statement settles it as any bill (`finance_series_round` per firm, `finance_series_sweep` for every firm with its books open, the daily sweep `finance-series`). Who operates the books stops a series with its reason and the second factor (`stop_finance_series`): its bills still open are cancelled with that reason, and the stopped series keeps ONE from making that counterparty a series again. Who reads the books reads the series (`firm_finance_series`): `/financeiro/contas` lists them on the views to pay and to receive, each with its next bill and "Parar a série", and a bill ONE wrote for one says so. The view "Para cobrar ou informar" opens on the default of the receivables (`receivableAging` of `lib/finance/bills.ts`, pure and tested): how many and how much by how long past their day (1 to 30, 31 to 60, 61 to 90, 91 to 180 and over 180 days) and who owes the most. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a bill paid twice in one month, or whose value moved more than the spread (a power bill in summer), is not made a series; the day is the average of the days it was paid, at most the 28th; a series is made only from the entries of the books, so a month whose statement was never imported breaks it.
- **The receipt to the client (2026-11-10).** Migrations `0152_client_receipts` and `0153_receipt_files` (pgTAP `0151`, 13 assertions with two firms; `0003` and `0045` follow it; both applied to the development project the same day, where `0151` passes). Each payment of a bill to receive of a case (a person's, or ONE's from the statement) owes a receipt (`finance_receipts`, by a trigger on `finance_bill_payments`), from the person of the case the bill names, else its first adult claimant, else who answers for a minor (`case_bill_payer`), for what the firm recorded as received (what went to the bill and the interest above it). Where the firm lets ONE (`client_receipt`, a decision of the catalog with no task, ONE's by default), the backend claims it under a number (`claim_finance_receipts`), reads what it says (`finance_receipt_to_issue`: the firm and its CNPJ when `billing_cpf_cnpj` has one, the payer and their CPF, the value, the day, what for, the case's number) and issues it as a document of the firm with its public code (kind `recibo`, one per receipt whatever the tries: `lib/finance/receipt-work.ts`, the words and the value written out in `lib/finance/receipts.ts`, pure and tested), recorded by `record_finance_receipt` (a try that fails gives it back, and after the setting's five it waits for a person); right after a person settles a bill, decides a line or finishes a statement, and in the sweep `finance-receipts` (also while ONE is used). Kept to a person, a receipt waits until who operates the books asks for it (`request_finance_receipt`, the second factor), as one that failed does. A payment undone (the entry cancelled, ONE's act undone) voids its receipt and revokes its document, which the public check then says. A receipt's document and file are read only by who reads the books (the policies of `issued_documents` and `files` redefined; whether a file is a receipt's is asked past the policies, `file_is_receipt` of 0153, since asked under them a receipt hidden from its reader let its file show), and the client reads theirs on their link, under the money (`client_link_receipts`, `client_link_receipt_file`, the backend's; `c/[token]/client-receipts.tsx`, hidden while there is none). `/financeiro/contas` says on each receivable of a case where its receipt stands, with "Ver o recibo" or "Emitir o recibo", and the receipts count in the work of ONE (`recibo_cliente`, said only to who reads the books). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a receipt is no invoice (the firm issues its NFS-e outside ONE, phase 12); the words of the receipt are the platform's, for the curator; a bill of no case, or of a case with nobody named, gets no receipt; the receipts of payments recorded before 0152 are not issued; the receipt is not sent on WhatsApp (the client reads it on the link).
- **The open bills of the old system, by spreadsheet (2026-11-10).** Migration `0154_finance_import` (pgTAP `0152`, 8 assertions with two firms; `0003` follows it). A firm that arrives from another system brings the bills it still has to pay and to receive from a CSV or an Excel workbook, read in the person's browser by the portfolio's own reader and never uploaded (`lib/finance/legacy-bills.ts`, pure and tested: each column found by the names systems give it, "Tipo", "Favorecido", "Cliente", "Descrição", "Valor", "Vencimento", "Competência", "Categoria", "Processo", "Linha digitável"; the way read from a column, a negative value or what the person says for a file of one way; each line a bill or left out with why; `lib/finance/legacy-file.ts` hashes each line's key and keeps a typed line only where it checks). Who operates the books sends the lines a few hundred a call with the second factor (`import_finance_bills`, `finance_imports`): each becomes a bill of `source` `import`, its category the firm's of that name and way, else the chart's "Outras" of that way, said on the line, and its case the firm's of that lawsuit's number; a line is told apart by its key (`finance_bills.import_key`), so the same file brought twice opens nothing twice, while two equal installments are two bills. An import is undone whole (`undo_finance_import`): each of its bills still open with nothing paid is cancelled with the reason, never deleted, and one paid stays; the same file then brings again what the undoing cancelled. The app: "Importar contas em aberto" in the "Mais ações" of `/financeiro/contas` (`contas/import-panel.tsx` in `SidePanel`: the file, the way when it has none, what it brings before anything is written, the lines left out and why, the progress, what came of it, and the latest imports with "Desfazer a importação" confirmed with what it cancels and what stays; `lib/finance/import-actions.ts`). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the names of the columns of the pilot firm's system were not seen, so a file whose columns are named otherwise is refused with what to rename (no screen to say which column is which yet); a bill already paid in the old system is not brought (only what is open); a category is matched by its exact name; the receipt of a client's bill brought this way is issued only once it is received in ONE.
- **The client's money in the books, read from the statement (2026-11-10).** Migration `0155_client_money_books` (pgTAP `0153`, 9 assertions with two firms; `finance_decide_lines` redefined from 0129, `client_link_money` and `firm_money_shares` from 0135). What the first cut of phase 10 left to a person, ONE reads in the bank's statement: a credit the books read as a client's money that is the money of a release the firm waits for (an `alvara` with nothing received, ordered in the setting's 180 days before) is that release received, by ONE (`money_release_for_line`: the memo carrying the lawsuit's number, else the only release waiting whose ordered value the credit fits, up to the setting's 25 percent above it, a judicial deposit earning its correction; `lawsuit_facts.received_by_one`, `received_entry_id`), and the split (0135) and the success fee (0131) follow as when a person records it; a debit of the same value as a client's part owed, its memo with the client's name (first and last word) or the middle six digits of their CPF, as banks mask it, is that part passed on, by ONE, on the line's day (`money_share_for_line`; `money_shares.paid_by_one`, `paid_entry_id`). Once a release that fell in the firm's account is split, the fees and the costs the firm kept leave the client's money and enter as revenue in that account, two entries that move nothing (`money_releases_keep`, `money_releases.entry_id`, `kept_at`), and settle the success fee of that release and the costs the client owed back, oldest first (the success fee's payment then owes its receipt, 0152). Undoing ONE's line undoes what it recorded (`finance_entries_undo_money`): a part goes back to owed; a release not split yet goes back to waiting, its release row and its unpaid success fee with it; once split, it stays received for a person to correct on the case. The client's link asks for the account they receive in as soon as money is expected on the case (a settlement, a judgment won, a deposit or a release; `client_link_money.expected`), not only once a part exists. The app says it where it is read: the firm's list of parts ("Repassado em ..., lido do extrato pelo ONE, que pode errar"), the case's card "Resultado" ("Recebido ... lido do extrato pelo ONE") and the client's card "Valores do seu processo". The test court's statement carries the lawsuit's number on a release and the client's name and masked CPF on a Pix, so importing it after a release and again after the split walks the whole way. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a release whose money came with no lawsuit's number in the memo and beside another waiting release of a value it also fits is left to a person; a Pix to the client from another account of the firm, or split in two, is not matched; the fees kept are written on the day of the split, not on the day the money arrived; a part held and later decided does not move its fee into revenue by itself.
- **The accountability statement to the client (2026-11-10).** Migration `0156_money_statements` (pgTAP `0154`, 9 assertions with two firms; `0003` follows it; `one_work_acts` redefined from 0152, the policy of `issued_documents` and `file_is_receipt` from 0152 and 0153). A client's part passed on (by a person, or by ONE from the statement) or paid by the court straight to them owes its accountability statement (`money_statements`, by a trigger on `money_shares`), which ONE issues as a document of the firm with its public code (kind `prestacao`; `lib/finance/statement-work.ts`, the words in `lib/finance/money-statement.ts`, pure and tested): what the court released and when, the client's part, the fees kept with their percent of the fee contract, the costs reimbursed, what was held and why, what was passed on, when and to which account by its last digits, and that the firm never asks for money to release a value; nothing in it names the platform. It follows the receipts' decision (`client_receipt`, "Emitir o recibo e a prestação de contas ao cliente"): kept to a person, it waits for who operates the books to ask for it (`request_money_statement`, the second factor), as one that failed does. A part that goes back to owed (its Pix undone) voids its statement and revokes its document; passed on again, it owes a new round (`round`, its own document). Its document and file are read only by who reads the books. It is issued right after a part is passed on, a statement is finished and in the sweep of the client's money (`client-money`, which also splits the releases ONE read). The app: the firm's list of parts says where each statement stands, with "Ver a prestação de contas" or "Emitir a prestação de contas"; the client's card "Valores do seu processo" offers "Baixar a prestação de contas" under each part passed on (`client_link_money_statements`, `client_link_money_statement_file`); it counts in the work of ONE (`prestacao_contas`). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the client is not told on WhatsApp that it was issued (a template of its own would carry it); its words are the platform's, for the curator; a part held and decided later says what was held when it was passed on.
- **The documents of a client who comes back (2026-11-10).** Migration `0157_document_reuse` (pgTAP `0155`, 8 assertions with two firms), the plan's decision 6 and phase 9's limit. While a new case is gathered (`intake`, `documents`, `signature`), each of its claimants and guardians who has, in another case of the same firm (a test case only from a test case), an accepted identity, or a proof of address accepted within the setting's 90 days (`cases.document_reuse`), owes it in the new case, unless the case already has one of that person and kind being sent, kept or accepted (`document_reuse_candidates`, a view only the backend reads; `document_reuses_due`). The backend copies the file in storage (a document's file is one case's) and the database records the copy as an accepted document of the new case, its file measured as the source was, said as brought from the earlier one (`case_documents.source` `reuse`, `reused_from`; `record_document_reuse`, which takes a copy only at the path it files it and answers `not_due` when the case no longer owes it, the copy then removed), told in the timeline as ONE's (`document_reused`, with the earlier case's number). Never the same source twice in a case: a copy a person refuses has the client asked for another, as any document. The sweep `document-reuse` (every five minutes, also while ONE is used; `lib/cases/document-reuse-work.ts`). The case's card of documents says "trazido de um caso anterior do cliente". Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a proof of address is recent by the day the firm accepted it, not by the date printed on the bill; a power of attorney or a contract is never brought (each case signs its own); a copy whose record never came is an object nobody names until the storage sweep removes it; there is no decision of `autonomy_decisions` for it (ONE brings what was accepted, and a person refuses a copy as any document).
- **The released money goes to the firm's account by default (2026-11-10).** The owner's decision of that day, replacing the plan's of 2026-11-07: the money a court releases falls in the firm's account, which passes the client's part on; the court paying the client directly, the fees set apart (Estatuto, art. 22, § 4º), is the second way, taken only where the firm or the case chose it and the court accepts it. Migration `0158_money_path` (pgTAP `0156`, 8 assertions with two firms): `court_payment_paths.direct` says only that the court accepts paying directly, and no longer chooses the way; the firm says its way (`organizations.money_path`, `firm` by default; `set_money_path`, who configures the books, the second factor) and a case may say another (`cases.money_path`; `set_case_money_path`, who operates the books); a release takes `direct` only when the way chosen is direct and its court accepts it (`lawsuit_facts_money_release` redefined from 0135), and one registered keeps the way it took. `case_money_path` is what the case's card reads (the way chosen, whose, whether its court accepts). The app: the card "Caminho do dinheiro liberado" in `/financeiro/configuracao` and the way of the case, with its choice, in the case's card "Dinheiro do caso", shown once money is expected (`components/finance/money-path.tsx`). The plan of the finance says it in its decisions and phase 10.
- **Phase 7, second cut: the forms of the fees, the split with another lawyer, the terms read from the firm's contract and a cost read from its receipt (2026-11-10).** What the first cut left as its known limits. **More forms of the fees** (migration `0159_fee_forms`, pgTAP `0157`, 9 assertions with two firms; `0003` holds a row of the new table, `0127` counts the new category of the chart; applied to the development project the same day): the terms and a case's fees take a monthly fee for a number of months (ONE writes one bill a month from the month after the contract, "Mensalidade dos honorários"), a fee per hearing held and per appeal the firm files for the client (a bill of `source` `act` written once per act by a trigger: a hearing set as held, `hearing:<id>`, a fact `recurso` by the client, `appeal:<id>`; the airline's appeal owes none), and the contract's fine and monthly interest on a fee paid late (`late_fine_percent`, 0 to 20, `late_interest_percent`, 0 to 10). The books' list of bills says, on a fee receivable past its day, what it asks today by the contract (`lateCharge` of `lib/cases/fees.ts`: the fine once and the interest by the days late, thirty a month, on what is still owed; ONE charges nobody). **The split with another lawyer** (`case_fee_splits`): a partner, a lawyer or the controladoria who reads the books splits a case's fees with a lawyer or law firm of the bar (a CPF or CNPJ that checks, a registration written as the bar writes it, "OAB/GO 12345" kept as "GO 12345", a percent, the splits of a case under 100), with the second factor and on the record (`save_fee_split`); nobody without a registration shares fees (Estatuto, art. 34, III and IV: an agency never does); each payment of a fee of the case then owes that lawyer their percent as a bill to pay ONE writes (`split:<payment>:<split>`, category "Honorários de advogados parceiros", `despesa.parceiros`), cancelled when the payment is undone and still unpaid; a split ends with its reason (`end_fee_split`), what was written of it staying. **The terms read from the firm's contract** (`0160_fee_terms_reading`, pgTAP `0158`, 5 assertions with two firms): the panel of the terms in `/financeiro/configuracao` opens new terms on ONE's reading of the firm's fee contract (the library's model that writes the contract, the default first), and offers it again on terms that exist; the task `financeiro.honorarios.leitura` (Sonnet 5.5) answers each value with the contract's own words, and a value fills its field only when those words are in the contract and say that very number (`readFeeTermsOutput` of `lib/cases/fee-terms-reading.ts`: money, a percent in figures or words, a count in figures or words), each field marked "Lido do contrato" until somebody writes over it, what did not check said by name; only who decides for the firm, never in a support visit, within the setting `finance.terms_reads` (20 a day, `fee_terms_reading_allowed`); the reading decides nothing, the person confirms the terms with the second factor as before. Tried with the real model on two invented contracts: every value read and checked in 4 to 6 seconds. **A cost read from its receipt** (`0162_cost_reading`, pgTAP `0161`, 4 assertions with two firms; `prefill_reading_allowed` redefined from 0085 to count it): "Registrar custo" on a case opens on the receipt (a court's guide, a receipt, an invoice, a boleto or a proof of payment; "Preencher à mão" the other way in), the task `caso.custo.leitura` (Sonnet 5.5) reads what was paid, the value and the day (`costReadingOf` of `lib/cases/document-prefill.ts`, the reading `case_cost` of the case's forms, within the firm's readings of a day), each field marked "Lido do comprovante", and the receipt is kept with the cost (`case_costs.file_id`). Tried with the real model on a drawn court's guide and a Pix receipt: both read in 3 to 4 seconds. The app: the card "Honorários e custos" of a case says the new forms in its line of the fees and has "Divisão com outro advogado" (who reads the books; "Dividir com outro advogado" in `SidePanel` with the second factor in place, "Encerrar a divisão" in a `ConfirmActionDialog` with the reason); the terms' panel has the monthly fee, the fees per act and the late charges. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a fee per act is written only while the books are open, and an act of before the books opened owes none; the fine and interest are said, never written as a bill; the split is of what the firm receives of the case's fees, never of the client's money; the contract is read from the library's model, so a firm whose contract is only a PDF sends it to the library first (0079); the card "Honorários e custos" does not yet lock the case through `useCaseAction` while it writes.
- **Phase 12, second cut: a closed month refuses a person's change, the budget, the month by cost centre and by partner, the success fees expected, and the books in the portfolio and in the accountant's package (2026-11-10).** What the first cut left as its limits. Migration `0163_finance_reports_more` (pgTAP `0159`, 9 assertions with two firms; `0003` holds a row of the new table): a month closed and not reopened refuses an entry written by hand and any entry a person cancels or changes whose day or month of competence is in it (`finance_month_closed`, a trigger on `finance_entries` raising `month_closed`, which the books' actions say as "Este mês está fechado"), while ONE's entries from the bank's statement still enter, the report saying how many came after the closing (`closing.changed_after`); `finance_budgets` holds what each category of income and expense should come to in a month, saved by who configures the books for that month and up to eleven after it with the second factor (`save_finance_budget`, on the record), and the report sets each against what the month came to by competence (`finance_budget_lines`, with what came outside the budget); the month's lines say income and expense by cost centre and the partners' categories (`socio.*`) by the counterparty written (`finance_month_lines` redefined from 0137); the forecast says, apart from its weeks, the success fees expected from the judgments won and the settlements of open cases with fees whose money did not come (the condemnation, each claimant's when the court says so, times the case's percent, never under its minimum), and how much of it already became final (`finance_forecast` redefined). Migration `0164_portfolio_books` (pgTAP `0162`, 5 assertions with two firms; `0118` counts the new sheets): "Levar a carteira inteira" carries the books, the sheets `lancamentos` (every entry, the cancelled ones with when and why) and `contas` (every bill with where it stands), whatever case they name (`portfolio_counts`, `firm_portfolio_page` redefined from 0118). The accountant's package gains the month's movement, by day, account, kind, category, cost centre, value and case number, never who it was of nor its words (`readFinanceMonthMovement`). The app: `/financeiro/relatorios` has "Orçamento do mês" (each category with what was planned, what came and the difference, an expense over or an income under its plan marked; "Definir orçamento" in `SidePanel` for who configures), "Por centro de custo", "Por sócio", the expected success fees in the forecast, and on the closing how many entries came after it. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the statement's entries still enter a closed month (a bank's fact), so a closed month may need reopening; the budget is a figure per category and month, not a projection of revenue; the expected fees are the court's figures, never updated by Lei 14.905/2024; the accountant is not registered nor sent the package by e-mail (the e-mail package carries no attachment yet), and an invoice the firm issued is typed, not read by ONE's AI.
- **The invoice the firm issued, read by ONE (2026-11-10).** Phase 12's limit of an invoice typed by hand. Migration `0165_invoice_reading` (pgTAP `0163`, 6 assertions with two firms): "Registrar a nota emitida" in `/financeiro/relatorios` opens on the invoice's PDF or photo, sent as a document of the books (`reserve_finance_upload`, 0140); the task `financeiro.nota.leitura` (Sonnet 5.5, a shadow task, within the books' readings of a day, `finance_document_reading_allowed` redefined) reads its number, the day it was issued, its value, its taker and its verification code against the fee it is of (`readInvoiceOutput` of `lib/finance/invoice-reading.ts`: a number as printed, never a day to come, and a value that is not the fee's said so); the number and the day fill the panel marked "Lido da nota", typing them stays the other way in, and the file is kept with the invoice (`finance_invoices.file_id`, `record_finance_invoice` taking it, a new record without one keeping the file it had; the sweep of unused documents never takes it back), opened from the month's invoices by "Ver a nota". `discard_file` never forgets an invoice's file (`0175_invoice_file_kept`, agreed with the Casos session: migrations apply by name, so `discard_file` is redefined after the latest that redefined it, 0170, and any later redefinition starts from 0175's body). Tried with the real model on a drawn NFS-e: number, day, value, taker and code read in 3.5 seconds. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: an invoice's XML is not read (only its PDF or a photo); the accountant is still not registered nor sent the package by e-mail.
- **The firm's accountant and the month's package by e-mail (2026-11-10).** Phase 12's last limit. Migration `0166_finance_accountant` (pgTAP `0164`, 8 assertions with two firms; `0003` holds a row of each new table): who configures the books registers the firm's accountant (`finance_accountants`: a name, an e-mail, the accounting office and the CRC, one per firm; `save_finance_accountant`, `remove_finance_accountant`, the second factor, never a support visit, on the record), in the card "Contador" of `/financeiro/configuracao` (`configuracao/accountant-card.tsx`: the panel, the latest sendings with what came of each, "Tirar o contador" after a confirmation); who operates the books sends a month's package with "Enviar ao contador" in `/financeiro/relatorios`, to that address and no other, after a confirmation that says where it goes and what it holds, told in an `OutcomeDialog` (`begin_finance_package_send` within the setting `finance.package_sends`, 10 a day, a month that has begun, on the record with the month and never the file; `finish_finance_package_send` with what the provider said, `finance_package_sends`). The server writes the very workbook the download writes (`accountantPackage` of `lib/finance/accountant-package.ts`, pure, shared by both) and keeps no copy; the e-mail (`packages/email`, template `accountant-package`, the first with an attachment: `Message.attachments`) is the firm's, a reply going to who sent it. The button shows only where an e-mail leaves ONE (`emailReady`). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: nothing ran against the provider yet (`RESEND_API_KEY` and a sender of ONE's domain); the package holds the managerial reports and the month's movement, not the receipts' files; a package past the provider's size for an attachment fails and says to download it.
- **The internal deadline by kind of act (2026-11-10).** Phase 8's limit of one internal day for every act. Migration `0167_internal_days_by_act` (pgTAP `0165`, 5 assertions with two firms): an act of the list may have internal days of its own (`organizations.internal_deadline_by_act`, its slug and its days, 0 to 15, 0 switching the internal day off for that act), the firm's days holding for every other act and for a deadline of no act; a partner, who manages a firm with none, or the controladoria sets them (`set_internal_deadline_days_for_act`, taken off with null, never a support visit, on the record); the agenda, the morning's e-mail and the calendar link hand them out beside the firm's days (`firm_agenda`, `agenda_digests_owed`, `agenda_calendar_feed` redefined from 0132), and the app counts each internal day by its act (`internalDaysFrom` and `internalDaysFor` of `lib/agenda/agenda.ts`, read by `/prazos`, the agenda, the e-mail and the link). The agenda's panel ("Assinar no calendário") lists each act under the firm's days with "Como o escritório" or its own days.
- **Números by court unit, and the judgments of the months ahead (2026-11-10).** Phase 11's limits of no cut by the judge's unit and no projection. Migration `0168_numbers_unit` (pgTAP `0166`, 2 assertions with two firms): each lawsuit of the set says the unit the court records (`firm_lawsuit_numbers` redefined from 0136), and `/numeros` groups by "Vara ou juizado" too (`lib/numbers/lawsuits.ts`, the unit a dimension, in the sheet as a column). The card "Previsão de julgamentos" says, for the next six months, the judgments expected and the wins expected from the firm's own history (`winsProjection`: each lawsuit still waiting for its judgment expects it the median days after its filing its court took, won by its court's rate; one past its expected day counts this month; a court with fewer than five judged projects nothing of its lawsuits, said apart): an estimate of the firm's numbers, never a promise of a result. Known limits: the projection is by court, not by unit (a unit has seldom five judged); a unit is matched by the words the court records; the import of the old system's results and the jurimetry of public data are not sources yet.
- **A statement or a card bill in PDF (2026-11-10).** Phase 4's limit of a PDF refused. Migration `0169_statement_pdf` (pgTAP `0167`, 3 assertions with two firms; `0126` now refuses another format in the PDF's place): in `/financeiro/conciliacao` a PDF goes up as a document of the books (taken back by the sweep after a day), the task `financeiro.extrato.pdf` (Sonnet 5.5, a shadow task, within the books' readings of a day, `finance_document_reading_allowed` redefined from 0165) reads each line and the balances, and the app keeps them only when they add up to what the document prints (`readStatementPdfOutput` of `lib/finance/statement-pdf.ts`: a statement's opening balance plus its lines is its closing one; a card bill's lines, their sign turned, are its total; one cent off, a line not read whole, no balances or more than 600 lines, and nothing goes, the screen saying why and the way to the OFX); what it keeps goes to the books as an OFX's lines do, the statement of the format `pdf` (`begin_finance_statement` redefined from 0127), said on the screen as ONE's reading that may err. Tried with the real model on a drawn statement and a drawn card bill: every line read, both adding up, in 3.5 to 5 seconds. Known limits: a PDF of a scanned page is read like any other but a line misread makes the whole statement refused; a line of a PDF has no id of the bank's, so it is told apart by its key, as a CSV's.
- **The interest and the fine of a bill's payment, each apart (2026-11-11).** Phase 5's limit of one figure. Migration `0192_bill_late_charges` (pgTAP `0190`, 8 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project the same day, where `0190`, `0128` and `0159` pass): each payment above what a bill still owed says how much of it was the fine and how much the interest (`finance_bill_payments.fine_cents`, `interest_cents` and `split_by`, adding up to `extra_cents`; both zero is "not split"). A person says it when registering the payment (`settle_finance_bill`, redefined from 0129, with `fine_cents` and `interest_cents` that must add up to what was paid above the rest) or later (`split_finance_payment`: who operates the books, the second factor, on the record; both empty takes the split back). Where nobody says it, a fee of a case paid late is split by the case's contract (`finance_contract_split`: the contract's fine on what was owed, at most the extra, the rest interest; a contract with no interest splits only an extra that is exactly its fine), said as ONE's that may err. The month's income statement takes what was paid above a bill out of the bill's category and puts it on the financial line, the fines and the interest paid or received each apart, and what nobody split on a line of its own (`finance_month_lines`, redefined from 0163); the budget counts the category without it and the charges outside the budget (`finance_budget_lines`, redefined from 0163). The app: the payment's panel asks the fine of what was paid above the bill and shows the rest as interest, filled by the contract where there is one (`LateFields` in `contas/bill-panels.tsx`; `contractSplit` of `lib/finance/bills.ts`, the database's rule); each bill says its fine, its interest and what is not split (`lateChargesOf`); "Separar multa e juros" in its quick actions (`SplitPanel`). Known limits: a bill that is no fee of a case is split only by a person; the contract's split takes the contract's fine whatever the days late, and the rest as interest even when it is more than the contract's interest.
- **The result of each claim of a lawsuit (2026-11-11).** Phase 6's limit of the result per claim. The rule is `pedidos-v1` (`readClaims` of `@flyee/rules/outcome`, tested): from each clause of a judgment's operative part, what it decided of each claim it names (moral damages, material damages, lost profits, the refund of the ticket), granted with the value set beside it (each claimant's when the words say so) or denied; "deixo de condenar" is a denial, "danos morais e materiais" names both, and a clause that names a claim with no decision in it decides nothing. Migration `0193_lawsuit_claims` (pgTAP `0191`, 7 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project the same day, where `0191`, `0129`, `0135` and `0166` pass): a judgment keeps its claims (`lawsuit_facts.claims`, cleaned by `lawsuit_claims_clean`: the known kinds once each, a denial with no value), handed by the worker with the reading of the judgment's publication, whose claims take the place of what was read before (`lawsuit_fact_write`, redefined from 0130), or by a person who records the judgment (`save_lawsuit_fact` passes them through); the judgments read before are read again once from the words kept on them (`claims_read_at`, `record_fact_claims`, the backend's, in the sweep `lawsuit-outcomes`); the firm's numbers carry them (`firm_lawsuit_numbers`, redefined from 0168). The app: under a judgment on the case's card "Resultado", each claim with what was decided and its value; the fact's panel asks, for a judgment, what it decided of each claim; `/numeros` has "Por pedido" (each claim the judgments decided: how many, how many granted, the share granted and the median value per claimant, never of fewer than five; `claimStats` of `lib/numbers/lawsuits.ts`). The round trip of the outcomes (`outcome.db.test.ts`) reads the moral damages of its judgment. Known limits: a claim is read by rule from the words, so one named in words the rule does not know (a daily fine, an obligation to do) is not read; a judgment read before is read again only from the 400 characters kept of it.
- **The case journey plan, phase 0: its five defects put right (2026-11-10).** The plan is the Claude Doc "ONE · Plano: jornada do caso, da qualificação ao cumprimento" (https://claude.ai/artifact/N7DsTMR2T6fpcysWPV4Wqf); its migrations are 0141 to 0150 by agreement with the finance's session (0151 on), five used. **A judgment proposes its terms** (migration `0141_judgment_deadlines`, pgTAP `0140`, 10 assertions with two firms): a publication said to be the lawsuit's judgment (`publications.judgment`, a person's word or the rule's after ONE's triage, 0096 and 0107) has ONE propose embargos de declaração (CPC, art. 1.023; Lei 9.099/1995, art. 49) and the appeal of its lawsuit's kind (recurso inominado, art. 42; apelação, CPC art. 1.003, § 5º; none of an acórdão, none of a publication of no lawsuit), with the days of `deadline_acts`, where the firm lets ONE propose the deadlines of its publications (`publication_deadline`), each act once per publication whoever wrote it first, counted and confirmed as any deadline ONE proposes; a publication that stops being the judgment takes back what nobody confirmed; the app counts them right after the word on the judgment (`settleDeadlineCounts` in `lib/publications/actions.ts` and `triage-work.ts`). **The certificate that the judgment became final, published apart** (`0142_final_from_publication`, pgTAP `0141`, 7 assertions with two firms): every triaged publication but the judgment and a payment is read once more by the rule (`certifiesFinal` of `resultado-v1`: a certification or the past tense, never the order every judgment writes, nor a denial), and a certificate of a lawsuit of an open case becomes its fact `transito` as ONE's, the day the DJEN made it available (`record_publication_final`, `publications.final_read_at`; `lib/courts/outcome-work.ts`); what the DJEN brought more than 30 days before is history. The wait for the voluntary payment is not a deadline of the firm's (it is the defendant's) and joins item 4.6 of the plan. **Resolução ANAC 813/2026** (`0143_anac_813`, pgTAP `0142`, 6 assertions with two firms; `0052` follows it, 23 sources): read in the Diário Oficial on 2026-11-10 (ANAC's page refuses automatic reading), with Resolução 819/2026, which put its art. 20 in force on 2026-11-25; the platform's sources of arts. 20, 26 and 27 of Resolução 400 take the new words and keep the ones of 2016 (`legal_sources.quote_before`, `in_force_from`), art. 27-B joins them, and a piece is handed the words in force on the day of its case's flight, said as such (`case_draft_to_work`); a firm's own source of a key keeps its words. The flight's alert no longer names the means of communication after one hour, which art. 27, I lost (`flight-problem-v3`, `modules/direito-do-passageiro/flight-alerts/words.ts`). **The comarca** (`0145_comarcas`, pgTAP `0144`, 8 assertions with two firms): `court_comarcas` is the platform's table of which comarca each municipality belongs to, by the IBGE's code, read by any account; seeded with Goiás, its 246 municipalities and 127 comarcas from Annex III of the compiled Lei estadual 16.435/2008 at the Casa Civil of Goiás (read on 2026-11-10, what the compilation strikes through left out, the IBGE's names kept where the law spells four otherwise), every row unconfirmed; a planned lawsuit whose comarca is the city as written takes the comarca of that city (`proceedings_comarca`, `comarca_of`: Brazabrantes goes to Goianira), a comarca a person wrote otherwise and a lawsuit the court has stay as they are, and `proceedings.comarca_checked` says whether the table knows it; the case's card "Processos" says the city's comarca, that ONE may err, and "comarca não conferida" where the table does not know it. **A term from the earlier of the two intimations** (`0144_deadline_portal_day`, pgTAP `0143`, 10 assertions with two firms): the rule is `prazo-v2` (`@flyee/rules/deadlines`, `portalOn`): the same act intimated in the court's own system is made on the day the lawyer read it, the next business day when read on a day with none, or on the tenth day unread (Lei 11.419/2006, art. 5º), and the term runs from the earlier of that and the DJEN's, said as an assumption (`portal_first`) while the STJ's Tema 1.180 is not settled; who signs or runs the deadlines gives that day in the deadline's panel (`deadlines.portal_on`, `set_deadline_portal_day`: a term of a publication, from 30 days before it to today; counted and confirmed again), and the backend hands it to the rule (`deadline_to_count`, `record_deadline_count` taking a start before the publication). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the day of the intimation in the system is typed by a person, since reading it from the court's movements waits for the real titles of Projudi's; the units of each comarca and their competence are not in the table yet, and only Goiás is; the comarca table follows the compiled law, not yet the TJGO's own acts; a planned lawsuit planned before 0145 was moved to its comarca when 0145 was applied.
- **A firm clears its cases and clients to start its tests again (2026-11-10).** The owner, cleaning the development project by hand before testing what was built, asked for it as a function of the firm's settings, taking all of the firm's cases, clients and conversations and, with them, the partner agency's passengers at the test chat's numbers. Migration `0161_clear_firm_data` (pgTAP `0160`, 30 assertions with two firms; applied to the development project on 2026-11-10, where `0160` and `0012` pass): `clear_firm_data` takes every case of the firm with what hangs on it, every client (`people`), every WhatsApp conversation and request to stop, the trips ONE follows for the firm, the publications, deadlines and hearings (a hearing still owes the removal of its event from the firm's calendar), the portfolio imports, and the agency's passengers at the test chat's numbers, "(00) 98888-000N", with the children they answer for, their trips and documents (`firm_clear_travelers`); the AI's answers about what went leave the queue and each run keeps its cost and loses its output, as when a person's data are erased (0133); the bell's lines that lead to what went are taken out; a file goes only when no row that stays names it, read from every foreign key that points at `files` (so a reference added later is honoured), and never the books' nor the library's uploads; its bytes are queued in `pax_file_removals` (0072) and removed by the app right after the answer, the daily sweep removing what was left. What stays: the team, the setup and every setting, the library, the connections and the test chat, the partnerships and the agency's other passengers, the books (an entry or a bill of a case that went loses its link), the documents ONE issued (their public code still answers), the audit trail. It is guarded as the gravest act of the firm: who decides for it (`decides_for_firm`), with the second factor, never in a support visit, the word "apagar" typed and checked by the database too, the firm's row locked meanwhile, on the record as `firm.data_cleared` with how many of each went and nothing of anybody, and the firm's other partners and who manages it told on the bell (`firmDataCleared`). The firm's numbering of cases starts at 1 again. `firm_clear_preview` counts what would go for the confirmation. The app: `/settings/dados` ("Dados do escritório", in the settings menu for a partner and for who manages the firm; `settings/dados/data-view.tsx`): the card "Apagar casos e clientes" says what goes, counted, and what stays; the confirmation counts again, asks the second factor in place and the word typed (`ConfirmActionDialog` gained `typedWord`, `children` and `blocked`), and what came of it is said in an `OutcomeDialog`; `lib/firm/clear-data.ts` holds the pure rules (tested), `clear-data-actions.ts` the action. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: in a firm in use it deletes real data and cannot be undone (the screen says so before anything is asked); a finance entry or bill of a case that went stays in the books with no case; the agency's passengers at real numbers stay, and so does what the agency keeps of them.
- **The case journey plan, phase 1: the qualification, the funnel and the closing (2026-11-10).** The owner's definitions of that day: a closing is the power of attorney or the contract signed and then the documents the case needs sent; a delay qualifies from 4 hours late at the arrival; a lead is who got in touch with ONE or with the firm and did not sign the power of attorney. **What counts as a closing** (`0146_closings`, pgTAP `0145`, 8 assertions with two firms; `0114` follows it): `case_closings` says when each case was signed (the first power of attorney or contract that stands signed: one ONE issued whose envelope came back signed, or one the firm added already signed; never ONE's unsigned paper nor one sent to be signed), had its papers (each document `cases.required_documents` names answered by one nobody refused, on the day the first came) and closed (the later of the two), once per case; `firm_closing_goals` counts them by the rule `fechamento-v1` in each goal's period and part of the cases, the real cases only, or the test cases while the firm has none (`tests_counted`, said on `/controladoria` and `/controladoria/metas`, whose "i" says what a closing is). **The rule of qualification** (`@flyee/rules/qualification`, `qualificacao-v1`, 29 cases in a table): a cancellation qualifies unless told 72 hours or more before with none of the owner's five exceptions; a change told late qualifies above 30 minutes (domestic) or one hour (international), told early only with an exception; a delay or a lost connection from 4 hours late at the arrival; boarding denied and a bag always; a downgrade or another problem is a person's (`review`), and a fact the rule needs and lacks is said (`pending`: the arrival, the change, the day); prescription in it (5 years domestic, CDC art. 27; international 2 years for the material damage and 5 for the moral one, STF Temas 210 and 1.240). Migration `0147_qualification` (pgTAP `0146`, 9 assertions with two firms): ONE runs it on every look at a case (`case_qualification_facts`: the incident, the flight day, the affected leg and its arrival as a source said it, a leg abroad, ONE's word on the flight, the interview's answers on the notice and the arrival; `caseQualification` in `lib/cases/qualification.ts`) and the database keeps its word (`cases.qualification`, `record_case_qualification`, told in the timeline when it changes); a case that does not qualify holds its lawsuits (`not_qualified`, the firm's lawyer's wait) until a partner or a lawyer goes on with it, with the reason (`release_case_qualification`, on the record and in the timeline), which is how the firm opens one as an exception; the card "Como este caso vai a juízo" says the rule's word as ONE's that may err, with "Seguir mesmo assim" (`casos/[id]/components/qualification-block.tsx`). At the passenger's yes to ONE's word about a flight, a problem the rule does not qualify opens no case (`one_alert_not_qualified`: the answer and the rule's word kept on the alert, the passenger kept as a person of the firm with the reason, `flight_alerts.person_id`, the firm's word in the conversation where it lets ONE answer, else a person called). The 72-hour notice, which only warned, is the rule's now: the interview's answer that the airline told a week before makes a cancellation not qualify. **The funnel** (`0148_funnel`, pgTAP `0147`, 7 assertions with two firms): each person shows the furthest point of any of their opportunities (`people_funnel`): cliente (signed, or a lawsuit in court), lead (a case going with no signature, or a conversation with the firm and no case), não qualificado (the rule said no and nobody went on, with the reasons) or contato; Pessoas has the four as views of the group "Funil", counted as the lists are (`firm_people`, `firm_people_numbers.funnel`), and each row says its point and, for one not qualified, why. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: ONE's word on a flight carries no notice nor the size of a change, so at the yes a cancellation qualifies and a change waits for its size (the interview's answers decide later); the exceptions known by rule are only the trip to the airport (the interview's "aviso_local"), the others a lawyer says by going on with the case; a conversation with no person of the firm is no lead yet (phase 2 makes every contact a person); a closing is computed, so a document refused after a closing moves the closing to the one sent after it.
- **The case journey plan, phase 2, first part: the firm registers its own passengers (2026-11-10).** Item 2.2 of the plan: a firm with no agency, or a client of its own who travels, has ONE follow the trip as an agency's passenger is followed. Migration `0170_firm_passengers` (pgTAP `0169`, 9 assertions with two firms; `0003` holds a row of the new table): a passenger of the firm is a person of the firm (`people`) with no case, written by anybody of the firm (`firm_save_person`, never a support visit, on the record; a CPF the firm has is that person's, answered with them, `cpf_taken`), and the trip ONE follows of them is a followed trip of theirs (`tracked_trips.person_id`, `consent_channel` `firm`), registered with the passenger's agreement in the platform's words (`platform_settings` `firm_trip_consent`, read by the panel where the database keeps them) and how it was given (`firm_trip_consent_how`: WhatsApp, telephone, in person, e-mail, the contract), refused with no WhatsApp, to a WhatsApp that asked to stop, with legs that are no flights or a trip that ended (`firm_follow_trip`); the documents the person sent for it (an identity, a proof of address, a ticket, a boarding pass, only files that person uploaded) stay with the trip (`tracked_trip_documents`, never forgotten under it, `discard_file` redefined) and, at the passenger's yes, the case takes the firm's person, never a second record of them, and those documents, each of its kind and person, checked by ONE as any document that arrives (`one_open_case_from_alert` redefined from 0074). A trip ONE follows of a person with no case makes them a lead (`people_funnel` redefined from 0148); the firm stops following it (`firm_stop_trip`, on the record). A trip at a test chat's number, with the firm's test chat on, is the test chat's (label `escritorio`, one per firm, a new one taking its place), tested with the agency's passenger's tools, its days the firm's (`test_chat_set_legs` redefined from 0084). ONE's first word to a real passenger still waits for the platform's release (0051). The app: "Novo passageiro" in the bar of Pessoas and in the top's "Adicionar" (group "Pessoas"), the module's panel loaded through the registry (`firmPassenger`, `components/people/module-people.tsx`), which starts from the identity and the ticket, ONE reading the name, the CPF and the flights into the form (typing is the contingency), then the agreement in the platform's words and how it was given, and says in an `OutcomeDialog` what now holds (`modules/direito-do-passageiro/passengers/new-passenger-panel.tsx`); on a person's page, "Viagens que o ONE acompanha" (`personSection`, `passengers/person-trips.tsx`: each trip with where it stands, its flights, the agreement's day, the documents kept with it, the case its yes opened, "Parar de acompanhar" confirmed with what stops and what stays, and "Acompanhar uma viagem" for a client who travels again); the test chat puts the firm's passenger in front as the agency's (`testTripInFront`). `passengers/actions.ts` holds the writes and the read, `passengers.ts` the pure rules (tested). The round trip `apps/web/src/modules/direito-do-passageiro/passengers/passengers.db.test.ts` (part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`) registers a passenger at the test WhatsApp with the identity, cancels the flight in the test chat, has the passenger answer SIM and finds the case with the firm's person and the identity, then the same CPF under another name taken as the same person and a trip stopped. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a child travels on the trip of the adult who answers for them and is not registered here; the agreement is the firm's word of it (no link for the passenger to agree on yet); the test chat holds one passenger of the firm at a time, a new one taking the place of the one before; the documents of a trip stopped stay with it.
- **The case journey plan, phase 2, second part: an agency's passenger who agrees is the firm's lead (2026-11-10).** Item 2.3 of the plan, by the owner's decisions of 2026-11-07: the firm sees a passenger's whole record as soon as the passenger agrees, the agreement saying so (LGPD, art. 7, § 5), and the firm, not ONE, answers for it (the soliciting risk of EAOAB art. 34, IV passed to the firm by its contract with ONE). Migration `0171_agency_leads` (pgTAP `0170`, 12 assertions with two firms and two agencies): the words before the agency's clause say the data go to the firm when the passenger agrees (`partners` `consent` `base`, `shares_data`), and an answer given in such words says so (`pax_consents.shares_data`); an agreement given before, in the words that passed the data only at the passenger's yes to a problem, passes nothing. Each person a yes that shares the data covers (the holder and the children they answer for) becomes a person of the firm (`pax_lead_to_firm`, on the firm's record as `pax.lead`): the firm's own person of that CPF under a name that is theirs, or a new one, the firm's record kept and what it lacks taken from the agency's; the passenger knows which person of the firm they are (`pax_travelers.firm_person_id`, `lead_at`, `lead_new`), so the case their yes to a problem opens is that person's (`pax_client_of` redefined), what the lead brought keeping the mark of the document it was read from (`pax_hand_over` redefined from 0074), and what the agency changes later never reaches the firm. Taking the agreement back takes back a person the agreement made where nothing of the firm holds them (no case, no trip the firm follows, no other passenger pointing at them; `pax_lead_withdrawn`, `pax.lead_withdrawn`), and never a person the firm had. A passenger whose yes shares the data and who has no case is a lead (`people_funnel` redefined from 0170); the firm reads the agency and the trips ONE follows of them (`firm_person_agency`); and each partnership by its own numbers (`firm_partnership_facts` redefined from 0084: `passengers`, the passengers registered, without agreement, leads and cases). The app: the agency's card in `/settings/parceiros` says the four numbers above its forecast (`partnershipPassengersOf` of `lib/partners/partner.ts`); a person's page says the agency and its trips beside the firm's own (`passengers/person-trips.tsx`, `person-agency.tsx`). The agency's round trip (`agency.db.test.ts`) finds the passenger a lead of the firm from the yes. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the documents still pass to the firm only at the passenger's yes to a problem (the words say so); a passenger who agreed before 0171 passes nothing until they agree again; the words are the platform's, for the curator; a person the agreement made and that the firm then holds (a case, a trip of its own) stays when the agreement is taken back, for the firm to erase on request (`erase_person`).
- **The case journey plan, phase 2, third part: the way back is still followed (2026-11-10).** Item 2.4 of the plan: a trip whose problem became a case was read no more, and a trip had one alert per kind of problem, so a round trip whose outbound became a case was never told of a problem on its return. The rule is `flight-problem-v4` (`flightProblems` of `@flyee/rules/flight-problem`): each journey of a trip, in the order they fly, has its own problem (its cancelled leg, else its changed one, else its last leg landing late), with its number and its legs; `flightProblem` keeps v3's word for the trip. Migration `0172_return_flight` (pgTAP `0171`, 8 assertions with two firms): an alert is of a journey (`flight_alerts.journey`, `legs`), one per journey and kind (`record_flight_alert` takes them; one that does not say its legs, on a trip with a case, keeps nothing); the yes to it opens a case with that journey's legs alone and says which case each leg and each alert went into (`tracked_legs.case_id`, `flight_alerts.case_id`; `one_open_case_from_alert` and `one_alert_not_qualified` redefined); a trip that became a case keeps its first case, stays `case_opened` and is read while it has a leg no case took (`trip_still_followed`, `claim_trip_watch`), a problem there told as any other (`reserve_flight_alert`) and its yes opening another case of the same passenger; a no or a stop to such an alert ends what was left of the trip, never its case (`tracked_trips.rest_closed`, `answer_flight_alert`); a test trip started over frees its legs (`test_chat_set_legs`). The trips that became cases before 0172 took every leg into their case and are read no more. The worker keeps one alert per journey (`evaluateTrip` in the module's `flight-alerts/work.ts`), and a person's card of trips says "O ONE acompanha o resto da viagem". The round trip of the firm's passenger (`passengers.db.test.ts`) cancels the way out in the test chat, has the passenger answer SIM, delays the way back six hours and has them answer SIM again: two cases, each with its own flight. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the second problem opens a case of its own, never a second claim of the first case (a lawyer may join them); the documents the agency or the firm sent went with the first case, and the second takes the client's identity and recent proof of address from it by itself (0157).
- **The case journey plan, phase 2, fourth part: the day of the trip (2026-11-10).** Item 2.6 of the plan, from the owner's passenger flow: ONE writes to the passenger of a trip it follows about two hours before each journey leaves, wishing a good trip and saying the firm follows the flight, and half an hour after it lands, asking whether all went well and inviting them to tell what went wrong. Migration `0173_trip_day` (pgTAP `0172`, 7 assertions with two firms; `0003` and `0041` follow it): two notice kinds, `trip_departure` ("Boa viagem") and `trip_arrival` ("Chegada da viagem"), each the firm's own template as the other kinds are (the test chats have them on at once); `trip_day_trips` gives the backend the trips whose day may owe a word now (followed by 0172's rule, with a leg leaving in the next hours or landed in the last ones; one trip named whatever its hours), each leg's moments as instants at its own airport (`leg_instant`: the airport's offset, Brasília's where none is known); `reserve_trip_day_message` writes each word once per journey and kind (`trip_day_messages`), under the rules of ONE's first word (the agreement, never to who asked to stop, a real passenger only once the platform released its alerts, the firm's number of the trip's kind), and keeps what cannot go as skipped with why (`problem_told` for a journey whose problem the passenger was told of, `not_released`, `no_template`, `opted_out`), so it is never tried again. Which journey owes what is the module's pure rule (`flight-alerts/trip-day.ts`: the journeys as `flight-problem-v4` cuts them, the good trip within two hours of the first leg's departure, the question from half an hour to three hours after the last leg lands, nothing of a journey cancelled, told of a problem or taken by a case), sent by the sweep `trip-day` (every five minutes, also while ONE is used; `trip-day-work.ts`) and, in the test chat, right as the tester makes a leg leave ("Simular partida") or land ("Simular pouso"). The two kinds are in the firm's notices (`lib/notices/notices.ts`). The round trip of the firm's passenger (`passengers.db.test.ts`) has the leg leave and land in the test chat and finds each word written once. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: what the passenger answers to "Correu tudo bem?" is read by ONE in the conversation as any message (a problem told there becomes a case with item 2.1); a real passenger's words wait for the platform's release, as ONE's first word does; the firm submits the two templates to Meta like the other kinds.
- **The case journey plan, phase 2, fifth part: the passenger's welcome (2026-11-10).** Item 2.5 of the plan, from the owner's passenger flow: once a passenger the agency or the firm registered agrees, ONE writes to them in the firm's name, once, saying the firm follows their flights and what a passenger is owed by ANAC's rules, with what to keep in case something goes wrong. Migration `0174_passenger_welcome` (pgTAP `0173`, 6 assertions with two firms; `0003` and `0041` follow it): the notice kind `welcome` ("Boas-vindas ao passageiro"), the firm's own template as the other kinds are, whose text says Resolução 400 in the words of Resolução 813/2026 (food from two hours of waiting, lodging from four when a night must be spent, and the choice of another flight, the refund or another way to travel for a cancellation or a delay of four hours or more; the means of communication, revoked, are not named) and to keep the ticket, the receipts and photos of the airport's board; `welcomes_owed` (the trips followed whose passenger agreed in the last three days, of a WhatsApp the firm never welcomed) and `reserve_passenger_welcome` (once per WhatsApp and firm, `passenger_welcomes`, under the rules of ONE's first word; a real passenger before the platform's release, or a trip that closed, stays owed, while a stop, a firm with no template or no number is kept for good). The app writes it right after the firm registers a passenger (`registerPassenger`) and after the passenger's yes on the agency's link (`answerAgreement`), and the sweep `passenger-welcome` writes what is left (`flight-alerts/welcome-work.ts`); the kind is in the firm's notices. The round trip of the firm's passenger (`passengers.db.test.ts`) finds the welcome written once. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the educational text is the platform's, for the curator, and Meta may call a template with it marketing, which keeps the kind off (the firm then edits its words as with any kind); a passenger who agreed more than three days before 0174 is not welcomed.
- **The case journey plan, phase 2, sixth part: any passenger on the firm's WhatsApp (2026-11-10).** Item 2.1 of the plan, by the owner's decision of 2026-11-07: who wrote to the firm's number with no alert of ONE's and no case waited for a person; now ONE takes them in. Migration `0176_whatsapp_intake` (pgTAP `0174`, 7 assertions with two firms; `0061` follows the notice kinds of 0173 and 0174): the task `atendimento.entrada` (Sonnet 5.5) reads the conversation, with every identifier taken out, and answers whether it is a problem with a flight and what of it the person said (the problem, the airline, the flight, the day, from where to where, how late they arrived, when they were told, the name of who travelled), deciding nothing; the facts gathered are the conversation's (`wa_conversations.intake`, kept and cleaned by `record_conversation_intake`: each fact of its shape, a later word over an earlier one); ONE asks what is still missing one question at a time, opening with a word of care (`lib/conversations/intake.ts`: the problem, the flight or its route, the day, how late for a delay or a lost connection, when told for a cancellation or a change, the name); once nothing is, the rule of qualification decides (`qualificacao-v1`): a problem that qualifies opens the case as a yes to an alert does, with the person at that WhatsApp (or a new one by the name told), the airline, the day and the leg as told, the client's link with the next steps and the interview right after (`one_open_case_from_intake`, origin `organic`, where the firm lets ONE open a case from the chat; never a second case of the same intake, nor one for a contact whose case goes on); one that does not is said kindly and the person kept as not qualified with the rule's reasons (`one_intake_not_qualified`, `wa_conversations.intake_person_id`, `people_funnel` redefined from 0171); what the rule cannot decide alone (a downgrade, another problem) calls a person. A word that is no problem with a flight goes to ONE's usual answer, as before. The round trip `apps/web/src/lib/conversations/intake.db.test.ts` (part of `npm run test:db:roundtrip`) has a passenger tell a delay in two messages and get the case with its link, another told a delay too short kindly and kept as not qualified, and a greeting answered as ONE answers. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the task never ran against the real model yet; a photo of the ticket sent before the case opens stays in the conversation for a person (the case asks for it again on the link); every message of a contact with no case is read by the task once more before ONE's usual answer, which is one more run of the model; the first leg is the only one told, and the documents complete the trip.
- **The case journey plan, phase 3, first part: essential and dispensable documents, and the priority of an elderly claimant (2026-11-10).** Items 3.1 and 3.3 of the plan. **What a case needs is by its kind of problem** (migration `0177_document_needs`, pgTAP `0175`, 7 assertions with two firms; `0003` holds a row of the new table): the setting `cases.documents_by_problem` says, for each kind (a bag's, and the default), the essential documents, which hold the lawsuits as `required_documents` did (each claimant's identity, the proof of address, the ticket or the boarding pass; and for a bag the airline's report of the irregularity, the RIB, a kind of its own, `baggage_report`, which goes to court with the papers of the trip), and the dispensable ones (the receipts of what was spent, the prints of the airline's messages or photos), asked once: the case goes on with the client's "não tenho" or after the setting's 7 days (`dispensable_days`). `case_required_documents` is the one reading of a case's essentials (the worker and the case screen read them by the case's kind of problem, `requiredFor` of `lib/cases/cases.ts`), `case_dispensables` says where each dispensable stands (sent, not had, past its days, asked), the client answers on their link (`client_link_not_have`, the backend's, `clientNotHave`) or a person of the firm for them (`record_document_not_have`, never a support visit; `document_not_had` in the timeline), and `client_link_open` gives both.
