The app: on the link, each dispensable a line with "Enviar" and "Não tenho" (`c/[token]/client-folder.tsx`); on the case's card of documents, "Também ajudam o caso" with where each stands and "O cliente não tem" (`documents-card.tsx`, `lib/cases/document-needs-actions.ts`). **The priority of processing** (migration `0178_priority`, pgTAP `0176`, 5 assertions with two firms): the rule is `especificidades-v1` (`@flyee/rules/specificities`: a claimant of 60 or more has the priority of processing, Lei 10.741/2003, art. 71, and CPC, art. 1.048, I, and one of 80 or more the special priority, art. 71, § 5º, by the birth date ONE read of their documents); the database reads the same ages (`lawsuit_priority`) and the piece writes the request by name and age where its model carries `[[PRIORIDADE]]` (`case_draft_filled` redefined from 0125), which the platform's two models now carry after the line naming the defendant, a lawsuit with nobody of that age leaving the place empty; a sixth version of `caso.peca.minuta` says the mark is the database's, and the editor lists it (`DRAFT_MARKS`). The filing through the MNI marks the priority by the court's own code (`int:prioridade`, `codes.priorities` of the court's channel: `elderly`, else the same code for `elderly_80`; never a code guessed, the test court's placeholder `IDOSO`), and the card "Como este caso vai a juízo" says who has it and that ONE read the age and may err. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the dispensables are asked on the link (a file sent in the conversation answers one; "não tenho" is said on the link or by the firm); a court's code of the priority is the operator's to write in its channel's table; the priority of a person with a disability or a serious illness (CPC, art. 1.048, I) has no document ONE reads yet.
- **The case journey plan, phase 3, second part: the competent unit before the piece, and the complaint before the lawsuit only where the unit asks it (2026-11-10).** Items 3.4 and 3.5 of the plan. **The units of each comarca** (migration `0179_court_units`, pgTAP `0177`, 5 assertions with two firms): `court_units` is the platform's table, read by any account, of each comarca's units by kind (`juizado`, `comum`), how a lawsuit reaches them (`scope` `comarca`: distributed by lottery among the comarca's units of that competence, CPC art. 285, so the petition is addressed to them all; `unit`, one alone), the act it was read in, whether the operator confirmed it, and its profile (`prior_complaint`: `required`, `not_required` or unknown, with where it was read); seeded with the TJGO, one row per comarca and kind of the 127 of 0145, "Juizado Especial Cível da Comarca de X" and "Vara Cível da Comarca de X", unconfirmed. A planned lawsuit takes the unit of its court, comarca and kind (`proceedings_unit`, after `proceedings_comarca`; `proceedings.unit_id`, `unit_by_one`), which the piece's `[[JUIZO]]` writes; a unit a person wrote stays theirs, and one the operator renames is taken by the lawsuits ONE addressed to it. The operator writes a unit and its profile (`admin_save_court_unit`, on the record, waking the cases of its planned lawsuits) in the card "Unidades e reclamação prévia" of `/admin/calendario` (`court-units-card.tsx`, `court-unit-actions.ts`, `lib/admin/court-units.ts`), and the case's card "Processos" says the unit ONE named, that it may err, whether the comarca's units were checked and whether the unit asks the complaint. **The complaint before the lawsuit** (migration `0180_prior_complaint`, pgTAP `0178`, 7 assertions with two firms): where a planned lawsuit's unit asks it, ONE writes no piece of it while the case's complaint is owed or its channel's days run (`case_prior_complaint`: not required, owed, waiting, done; `advance_piece_due` redefined from 0039; the waits `prior_complaint_owed` and `prior_complaint_waiting`, the client's, `wait_owner` redefined from 0125). ONE files nothing at the channel: the client's link guides the client step by step to consumidor.gov.br and takes the protocol and the day (`client_link_record_complaint`: the case's complaint, `by_client`, due on the channel's days, `complaint_answer_days`), then asks what the airline answered (`client_link_complaint_answer`: it settled, with what it paid, or it answered and settled nothing; `c/[token]/client-prior-complaint.tsx`, `recordClientComplaint`, `answerClientComplaint`), and a complaint nobody answered by its day is closed by ONE as unanswered in its look of the case (`advance_prior_complaint`, called by the worker before the steps; `case_advance_steps_due` redefined from 0098). What the airline paid is kept (`admin_complaints.paid_cents`; `save_admin_complaint` redefined from 0026 takes it, and the case's card asks it of a complaint that settled), the piece is handed it with the rest of the complaint (`case_draft_to_work` redefined from 0143), and a seventh version of `caso.peca.minuta` says the complaint is the attempt to settle, and that what was paid is a fact and leaves the material damage it asks. A change of a complaint wakes its case. The card "Reclamação antes do processo" says which unit asks it and where it stands, and `lib/cases/prior-complaint.ts` holds what the screens read. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: every unit is a whole comarca's until the operator reads the court's acts (the regional and specialized units of a large comarca are not in the table yet), and no unit has a profile until the operator writes it (the jurimetry of phase 5 reads it later), so no case waits for a complaint until then; the client is guided on the link only (ONE does not ask it on WhatsApp yet); the complaint's print or PDF is sent by the client as a document of the case (the dispensable "prints"), which goes to the filing package; a complaint on another channel the firm recorded counts as the attempt.
- **The case journey plan, phase 3, third part: the client signs first (2026-11-10).** Item 3.2 of the plan. Migration `0181_signature_first` (pgTAP `0179`, 5 assertions with two firms): the power of attorney and the contract are due once the person's identity and proof of address were accepted, which is what the papers ask, before the client says "Terminei" (`case_papers_due` redefined from 0125, `paper_documents_accepted`), so the signature, the closing of 0146, comes earlier; and where ONE sends the papers (the firm lets it, a power of attorney exists to send, an adult of the case has a link), what ONE asks after the signature waits for it (`case_waits_signature`, `case_signed_at`): the interview (`interview_owed` redefined from 0095), started right after a signature is confirmed or simulated (`queueInterview` in `lib/signatures/actions.ts`) and by the sweep otherwise, and the dispensable documents (`case_dispensables` redefined from 0177: `later` until then, said on the case's card, and their days counted from the signature). ONE's message with the link says so (`caseStepsMessage` with `signFirst`, read by `firm_signs_first` before the case exists). A firm that keeps the papers to a person, or a case with no link to sign by, asks as before; the conversation's round trip runs on that order (`agent.db.test.ts`). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a case waits for the signature while its power of attorney is still being issued (the papers wait for the identity and the proof of address the firm or ONE accepted), so a client who never sends them is never interviewed until a person goes on with the case; the contract goes with the power of attorney only where the firm has its own model (the platform proposes none).
- **The case journey plan, phase 3, fourth part: the client checks the facts of the initial (2026-11-11).** Item 3.6 of the plan. Migration `0182_piece_facts` (pgTAP `0180`, 8 assertions with two firms; `0003` holds a row of the new table, `0098`, `0124` and `0175` say the order their files read). Once a piece of a planned lawsuit is ready (its latest version's citations read, nothing left to complete), of a case with an adult who has a link in force (`case_facts_reachable`), ONE owes the client a check of its facts (`piece_fact_checks`, one per piece and version; claimed under a number by `claim_piece_fact_checks`): the backend hands the piece's text with every identifier under a numbered mark (`piece_fact_check_to_write`, `case_text_keyed`, the marks kept on the check in a column no member is granted) to the task `caso.peca.fatos_cliente` (Sonnet 5.5: the facts the petition tells, in plain words, to the passenger in the second person, nothing added, no request, value or law), and the database reads the run's answer and puts the names back (`record_piece_fact_check`, `case_text_restored`). The client confirms the facts on their link, or says what is wrong in their own words (`client_link_piece_facts`, `client_link_answer_facts`; `c/[token]/client-piece-facts.tsx`, `answerClientFacts`); ONE tells them in the conversation it holds when its window is open, and reminds them once after the setting's 24 hours (`piece_fact_messages_owed`, `one_say_piece_facts`; `drafts.facts_remind_hours`). A correction of a piece no lawyer approved sends it back to the AI with the client's words, as a lawyer sends one back (0042: what waited of it in the queue is withdrawn, `answer_piece_facts`), and the new version is asked again; one already approved waits for a lawyer to reopen it. ONE approves a piece alone only once its client confirmed it (`approve_case_draft_unattended` and its sweep, redefined from 0125; a rewritten piece is a lawyer's to approve, as before), and the filing waits for the confirmation, last of what it waits for (`filing_blocker`, redefined from 0125, `lawsuit_facts_blocker`: `facts_unconfirmed`, the client's, and `facts_corrected`, a lawyer's; `wait_owner` redefined from 0180). A person of the firm records the client's word as the client told them, saying how (`record_piece_facts_answer`), and a partner or a lawyer goes on without it, saying why, with the second factor, on the record (`waive_piece_facts`). The case follows the checks by itself (`case_pulse`, redefined from 0135, with the answers on dispensable documents too). The app: the line under each lawsuit of the card "Peças" (`casos/[id]/components/piece-facts-line.tsx`: ONE writing the facts, asked since when with the text, confirmed and how, what the client said is wrong and what follows, gone on without and why; "Registrar a resposta do cliente" and "Seguir sem a confirmação"), `lib/cases/piece-facts.ts` (pure, tested), `piece-facts-work.ts` (the worker and the sweep `piece-facts`, every five minutes and while ONE is used) and `piece-facts-actions.ts`. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the client's correction reaches the AI as the client wrote it, with no mark on what identifies a person (as a lawyer's note goes, 0042); a correction of a piece a lawyer already approved, or that the AI may not rewrite any more (`max_revisions`), waits for a lawyer; the order of the facts (0026) is still shown on the link, though the facts of the initial are what holds the filing now; the task never ran against the real model yet.
- **The case journey plan, phase 4, first part: the judgment's options and costs, the amount due, and the referral (2026-11-11).** Items 4.5, 4.6 and 4.7 of the plan. **The judgment** (migration `0185_judgment_paths`, pgTAP `0183`, 5 assertions with two firms; `0003` and `0012` follow it): the rule is `caminhos-v1` (`@flyee/rules/paths`: after a judgment, accept it, the embargos de declaração (CPC, art. 1.022; Lei 9.099/1995, art. 48) or the appeal, the recurso inominado in the small-claims court (art. 41) with its preparo (art. 42, § 1º, and art. 54) and the other side's fees if it loses (art. 55), the apelação in the civil court; after the turma recursal only the recurso extraordinário or, where the court has a panel for it, the uniformization, never the recurso especial (Súmula 203 do STJ); free justice exempts the preparo and suspends what losing costs). Each judgment in force has its paths (`judgment_paths`, made by a trigger on `lawsuit_facts`); the client reads the judgment and what an appeal costs on their link and says they agree, or not (`client_link_judgment_paths`, `client_link_answer_judgment_path`; `c/[token]/client-judgment.tsx`), or a person of the firm records it saying how (`record_judgment_path_answer`); a partner or a lawyer decides, with the second factor, never a support visit, on the record (`decide_judgment_path`), and accepting it takes back the deadlines of the embargos and of the appeal ONE proposed from the judgment and nobody confirmed (0141). The card "Resultado" shows, under each lawsuit, ONE's recommendation (`recommendPath` of `lib/cases/judgment-paths.ts`: accept a judgment that granted what was asked or most of it, appeal one that granted nothing or less than half; never the embargos, which ask a reading of the judgment), what an appeal costs, the client's word and the decision (`casos/[id]/components/judgment-path-block.tsx`). **The amount due** (migration `0184_amount_due`, pgTAP `0182`, 5 assertions with two firms): the rule is `atualizacao-v1` (`@flyee/rules/update`: from 30/08/2024 the IPCA and the Selic less the IPCA, never below zero in a month, Lei 14.905/2024; the months before follow the judgment's own terms, which ONE does not read and says so; a month with no published rate counts at zero and is named; the shortfall of a deposit and the fine of 10 percent of art. 523, § 1º, of the CPC, FONAJE 97), with the Banco Central's monthly rates read by a new adapter (`packages/indices`: the SGS's series 433, the IPCA, and 4390, the Selic of the month, with no key; a fake; a contract test; `INDICES_FAKE=1` outside production). A lawsuit whose judgment condemned the airline and became final is owed a computation (`lawsuit_amounts_owed`: once a day, and when a deposit came), recorded with its next step (`record_lawsuit_amount`, `lawsuit_amounts`: wait for the airline's 15 days, the levantamento of a whole deposit, the complementação of a short one, the cumprimento with the fine; told in the timeline when it changes), by the daily sweep `lawsuit-amounts` (`lib/courts/amount.ts`, pure and tested, `amount-work.ts`); the card "Resultado" says the amount, how it was reached, that ONE may err and the next step. **The referral** (migration `0183_referrals`, pgTAP `0181`, 5 assertions with two firms): once the client confirmed on their link that their money arrived, ONE thanks them in the firm's name in the conversation it holds, inside the 24 hours, and asks them to pass the firm's contact to whoever else had a problem with a flight, offering nothing in exchange (`referral_asks_owed`, `one_ask_referral`, `cases.referral_asked_at`, once per case; `lib/conversations/referral-work.ts`, right after the confirmation and by the sweep `referral-asks`); who then writes to the firm saying who referred them is a person of the firm at once, a lead with the name they gave (`people.referred_by`; a second version of `atendimento.entrada` reads `indicado_por`, `intake_facts_clean` and `record_conversation_intake` redefined from 0176), the case it opens is of the origin referral (a trigger on `wa_conversations`), and the firm's numbers count referrals (`people_facts`, redefined from 0138). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the recommendation compares values and reads no judgment, so the embargos are a lawyer's to see; the preparo's value is each court's table, not in ONE; the interest runs from the filing, as ONE does not read the day of the citation; a settlement is not updated (its terms are the agreement's); ONE says what to ask and does not write the petition of the levantamento, the complementação or the cumprimento yet (the intermediate pieces of item 4.2); the thanks goes only inside the 24 hours of the conversation (no template of its own yet); neither the intake's new field nor the BCB adapter ran against the live model or series yet.
- **The case journey plan, phase 4, second part: the cycle of the publications (2026-11-11).** Item 4.1 of the plan. Migration `0186_publication_actions` (pgTAP `0184`, 5 assertions with two firms; `0003` and `0012` follow it): each publication triaged (by a person or by ONE), of a firm's case, active and whole, captured in the setting's 30 days (`publication_actions.days`), owes a reading of what it asks (`publication_actions`, made by a trigger on `publications`): the task `publicacao.acao` (Sonnet 5.5) writes a summary for the firm, a summary for the client when the publication touches them, one action from a fixed list (nothing to do, a deadline, a manifestation, the réplica, contrarrazões, embargos, an appeal, the cumprimento, the levantamento, a document to add: the list is the rule's, the AI only chooses and explains) and what each side owes (the client a document or an answer, the firm a decision); the backend claims each under a number (`claim_publication_actions`, `publication_action_to_write`) and the database reads the run's answer and keeps only what fits the list (`record_publication_action`; `publication_action` in the timeline as the AI's). ONE decides nothing here. The worker runs right after a triage (a person's or ONE's) and in the sweep `publication-actions` (`lib/publications/action-work.ts`, `actions-read.ts`). The app: under each publication of the case's card "Publicações", what ONE read, the action it recommends and why, what each side owes and what the client reads, said as ONE's and that ONE may err (`casos/[id]/components/publication-action-box.tsx`); on the client's link, "Novidades do seu processo", the client's summaries and what the firm asks of them (`client_link_publication_news`, `c/[token]/client-publication-news.tsx`). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: what the client owes is said on the link, not asked on WhatsApp yet, and what the firm owes is said on the publication, not yet a line of the center of what waits; the court's text reaches the model as the triage's does (public data of the gazette); the task never ran against the real model yet; the petition the action asks for is the intermediate pieces' (item 4.2), not built yet.
- **The case journey plan, phase 4, third part: the hearing, before and after (2026-11-11).** Item 4.4 of the plan. Migration `0187_hearing_reports` (pgTAP `0185`, 8 assertions with two firms; `0003` holds a row of each new table). **Before**: who decides for the firm says the value it settles for at least, per kind of problem (`firm_settlement_floors`, `set_firm_settlement_floor`, the second factor, never a support visit, on the record; the card "Valor mínimo de acordo" of `/settings/autonomia`, `settlement-floor-card.tsx`), and two business days before a hearing ONE writes the brief for who goes to it (the hearing, the lawsuit, the airline, what each claimant asks, the facts the client confirmed of the initial (0182), the documents accepted, the firm's floor, and that ONE did not read the airline's defence yet; `hearing_briefs_owed` and `record_hearing_brief`, once per hearing, `hearing_brief` in the timeline), e-mails it to the attendee who is of the firm (`sendHearingBriefEmail`, the template `hearing-brief` of `packages/email`) and keeps it on the hearing, read on the case's card "Audiências" under the hearing ("Ver o resumo para a audiência", said as ONE's); `lib/hearings/brief.ts` (pure, tested) and `brief-work.ts` (the sweep `hearing-briefs`). **After**: who went sends the result to the firm's WhatsApp, in a text or an audio; ONE knows a person of the firm by the WhatsApp of their profile (`profiles.whatsapp`, `set_my_whatsapp`, the card "Seu WhatsApp" of `/settings`) and an attendee from outside by the one written on the hearing (`hearings.attendee_phone`, `set_hearing_attendee_phone`, the field "WhatsApp de quem vai" of the hearing's panel), finds the hearing of theirs from three days ago to two hours ahead with nothing said of it (`conversation_hearing_report`: with more than one, ONE asks which by the case's number), reads the result (task `audiencia.resultado`, Sonnet 5.5: a settlement with its value and when it is paid, none, an instruction set, a judgment given there, not held, or not said) and records it from the run (`record_hearing_report`, `hearing_reports`): the hearing held with what came of it, the settlement as a fact of the lawsuit as ONE's (confirmed as the firm keeps `record_outcome`), and its payment proposed as a deadline to follow (the act `pagamento_acordo`, in calendar days), each said back in the conversation with that ONE may err; a hearing not held or a message that says nothing changes nothing, and a message of somebody who is neither is answered as usual (`takeHearingReport` at the start of ONE's turn, `lib/conversations/hearing-report.ts` and `hearing-report-work.ts`). **On WhatsApp too** (migration `0188_hearing_staff_messages`, pgTAP `0186`, 5 assertions with two firms; `0003`, `0041` and `0061` follow it): the brief is told on the WhatsApp of who goes beside the e-mail, and an hour after a hearing started with nothing reported ONE asks who went how it went (`hearing_results_owed`, the sweep `hearing-results`), each as the firm's template of the notice kinds `hearing_brief` and `hearing_result` (switched on as the other kinds, the test chat's at once), at the WhatsApp of the attendee's profile or the one on the hearing (`hearing_staff_phone`), once per hearing and kind (`reserve_hearing_staff_message`, `hearing_staff_messages`: what cannot go kept as skipped with why), through the test chat's number for a test case; the reply is read by the report above. The act `pagamento_acordo` is the fourteenth of the list (`0037` counts it). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the airline's defence is not read yet, so the brief says so; the floor is one value per kind of problem and person, not per case; an instruction set or a judgment given in the hearing is recorded as held with the note, and the new hearing or the judgment is a person's to write; the task never ran against the real model yet.
- **The case journey plan, phase 4, fourth part: the intermediate pieces and the réplica (2026-11-11).** Items 4.2 and 4.3 of the plan. A lawsuit the court has has pieces of its own, each of its kind, through the drafts of 0029 (the AI's first text, the editor, the gate of the library, the approval as the text a lawyer read), never made the case's petition. Migration `0189_intermediate_pieces` (pgTAP `0187`, 9 assertions with two firms; `0003` and `0037` follow it): the kinds are data (`piece_kinds`: a manifestation, the joining of a document, the réplica, contrarrazões, embargos de declaração, the appeal, the cumprimento, the levantamento and the complementação, each with what asks for it, the act of its deadline by kind of lawsuit, what the AI is told it is and the platform's model of it, provisional, for the curator); a draft says its kind and what asked for it (`case_drafts.piece_kind`, `publication_id`, `source_key`, one in course per lawsuit and kind, what asked asking once). A person asks for one on the case (`request_intermediate_piece`, a lawsuit filed with its number), and ONE by itself where the firm lets it start pieces (`start_piece`, 0039; `intermediate_pieces_owed`, `request_intermediate_piece_by_one`, the sweep `intermediate-pieces`): what a publication asks (0186's action, in the setting's 30 days) and what a condemnation's next step is (0184: the levantamento, the complementação, the cumprimento); an appeal or embargos only once a lawyer decided that path (0185). Each carries ONE's recommendation to file it or not with why, read from what asked for it (`piece_recommendation`). An eighth version of `caso.peca.minuta` writes them: the worker hands the kind, the lawsuit's number, the publication's text, the facts of the lawsuit and the amounts (`intermediate_piece_facts`), and for a réplica the airline's defence as a PDF (4.3: the firm sends it on the card, `attach_case_defense`, a document of kind `defense`; the réplica waits for it, `waits_for`, and a trigger lets it be written once it arrives). Approved, the text is a PDF kept as a document of kind `intermediate_petition` (`record_piece_issued`). A partner or a lawyer decides with the second factor, never a support visit (`decide_intermediate_piece`): held, the piece is given up with the next steps as the lawyer wrote them; to file, ONE files it through the court's own channel with the lawsuit's number (`PetitionFiling` and `filePetition` in `packages/courts`, the MNI's `entregarManifestacaoProcessual` with `numeroProcesso`; `claim_piece_filings`, `piece_filing_to_send`, `record_piece_filing`, the sweep `piece-filings`, `lib/cases/pieces-work.ts`), or for a test case the test court, keeping the protocol and the receipt; a filing with no answer is never sent again by itself. Where ONE cannot file (`piece_filing_blocker`: no channel, a channel that asks a fixed address the platform has none of yet, `court_filing_channels.fixed_ip` and the setting `filing_submit.egress_fixed_ip`, no code of the document at the court, a court that takes files already signed) the lawyer files it in the court's system and says so with the protocol and the day (`record_piece_filed`). The app: the card "Peças do processo" on the case (`casos/[id]/components/intermediate-pieces-card.tsx`, the line's rule in `lib/cases/pieces.ts`, pure and tested; `case_piece_filings` says what keeps ONE from filing each): each piece with where it stands, ONE's recommendation and why, "Revisar o texto" in the piece's editor (`DraftDialog`, exported from the card "Peças", which keeps the initial alone), "Protocolar" and "Não protocolar" confirmed with what follows, the PDF and the receipt, "Registrar o protocolo", "Enviar a contestação" while a réplica waits, and "Pedir uma peça" in `SidePanel`; `lib/cases/piece-actions.ts` holds the writes; the timeline names each step. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the firm writes no model of its own for an intermediate piece yet (the platform's models, provisional, for the curator; each piece is edited in the editor); the defence is the firm's upload (the MNI's consultation of the lawsuit's documents is not built); where a court takes files already signed (the Projudi of TJGO), the lawyer signs and files the piece, as ONE's cloud signature covers only the initial's package; the code of the document type at each court is the operator's to write in the channel's codes (`document_types.intermediate_petition`); a fixed address, where a court asks one, is the owner's decision (Vercel's on the Pro plan, or the VPS as the exit); the task's eighth version never ran against the real model yet.
- **The case journey plan, phase 5, first cut: the jurimetry (2026-11-11).** How the courts decide passengers' lawsuits, by unit and by judge, read from the market's public decisions, to prepare a piece and to project, never to choose a judge (distribution by lottery, CPC, art. 285; the natural judge, CF, art. 5º, XXXVII and LIII; choosing one is a sign of abusive litigation, Recomendação CNJ 159/2024). `packages/jurimetrics` is the adapter (`MarketSource.decisionsOn({ court, party, day })`; the DJEN's public API by party and court, a day at a time, each call waiting its turn, a day of more than its pages said incomplete; a fake; a contract test, 7 tests) with the rule that reads a decision from its text (`mercado-v1`: the result by `resultado-v1`'s reading of the operative part or the lawsuit extinguished, the moral damages set, the kind of problem, the judge from the signature's line, an appeal's panel, an extinction for want of a prior complaint); nothing of a passenger and no lawsuit's number is kept, and the grounds are read at that moment and never kept. Migration `0190_jurimetrics` (pgTAP `0188`, 8 assertions with two firms): what is read is planned per court and airline (`market_captures`, seeded with the TJGO and each operator's company as named in court, from the setting's 180 days back; `market_days_due`, `record_market_day`, `record_market_error`; the sweep `market-reading`, the clock's only, every 15 minutes, a few days a run, where `JURIMETRICS_CAPTURE` is on: production unless 0, elsewhere only with 1, as the DJEN's reading is), each decision once (`market_decisions`, read by no member row by row); the numbers of a set of decisions with their samples, nothing of fewer than the setting's five as a rate or a median (`market_numbers`): the decisions read, the success rate, the median moral damages by kind of problem, the extinctions for want of a prior complaint (what item 3.5 reads); the unit's, the judge's and the court's (`market_profile`, 5.2 and 5.4) and the projection by unit (`market_projection`, 5.5), read by anybody signed in. The theses (5.3) are a catalog that is data (`jurimetry_theses`: eight, each with the words the rule `teses-v1` reads it by, `lib/jurimetrics/theses.ts`, pure and tested), read in each decision's grounds at the moment it is read and kept in the shadow until the operator said the thesis of a sample at the source and the rule agreed in the setting's share (30 checked, 90 percent; `admin_market_thesis_sample`, `admin_check_market_thesis`, `admin_release_market_theses` with the second factor); once released, a piece reads the theses its unit's decisions rest on (`unit_theses_for_piece`, the ninth version of `caso.peca.minuta`). The app: under each lawsuit of the case, "Ver como este juízo costuma decidir" (`casos/[id]/components/market-profile.tsx`: the unit, the judge as the court's course names them and the whole court, the success rate and the extinctions as meters, the medians by problem, each with its sample, said as public decisions read by ONE, which may err, and never a way to choose a judge), the card "O mercado, por vara ou juizado" in `/numeros` beside the firm's own numbers (`numeros/market-card.tsx`), and the operator's card "Jurimetria" in `/admin/ai` (each court and airline read, how far and what failed, the theses' measure, the sample to check at the DJEN's certificate and the release). `lib/jurimetrics/capture.ts` is the reading, `actions.ts` the reads, `profile.ts` the words. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the theses are read by rule, not by the AI task the plan names (a run of the AI belongs to a firm, and the market's reading is the platform's; the task waits for a way to charge the platform's own runs); the days to judgment are not read (a decision says no filing day); only the TJGO is read, the operator adds courts by migration until a console exists; a unit and a judge are matched by the words the court writes; the paid sources (Predictus, Escavador, Judit) and the DataJud wait for their keys and the CNJ's authorization; the DJEN's search by party was not run against the API yet, and it shares the address's window with the reading of the publications; the revenue projection of the finance plan does not read this panel yet.
- **The case journey plan, phase 6: the firm's own words in ONE's messages (2026-11-11).** Each message ONE writes in a conversation as free text may take the firm's own words, checked before they count. Migration `0191_firm_messages` (pgTAP `0189`, 6 assertions with two firms; `0003` holds a row of the new table): the messages are data (`one_messages`: the link and the next steps, "recebemos tudo", the flight that does not make a case, the passenger who does not want to go on, a person who continues; where each goes, the values it must carry and the ones it may, ONE's own words as an example); the firm's versions are kept, never rewritten (`firm_message_texts`, one in force per message); who decides for the firm writes one (`save_firm_message`, never a support visit, on the record), puts an earlier one back (`restore_firm_message`) or goes back to ONE's words (`reset_firm_message`); the database refuses a text without a value it must carry (the link's message without `{link}`), with a value it does not know, a dash or an exclamation mark, a promise of a result or of the wrong size (`firm_message_problem`). The app: `/settings/mensagens` ("Mensagens do ONE", in the settings menu; `settings/mensagens/messages-view.tsx`): each message with where it goes, the words in force, the firm's or ONE's, the values it carries, and for who decides for the firm the version written in `SidePanel`, checked as it is typed (`lib/firm/messages.ts`, pure and tested, the database's checks said at once), with ONE's suggestion one click away ("Aceitar sugestão": the marks made plain, a promise taken out, the link put back) and what the bar's rules on advertising weigh (a price or a fee spoken of) said for the firm to weigh; the backend writes the firm's words where it wrote ONE's (`firmMessageText` of `lib/firm/messages-read.ts`, filled by `fillMessage`; the case's number filled by the database as before). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: five messages so far, the interview's questions and the answers of ONE's model in the conversation stay ONE's; the suggestion is ONE's rule, not a rewrite by the AI; a message outside the 24 hours is a template the vendor approves, written in the notices of `/settings/connections` as before.
- **M3, sprint S10, the partner's weekly digest and the watch over the DJEN (2026-11-11).** What the sprint still had besides the billing, the support and the console. **The weekly digest** (migration `0200_weekly_digest`, pgTAP `0200`, 9 assertions with two firms; `0003` holds a row of the new table): every Monday morning of Brasília (7 to noon), each person who decides for a firm (`decides_for_firm`; `decides_for_firm_of` names the person for the backend) is e-mailed the firm's week before in numbers (`weekly_digest_facts`: the cases opened, the closings of 0146, the lawsuits filed and the cases ended, each beside the week before; the judgments by result, the settlements, the judgments become final and the releases received with their sum; this week's deadlines and hearings; what waits now: deadlines past their day, the cases stagnant at their step by `estoque-v1`, the AI's answers that wait for a person, the awareness owed of ONE's triage; and ONE's tasks of the week with the hours by the firm's own estimates, 0120), the test cases counted only while the firm has no real case and said so. Numbers only: nothing of a client, a lawsuit's number or a case's value; each part leads to its page of ONE. Each e-mail is reserved before it goes (`weekly_digests`, one per person and week: `weekly_digests_owed`, `reserve_weekly_digest`, `finish_weekly_digest`), a week of nothing is recorded and sends nothing, the provider's quota gives the turn back (the sweep `weekly-digests`, the clock's alone; `lib/firm/weekly-digest-work.ts`), and nothing is reserved without `RESEND_API_KEY`. The person stops it in one click (the e-mail's link and the mail client's button, RFC 8058, `/api/public/resumo/parar`, `stop_weekly_digest`) or in their settings (`agenda_preferences.weekly_off`, `set_weekly_digest`, on the record), where the card "Resumo semanal do escritório" (`settings/components/weekly-digest-card.tsx`, only for who decides) also shows the week before in the very words of the next e-mail (`firm_weekly_digest`; the words are the e-mail package's, `weeklyDigestSections` of `packages/email/src/weekly.ts`, template `weekly-digest`). **The watch over the DJEN** (migration `0201_djen_watch`, pgTAP `0201`, 8 assertions with two firms; `0012` lists the new table as the backend's): once an hour (the setting `djen_watch`) the backend looks at the public API (`probe` of the DJEN client, `@flyee/courts/djen-systems` naming each link's system and each communication's kind; a fake and the contract test): what one state court made available on the last weekday, the courts in turn (`djen_probe_due`), recorded with counts only (`djen_probes`, `record_djen_probe`: answered, failed or rate-limited, how long, how many, how many with no text, the links by kind and system; the sweep `djen-watch`, the clock's alone, only where the DJEN is read). The database says since when the DJEN does not answer and whether it publishes its texts empty (`djen_watch_state`); the operator reads it at once in `/admin/ai` (the card "Vigia do DJEN", `admin_djen_watch`), and a firm that reads the DJEN is told only past the setting's 24 hours, under "O ONE precisa de você" of the center and the home, to who works the deadlines (`firm_djen_outage`: ONE tries again by itself, and meanwhile the intimations are read in each court's system). A court whose links of a kind of lawsuit point mostly to another system than the platform's table says (the setting's 25 percent of at least 20 links in 30 days) is a signal (`court_system_signals`): the operator reads it beside the watch, and who approves the system ONE named for a planned lawsuit of that court and kind reads it on the case before approving (`court_system_signal`, `casos/[id]/components/court-system.tsx`). Known limits, for the owner to weigh: neither ran against its provider here (the digest waits for `RESEND_API_KEY`, the watch looks only where `DJEN_CAPTURE` is on); a look reads the first page of a court's day (100 communications), so a court is seen about once a day; a signal says only where the links point, and the operator confirms the court's act before the table changes; an outage of a single court is not told apart from the DJEN's.
- **M3, sprint S10, ONE's own billing, first cut (2026-11-11): Asaas in reais, the tiers as data, the metering, the alerts, the change of tier and the panel of use.** The inherited ladder of the previous product (Stripe and dollars, the one-time access, the rungs `kit`, `club` and `pro` written in the code) leaves the app. `packages/billing` bills through Asaas alone: the Stripe provider, its dependency, the dollar and the currency switch are gone (`currency.ts` routes reais to Asaas, `currencyForLocale` is reais whatever the page); its entitlements are ONE's keys (`processos_ativos_max`, `assentos_max`, `whatsapp_numeros_max`, the add-on `monitor_voo`; `ceilingsOf`, `isTierSlug`); the provider gains `changeSubscription` (Asaas's "Atualizar assinatura existente": the value from the next charge on, the pending charges following it); and it has a fake (`@flyee/billing/fake`) and a contract test run against the fake and against the Asaas client talking to a stand-in in the shapes of Asaas's reference (`src/contract.test.ts`: a checkout opening a subscription, a change of tier, an end set and lifted, a paid charge read only with the account's token). Migration `0202_one_billing` (pgTAP `0202`, 9 assertions with two firms; `0003` holds a row of each new table, `0005` gives its plans their rank): a tier is a slug the operator writes on a plan and orders by `plans.rank` (the old check of `kit`, `club` and `pro` gone, the plans before ranked as they were), and `org_entitlements` (redefined from 0005) names the firm's tier by the live source of the highest rank. What a firm uses is measured once a day (`usage_ledger`: its open cases that are not a test, the people of its team, its numbers at the vendor; `usage_now`, `record_usage_day`, the sweep `usage-meter`, the clock's alone), and at the setting's 80 and 100 percent of a ceiling (`billing.usage_alert_percents`) who manages the firm's account and who decides for it are told on the bell once a month (`usage_alerts`; `lib/billing-usage.ts`, `billing-usage-work.ts`). ONE never stops a firm's work at a ceiling: a case past it opens and goes on, and the firm is asked to change its tier (the team's ceiling, 0003, is the one limit already kept). A change of tier goes on the subscription in force at Asaas: begun by who manages the account, with the second factor, never in a support visit, to a plan on sale in reais of the same period (`begin_plan_change`), Asaas takes the new value, and the backend records it once, on the record (`commit_plan_change`, `billing.plan_changed`); a plan of the other period still starts a new subscription. The app: `/settings/billing` has the card "Consumo da faixa" (`settings/billing/components/usage-card.tsx`, over `firm_usage`: each metric against its ceiling with the month's highest, the month's alerts, the flight monitor) and the grid of the tiers (`plans-grid.tsx`: the public plans of the period in the order of their rank, each with its ceilings and rights, "Mudar para esta faixa" with the second factor in place, `actions-plan.ts`); `/assinatura` sells a tier named by `?plan=` and sends a firm with none to the tiers; the webhook takes Asaas alone; the operator's plan form writes the tier, its rank and ONE's limits, in reais, recurring only. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: no tier is priced yet (the operator writes the plans in `/admin/billing`, which is the owner's decision of the prices and limits); what counts as an active case (an open case that is not a test) is a proposal; the AI included in a tier (`ia_incluida`) is not measured yet; the flight monitor is sold as an add-on of the operator's catalog and gates nothing yet; a change of tier applies to the next charge with no proration, and drops a coupon the subscription had; nothing ran against Asaas's sandbox from here (the provider is proven by the contract test's stand-in); the database keeps the previous product's access and grant machinery, never sold again, and the checkout form its unused dollar branch.
- **M3, sprint S10, the assisted support with the new fence, the firms' console by firm and the backup adapted (2026-11-11).** The last items of the sprint. **A support visit needs the firm's word** (migration `0203_support_authorization`, pgTAP `0203`, 9 assertions with two firms; `0003` holds a row of the new table): a firm's data are its clients', so the operator's support visit (0009, read only by 0010) opens into a firm only under its authorization in force (`support_authorizations`): who manages the firm's account, or who decides for it, lets the support in for some hours (1 to the setting's 168, `support_access`), with the second factor and never in a support visit, saying what for if they want, and ends it at any moment (`grant_support_access`, `end_support_access`, on the record); the database refuses to open a visit into a firm with none (a trigger on `impersonation_sessions` for the service role that opens them, `support_not_authorized`) and keeps which authorization a visit came under; the app asks first (`admin_support_access_for` in `startImpersonation`). The operator's dialog of a visit says whether the firm let the support in, until when, and when not asks the firm with the reason (`admin_request_support_access`: who manages the firm is told on the bell); everybody of the firm reads on `/settings` (the card "Acesso do suporte do ONE", `settings/components/support-access-card.tsx`) whether the support may enter, who let it in and until when, and the last visits with what the operator said each was for (`firm_support_access`). A visit already in course when the authorization ends lasts its own minutes, and writes nothing. **The firms' console, by firm** (migration `0204_firm_operation`, pgTAP `0204`, 4 assertions with two firms): the dossier of a firm in `/admin/escritorios` opens its operation (`admin_firm_operation`, `components/firm-operation.tsx`): its use against its tier (0202), its cases going, of the month and of test, its WhatsApp (numbers, messages in and out in 30 days, sends that failed in 7), the runs and cost of its AI this month, its connections at vendors, its calendar, its DJEN readings, and whether it let the support in, with what is wrong said first. Counts and states only. **The backup adapted** (migration `0205_file_copies`, pgTAP `0205`, 5 assertions; `0012` lists the new tables as the backend's): the logical backup writes each table in parts of 50,000 rows (`packages/backup`, `<table>.part-0002.jsonl.gz` after the first), so what it holds in memory is one part; and each stored file of a firm is copied into a second private bucket (`firm-files-copy`) by the sweep `files-copy` (`lib/files/copy-work.ts`; `files_to_copy`, `record_file_copy`, `file_copies`), whose copy goes when the file's record goes (a trigger queues it in the same statement, `file_copy_removals`), so an erasure reaches the copy. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the copy of the files lives in the same Supabase project (it protects against a deletion by mistake, not against the loss of the project; the point-in-time recovery of Supabase and a copy outside it are the owner's checklist); an agency's people are visited with no authorization of the agency yet; the operator's request reaches the firm on the bell only, not by e-mail; the support visits of the development project now need the firm's authorization too.
- **M4, sprint S11, the CRM and the check of every message, and M6, the migration by the OAB and its x-ray (2026-11-11).** What the plan had of both that waits for nobody. **Where a lead came from, and ONE takes up a silent one** (migration `0207_crm`, pgTAP `0207`, 7 assertions with two firms): a message that comes from an ad that opens WhatsApp (Meta's `referral` of a message, read by `packages/whatsapp` into `adReferral`: the kind and id of the source, its address, the headline and the click's id, from the reference of the Cloud API; its test in `webhook.test.ts`) is kept on its conversation once, the first (`wa_record_ad_referral`, from the backend's intake), and a case opened from that conversation says it came from an ad (`wa_conversations_ad_case`); the thread says it in a line of its own ("A pessoa chegou pelo anúncio"). A lead whose intake stopped with ONE's question unanswered is written to again by ONE, in the 24 hours only (`lead_followups_owed`: no case yet, the last word ONE's, no person of the firm in the conversation, never who said SAIR; after the setting's hours, `crm.followup_hours`, 3 and then 20), at most twice, each reserved before it goes (`one_lead_followup`) and said in the thread ("O ONE retomou a conversa"); the person's answer sets the count back (a trigger on `wa_messages`). Its words are a rule (`leadFollowupText` of `lib/conversations/intake.ts`: the question still owed, the second time with how to stop), and the sweep is `lead-followups` (every 15 minutes, while ONE is used; `lib/conversations/followup-work.ts`). **Every message is checked against the bar's rules before it goes** (`checkMessage` of `packages/whatsapp/src/rules.ts`, pure and tested: a promise of a result, a word of price, fees, free service or discount, a pressure to decide now): a reply of ONE's attendant that falls under one is never sent and the conversation calls a person with why (`lib/conversations/agent-work.ts`); a person of the firm who writes one sees in the composer what it weighs and sends it only by "Enviar mesmo assim", on the record (`whatsapp.message_flagged_sent`, with the codes, never the text). **The lawsuits the DJEN already shows, for the migration** (migration `0208_djen_lawsuits`, pgTAP `0208`, 4 assertions with two firms): from the publications ONE read by the firm's registrations at the bar, the lawsuits that are no lawsuit of a case of ONE yet (`firm_djen_lawsuits`: the number, the court, the unit, the class, the first and last day, how many publications, and the airline when the texts name one of the table alone), for who brings the portfolio; `/casos/importar` shows them in the card "Processos que o DJEN já mostra" and gives them as a spreadsheet in the import's own columns (`lib/import/djen-lawsuits.ts`), the client and the CPF left for the firm to fill, which the import then reads as any spreadsheet. **The x-ray by the OAB** (`lib/publications/xray.ts`, pure and tested; `admin/ai/xray-actions.ts`): the operator types a registration and a span of days (15 to 90) in `/admin/ai` and reads, from the DJEN's public API at that moment, the lawsuits and publications in numbers only: by court, by kind of lawsuit, by month and by the airline a lawsuit's texts name alone; nothing of it is kept, and each reading is on the record by the registration. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: a follow-up outside the 24 hours needs a template approved at Meta, so ONE writes again only inside them; the check of a message reads words, so a promise said otherwise passes and a word in another sense is flagged (the person sends it all the same); the ad's referral was read in Meta's reference and never came from a real ad here; the lawsuits of the DJEN have no client, which the firm completes in the spreadsheet; the x-ray reads at most what the DJEN's window gives in one query, and says so when a registration has more.
- **M4, sprint S12, the evidence kit: the link records the date and the hour of each proof (2026-11-11).** What a passenger sends of the moment of the problem (a photo of the board, of the queue, of the airline's notice, a receipt of what they spent, the boarding pass) is worth more the nearer it is to the flight, and the firm's link already took it (`evidence`, `receipt`, `boarding_pass`); what was not kept was the hour. Each file already had the moment the backend received and measured its bytes (`files.confirmed_at`, the platform's clock) and its fingerprint (`files.sha256`). Migration `0209_evidence_moments` (pgTAP `0209`, 9 assertions with two firms) adds what the photo says of itself: the moment its camera wrote in its EXIF (`case_documents.taken_local`, the device's local time, and `taken_offset` when the camera wrote one), kept once by the backend (`keep_document_taken`, the service role only, never written over, only of a proof), read by the document worker from the original whatever way the photo came (`lib/cases/documents-work.ts`, with `photoTakenAt` of the new `@flyee/documents/taken`: a small parser of the TIFF block over what sharp gives, tested on blocks written by hand and on a real JPEG). `case_evidence_moments` gives a case's kit to anybody of its firm in one answer: its legs with their scheduled departure and arrival as instants (the airport's offset, `leg_instant`), the arrival on record, what ONE told the passenger of the flight (the alerts of the case that went), and each proof that was not refused, with who sent it and how, the moment ONE received it, the moment it says it was taken and its fingerprint. The dossier of the case lays it out in the order things happened, under "Provas do momento" (`lib/cases/evidence-moments.ts`, pure and tested: each proof said against the departure of the leg the problem was on, "55 minutos depois da partida prevista do voo LA 3456", the photo's own moment in Brasília's time when its device wrote its offset and as the device wrote it otherwise, and a file that says nothing of when it was made said so), and the section says nothing was sent when no proof was. On the client's link, the photos and the receipts it asks for say that ONE records the day and the hour each one arrives. The round trip of `document-quality.db.test.ts` carries a photo with its EXIF through the worker and reads the kit as the firm and as another firm. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the moment a photo says it was taken is the device's clock, which its owner can set, so it is shown beside ONE's and never alone; WhatsApp and many apps strip the EXIF, so a photo sent through the conversation has only ONE's moment; the kit lives in the dossier, the firm's document of record, and is not a file of its own in the filing package; ONE's moment is when the bytes reached the platform, not when the passenger chose them on a link that waited for a connection.
- **M5, the numbers the plan still asked for (2026-11-11): the range of the settlements, the margin of the time to the judgment, the numbers by lawyer with how alike their pieces are, and ONE's usage events.** `/numeros` already grouped the firm's lawsuits by airline, kind of problem, court (the state's), comarca, unit and kind of lawsuit, with the firm's own time beside the court's (phases 11 and 5 of the plans of 2026-11-07). Now each group also says **the settlements per claimant** ("Acordo por autor": the median and, in brackets, where the middle half lies; the value the database already gave, `settled_cents`, split among the lawsuit's claimants; nothing of fewer than five), **the margin of the days to the judgment** at 95% confidence (the two values of the sample between which the median of all such lawsuits lies, by the ranks the binomial gives, `medianInterval`; none while the sample is too small for them, and on the tile "Até a sentença" as its hint) and **how alike the approved pieces were** to the firm's most alike one ("Semelhança das peças", the median of `case_drafts.alike`, what the courts look at first in mass litigation). The new grouping **"Advogado"** counts each lawsuit for each of the firm's lawyers on it, by the name their profile gives, and one with none as not recorded: volume, results, time and likeness by lawyer, and by court and unit as before. Migration `0210_numbers_by_lawyer` (pgTAP `0210`, 5 assertions with two firms) redefines `firm_lawsuit_numbers` with each lawsuit's lawyers and the likeness of its approved piece; the rules are pure and tested in `lib/numbers/lawsuits.ts`. **ONE's usage events** (migration `0211_usage_events`, pgTAP `0211`, 8 assertions with two firms): the vocabulary of 0007 was the platform layer's and the previous product's; it now knows `case.opened`, `case.closed`, `lawsuit.filed`, `piece.approved`, `client_link.finished`, `conversation.started` and `conversation.from_ad`, each written by a trigger on its own row where the thing happens (never by a screen, so none is forgotten or inflated, and a trigger never fails the write it watches), with closed values only (ONE or a person, the module's slug, the kind of lawsuit, a test, where a case came from) and read by the operator alone. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: the margin is of the median, not of one lawsuit's time (a lawsuit may well take longer); the likeness is measured against the firm's own pieces, not the market's; a lawsuit with several lawyers counts for each, so the groups by lawyer add up to more than the whole; no screen reads the usage events yet (the operator reads them in the database), and the events of the previous product stay known for the rows written before.
- **M6, the module versioned as data (2026-11-11).** What a module's cases read was edited in place: the documents each kind of problem asks (a platform setting, 0177), the instructions of each AI task (one version in force per task, 0016) and the platform's piece models (0045, 0189); a change reached every case at once, the ones in course included, and nothing said which a case had followed. The owner decided the same day that only the operator publishes a version, that nobody edits one once published, that each case keeps the version it was opened under and a new case takes the latest published, and that a version withdrawn for a defect sends its cases to the latest published ("Product decisions" of `CLAUDE.md`). Migration `0212_module_versions` (pgTAP `0212`, 18 assertions with two firms; the mold is the previous product's `guard_module_version`): `module_versions` (draft, published, withdrawn; a published version never edited nor deleted, one draft at a time per module, read by the operator alone), each a snapshot of what is in force when prepared (`module_snapshot`: the documents by problem, the version of each AI task the module names in `product_modules.version_tasks`, the platform's models of the initial by their id and of each intermediate piece by its kind). A case is stamped with its module's latest published version when created (`cases.module_version_id`, a trigger) and reads through it (`case_module_manifest`, the latest published once its own is withdrawn): the documents it asks (`case_problem_documents`, read by `case_required_documents`, `case_dispensables`, the case's screen and ONE's carrying of the case through `case_module_version` and `case_version_documents`), the prompt version of each run of the module's tasks (`start_ai_run` by `module_prompt_version`: a run whose subject is of a case, a document, a draft, an interview, a check of a piece's facts or a hearing, takes its case's version; a run of no case, such as a conversation, takes the module's latest published) and the platform's model a piece starts from (`request_case_draft`, `request_intermediate_piece`). Version 1 is what was in force on 2026-11-11, and every case there was is stamped with it, so nothing changed that day. The operator reads in `/admin/modulos` each module's version in force, what changed since it in what is in force now (`lib/admin/module-versions.ts`, `manifestChanges`, pure and tested), prepares the next version with a note, publishes it, discards a draft and withdraws a published version with its reason (`admin_draft_module_version`, `admin_publish_module_version`, `admin_discard_module_version`, `admin_withdraw_module_version`, each on the record; the last published is never withdrawn); the dossier names the version of its case. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: ONE's own messages are not in a version, since the code changes their words by the case (a minor, an agency, who signs first), and the firm's own words of them are versioned by the firm (0191); the fixed questions of the intake are a rule in code, and the attendant's script is its prompt, which the version pins; the WhatsApp templates are each firm's, approved by Meta; a conversation is no case, so the attendant follows the latest published version; the instructions of an intermediate piece's kind are read as they are, only its model is pinned; a new prompt version of a module's task, a new document list or a new platform model reaches the module's cases only once a version that holds it is published.
- **The settings, each in one home (2026-11-11).** The owner asked for the settings screens to be read for redundancies and responsibilities, and approved the separation drawn on the canvas "ONE · Configurações" the same day. Ten defects: the weekly digest and the support's access sat on the person's own page; Equipe held what is a person's (the bar, the certificates, their cloud signature, leaving the firm); "Dados do escritório" named both the firm's name card and the clearing of test data; "O que o ONE decide" mixed ONE's 22 decisions, in one flat list, with the firm's rules (who signs, the bill in somebody else's name) and the passenger module's values; the triage of the publications had a decision there and a partner's switch inside `/publicacoes`; what the client reads was in three places; the firm's internal deadline was in the person's agenda panel; and the Valor da Causa Pedido was in two places of the core. Now "Sua conta" is Perfil (with leaving), Segurança and Atuação profissional (`/settings/atuacao`); "Escritório" is Dados do escritório (`/settings/escritorio`), Equipe, Regras dos casos (`/settings/regras`), O que o ONE decide (grouped by the stage of the case, with the triage's switch inside its decision), Mensagens ao cliente (with the notices, out of Conexões), Conexões, Plano e pagamentos and Apagar dados de teste; and each contracted module has a group of its own (`firmSettings` of `ModuleDefinition`), the passenger module's with `/settings/direito-do-passageiro` (the values by kind of problem, the settlement floor and the agencies' clause) and its partner agencies. The agenda's panel and `/publicacoes` say how their setting is and lead to its home; the setups use the home's components (the clause's fields are one component of the module). No migration: no rule, permission or table changed. The record of each page is in `apps/web/ARCHITECTURE.md`, "Settings and connections".
- **The review against the CPC, the Lei 9.099/1995 and the Regimento Interno of TJGO, and what it fixed (2026-11-11).** The user asked for what is built to be read against the three norms. What already followed them: the minor and the ceiling per claimant (Lei 9.099, arts. 3 and 8), the forum at the domicile (art. 4, III), the count of the terms (CPC, arts. 216, 219, 220 and 224; Lei 9.099, art. 12-A), the terms of the acts of `deadline_acts`, the calendar of the tests against art. 123 of the Regimento, the powers of art. 105 in the power of attorney, the Ministério Público and the free justice by claimant in the civil court. Four gaps of the terms, fixed by migration `0213_judgment_terms_everywhere` (pgTAP `0213`, 12 assertions with two firms): a judgment given at the hearing now proposes the embargos and the appeal from the hearing's day, where the parties are intimated (Lei 9.099, art. 19, § 1º; CPC, art. 1.003, § 1º), through `record_hearing_report`, and its publication later proposes no second term of an act counted from a day in the 30 days before it; a judgment that homologates a settlement proposes no appeal (Lei 9.099, art. 41; CPC, art. 1.000); the decision of the embargos de declaração, of a lawsuit whose last judgment is a sentença with no final judgment after it, takes back the appeal ONE proposed before and nobody confirmed and proposes it again from itself (Lei 9.099, art. 50; CPC, art. 1.026; never after an acórdão); and a recurso of the small-claims court marked filed proposes its preparo in 48 hours (art. 42, § 1º; act `preparo_recurso_inominado`, 2 calendar days). The words are read by `acordo-v1` and `embargos-v1` of `@flyee/rules/judgment` and by the same forms in the database (`reading_homologates_settlement`, `reading_decides_embargos`); each term is ONE's through `one_propose_term`, only where the firm lets ONE propose the deadlines of its publications. The term to pay a condemnation (migration `0214_payment_term_by_kind`, pgTAP `0214`, 5 assertions): in the small-claims court 15 business days from the final judgment over the court's calendar (Lei 9.099, arts. 12-A and 52, III and IV), then the cumprimento with the fine of 10 percent and no fees; in the civil court the request comes first, with no fine until the intimation's term passes (CPC, arts. 513, § 2º, I, and 523); the amount keeps the lawsuit's kind and the last day (`lawsuit_kind`, `pay_until`), counted by `paymentTermEnds` of `lib/courts/amount.ts` in the backend's sweep, and the card "Resultado" says each (`amount-step-request` is the civil court's). A recurso's count names the court's own days it went past, for the lawyer to prove when filing (CPC, art. 1.003, § 6º; `localDaysToProve` of `lib/deadlines/deadlines.ts`, in `DeadlineCount`). The petition names the airline's head office after its CNPJ (migration `0215_defendant_head_office`, pgTAP `0215`, 4 assertions; CPC, art. 319, II; Lei 9.099, art. 14, § 1º, I): `airlines.address`, recorded for the four Brazilian operators from the Receita Federal's open data on 2026-11-11, and `[[RE]]` of `case_draft_filled` writes it; pgTAP `0032` gives its invented airline no address. Prescription was already a rule (`qualificacao-v1`). **Left, each with its reason:** the value of the cause "que este Juízo arbitrar" (0064) goes against CPC, arts. 291, 292, V, and 319, V, and is the owner's decision of 2026-10-27 to revisit; a claimant of 16 or 17 signs nothing although assisted (CC, art. 4, I; CPC, art. 71), and how they sign is a choice of the flow; the claim to the Seção Cível against an acórdão of a turma recursal (Regimento, art. 16, V) and the session of judgment with the inscription for oral argument (arts. 121, 147 and 150) wait for the screens of the second degree, which ONE does not have; the free justice asked in the recurso of the small-claims court (CPC, art. 99, § 7º); the forum at the defendant's seat (Lei 9.099, art. 4, I), which the owner's rule refuses unless it is a city of the trip; an adult under curatorship and an airline whose bankruptcy was decreed (art. 8), which no record says; and the reminder of a hearing, which could say what missing it costs (art. 51, I; CPC, art. 334, § 8º).
- **The case's trail shows what an action moved (2026-11-11).** The owner asked that an action in a card that moves the case bring the screen to the trail, so the person knows what they did moved the case. `useCaseActions` now takes the trail as the screen reads it (the steps, who has the case and the lines of what happens now, `case-view.tsx`) and compares it before the action and once the case read again is on screen; when it changed, `showTrail` (`journey-card.tsx`) rings "Andamento do caso" for a moment and, when its head is out of the upper half of the screen, scrolls to it (smooth, or at once for who asked for less motion), never from under a field being typed in; the focus goes back to the button without scrolling, and what came of it in the card is one "Ver" away in a toast. A dialog that saved on its own does the same; a change made by ONE or the client, read by the screen by itself, never moves it. No migration.
- **The review's follow-up: accepting a judgment, and where the firm is in the power of attorney (2026-11-11).** Accepting a judgment now takes back every term ONE proposed of it and nobody confirmed: from its publication as before, from the day it was given at a hearing, and from the decision of its embargos (migration `0216_accept_takes_every_term`, `decide_judgment_path` redefined from 0185; pgTAP `0216`, 3 assertions with two firms). The power of attorney says where the firm is (CPC, art. 105, §§ 2º and 3º, and art. 287; migration `0217_firm_office`, pgTAP `0217`, 5 assertions with two firms): the firm gives its address, its professional e-mail and, as a society, its registration at the bar (`organizations.office_*`, `oab_society_uf`, `oab_society_number`, saved by who manages the firm account through `set_firm_office`, on the record), and `[[ESCRITORIO]]` (`case_paper_filled` redefined from 0093, `firm_office_words`) writes them after the firm's name and CNPJ; a firm that gave none is named as before, so no paper waits for it. "Dados do escritório" has the card (`settings/team/components/office-card.tsx`, the form `components/firm/office-form.tsx`, the CEP filling the address), and the setup's step of the papers says whether the power of attorney goes with the address and opens the same form over the step (`setup-view.tsx`). Still left from the review: the free justice asked in the recurso of the small-claims court (it reaches the client's link, the declaration, the gates of the piece and of the filing, and a new version of the module for the recurso's model) and the session of judgment with the inscription for oral argument (a record and a place in the agenda of their own), each a slice of its own; the owner's decisions (the value of the cause, the forum at the defendant's seat, the words of the hearing's reminder) and the user's choice of how a claimant of 16 or 17 signs.
- **The chat's script, its first part (2026-11-12).** The owner wrote to a firm's WhatsApp as a passenger and found the intake abrupt: nobody said who spoke, the case opened right after the name with no yes asked and no word on what it costs, the summary of what was understood never came (a "25/10" with no year went unconfirmed), who travelled along was asked after the case opened, the case's number (a 1) told the client the firm was new, and the last message piled four steps over a long link. The owner approved on 2026-11-12 a script that keeps the whole conversation in the chat up to the signature (the Claude Doc "ONE · Roteiro do atendimento pelo WhatsApp", https://claude.ai/artifact/1yVHcVZzFgYc3YsebQGp3S), with every recommendation of its list: the case opens only at "Quero seguir", the firm writes its fees and its answers to common doubts, no case number goes to a client, the CPF is shown whole where the client checks it, a fellow adult passenger is asked to write to the firm (until the opinion on soliciting clients says otherwise). This first part redoes the intake: migration `0227_chat_intake` (pgTAP `0227`, 10 assertions with two firms; `intake_facts_clean`, `record_conversation_intake` with `target_clear`, `intake_person` and `one_open_case_from_intake` redefined, the messages `fees` and `faq` in `one_messages`, the third version of `atendimento.entrada` reading who travelled along and answers by number, published as a new version of the passenger module) and `lib/conversations/intake.ts` (pure, tested: what is missing, the name first and the companions last; the numbered options and `choiceOf`; the opening that names the firm and its assistant; the care or thanks said once; the summary with the year; the offer and the doubts). `takeIntake` in `agent-work.ts` keeps where the intake stands in `wa_conversations.intake ->> 'etapa'` (`resumo`, `corrigir`, `oferta`, `duvidas`, `recusou`): a reply that is one of the options is taken as it is, anything else is read by the task; nothing missing, the summary; confirmed, the rule; qualified, the offer; "Agora não" kept kindly and offered again when the person writes; "Tenho dúvidas" answered with the firm's own text, else by a person. The case's opening message asks the identity in the chat and gives the link only as another place to do the same. A first name of two letters, which no person's record takes, gives way to the WhatsApp profile's name until the identity says the full one. The second part asks each document in turn: after the files of a turn are said back, the next one the case still owes, by whose it is (migration `0230_chat_papers`, pgTAP `0230`, 8 assertions: `conversation_papers_owed` over the case's version of the documents, `conversation_gathered_case`, the rule of 0050 said once, and `one_dispensables_not_had`, the client's "Não tenho" kept as on the link; `lib/conversations/papers.ts`, pure, tested). The third sends the links to sign in the chat: once the papers are open at ZapSign, ONE writes each paper's own address to who signs, in the conversation it carries with them inside the 24 hours, once, never to who asked to stop (migration `0231_chat_signature`, pgTAP `0231`, 7 assertions: `case_papers.told_in_chat_at`, `signature_links_owed`, `one_tell_signature_links`, marked in the same transaction as the message; the sweep `signature-links` and a call right after `openReservedEnvelopes`); a paper signed by gov.br is said to be on the link. The fourth checks the client's data in the chat: once the identity and the proof of address were accepted, ONE shows what it read in one message, the client confirms (the marks of what was read go: it is theirs now) or corrects a field (a CPF by its check digits, a date, an address by its CEP and number), and ONE asks what the papers need and no document gives, the nationality, the marital status, the profession (migration `0232_chat_data`, pgTAP `0232`, 9 assertions: `wa_conversations.chat_step`, `conversation_person`, `record_chat_step`, `one_person_fields` through `apply_person_fields` written by the client, and `chat_data_checks_owed` with `one_start_data_check` for a document a person of the firm accepted later, the sweep `chat-data-checks`; `lib/conversations/data-check.ts`, pure, tested, and a round trip). The fifth asks in the chat what was asked on the link only: the word on free justice and on the civil court, one at a time, the civil court's own wording shown and kept with the answer, each kept as on the link by the client with the channel it came by; and the facts of the action, which ONE told in the chat and the client answered on the link, are written in the chat to confirm or correct there (migration `0233_chat_questions`, pgTAP `0233`, 9 assertions: `chat_questions_owed`, `one_ask_chat_question`, `one_answer_chat_question`, `one_answer_piece_facts` by "chat"; the sweep `chat-questions`; `lib/conversations/questions.ts`). The sixth takes in who travelled along: a child the passenger named with an age under 18 joins the case as a claimant the passenger answers for, and the child's certidão or RG is asked by name, as a child's before the birth date is read; an adult is asked to write to the firm themself, since each adult tells their case and signs their own power of attorney, and ONE writes to nobody whose number a client gave (migration `0234_chat_companions`, pgTAP `0234`, 5 assertions: `one_open_case_from_intake` with `target_children`, `conversation_papers_owed` redefined; `companionsOf` in `lib/conversations/intake.ts`). The seventh shows the choices as buttons where the vendor has them: a message of ONE that ends in numbered options keeps its text as it is (the panel and WhatsApp Web read the numbers), and the outbox hands the official API its choices, sent as buttons up to three of twenty letters and as a list up to ten of twenty-four, the person's tap coming back as the option's words, which the script reads as it reads a number (`choicesOf` and `fitsButtons` in `@flyee/whatsapp/rules`, the Cloud API's `interactive` message in its provider, with the contract test; every option of the script fits a row). Not run against Meta yet, like the rest of the provider. The script is built whole; what it still waits on is the owner's: the firm's texts on fees and doubts are written per firm in "Mensagens ao cliente", the templates for the reminders outside the 24 hours wait on the Meta app, and the link's address on the official domain.
- **The chat, one thing at a time (2026-11-12).** The owner's test client was asked what her account missed in one message, four questions at once (0092), and did not answer; two hours later the check of her data (0232) started over the question that waited, her "1" answered the data, and the account stayed open with nobody asking again, so the case screen showed it waiting for the client. Now ONE asks the account's questions one at a time ("Pergunta 1 de 4: ...", each next one after an answer, the answers put together for the account as an answer on the link is, `lib/cases/account.ts`), asks a question that waits again when the client writes after another step ("Voltando ao seu relato:"), and starts nothing over a question that waits: the account's questions wait for a step of the chat to end, and the check of data and the client's words before court wait for the account's answers (migration `0235_chat_one_at_a_time`, pgTAP `0235`, 5 assertions: `conversation_account_waits`, `account_asks_owed`, `chat_data_checks_owed` and `chat_questions_owed` redefined; a round trip).
- **The identity's name, and the first document alone (2026-11-12).** The owner, as a client, typed "Lidya" and sent an identity that says Nidya: the check refused it for the name and, in the same message, asked for the proof of address. A name typed in a chat (or how the person asked to be called, 0227) is not settled: an identity in another name is now accepted and its name, CPF and birth date become the person's while no document, issued paper or registry settled the name (`person_name_settled`), and the chat says the name it will use; once the name is settled, an identity in another one is refused for it, saying whose to send. The check's sixth instructions never refuse an identity for its name, and after a document refused ONE asks no next one. The case opened in the chat asks the identity alone, in two lines, with no link: the firm's own text of that moment no longer needs `{link}` (migration `0236_identity_name`, pgTAP `0236`, 8 assertions; `apply_document_check` redefined from 0123).
- **Not run yet:** the screens the first walk did not reach with data (the publications, the deadlines, the hearings, the filing package and its signature, the import, the dossier), the WhatsApp provider against Meta (ONE has no Meta app: the contract tests answer from the shapes of the vendor's reference, read on 2026-10-09, and the round trip is proven with the simulator's deliveries through the real parser and the real database functions), a CPF lookup against Serpro (the provider is proven with a scripted HTTP layer in the shapes of Serpro's reference, read on 2026-10-09), a HEIC photo through the deployed `sharp` (the job is proven by the database tests and the pure rules; the provider by its fake), ZapSign (the provider is proven with a stand-in in the shapes of its reference, read on 2026-10-09), REDEMET (the same, from its reference read on 2026-10-09; the key comes from a registration the owner makes), the DJEN from the app and from where the app is deployed (the client's rules were checked against the public API by hand on 2026-10-09, from this machine and by the shape of the answers only; the suite itself answers from a stand-in; whether the API answers the region the functions run in, and what limit it meets there, is seen only with one real reading from a preview with `DJEN_CAPTURE=1`), the shadow triage against a real model, a transcription of a real audio by a real model (the path is proven by the database tests, the executor's audio guard and the fake provider; it needs `GEMINI_API_KEY` in the server's environment, or another provider that hears audio named in the task's prompt version), the reading of ANAC's record from where the app is deployed (a month's file was read from this machine on 2026-10-10; the worker's reading is proven with a stand-in in the file's form) and FlightAware (the provider is proven with a stand-in in the shapes of its reference, read on 2026-10-10), the course of the lawsuits from where the app is deployed (Projudi was asked by hand from this machine on 2026-10-11, by the shape of its answers; Escavador and Judit never ran: their providers answer from stand-ins in the shapes of their documents), the import of a real export of a management system (the reader is proven with workbooks and text exports the tests build), the triage with a sample against a real model (the path is proven by the database tests with the model's answer written in), a package of eproc or PJe filed in the court's system (the limits and types are the courts' manuals, unconfirmed), the firm's calendar against Google (the provider is proven with a stand-in in the shapes of Google's references, read on 2026-10-11; the worker with the fake) the notices to clients against Meta (a template is submitted to the simulator, which approves it at once; Meta reviews a utility template, and may call it marketing, which keeps the kind off) and a text of a piece rewritten by a real model (the path is proven by the database tests and the round trip with the model told what to write; whether a model keeps every numbered mark where it found it, and changes only what was asked, is seen only with a real run: a mark it writes another way stays in the text and keeps the piece from being approved, and one it drops or repeats is said to the lawyer beside the text and shows in what changed), the measure of a photo a client really took (the rule is proven on pictures the tests draw, and the link's path by a round trip against the embedded database with the same pictures; where its limits sit for real photos is learned from the pilot firm's), and a lawyer's signature at a real PSC with a `.p7s` taken by the Projudi of TJGO (the client is proven with a stand-in in the norm's shapes, the `.p7s` by OpenSSL's CAdES check against the fake's AC). The database side is proven by pgTAP on an embedded PostgreSQL and on the development project, and the app by types, lint, unit tests, the reference check and the build; the screens had their first browser pass against the development project on 2026-10-21 (above).
- **Safety, done:** the credential files of other products were moved out of the repo; `apps/web/.env.local` is a blank ONE file; the deploy runs only from GitHub Actions, with a Vercel token and no Git integration on the Vercel side; the local Supabase project is `one-local`; the agent permission `Bash(node:*)` was removed.
