- **Still carrying the previous product, by the milestone that rewrites it:** the database's machinery of the one-time access and its grants and the e-mails of the kit (`kit-receipt`, `access-ended`), no longer sold since 0202 (no priced plan of ONE is seeded: the operator writes them); the auth e-mail templates in `supabase/templates` (with the ONE Supabase project); the server-side conversion events at sign-up and checkout (`lib/meta-capi.ts`, `lib/ga4-mp.ts`), inert while their keys are blank; the `marketing-page` skill and `docs/DESIGN.md`, whose examples are the previous product's.
- **Decided by the owner on 2026-10-14** (the three decisions the points of 2026-10-13 depended on): what ONE decides alone reaches the lawyer's own acts too (the text of a piece, the decision to file or hold, a deadline), by a parametrization each firm changes, with automation first; a lawyer's certificate, password or second factor is stored when an automation of ONE needs it (the filing in a court's system: the custody and the channel are designed from the notes of 2026-10-14 on the MNI, the courts' systems and the cloud certificates, kept out of the repository); and a family with a minor goes to the civil court together (built, the fifth part of S10).
- **Decided by the owner on 2026-10-15** (on the filing by ONE in a court's own channel, after the research of 2026-10-14): build first what needs no court's authorization (the lawyer's cloud signature through a PSC, the signed file a court takes, the MNI client, each with a stand-in where the vendor or the court stands); never build an automation a court considers fraud, but only where that is certain (a written rule of the court or the CNJ that forbids it); where it is only a forecast, the direct automation (ONE in the court system's own screen, with the lawyer's credentials in custody) may be built. Each court is checked against its written rules before its screen is automated.
- **Decided by the owner on 2026-10-27** (on the travel agency linked to a firm): the passenger's documents an agency attaches come into ONE and ONE uses them, the passenger's agreement given in the agency's contract with them; this replaces "no data passed" of 2026-10-08. Not built yet: how an agency enters ONE (a link of the firm's, an account, an integration with the agency's system) is the owner's to choose, and the legal opinion on soliciting clients (already waited for) answers whether a firm may receive clients so, since the Estatuto da Advocacia (art. 34, IV) forbids taking causes with or without a third party, whatever the client agreed to; the agency's clause has to be a specific consent, highlighted among the others (LGPD, art. 7, § 5, and art. 8, §§ 1 and 4), with a parent's specific consent for a child's data (art. 14, § 1). What an agency attaches is then read as any document (above).
- **Decided by the owner on 2026-11-05** (the audit of the case screen): the case and `/inicio` follow ONE by themselves; a card opens when something comes to ask the firm there and closes once it is done, and its head says where it stands; whether a decision is the judgment is asked only when its words do not say it; the 72-hour notice only warns while the platform is tested, and becomes a block before production (not opening the case, or waiting for a lawyer to release it: to be decided).

The header line of an export of Astrea by the pilot firm (the names of its columns only, never a client's data), so the import recognises them by itself; until then a person confirms each column. The test of the filing station in eproc and in PJe, done with a lawyer (the login, the signer, the real file limits and the types of each file), each of which becomes a row of `court_systems`; and the goal a category of the AI's triage must meet to leave the shadow (the setting's numbers are a proposal). A Google Cloud project of the platform with the Calendar API on and an OAuth consent screen asking `calendar.app.created` (its client in `GOOGLE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` and `GOOGLE_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`), published out of Testing with the verification Google asks for, so a firm's grant does not end after seven days. The Supabase development project exists since 2026-10-21 (in us-east-1); staging and production are still to be made (the checklist puts them in São Paulo). The whole list, kept for the owner as a checklist with a state per item, is the Claude Doc "ONE · O que providenciar fora da plataforma" (https://claude.ai/code/artifact/2b7654a2-a43b-407e-b042-c27c43ea3664): it adds the Vercel project on the Pro plan with its functions in São Paulo, the Supabase projects in São Paulo, the Inngest account, ONE's domain and the Resend account with it, the Anthropic and Gemini keys, the e-mail of the platform's first operator, the MNI access at TJGO (asked by the pilot firm to its software engineering, with Projudi's test environment; it is what lets ONE file the lawsuits by itself, 0101, and what else the court must give is in `packages/courts/README.md`), an ICP-Brasil SSL certificate for ONE's domain and ONE's registration as an application at BirdID and SerproID, a lawyer of the pilot with a cloud certificate, the Asaas account, the texts a client accepts on the link, and the curator of the module's default content.

- **"Nova pessoa" with any module or none, and the funnel in the firm and the agency (2026-11-11).** The owner asked that a person be registered by one generic name, Contato, Lead and Cliente being the funnel defined before, and the module's own steps coming after; and the same in the partner's area, where the third stage reads "Com o escritório" (the agency is never told whether the passenger signed). - **Real WhatsApp: the Chatwoot panel on ONE's own server, a number connected by its token or by WhatsApp Web, and the test firm in the test chat's place (2026-11-11).** The owner decided on 2026-11-09 to leave the simulator for a real WhatsApp with the `whatsapp-support` kit (Chatwoot) as the attendance panel, ONE keeping the vendor's side (it receives and sends, under its database's rules, with its own AI), and on 2026-11-11 to stop using the test chat, to have a way to connect a number unofficially, and four decisions on the panel (a message the bar's rules weigh fails with why and the same text sent again within 10 minutes counts as sent anyway; reopening a conversation after the 24 hours stays in `/atendimento`; the panel's auto-resolution off; attachments copied to the panel, a person's deletion and the retention reaching it). **The panel**: the kit 1.0.0 is in `packages/atendimento` (model `por-cliente`, one account per firm; `escolhas.json` with ONE's brand), with ONE's own deploy files in `packages/atendimento/implantacao` (`pilha-one.sh`, `compose.one.yml`, `one.caddy`), on the VPS the operator bought for ONE and Orkestree (Hostinger KVM 4 in Campinas, `srv2147904`), each system with its own stack (`PILHA=one`) behind one edge Caddy; the panel answers at `https://atendimentoone.orkestree.com` (a provisional address of the Orkestree's domain), with the Chatwoot telemetry and push relay off and the page that makes the super administrator closed once the operator made it; the Platform App's token is `CHATWOOT_PLATFORM_TOKEN` (Vercel and `.env.local`). `metaPlatform` adds `META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID`, which only the window needs, so a number no longer goes deaf for lack of it); who manages a firm connects its number with the account's id, the number's id and the permanent token of a system user of the firm's business, checked against the account before it is sealed (`connectMetaNumberByToken`), the six digits asked only of a number not on the Cloud API yet (`getNumberInfo` now says `onCloudApi`, `platform_type` `CLOUD_API`), and a number that also lives on the phone app connects only through the window. **WhatsApp Web, by the platform's OpenWA** (migration `0221_openwa`, pgTAP `0221`, 5 assertions with two firms): `@flyee/whatsapp/providers/openwa` (`OpenWaProvider`: text, image and document, its media by chat and message, no template; the sessions; the reading of a delivery and its `X-OpenWA-Signature`; written from OpenWA 0.29.1's specification and checked against the deployed OpenWA on 2026-11-11 by a session made and removed, its QR and its webhook; 7 tests); `wa_numbers.provider` takes `openwa` (its session is its `phone_number_id` and `waba_id`), a real number as one of Meta (`register_wa_number`, `test_chat_link`, `usage_now` redefined); a firm links its number by reading a QR after accepting the risk on the connect card (`connectOpenWaNumber`, `openWaLinkState`, the QR read again every 3 seconds), the deliveries come to `/api/webhooks/openwa` and go through the same door as Meta's (`handleOpenWaDelivery`), and a message the number sent while one of ONE to that person is being sent is taken later (`wa_sending_to`, answered 503 for OpenWA to bring it again) so ONE's own is never read as the phone's; OpenWA runs in ONE's stack (`waone.orkestree.com`, only `/api/*`), configured by `OPENWA_URL`, `OPENWA_API_KEY` and `OPENWA_WEBHOOK_SECRET`. **The test firm** (migration `0220_test_firms`, pgTAP `0220`, 10 assertions with two firms; `0003` and `0012` follow it): the operator marks a firm as a test firm with a note in its dossier (`admin_set_test_firm`, the second factor, on the record; `/admin/escritorios`), and every case and every trip ONE follows of that firm is a test from then on (and as it is written: the triggers `cases_test_firm` and `tracked_trips_test_firm`), its flights never read at FlightAware; the firm's link (`test_firms.token`, read by who manages it in `/settings/connections` and in the module's area, `firm_test_tools`) opens its tools at `/t/<token>` (each test trip with the tools that cancel it, make it six hours late, make it leave or land, or start it over: `test_firm_set_legs`, the test chat's of 0172) and the test court at `/t/<token>/tribunal` (`test_court_org` takes the link); a test trip of a test firm is told and answered through its real number (`reserve_flight_alert`, `reserve_trip_day_message`, `reserve_passenger_welcome`, `reserve_hearing_staff_message` and `conversation_pending_alert` redefined with that one change). The test chat is offered no longer: the firm's setup, the module's setup (which now connects the number in place) and the connections screen show a test chat only while one is on, to be turned off. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: nothing ran against Meta (the platform's app at Meta does not exist); no OpenWA session was linked to a real phone yet; the 0220 and the 0221 are not applied to the development project yet; WhatsApp Web sends nothing outside the 24 hours (no template), so a client's notices outside them do not go on such a number; the test court and the tools are reached by the firm's link, not yet from a test case's own filing card.
- **The review's last two slices: free justice in the recurso of the small-claims court, and the session of judgment with the registration for oral argument (2026-11-12).** **Free justice in the recurso** (migration `0218_appeal_free_justice`, pgTAP `0218`, 12 assertions with two firms; roundtrip `apps/web/src/lib/cases/appeal-free-justice.db.test.ts`): the recurso inominado has costs, the preparo, spared only to who has free justice (Lei 9.099/1995, art. 54, parágrafo único), and a claimant asks it in the recurso itself, spared proving the preparo until the court decides (CPC, art. 99, § 7º). Once the firm decides the recurso on a judgment of the small-claims court (0185), or a recurso of the lawsuit is in course, and until it is filed (`lawsuit_appeal_open`), each claimant is asked as the civil court's are (`free_justice_needs` redefined): on their link, where the question is open whatever the case's stage (`client_link_free_justice` says `appeal`; `client-free-justice.tsx`), or through the firm, in the judgment's block of the card "Resultado" (`judgment-path-block.tsx` with `FreeJusticeAnswers appeal`; the rules card keeps the answers of the start). Who asks signs the same declaration of hardship: a case with the court issues it through a line of its own (`appeal_papers_due`, `appeal_paper_to_issue`, `record_appeal_paper`, `appeal_papers_owed`; `issueAppealPapers` and `appealPapersRound` of `lib/cases/advance-work.ts`, right after the answer and in the sweep `appeal-papers`), never by waking ONE's carrying on of a case before its filing. The recurso writes the request by name where its model puts `[[GRATUIDADE]]` and `[[PEDIDO_GRATUIDADE]]` (`case_draft_filled` redefined from 0215); the platform's model of the recurso takes the marks in a new published version of the module, built from the latest published one with that model alone changed (a draft the operator had prepared is discarded: prepared again it holds the new model), and the version before it, which differs in the recurso alone, is withdrawn for the defect by migration `0222_recurso_version_withdrawn` (pgTAP `0222`, 4 assertions with two firms; 0218 was already on the development project when this was seen), so its cases (the ones already with the court, which are the ones that appeal) read the new recurso, with no change of their documents or prompts (the owner's rule of 0212 for a defect; pointed out by the session of 0212); a recurso written before, while somebody asks, waits for a lawyer to put the mark (`free_justice_mark_missing`); a recurso is not approved while a claimant has not answered (`free_justice_piece_blocker` redefined). ONE files a piece alone with no attachment (0189), so a recurso that asks free justice is the lawyer's to file with the declaration (`piece_filing_blocker` redefined: `free_justice_declaration`, `pieces-blocker-free-justice`), and the 48 hours of the preparo are proposed only when somebody of the lawsuit does not ask (`case_drafts_preparo` redefined from 0213). **The session of judgment** (migration `0219_judgment_sessions`, pgTAP `0219`, 11 assertions with two firms; `@flyee/rules/sessions`, `sustentacao-v1`, with `businessDayBefore` of `@flyee/rules/deadlines`, 5 tests): each court's rule of registration is data (`oral_argument_rules`), seeded for the TJGO from its acts read on 2026-11-12: the Turmas Recursais, until 10h of the business day before a virtual session starts, in the system (Resolução nº 225/2023, art. 78, § 1º; oral argument open in the recurso inominado, art. 107, never in embargos, art. 110), and for a presencial one until it opens (CPC, art. 937, § 2º; by videoconference asked until the day before, art. 66, § 1º); the chambers, 10h of the business day before a virtual session and the opening of a presencial one (Regimento Interno do TJGO, art. 150). A session is a record of its own (`judgment_sessions`), never a hearing, and the client hears nothing of it: ONE records it from the pauta it reads, where the firm lets ONE put on its agenda what a publication sets (`publication_hearing`), the first day the text names after its own, its one hour, virtual when the text says so, and never from a text that also moves or withdraws something (`one_record_judgment_session`, triggers on `publications`); a new pauta of the lawsuit replaces the session to come, with why. A person records one ONE did not read, withdraws one with why, and a partner or a lawyer says whether the firm registered to argue or will not (`record_judgment_session`, `withdraw_judgment_session`, `decide_oral_argument`, on the record). The backend counts the limit over the court's calendar by the session's revision (`sessions_to_count`, `record_session_limit`; `lib/courts/session-work.ts`, the sweep `session-limits`), passing weekends, national holidays, days the court was closed and days of partial business, each said; a court with no rule here is said so, never guessed. The case shows the session and the limit in each lawsuit of the card "Resultado" (`judgment-session-block.tsx`, `lib/courts/sessions.ts`), and the agenda, the morning's e-mail and the calendar link hand out the session on its day and, while nobody said whether the firm argues, the limit on its own (`agenda_items` redefined from 0132; `AgendaSession`, the entries `session` and `oral` of `lib/agenda/agenda.ts`), and the case reads itself again when one changes (`case_pulse` redefined from 0182); pgTAP `0003` holds a session of each firm. **Left:** the claim to the Seção Cível against an acórdão (Regimento, art. 16, V), which waits for a screen of the acórdão; the rules of other courts, each a row of `oral_argument_rules` once read in its acts; a pauta read from a text with no day after its own is a person's to record; a family whose guardian already signed a declaration at the start in the civil court signs no second one for the recurso (one paper per person and kind, 0054); the owner's decisions and the user's choice listed above. 0218, 0219 and 0222 are on the development project (2026-11-12): the first version of each module is withdrawn there, and its one case (a test) reads the new recurso.
- **The home laid out by what it holds (2026-11-12).** The owner found `/inicio` spread out and mostly empty: the line of what ONE did this month across the page, "O escritório precisa agir" with one thing in half of its card, and "Para acompanhar" a heading over one tile a quarter of the page wide. Under the greeting, what holds a case now takes the wide column, and what to follow and ONE's line of the month the side one; a column is drawn only with something in it, and alone it takes the whole width; the portfolio's card stays across, under both, and the setup's list and the alerts across, above. "Para acompanhar" is a card of its own (`CountList`, `components/product/count-list.tsx`, in the catalog): one line per count with its icon, the number and a chevron, one column at the side and two or three when the card is wide (a container query). "O escritório precisa agir" says what it is behind the "i" and lays its things in as many columns as the card holds, one alone across it; ONE's line reads its own width, its link under the sentence in a narrow column (`one-work-line.tsx`; `saysOneWork` of `lib/one-work/work.ts`, with its test); the loading skeleton has the new shape. Seen on a page of invented data at 1800, 1440 and 390 pixels and in the dark, in five states (the owner's screenshot, a busy day, nothing waiting, only things to follow, only what holds a case), the page then removed. No migration.
- **The review's next slices: the assisted minor signs, and the acórdão with the reclamação (2026-11-12).** The user agreed with the recommendations of 2026-11-12. The three decisions that are the owner's (the value of the cause, the forum at the airline's head office, the words of the hearing's reminder) went to the owner in the Claude Doc "ONE · Três decisões sobre as petições e o aviso de audiência" (https://claude.ai/artifact/6ZinRCnfiZ1gsYpJ1Q6chD), each with the law, the risk, the recommendation and the alternative. **The minor of 16 or 17 signs beside who assists them** (migration `0224_assisted_signs`, pgTAP `0224`, 5 assertions with two firms; Código Civil, art. 4º, I; CPC, art. 71): the user chose one document with both signatures on the adult's link. Each paper ONE issues to a person (`advance_record_paper` redefined from 0125) has, after them, each minor of 16 or 17 they assist in the case as a signer of its own (`paper_cosigners`): the power of attorney, and the declaration of hardship when that minor asks free justice; a minor under 16 is represented and signs nothing. By ZapSign each signer has their own address and the adult's link shows the minor's part with its button (`client_link_signatures` redefined from 0023: `assisted`; `client-tasks.tsx`); by gov.br the minor signs the same file with their own account before the adult sends it, and the firm's confirmation names each signature to check (`signatures-card.tsx`). The filing already waits for every power of attorney signed (0054), so it waits for the minor's. **The acórdão and what may follow it** (migration `0225_acordao_paths`, pgTAP `0225`, 8 assertions with two firms; `acordao-v1` of `@flyee/rules/outcome` and `caminhos-v2` of `@flyee/rules/paths`): an acórdão is a fact of its lawsuit (`lawsuit_facts.kind` `acordao`, `appeal_result`), read from its publication by its type or its words (`isAcordao`, `readAcordao`: granted, in part, denied or not heard; two results in one acórdão are a person's to read), never again as a second judgment (`outcome-work.ts` reads an acórdão first, whether or not the triage said it is the judgment); it is said for the client by who appealed (`lawsuit_appellant`, `acordao_for_client`), and one against the client or one the rule cannot tell for whom asks the lawyers' awareness ("acórdão contra o cliente", "acórdão para o advogado ler"). Each acórdão has its options (`judgment_paths.stage`, `judgment_path_options(kind, stage)`): in the small-claims court the embargos, the recurso extraordinário (CF, art. 102, III; Súmula 640 do STF) and the reclamação to the court of justice (Resolução STJ nº 3/2016, art. 1º; in the TJGO its Seções Cíveis, Regimento Interno, art. 16, V; CPC, arts. 988 to 993, before the acórdão becomes final, art. 988, § 5º, I: no term of its own), in place of `caminhos-v1`'s request to settle diverging panels; in the civil court the recurso especial and the extraordinário. ONE proposes from its publication the embargos (5 days), the recurso extraordinário (15) and, in the civil court, the recurso especial (15), where the firm lets it (`one_propose_acordao_deadlines`); a lawyer decides with the second factor (`decide_judgment_path` redefined from 0216, by stage), ONE recommending accepting an acórdão in the client's favour and none otherwise; accepting it takes back what ONE proposed of it; a person records one by hand (`save_lawsuit_fact` redefined from 0130); the client's link keeps asking only about appealing a judgment (`client_link_judgment_paths` redefined from 0185). The case's card "Resultado" shows the latest decision of each lawsuit, its acórdão once there is one (`judgment-path-block.tsx`).
- **The panel of attendance joined to ONE (2026-11-12).** The user asked why the panel did not work, since the kit was put in so the team answers in Chatwoot and not on the firm's phone: ONE's link to the panel had not been built. Before it, the kit was updated in `packages/atendimento` (its base and deploy models of 2026-11-12: the edge Caddy `caddy:2.11.9` with `expected_underscore_headers`, already on the server; the bot's media, which ONE does not run; the theme unchanged). **The adapter** `@flyee/chatwoot` (`packages/chatwoot`: the interface, the HTTP provider with the token as a bearer, a fake that also signs the inbox's deliveries, the reading of a delivery and its `X-Chatwoot-Signature`, and a contract test of 16 cases against the fake and a stand-in in the shapes of Chatwoot 4.19.1, read in its code; the HTTP provider also ran once against the platform's instance in a throwaway account it deleted). **The database** (migration `0223_chatwoot`, pgTAP `0223`, 22 assertions with two firms; `0003` and `0012` follow it): each firm's account (`chatwoot_accounts`: the account, its service person named ONE, the person of the firm's phone "Celular do WhatsApp" and the API inbox, made step by step and claimed so two servers never make two; only Chatwoot's numbers, no token: the platform's comes from the environment and a person's is read through the Platform API when needed), its people (`chatwoot_staff`), and a queue of what to take out of the panel (`chatwoot_deletions`: a firm's account when the firm goes, a conversation or its contact when it leaves ONE, a person when they leave the firm). A conversation is marked when a message comes, goes or changes, when who answers changes or when ONE calls a person (triggers on `wa_messages` and `wa_conversations`), and claimed once (`chatwoot_claim_conversations`). What a person writes in the panel is reserved in the outbox as theirs under the rules of a message written in ONE (`chatwoot_person_writes`: a member, an active number, the 24 hours), once per message, and hands them the conversation (0048); a status set in the panel says who answers (`chatwoot_panel_status`: open or snoozed is a person's, pending or resolved is ONE's, and a resolved one is turned to pending, so the client's next message does not reopen it as a person's). Entering is asked in the person's own session (`chatwoot_panel_entry`), never by a support visit. Every write of the backend goes through the database (`chatwoot_keep_account`, `chatwoot_keep_conversation`, `chatwoot_keep_message`, `chatwoot_keep_person`, `chatwoot_flagged_again`, which keeps the ten minutes, and `chatwoot_deletion_done`), and what binds is on the record there (`whatsapp.message_from_panel`, `whatsapp.message_flagged_sent`, `atendimento.panel_joined`). **The app** (`apps/web/src/lib/chatwoot`): the mirror writes each conversation in order, the client's messages as theirs, ONE's as ONE, the phone's as "Celular do WhatsApp", a person's as them once they are in the panel, each file with its message (up to 15 MB), the status of what the firm sent and a private note when ONE calls a person; it runs right after a delivery, a send, a stored file or a change of who answers, and in the sweep `chatwoot-mirror`, with `chatwoot-upkeep` making the account of every firm with a real number and working the queue. The webhook (`/api/webhooks/chatwoot`) takes only what the inbox signed; a message the bar's rules flag fails in the panel with why and a note, and the same text written again within 10 minutes goes (on the record as `whatsapp.message_flagged_sent`); one past the 24 hours, or with a file, fails with why (a file still goes from ONE's Atendimento); one ONE sends goes right after the answer. The panel closes a reply after 24 hours by itself (the inbox's `agent_reply_time_window`), the account's functions are the kit's choices (`CHATWOOT_FEATURES_OFF`, kept equal to `escolhas.json` by a test), and nothing in it resolves by itself. "Abrir o painel" on `/atendimento` (`/atendimento/painel`) makes the person an agent of the firm's account the first time (a person who administers another account, as the instance's super administrator, is refused; a firm they left is left), with every conversation in view, and enters signed in. Proven by a round trip against the embedded database with the panel's fake (`apps/web/src/lib/chatwoot/panel.db.test.ts`). Configured by `CHATWOOT_URL` and `CHATWOOT_PLATFORM_TOKEN`. **Left:** a file a person attaches in the panel (it fails there with why; ONE's Atendimento sends files); a template outside the 24 hours stays in ONE's Atendimento (the owner's decision of 2026-11-11); the team's groups of the kit (D26) and its bot (ONE's AI is the only one).
- **The registration for oral argument in the TJSP, the TJRJ and the TJMG (2026-11-12).** Migration `0226_oral_argument_rules_more_courts` (pgTAP `0226`, 5 assertions with two firms; `sustentacao-v2` of `@flyee/rules/sessions`, 7 tests): each court's rule read in its own acts that day (the research's sources: Resolução CNJ nº 591/2024; TJSP Resolução OE 984/2025, Regimento Interno art. 146, Normas da Corregedoria art. 714; TJRJ Regimento Interno arts. 97, 98-A and 108; TJMG Aviso Conjunto 160/PR/2025, Portaria Conjunta 1.521/PR/2024, Código de Normas dos Juizados art. 176). Two limits join the TJGO's: some hours before the session or before its day (`hours_before`: the CNJ's 48 hours of a virtual session, the 24 of a presencial session of the TJSP and of a hybrid one of the TJMG, the 48 before the day of a videoconference in the TJRJ) and some days counted as a term from the publication of the pauta (`days_after_publication`: 5 business days in the TJMG's turmas recursais), the pauta's day of availability given to the count (`sessions_to_count` redefined from 0219); where a court allows a later registration in person, the basis says it and the limit is the earlier one. Seeded nine rules: the TJSP's chambers and Colégio Recursal, the TJRJ's chambers, the TJMG's chambers and its turmas recursais in a virtual session. **Not seeded:** the TJRJ's turmas recursais (their regimento, Res. CM 04/2022, and the Ato Normativo COJES 01/2023 sit behind a check of the court's library that was not bypassed; the editais speak of 3 days from the pauta) and a presencial session of the TJMG's turmas recursais (no written term): ONE says it has no rule there. A session counted before with no rule is counted again. The CNJ's PCA 0003075-71.2023.2.00.0000 (an injunction of 13/02/2026 to the TJSP, extended to the TJRJ in March) prefers the live argument once a timely request is made: the limits stay the same, and the Plenary is still to judge it.
- **The controladoria's board after a plans page, and its cards in line (2026-11-12).** The owner sent a template's plans page as the model for the board's columns, asked the steps told apart by colour and the content organized even when empty, and found the cards of the page out of line. Each column of the board is now a card in the plans' shape: the icon of its step on the colour of its part of the way, its name, the number of cases, the median and the oldest side by side, the stagnant ones, "Ver na lista", then its cases and "Mostrar mais". The columns share their rows (a CSS subgrid), so their parts line up whatever each holds, and a column with no case says "Nenhum caso" where its numbers and button would be. The parts of the way are named over their columns, each in one colour (Contratação, Ajuizamento, Em juízo, Desfecho), the colour the page's numbers already give the same thing; a colour for each of the nine steps was not possible with five hues that are not red. The eight steps fit across 1920 pixels; narrower, the board scrolls sideways, with arrows beside its title while there is more to a side. Under it, "Com quem está" (a `CountList`, each holder with its icon) and "Tribunal" (a card with its own head, what moved on the left and what is quiet or never read on the right, `CountRows` `split` of `count-list.tsx`) share one height, where the court had been a heading over seven cards, the last alone in its row; the goals follow, beside each module's stock where there is more than one. The court's moves take the court's blue. The loading skeleton has the new shape (`PageSkeleton` `board`). Seen on a page of invented data at 1920, 1440 and 390 pixels and in the dark (the owner's screen, a busy firm, nothing going), the page then removed. No migration.
- **The board's column that clipped itself (2026-11-12).** The owner found the column of Sentença cut on the right (its days, its button and its case) on a firm whose one case has a long client's name. Each column is a grid of one column for its subgrid's rows, and that column was `auto`: it grew to the name, which did not wrap, wider than the card, which clipped the rest. The column is now `minmax(0, 1fr)`, the case's card may shrink (`min-w-0`) and the client's name wraps over two lines instead of ending in an ellipsis. Seen on a page of invented data at 1792, 1440 and 390 pixels, nothing in a column wider than its card, the page then removed. No migration.
- **The panel heals what it lost (2026-11-12).** On the development project, test conversations taken out of ONE queued their contact to be taken out of the panel while the client's new conversation had already found that same contact there by the number; the sweep then took the contact out with its conversations, and every round of the new conversation met a conversation the panel no longer had, so the panel stayed empty while `/atendimento` had the conversation. Migration `0229_chatwoot_recover` (pgTAP `0229`, 6 assertions with two firms; pgTAP `0223` counts the conversation queued too): a conversation taken out of ONE always takes its own conversation out of the panel and queues its contact only when no other conversation of the firm has it, and the sweep asks again before taking a contact out (`chatwoot_contact_in_use`); a conversation the panel answers 404 for is forgotten (`chatwoot_forget_conversation`: its ids in the panel and its messages' cleared) and written there again in the same round (`lib/chatwoot/mirror.ts`), proven in the round trip with the panel's fake. Applied to the development project, where the lost conversation was forgotten to be written again.
- **The panel, the one place of the conversations (2026-11-12).** The user asked why there were two places to talk with a client and agreed that the panel be the only one: the menu's "Atendimento" opens the panel signed in, in a tab of its own; `/atendimento` keeps its numbers and its list (from the home's tiles) and, per conversation, only what the panel does not send yet (an approved template after the 24 hours, a file), with "Abrir no painel" in place of the messages. The panel's list opens on "Todos" rather than "Minhas" (asked by the user: ONE's conversations are the bot's and seldom have somebody assigned), by a script ONE adds after the kit's theme (`packages/atendimento/implantacao/painel-acrescimo.html`), which clicks the third tab once each time the list appears and changes nothing else; deployed to the server and seen in a browser signed in by a one-time link. No migration. While ONE answers, the panel's conversation is assigned to the account's robot ONE, made with no address (Chatwoot posts it nothing), so the panel offers "Assumir atendimento" as it does for the kit's bot; taking it assigns the person and opens it, which ONE reads as the takeover (asked by the user, who saw it in the Atrupe's panel).

- **A client's PDF through OpenWA (2026-11-13).** A client sent her boarding pass as a PDF four times and each was refused as a type not allowed, though the purpose `whatsapp` lists `application/pdf`: OpenWA answers the download of a document as `application/octet-stream` (a photo as `image/jpeg`), and the backend took the vendor's type over the message's own. The type a media is reserved under is now what its bytes are, then a vendor's type that names a format, then the message's (`lib/whatsapp/media-type.ts`, with its test); the confirmation still checks the bytes against the purpose. ONE's message of a turn's files says a line once however many files it stands for (the two PDFs had been told twice in one message). The menu now opens `/atendimento` (every member of the firm already reads every conversation there: `wa_conversations` and `firm_inbox` read by membership, never by who answers); "Abrir o painel" in its head enters the panel on the firm's account, and a conversation's "Abrir esta conversa no painel" enters it on that conversation (`/atendimento/painel?c=`, `openPanel` adding `sso_account_id` and `sso_conversation_id` to the link that enters signed in, read by Chatwoot 4.19.1's `getLoginRedirectURL`, checked in its code; a conversation the person cannot read, or one not in the panel yet, opens its list). - **No menu of the modules (2026-11-14).** The owner found the menu "Módulos" a repetition: the access is almost always through Casos. Checked: everything a module's area held was elsewhere (each page of the work under the module with the choice on top, "Andamento dos casos" and ONE's line on the home, the import in Casos, the partners and the module's settings in the settings); only the overview of the modules, contracted, to come and to add, was its own. Built: the item and its submenu left the menu; the overview went to Configurações › Escritório, "Módulos", beside "Plano e pagamentos" (`/settings/modulos`); `/modulos` and `/modulos/<key>` lead there and to `/casos?modulo=<key>` (`next.config.mjs`); the module's area and its texts went; the crumbs of a page under a module end on the module; the home's tiles of the modules became one discreet line under the greeting (each contracted module to its cases, "Em breve", "Ver módulos"). The owner's decision of 2026-10-30 in CLAUDE.md says so. The menu's tests moved to the operator's group. No migration.
- **D4Sign as the firm's signature account, with its sandbox for the pilot (2026-11-17).** The owner chose D4Sign for the clients' signatures ("vamos usar esse sistema para assinatura, se possível já implemente o sandbox para operar nessa fase com o piloto"). Migration `0253_d4sign` (pgTAP `0253`, 10 assertions with two firms; the signature tests 0026, 0123, 0218, 0224 and 0231 pass with it): `signature_envelopes.method` takes `d4sign`; `firm_signature_account` (which account the firm has and in which environment, for anybody of it); and, redefined from their latest bodies with only the method changed, `request_signature`, `advance_record_paper` (0224), `record_appeal_paper` (0218), `cancel_signature`, `end_free_justice_paper` (0125), `end_residence_declaration` (0124), `confirm_signature`, `claim_signature_envelopes`, `signature_envelopes_to_poll`, `firm_has_signature_account` and `signature_links_owed` (0231). `@flyee/signatures/providers/d4sign` (the safes, the upload of a base64 PDF to the firm's safe, a webhook per document, the signers by e-mail, the send with no e-mail of D4Sign's, each signer's own address, the phase and the signatures, the signed PDF and the cancel; a document left half made is cancelled there), its stand-in in the contract (32 tests with the fake and ZapSign's); the base classes `AccountSignatureProvider`, `SignatureAuthError` and `SignatureUnavailableError` (ZapSign's errors are kinds of them). The app: `connectD4SignAccount` (the keys checked by reading the account's safes, one safe taken as it is and several offered, one account at a time) and `disconnectSignatureAccount` for either; the worker and the sweeps for either account; `/api/webhooks/d4sign`; the connection card with the choice of D4Sign or ZapSign; the case's card, the client's page, the chat's links and the dossier for either; the signature texts in terms of the firm's signature account. Read in D4Sign's own pages (its API reference, its help center, its Postman collection and its PHP SDK), never run against D4Sign: the pilot's sandbox account is where it gets checked. Known limits, for the owner to weigh: D4Sign allows 10 calls an hour by default and a document costs four and one per signer, so production needs a higher limit, asked of D4Sign's sales; D4Sign sends each signer a token by e-mail at the moment of signing, which cannot be turned off, so every client signing by D4Sign needs an e-mail on record (a WhatsApp or SMS token is a paid add-on); D4Sign takes no minor as a signer of their own, so a paper a minor of 16 or 17 signs beside who assists them (0224) is to be checked in the sandbox; D4Sign's HMAC is not used (a third key, and its pages disagree on what it covers): the webhook is taken by a secret in its own address and the state is read again; D4Sign keeps documents on AWS in Oregon and São Paulo, which the LGPD analysis of the firm's processor should name.
- **What D4Sign asks of ONE, before the pilot's sandbox (2026-11-17, migration 0254).** An independent review of 0253 found that D4Sign's limit of 10 calls an hour per account (its introduction: "Você terá um limite de 10 requisições por hora", raised by its sales) would fail most envelopes for good, and that ONE's own papers would fail for every client with no e-mail on record. Migration `0254_d4sign_limits` (pgTAP `0254`, 12 assertions with two firms; 0253's test now gives its client an e-mail and waits a day; the signature tests 0026, 0123, 0124, 0125, 0218, 0224 and 0231 pass with it): `signature_envelopes.retry_after` and `record_signature_retry` (an envelope D4Sign would not open for its limit goes back to the queue for an hour, and fails with its code once tried `signatures.retry_attempts` times, 24 by default), honoured by `claim_signature_envelopes`; `signature_envelopes_to_poll` asks a D4Sign envelope once a day; `signature_emails_problem`, by which `request_signature` refuses a paper by D4Sign to people with no e-mail of their own or sharing one, and `advance_record_paper` and `record_appeal_paper` send ONE's own papers by gov.br instead, the envelope saying why (`d4sign_email_missing`). The provider: the limit and the keys' end read from a status (429, 401) or from D4Sign's words only in a refusal or in a success that is only a message; a 403 is `provider_forbidden`, not the keys' end; a safe D4Sign does not know is `safe_not_found`; two signers on one e-mail are refused before anything leaves (`signer_email_shared`); the signed PDF comes from the state just read (`downloadSigned(id, known)`), one call fewer; the limit met at a webhook's registration ends the opening (contract: 37 tests). The worker: the account's other envelopes of a round wait with the first one the limit stopped, without a call; e-mails that could not be read are a short wait, not a failure; the poll and the cancels stop asking an account whose limit is reached. The app: `requestSignature` opens a D4Sign envelope right after the answer (`soon`); `disconnectSignatureAccount` refuses a connected account with papers still signing by it (`open_papers`); a D4Sign account connected again keeps its documents' webhook secret; the connections card shows the connected account first, offers a disconnect for one in error and says a refusal in the words of the provider being connected, with the safes as the account has them now; the case's card says a paper waiting for D4Sign's hour, the paper ONE sent by gov.br for want of an e-mail, and D4Sign's new refusals. Left to the sandbox: the order `/list` gives the signers (who signed when is read by the order of their `key_signer`), what D4Sign answers once its limit is reached, and a document left as a draft when its cancel met the limit too.
- **The robot runs in the lawyer's own browser: the extension's version 0.1 maps the screens (2026-11-18).** Asked whether the lawyer's consent lets ONE use their court login, the answer was no (the TJGO's password and key are "de uso pessoal e intransferível", Resolução 59/2016, art. 11; the Projudi asks a code by e-mail at the login, the eproc of TJRJ an authenticator app's), and the owner chose the robot in the lawyer's own browser, for TJGO and also for RJ. `apps/web/extension/` (Chrome, Manifest V3, bundled by its `build.mjs` like the clock; its README): version 0.1 maps the screens of the Projudi of TJGO and the eproc of TJRJ while a lawyer files by hand, the structure of each frame (fields, labels, a select's own list, buttons, step links, a table's column names and size, headings, the address's screen parameters) and the path (clicks and changes, a text field only as filled), saved as a file the lawyer sends; nothing of a value, a hidden field, a list of people, a table's rows, a token of the address, an e-mail or a long number is kept (`src/lib/court-extension/capture.ts`, 8 tests in jsdom, one of them holding the manifest's hosts to the systems it knows). `smoke.mjs` loads the built extension in Playwright's Chromium against a stand-in of the eproc's first step served in place of the court's host, with a frame, and passed. What TJRJ's own manual says of its eproc's five steps of a new lawsuit, and that its small-claims courts move to eproc by groups (Aviso Conjunto TJ/CGJ 38/2026: 2026-10-29 and 2027-01-22, the rest on the legacy DCP), is in the extension's README; TJRJ's act on eproc's use (Aviso Normativo Conjunto TJ/CGJ 19/2026) sits behind a check of its site and is read before the TJRJ filing is turned on. Next, once the maps exist: the filing (version 0.2), linked to the lawyer's ONE account, claimed in `filing_submissions` before the first field is typed.
- **The platform's AI account, watched, and the questions of a model read whole (2026-11-20, migration 0255).** The owner's power of attorney, sent in the setup's step of the papers, came back "o ONE tentou algumas vezes e não conseguiu": every run since 2026-11-06 had failed with no token spent (`provider_rejected`), Anthropic answering `401 invalid x-api-key` to the key in production, in `.env.local` and on the clock's server. The owner made a new key, set in Vercel and `.env.local`; it was written into the clock's `relogio.env` on the VPS through stdin and the clock recreated, and the first runs succeeded the same afternoon. What let it pass unseen for two weeks was built: a refusal of the platform's account is its own code (`provider_account`), marks the provider down in the database (`ai_provider_health`, a trigger on `ai_runs`) and up at its first success; while it is down the sweeps whose work is a model's wait (`Sweep.ai`, `soonAi`), the clock asks the provider once a beat whether the account is back (`ai-health`), the operators are told once each way (the bell and an e-mail) and `/admin/ai` says it at its top; a firm's screen that waits for the AI says the platform's AI is down (`platform_ai_status`), never the provider. pgTAP `0255` (18 assertions with two firms). The first question ONE asked of the owner's power of attorney (210 characters) was the label of its answer field and showed cut to one line: each question is now written whole above a field of three lines, why ONE asks it under it. Known limits: the first run that reveals a refused account still spends the try of its work (the documents', the statements' and the drafts' are given back only by the pause that follows); a provider down pauses the work of every provider; the drafts' sweep pauses the PDF of a piece approved while the AI is out until it is back. A white box seen over the top left of the setup's panel in the owner's screenshot was not found in the panel drawn alone, and is open.

- **The firm's areas and the way of a new contact on the WhatsApp: phase 1 of the plan "o escritório preparado antes do primeiro atendimento" (2026-11-20, migrations 0256 and 0257).** The owner approved the plan (the Claude Doc of 2026-11-20) with its seven decisions as recommended: a lawsuit of any area becomes a case of an area of the firm, Previdenciário and Trabalhista staying "Em breve" as modules; the channel is the setup's last step and ONE answers nobody new before it knows the firm, the pilot going on; an area the firm does not work in is refused with the firm's words, never pointing to another firm; personality and areas in every plan, the number of areas a limit of the plan; ONE may read the firm's site once, given its address; a tool the firm does not use leaves the menu for the settings; a module launched for an area the firm built is offered, never switched by itself. Built: the areas of law ONE knows (`law_areas`, twelve, as data) and the areas of each firm (`firm_areas`; a module brings its area, now and whenever a firm is given one); how the firm works (`firm_operations`, `organizations.operation_answered_at`), said in the setup's new step "A operação" with ONE's proposal of the areas from the firm's lawsuits (`@flyee/rules/areas`, `proposeFirmAreas`) and saved whole by `save_firm_operation` (the second factor, the plan's `areas_max`, on the record); each case's area (`cases.area_id`, a module's case given its module's area, every case there is included; `cases.case_type` for the areas to come); `firm_setup_facts` with `operation` and `areas`; and the six ways of a new contact (`atendimento.assunto`, `routeOf`, `takeNewContact`, `record_conversation_route`, `one_set_aside`, the firm's words `greeting`, `ask_summary`, `not_attended`). pgTAP `0256` (30 assertions with two firms) and `0257` (16); `@flyee/rules/areas` and `routing.test.ts` unit tests; the intake's round trip walks each way. Not yet applied to the development project. Known limits: an area of the firm ONE does not know has no script yet (phase 4): its contacts go to a person with what they need; the general conversation (`atendimento.conversa`) still assumes a passenger for a client with a case; the operation's answers on the tools wait for the lean panel (phase 5, a canvas first); the plans carry no `areas_max` until the owner sets one.

- **The firm's data read from the documents it sends (2026-11-20, migration 0271).** The owner sent the firm's power of attorney and fee contract in the setup and was still asked by hand for the address, the e-mail and the society's registration both papers wrote, while the CNPJ stayed empty and ONE asked whether the society, its CNPJ, its second lawyer and its address were the firm's. The fifth marking says what a firm's document writes of the firm (`dados_do_escritorio`) and asks nothing of it when the document names the firm's society or a lawyer of its record; the worker checks it against the document's words (`firmPaperOf`) and `fill_firm_office_from_paper` writes only what the record lacks (the office, the CNPJ and the telephone where the billing profile has none, the papers' lawyers in `office_lawyers`), marked in `office_read` until a person confirms it in "Dados do escritório", which says what ONE read and asks only what is missing. `template_imports.firm_read` hands the save its paragraphs of the firm's data; `set_firm_office` redefined from 0217, `template_import_to_work` from 0093; pgTAP `0271` (12 assertions with two firms). Also: a request that failed or was refused is said no more once the firm saved a model of its paper after it (the setup showed the outage's failure of 16:15 over the power of attorney saved at 19:15), and a failure says its file and that it was ONE's.

- **The firm's logo, colour and letterhead, on what it issues: phase 2 of the plan, first part (2026-11-20, migrations 0258 to 0260 and 0266).** The setup gained the step "O escritório", after the OAB, which starts from the documents the firm already uses (the owner, the same day: the firm's data read from them, the form only for what is missing): its letterhead and its power of attorney (the same panel as the step of the papers), then its logo and colour, then where it is, typed by hand only as the exception ("Preencher à mão"). The logo (PNG, JPEG, WebP or SVG; `detectMime` tells an SVG by its first element) is kept as a PNG that fits 800 by 400, its main colour read by a rule (`marca-v1`, `@flyee/documents/brand`), the person keeping it or choosing another; a firm with a Word letterhead and no logo has the largest picture of its header taken as one. The letterhead is a Word document written on it (its page, margins, header and footer with their pictures, compressed, and its font: `lib/brand/letterhead-docx.ts`) or a PDF of a blank page of the paper, measured and refused when it holds a piece's lines (`@flyee/documents/letterhead`); a page of example is drawn and the person confirms it before ONE uses it. Once confirmed, the pieces and the papers a client signs are drawn on it, and the receipts and statements to clients too, with ONE's verification mark lifted above its footer (`pageLayout`, `stampVerification`'s `at`; `lib/brand/letterhead-read.ts`). `firm_brands`, files of the purpose `brand`, `set_firm_logo`, `set_firm_color`, `set_firm_letterhead`, `confirm_firm_letterhead`, `remove_firm_letterhead` (the second factor, on the record), `firm_setup_facts.brand`; pgTAP `0258` (17) and `0260` (14); 0266 has `discard_file` (from 0265's body) refuse every file of the brand and each picture of the letterhead. 0259: a firm whose only number was the test provider's goes on being attended (decision 2 keeps the silence for a new firm alone). Fixed with it: phase 1 had left `0003_two_firms_isolation`, `0009_account_deletion` and `0024_firm_today` failing (the new tables, `cases.area_id` and the new facts). Built and tested, not used yet: a piece written into the firm's own Word document (`writeDocxOnLetterhead`) for the downloads in Word, and the firm's data read from the letterhead's lines (`officeOfLetterhead`), which joins one-2d's `fill_firm_office_from_paper` (0271) once that is in. Still to come in the phase: the client's pages in the firm's brand and the WhatsApp profile.

- **The client's page in the firm's brand, and the firm's texts in Word on its letterhead: phase 2, second part (2026-11-20, migration 0261).** The client's link shows the firm's logo above its name and its colour on the bar of the case's step of now, where it reads on white (`client_link_brand`, `readClientBrand`); pgTAP `0261` (6 assertions with two firms) and the function in `0012`'s list of what a link's holder calls. "Baixar em Word" of a model of the library and of the setup's papers writes the text into the firm's own Word document on its letterhead, once confirmed (`writeDocxOnLetterhead`, `textBlocks` of `lib/library/docx.ts`). Still to come in the phase: the WhatsApp profile in the firm's brand.

- **The firm's WhatsApp profile in its data: phase 2, third part (2026-11-20, migration 0262).** ONE writes the business profile of each active number from its firm's data (when it attends, what it attends, where it is, its e-mail) and keeps it in step by a sweep, never writing the same one twice (`whatsapp-profile`, `businessProfileOf`, `wa_numbers_profile_due`, `record_number_profile`). The adapter gained `updateProfile`: the Cloud API's `whatsapp_business_profile` from its reference (none of it ran against Meta yet), the simulator keeping it, the platform's OpenWA saying it sets none; the contract test covers it. pgTAP `0262` (7 assertions with two firms). Known limits: the profile's photo waits for the platform's app at Meta (its upload); a firm on OpenWA keeps the profile of its phone's WhatsApp.

- **The firm's personality, written by ONE and approved by the firm: phase 3 of the plan (2026-11-20, migration 0272).** The setup's step "Personalidade", before the channel: ONE writes the firm's profile by itself from what the setup collected (its name and place, its areas, its hours, its words on fees and doubts, and its site once, where the person gives the address), and the person approves it on three example messages or says in their own words what to change. Each version is kept (`firm_personalities`), ONE's writing is claimed and answered by the backend (`firm_personality_requests`, `request_firm_personality`, `claim_personality_requests`, `personality_request_input`, `record_personality_draft`, `fail_personality_request`, the sweep `firm-personality`), and the database refuses a profile that breaks the rules for everybody (`personality_problem`: a dash, an exclamation mark, an emoji, a promise), which the app also keeps on the model's answer (`keepRules`). ONE answers somebody new only once the operation is said and the profile approved (decision 2; `firm_attendance` redefined); the conversation and the doubt follow it as data, in new versions of their instructions in a new version of the passenger module, and each answer records the profile it followed (`firm_personality_uses`). `firm_setup_facts` gains `personality` and the filing session's `filing_way`. The site is read by `readSiteText` (only http and https, never a private network, at each redirect too, a few seconds and a few hundred kilobytes). pgTAP `0272` (20 assertions with two firms), `0003`, `0024` and `0256` brought up to it; the round trip `lib/firm/personality.db.test.ts`. Known limits: the fixed texts of the intake, the routing and the notices keep "você" whatever the profile's treatment; the site's name is looked up once more by the fetch after it was checked.
- **The model ONE makes from a document, seen working (2026-11-21).** The owner sent the firm's contract on the setup's step of the papers and could not tell whether anything was loading, and the screen looked broken. Found: on `/inicio`, where the firm's setup is drawn while unanswered, the header's search was still there (it was left out by the address `/configurar` only); the browser filled it with the owner's e-mail, and its list of "Nada encontrado." stood over the side panel, covering the start of its title. The panel showed a still list of steps under a sentence, an "i" for the steps to come, "Pode fechar: o modelo espera em Esperam você, na biblioteca" and a disabled "Escrever à mão", and it stopped reading after ten minutes. Built: the search and "Adicionar" are left out while a setup is on screen anywhere (`useMarkSetupShown`), and the search is no login to the browser (`type="search"`, `autocomplete="off"`, its list opened only with the person in it, a change with nobody in the field refused; `global-search.test.tsx`); the panel draws `AtWorkBody`, the body of `AtWorkNotice` in place: the turning ring, four steps with the sending first, "Enviado às", "Desistir", what closing does and where the model waits (the library or the setup's step), "Fechar" at its foot, "Escrever à mão" hidden while ONE works, and reading on every 20 s after ten minutes; the step's request is one `StateLine` whose mark turns while ONE works, the chip that repeated it gone. No migration. Checked on fixtures at 1440 and 390 (sending, reading, marking, the AI down, the first look). The lesson went into the `product-screen` skill and `app-ux.md`.

- **The script of each area of the firm, and the case ONE opens by it: phase 4 of the plan (2026-11-20, migrations 0273 and 0274).** An area the firm works in that no module serves gets a script of its own, versioned as a module is (0212) and the firm's alone: the kinds of case, the triage's questions with the firm's criterion on each option ("segue", "não atendemos", "uma pessoa decide"), the documents of each kind, the stages over the common way of a case, the work outside the lawsuit (the client's or the firm's), the pieces, the words on fees, doubts and a refusal, the courts and the sensitive data with how long they are kept. ONE writes it in the setup's step "Áreas" (`firm_area_scripts`, `firm_area_script_requests`, the task `escritorio.roteiro`, the sweep `area-script`) from the area, the firm's lawsuits of it, its profile and the documents it sends; the person corrects it, reads two conversations of example written with the texts ONE sends, and publishes it; the database refuses a script out of shape or against the rules for everybody (`area_script_problem`). On the WhatsApp, a subject of the area goes to its triage: the AI only reads a reply into an option (`atendimento.triagem`), the firm's table decides (`@flyee/rules/area-triage`, rule 1), and "Quero seguir" opens the case (`one_open_area_case`): of no module, of the area, its kind and the script's version, its documents by name on the client's link and in the chat, its work outside the lawsuit (`case_tasks`), with no trip and no lawsuit created by ONE. pgTAP `0273` (22 assertions) and `0274` (18), both with two firms; `0003`, `0024` and `0055` brought up to them (0055 had expected three versions of the conversation's instructions since 0272 published a fourth); the round trips `lib/areas/script.db.test.ts` and the area's triage in `lib/conversations/intake.db.test.ts`. Known limits: the acceptance with a real area of the pilot on the rehearsal firm waits for the owner; the setup is the only place to change an area's script (`/configurar?passo=areas`); a client's "Não tenho" to a dispensable document answers every dispensable of its kind (`case_document_answers` has no need yet); the client's work is marked by the firm, not on the client's link; a stage is shown, never tracked; the lawsuit, the piece and the filing of a case of an area are a person's, and the filing robot's subject still reads `incident` (one-5f takes it from the kind when area cases reach a package); a sensitive datum's time to keep is said in the script and not yet applied by the retention jobs.

- **The labour and the federal courts, and their deadlines: phase 6 of the plan (2026-11-21, migration 0275, rule `prazo-v3`).** The table of the courts held the state courts alone, so a lawsuit of a labour or a federal court had no court, no calendar and no system. It now holds the 24 TRT (segment 5 of the unique number) and the 6 TRF (segment 4), each with the states it covers and its time zones, of no single state (`uf` null; what reads a state's court still finds the TJ), and the system of each as the DJEN's communications of 18 November 2026 showed it (`scripts/djen-systems.mjs`: every TRT in PJe; TRF1, TRF3 and TRF5 in PJe; TRF2, TRF4 and TRF6 in eproc), unconfirmed until checked against each court's acts. The count of a deadline knows the justice of its court: the holidays of the Justiça Federal are the law's in a federal court (Lei nº 5.010/1966, art. 62); the labour courts count as the CLT says (art. 775, business days; art. 775-A, 20 December to 20 January), their days off being each court's calendar; the federal small-claims courts count as Lei nº 9.099/1995 does (Lei nº 10.259/2001, art. 1º) and no public entity has a term of its own there (art. 9º). pgTAP `0275` (reference data, 8 assertions) and `0025` brought up to it; the rule's tests (Holy Week and Carnival of 2026 and 2027, a term across Holy Week in a federal, a labour and a state court). Known limits: no TRT's or TRF's calendar is on record yet (the count says so, `calendar_unchecked`, and gives the earlier day); the systems of the regional courts are a sample's, not the courts' acts; the TST and the STJ are not in the table.

- **Three ways to file on a court's screen, asked in the onboarding (2026-11-20 and 21, migrations 0263 to 0265).** The owner asked for three ways, the lawyer's choice, accepting the risk in TJGO, TJRJ and TJSP: ONE's own browser with the lawyer's court login, the lawyer approving each batch and typing the court's code through ONE when asked; the extension in the lawyer's own session, ONE filling and sending the batch they confirm; and by hand, with ONE's package and guide. And everything a firm and a lawyer must say or acknowledge for them is asked in the setups, never left to the settings ("contraintuitivo e burocrático"), on a canvas the owner approved (https://claude.ai/artifact/7AP7Gng3dfohaLrseida4y); ONE learns a system's screens at its first filing there, never from a person's map. Built: `0263_filing_ways` (the screen systems as data, eproc TJSP and Projudi TJGO with both automated ways, eproc TJRJ none until its Aviso Normativo Conjunto 19/2026 is read; the firm's way, the most any lawyer of it may choose, and each lawyer's way per system, an automated one only with the acknowledgment of the text in force, 'protocolo-escritorio' and 'protocolo-advogado'; the court login as the lawyer's own connection, `Vault.sealLawyerCourtLogin`, ended by the lawyer, a partner, who manages the firm, the lawyer leaving, the firm lowering its way or the court refusing it; the extension's link by a code of seven letters), `0264_filing_batches` (a batch a lawyer approves at one system with the second factor; the court's code relayed sealed, two minutes, read once; the login opened only for an approved batch, `court_login_open`, each use on record; ONE's first filing at a system stops before the first button that may send for the lawyer to confirm, the database marking it as going before that press; the system's map, trusted once a filing that followed it was confirmed and taken; the AI task `protocolo.tela`, which reads a screen's structure and the facts by key, never a person's values) and `0265_filing_screen_file_kept`; `lib/court-robot/` (the runner shared by the extension and ONE's browser, with its stand-in test); the card "Protocolo nos tribunais" of `/settings/atuacao`; the block "O ONE protocola" of `/protocolo` (the batches in course with whose turn it is, the court's code, the confirmation of a learning filing, the ready packages approved in one dialog); and, on 21 November, the setups: "Como o escritório protocola" right after the bar in the firm's (the courts of its lawyers, the firm's way with its acknowledgment, and who answers and signs gives their own way, login and acknowledgment in the same step), "Como você protocola" after the registration in each lawyer's own, the firm's way marked, and a line in the home's list until the firm chose (`firm_setup_facts.filing_way`, which 0272 and 0273 carry). The fields are one component for the three places (`components/firm/filing-way-form.tsx`). A defect of the block on `/protocolo` was fixed with it: a lawyer who never chose a way was offered the firm's, and the database refused the batch; the packages now go only by the lawyer's own choice, and one line tells them what ONE could file once they choose. Known limits: no screen of a court was filed yet; a batch of ONE's browser waits for the worker on ONE's server, and one of the extension for its version 0.2, the next slices; checking a login with the court as it is given waits on the same worker, so a login is checked at the first batch; the screens were judged on their rules and tests, not yet walked in a browser.

- **The extension files a batch in the lawyer's own session: version 0.2 (2026-11-21, migration 0267).** The second of the owner's three ways to file: the lawyer who keeps their court login to themself files through ONE's extension, on the session they opened with their own login and second factor. Built: the extension's panel beside the court's screen (`apps/web/extension/`; the toolbar's button opens it, and version 0.1's mapping stays in it as a diagnosis). It links to the lawyer's account once with the code of seven letters ONE shows them, now also in the setups' step (`ExtensionLinkBox`), lists the batches they approved in ONE for it, and files one on the court's tab where they are logged in: ONE reads each screen as structure, follows the system's map or asks its AI through ONE (`protocolo.tela`), types the facts' values itself, attaches the package's files, and, while it learns the system, stops before the first button that may send for the lawyer's word in the panel, in ONE, or by their own press on the court's screen, which marks the filing as going and leaves nothing pressed for them (the runner's `moved`). What only the lawyer can do (log in, the court's code, a term, a question no fact answers) pauses the batch until they say go on. The number and the court's screen of the receipt come back to the case, the receipt as a PDF of the firm. ONE's side is `/api/extensao/v1/...` (`lib/filing-ways/extension-api.ts`): the link by its token's hash, each batch and filing found as that lawyer's of that firm, a screen checked before the AI reads it and a path before it becomes the platform's map (`extension-protocol.ts`); a path learned in a lawyer's browser is kept as one ONE still learns, never trusted by it, since every firm would follow a trusted map with no AI and no lawyer's word. Defects fixed on the way: the AI was told ONE still learned a system after the lawyer had confirmed the filing, so the court's summary would have waited with nothing to press and the filing ended unknown; a test case could be approved for a real court's screen (0267: only the test court files it); the sweep of the court's channel could take a batch's filing and then break, for every firm, on the check that a batch's filing is on the screen (0267); sending a batch's filing again left its package owed under an ended batch, going nowhere (0267: it is freed for a new batch); the case named a batch's filing the test court's, and one held for the lawyer's word "not released by the court" (`FilingByOne`, `caseNow`). Tests: the runner's, the actions on a page in jsdom, a batch's run with stand-ins of ONE, the tab and the panel, the backend's checks and calls, pgTAP `0267` (7 assertions with two firms), and `node apps/web/extension/smoke.mjs`, the built extension in Chromium against stand-ins of ONE and of four screens of the eproc of TJSP (linked by code, the value typed, nothing posted before the lawyer's word, the petition posted after it, the number and the receipt back, the path with no value). Known limits: no real court's screen was filed yet (the first is the eproc of TJSP with the pilot's lawyer); a batch whose panel was closed in the middle is only stopped and approved again; the receipt is the court's screen as read, the court's own receipt staying in its system; a court's dialog waits for the lawyer to answer it; the extension is installed by hand from a zip until it is in the Chrome Web Store; ONE's browser on the server, which also checks a login as it is given and makes a map trusted, is the next slice.

- **ONE's own browser files on the court's screen with the lawyer's login (2026-11-23, migration 0268).** The first of the owner's three ways to file, for a lawyer who gives ONE their court login: a service beside the platform's clock on ONE's server (`apps/web/clock/navegador.ts`, Playwright's Chromium with no window, in Playwright's image, shipped by the production deploy with the clock; `lib/court-browser/`). In turns it checks a login a lawyer just gave and files a batch a lawyer approved for it. A check opens the login through `claim_court_login_check` (0268: a login for ONE's browser at a system whose second factor is an app's code, never checked, at most five tries an hour apart, each opening kept in `court_login_uses` with the purpose 'check'), signs in and stops where the court asks its code: the court took the login (`court_login_checked`), or refused it and ONE erased it. A code by e-mail would reach the lawyer for nothing, so the Projudi of TJGO's logins are checked at the first batch. A batch opens the login through `court_login_open`, signs in on a page of the court's own domain over HTTPS and nowhere else (the eproc of TJSP signs in at the court's single sign-on, `sso.tjsp.jus.br`, a Keycloak; where each system opens is data, `court_screen_systems.start_path`), asks the lawyer the court's code through `/protocolo` when the court asks it (three tries), and files the batch with the extension's own run (`runBatch`, `runFiling`): the same page script reads and acts on the screen, the backend's half is the same code (`lib/filing-ways/screen-filing.ts`, taken out of the extension's calls), the screen held for the lawyer's word before the first filing at a system is kept as a picture they see in ONE (purpose `filing_screen`), the court's "confirma?" is answered yes only on a press that may send, the receipt is the court's screen printed as a PDF, and a map followed to a filing its lawyer confirmed and the court took becomes trusted (`trust_court_screen_map`), which only ONE's own browser does. A captcha, a question no fact answers or an error of the court stops the batch with why, said on `/protocolo` for a day (`stopReason`). The browser does not disguise itself. Fixed on the way: a login given again kept the court's check of the one before (0268 redefines `begin_court_login`). Checked: vitest of the sign-in reading in jsdom (a form that signs or sends is never a sign-in, the button of the code's boxes beside them), the runner's ended session and presses that may send, a batch's run with nobody at the screen, the stops' words; pgTAP `0268` (9 assertions with two firms) with 0003, 0004, 0009, 0012, 0018, 0029, 0032, 0091, 0109, 0188, 0244, 0250, 0263, 0264 and 0267; `node apps/web/clock/navegador-smoke.mjs`, ONE's browser in Chromium against stand-ins of the TJSP's single sign-on and of the eproc (two checks, the sign-in with the first code refused by the court, the screens, the picture before "Finalizar", the "confirma?" answered yes on the send, the receipt printed; nothing that left for another host, the login, the password and the codes never read by the AI nor logged; a stand-in court never answers with an HTTP redirect, which the browser follows outside the routes); the bundle loaded on the server in Playwright's image with the server's keys (a dry run in a folder of its own). 0268 is on the dev database and the new `receber.sh` is installed on the server (the one before kept beside it). Known limits: no real court's screen was filed yet; the service goes up with the next production deploy; a lawyer who leaves `/protocolo` while the court asks its code stops the batch after five minutes; the court's own receipt stays in its system, the receipt kept being its screen printed.
- **A test case that waited for its signature, said as ONE taking long (2026-11-23).** The owner, on a test case of the pilot's firm: the screen held "O ONE está demorando mais que o normal" for eight minutes over "O ONE pergunta ao cliente no WhatsApp o que aconteceu na viagem, depois que ele assinar", and the client had no link to sign. Three defects, found on the dev database. (1) The notice that holds the case screen while ONE works (`holdingWork`) held it for that line, which is ONE's work still to come and waits on the client: since 0252 it was not who has the case, and now it never holds the screen either (`toCome` of `lib/cases/now.ts`, one rule for both). (2) The papers to sign waited for the client's nationality, marital status and profession, which ONE asks in the chat's check of data (0232); the clock on ONE's server started that check, and its message failed (`openwa_not_configured`): the server's keys have no `OPENWA_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, and the configuration of the platform's OpenWA asked for it to send too. Sending through OpenWA now needs its address and key alone (`openWaSender`); the secret is still asked where a delivery is received and where a number is linked. (3) With that check waiting, the client's next document was answered "Recebi tudo o que eu precisava por agora... eu te mando aqui o link para assinar", which was not so: a turn that finds a check of data open asks its question again (`openDataAsk`), so a message that never arrived is asked again at the client's next word. Checked: vitest of the check left open, of the notice, of the top of the case and of the WhatsApp rules; the typecheck. The case of the test waits for its check of data to reach the client: after the deploy, the failed message is sent again or the client writes.
- **What ONE asks in the chat, said as it is (2026-11-23).** The owner, on the same test case: under "O ONE está fazendo agora" the top said "O ONE pergunta ao cliente no WhatsApp o que aconteceu na viagem, depois que ele assinar", which ONE was not doing, and under "O que trava o caso" it said "Dados de Sonia ...: Nacionalidade, Estado civil, Profissão. O cliente completa pelo link." while ONE asks those in the WhatsApp and had not asked them yet. What ONE does after what waits now is said apart, under "O que o ONE faz depois" (`CaseNow.later`): the account after the signature, and the data ONE asks once the client answered what comes first. The data ONE asks the passenger in the chat (0232) is ONE's work while it has not asked ("O ONE pergunta a ... no WhatsApp"), the client's once it did ("... responde no WhatsApp o que o ONE perguntou"), and the link's only when ONE cannot ask it there: `case_chat_data` says which, `case_data_in_chat` reads it for the case screen, `case_pulse` (redefined from 0219) follows it, and `case_standing` (redefined from 0274) counts it as the client's only once asked, ONE's (`data`, "Perguntando dados no WhatsApp") while it is to ask. Migration `0280_data_asked_in_chat`, pgTAP `0280` (14 assertions, two firms), with the whole embedded suite; vitest of the top of the case and of the reasons of who has the case. The signatures of that case went by gov.br: the firm has no signature account connected, and D4Sign's sandbox is connected by the firm in Conexões with that account's keys. When the client sent the papers back signed, the top said each file twice ("2 documentos esperam a conferência do escritório" and each envelope's "confira e confirme") and called the contract the power of attorney: a paper sent back signed is said once, by its envelope and by its own name (`case-now-envelope-received` with the paper's word), in the top and in `case_standing` alike. ONE does not check a gov.br signature by itself yet: the firm confirms it.
- **A paper sent back unsigned is refused where it arrives (2026-11-23).** The same test case: the files the client sent back as the power of attorney and the contract signed at gov.br were, byte for byte, the PDFs ONE issued (the same SHA-256, no signature in them), and the firm was asked to confirm them as signed. Now the backend, finishing an upload of the link that belongs to an envelope (`client_link_upload_to_finish`, replaced from 0024, says its `envelope_id`), reads whether the PDF carries a signature (`pdfSignatures` of the new `@flyee/documents/pdf-signature`: a signature dictionary whose `/ByteRange` covers the file around a signature value); with none, the upload is taken back and the client is told under the paper to sign it at gov.br and send what gov.br gives back (`paper_unsigned`). Migration `0281_signed_return_of_upload`, pgTAP `0281` (4 assertions, two firms); `node --test` of the rule (a PDF as issued, signed once and twice, a range that does not fit, a reserved field, not a PDF), and the residence round trip now sends the PDF unsigned first (refused, nothing kept) and then signed. Still open: ONE's own check of a signature that is there (valid, gov.br's, the client's CPF, over the document ONE issued), which waits for a file really signed at gov.br; until then the firm confirms it. The two unsigned files of that case arrived before the rule and wait for the firm, which must not confirm them.

- **Filing by hand, with ONE's guide (2026-11-23), the third of the three ways to file.** A lawyer who files in the court's system with their own login now has, on the case's card "Protocolo", the court's screen step by step by its own names, each value read from the case and ready to copy as the court's field takes it ("Ficha do protocolo", `casos/[id]/components/filing-guide.tsx` over `filingGuideOf` of `lib/cases/filing-sheet.ts`, read from `filing_package_sheet`): the eproc of TJSP and of TJRJ by their manuals for lawyers (Informações do processo, Assuntos, Partes autoras, Partes rés, Documentos, Finalizar), the PJe by its steps, and what every filing asks for a system no manual gave (the Projudi of TJGO, its files signed as before); the comarca, the rite, the class and the subjects with their codes in the CNJ's tables (`platform_settings.tpu`: 436 and 7, 4829 to 4832, 7779), the value of the cause or that the judge defines it, the other lawyers by their registration, each claimant with who answers for a minor, the address and the contact (a represented minor's is who answers for them), the airline that answers with its CNPJ and address in Brazil, and the priority of the elderly. And the e-mail and the mobile of each claimant are asked where the case's court asks them (`court_screen_systems.asks_contact`, the eproc of TJSP, whose manual of 13/04 and 20/07/2026 blocks a claimant without "endereço e contato (e-mail e celular)"), before the lawsuits, since ONE creates them and moves the case to `drafting` in one step, after which neither the link nor the chat asks data: one rule, `case_asks_contact` (the case's planned lawsuits, or, before any, for a case of a module, the state court of the holder's domicile), makes an adult's e-mail and mobile data the case lacks (`CONTACT_FIELDS` in `missingItems`, `lacks_chat` of `case_standing`, redefined from 0280), ONE's to ask in the chat until it asked: the check of data asks the e-mail after the profession (step `email`; "NÃO TENHO" lets it go on), the mobile being the WhatsApp the passenger writes from; the case screen (`case_asks_contact_of`), the client's link (`client_link_open`, redefined from 0274) and the backend before the lawsuits read the same rule. Not walked in a browser.
- **What ONE's browser waits for is told to the lawyer (2026-11-24).** The owner, through the Casos session ("pode passar o aviso ao advogado"): a batch of ONE's own browser stopped for its lawyer when the court asked their code (ONE waits five minutes) and when ONE, still learning a system, held its first filing before anything that may send it (thirty minutes), and nothing reached the lawyer outside /protocolo; a batch it stopped for any other reason (the code that never came, a login refused, a captcha, an answer that could not be read) left its lawsuits to approve again with nobody told. - **Changing the way to file before filing, and getting the extension (2026-11-24).** The owner asked how a lawyer changes ONE's way of filing before filing, and found that the extension had been chosen and nothing was installed. - **A lawsuit filed before its case was in ONE, and its history read from the DJEN (2026-11-24).** The owner tests ONE with the firm's lawsuits already filed, closed ones and ones still going, and the receipt of the case at the eproc of TJSP was a year old: the database took no filing day before the case existed in ONE, and once filed a lawsuit was followed only by what the DJEN made available from then on to the lawyer's registration, so a closed lawsuit would wait for ever and one still going would lose all that came before. The owner: "pode fazer as duas partes". (1) The receipt of a lawsuit filed before: on the case's card "Protocolo" a day before the case was opened says so under the fields, and "Anexar recibo" asks first "A ação já tinha sido protocolada?" with what follows (`ConfirmActionDialog`); `attach_filing_receipt` takes the lawyer's word (a fifth argument), keeps it on the receipt (`filing_receipts.filed_before_one`) and in the trail, and says each refusal apart (`filed_on_future`, `filed_before_case` with the day the case was opened, `filed_before_trip`), where it said "Confira as datas informadas." of another form for all of them. (2) Its history: ONE asks the DJEN by the lawsuit's number, once (`DjenClient.listByProcess`, checked on the public API on a lawsuit of the day's listing of TJSP: the number alone, no days, answers the whole history), right after the receipt and in the sweep `djen-history` (`lib/publications/history-work.ts`, `proceeding_history_reads`), and keeps each communication as a publication of the lawsuit marked `from_history`, which ONE triages and reads as any other; a lawyer may ask it on the case's card "Publicações" for a lawsuit recorded filed by hand before the case, or again after a reading that failed (`ask_proceeding_history`), and each publication says "Origem: Histórico do processo no DJEN". (3) What had passed comes in settled: a publication of the history owes no awareness (0104); a deadline counted from one whose day had passed is done, with why; a hearing read from one whose time had passed is held, its result not read, with why, and owes no fee per hearing (0159); its payment warns the client of nothing (0248's `payment_alert`); what is still ahead is as any other. The client is told of the filing only when it was of late, as before. Migration `0286_lawsuits_from_the_past`, pgTAP `0286` (35, two firms). Checked: the adapter's contract with the new search on the fake and on the stand-in of the API, vitest of the rule of the case's lines and of the backend's half, the whole pgTAP suite on an embedded database with the migration, the typecheck, check:messages and check:db-refs. Where it waits: the DJEN is read in this environment only once `DJEN_CAPTURE` allows it, and the clock's file says 0 (the owner's to change).
